The first decade focused on foundational linguistic infrastructure; the second shifted toward computational social science, social media analysis, and socially oriented applications. Rather than cataloguing outputs, the paper examines what the experience of building them revealed. Three counterintuitive lessons emerge: building datasets is as much a social process as a technical one; communities formed around shared tasks often matter more than the tasks themselves; and moving from language resources to computational social science exposes challenges that traditional NLP training does not address. We discuss three failures: a depression detection corpus that never reached clinical practice, a period of spreading across too many shared tasks without sufficient depth, and a long-standing assumption that Modern Standard Arabic infrastructure would transfer cleanly to dialectal tasks. These experiences suggest that the hardest problems in developing NLP for underserved communities are not linguistic but social, institutional, and epistemic, and require competencies the field rarely teaches.

</details>


### [38] [Assessing socio-economic climate impacts from text data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20793)
*Mariana Madruga de Brito,Brielen Madureira,Taís Maria Nunes Carvalho,Damien Delforge,Aglaé Jézéquel,Murathan Kurfalı,Ni Li,Gabriele Messori,Joakim Nivre,Barbara Pernici,Niko Speybroeck,Stefano Terzi,Wim Thiery,Bram Valkenborg,Jingxian Wang,Shorouq Zahra,Jakob Zscheischler,Jan Sodoge*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: This paper addresses the methodological fragmentation in text-as-data approaches for climate hazard socio-economic impact assessment by synthesizing current practices, identifying key challenges (impact definition, temporal/spatial biases, modeling selection), and proposing practical recommendations to establish best practice standards and improve dataset robustness for disaster risk management applications.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The text-as-data field for climate hazard impact assessment is rapidly expanding but remains methodologically fragmented. Critical issues including impact definition standards, temporal/spatial bias handling, and modeling strategy selection lack clear guidelines, limiting cross-study comparability and transparency.

Method: Synthesizes common practices from existing literature, identifies key methodological challenges specific to text-as-data approaches for socio-economic impact analysis, and develops evidence-based recommendations to address identified gaps.

Result: A comprehensive framework of best practices and recommendations for constructing reliable text-derived socio-economic impact datasets, addressing methodological challenges specific to climate hazard analysis.

Conclusion: Provides actionable guidance for constructing robust text-derived datasets that can improve disaster risk management and climate attribution studies, while promoting transparency and methodological comparability.

Abstract: Recent advances in natural language processing (NLP) and large language models (LLMs) have enabled the systematic use of large-scale textual data from news, social media, and reports to create datasets with socio-economic impacts of climate hazards such as floods, droughts, storms, and multi-hazard events. As the field of text-as-data for impact assessment expands, so does its methodological complexity. Yet research remains fragmented, with no clear guidelines for defining what constitutes an impact, handling temporal and spatial biases, and selecting appropriate modeling and post-processing strategies. This lack of coherence limits transparency and comparability across studies. Here, we address this gap by synthesising common practices, describing key challenges specific to the use of text-as-data methods for analyzing socio-economic impact data, and proposing recommendations to address them. By providing guidance on best practices, we aim to support the construction of robust text-derived socio-economic impact datasets that can more accurately inform disaster risk management and attribution studies.

</details>


### [39] [Refining and Reusing Annotation Guidelines for LLM Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20809)
*Kon Woo Kim,Jin-Dong Kim,Akiko Aizawa*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出通过迭代式审核框架重用和细化标注指南作为对齐机制，在生物医学命名实体识别任务上验证了指南整合、推理优化模型和最小监督审核三个假设的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在零样本标注任务中表现优异，但在金牌标准 benchmarks 的专业规范方面仍存在困难，需要一种有效的对齐机制来解决这一问题。

Method: 采用迭代式审核框架，模拟标注项目的早期阶段，通过系统性地重用和细化标注指南进行对齐。在三个生物医学 NER 任务（NCBI Disease、BC5CDR、BioRED）上测试了三个 LLM 家族（GPT、Gemini、DeepSeek）。

Result: 实验结果 empirically 验证了全部三个假设，迭代审核框架在有效细化指南方面展现出良好潜力。

Conclusion: 该方法验证有效，但分析也揭示了仍有 substantial 改进空间。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) demonstrate remarkable performance on zero-shot annotation tasks, they often struggle with the specialized conventions of gold-standard benchmarks. We propose the systematic reuse and refinement of annotation guidelines as an alignment mechanism, introducing an iterative moderation framework that simulates the early phases of annotation projects. We evaluate three hypotheses: (1) the efficacy of guideline integration, (2) the advantage of reasoning optimized models, and (3) the viability of moderation under minimal supervision. Testing across biomedical NER tasks (NCBI Disease, BC5CDR, BioRED) with three LLM families (GPT, Gemini, DeepSeek), our results empirically confirm all three hypotheses. While the iterative moderation framework shows good potential in effectively refining guidelines, our analysis also reveals substantial room for improvement.

</details>


### [40] [PulseCol: Periodically Refreshed Column-Sparse Attention for Accelerating Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20813)
*Yanyi Lyu,Letian Chen,Futing Sun,Miao Zhang,Weili Guan,Liqiang Nie*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PulseCol通过周期性刷新的列稀疏注意力机制加速扩散语言模型，在早期去噪步骤识别稀疏模式并跨迭代复用，相比FlashAttention实现最高1.95倍端到端加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing sparse attention methods for dLLMs rely on block-sparse computation, which is only applied in later iterations due to model performance sensitivity, yielding limited efficiency improvements and acceleration gains. There is a need for finer-grained sparsification that can be applied earlier and leverages reusable sparsity patterns.

Method: PulseCol introduces a periodically refreshed column-sparse attention mechanism. It replaces coarse block-level sparsity with finer-grained column-level sparsity to retain important attention interactions more precisely while exposing greater sparsity. The method identifies sparse patterns at early denoising steps and reuses them across iterations, refreshing only at a small number of intermediate steps to track the evolution of sparse attention patterns.

Result: Experiments show PulseCol achieves higher sparsity and greater practical speedup than prior sparse attention methods for dLLMs while maintaining model quality. With optimized GPU kernels for column-sparse attention, it delivers up to 1.95× end-to-end speedup over FlashAttention across several context lengths.

Conclusion: PulseCol provides a practical solution for accelerating dLLMs with optimized GPU kernels, achieving up to 1.95× end-to-end speedup while maintaining model quality.

Abstract: Inference in diffusion large language models (dLLMs) is computationally expensive, as full self-attention must be repeatedly executed at each step of the denoising process without KV cache. Recent sparse attention methods for dLLMs mitigate this cost via block-sparse computation, which is applied only in later iterations when model performance is less sensitive to coarse-grained sparse approximation, but yields limited improvements in computational efficiency and acceleration. This motivates a finer-grained sparsification strategy that can be applied from earlier iterations and leverages reusable sparsity patterns, enabling further efficiency gains. In this work, we introduce PulseCol, a periodically refreshed column-sparse attention method for accelerating diffusion language models. PulseCol replaces coarse block-level sparsity with a finer-grained column-sparse structure, allowing important attention interactions to be retained more precisely while exposing greater sparsity. Built on this column-level formulation, PulseCol further identifies sparse patterns at the early denoising step and reuses them across subsequent iterations, refreshing them only at a small number of intermediate steps to track the evolution of sparse attention patterns during denoising. Experiments show that PulseCol achieves higher sparsity and greater practical speedup than prior sparse attention methods for dLLMs, while maintaining model quality. Enabled by optimized GPU kernels for column-sparse attention, PulseCol delivers up to 1.95$\times$ end-to-end speedup over FlashAttention across several context lengths.

</details>


### [41] [MemGym: a Long-Horizon Memory Environment for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20833)
*Wujiang Xu,Yu Wang,Kai Mei,Kaiqu Liang,Zhenting Wang,Mingyu Jin,Han Zhang,Shi-Xiong Zhang,Wenyue Hua,Sambit Sahu,Dimitris N. Metaxas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MemGym是一个面向LLM Agent记忆能力的评测基准，通过统一接口整合现有Agent环境和新合成管道，在5条评测轨道上实现记忆能力与其他能力（推理、检索、工具使用）的解耦，并引入轻量级奖励模型MemRM使编码环境评估具备学术可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing memory benchmarks focus on personalized information retention in multi-turn chat but miss the dynamic memory formation during extended agent execution. Consequently, developed memory systems transfer poorly to real-world agentic scenarios like coding and web navigation.

Method: Authors propose MemGym, a unified benchmark with memory-reasoning interface integrating existing agent gyms (tau2-bench, SWE-Gym, WebArena-Infinity) and novel synthetic pipelines (MEMGYM-DR, MEMGYM-CODEQA). They introduce memory-isolated scoring methodology to decouple memory performance from other capabilities, and train MemRM (Qwen3-1.7B with QLoRA) as a lightweight reward model to replace computationally expensive Docker rollouts for coding evaluation.

Result: MemGym provides five evaluation tracks across four agentic regimes (tool-use dialogue, deep-research search, coding, computer use) with length-controllable and ablation-verified synthetic pipelines tightly aligned with downstream scenarios.

Conclusion: MemGym establishes a standardized benchmark framework enabling reliable ranking of memory strategies by isolating memory performance from reasoning, retrieval, and tool-use confounders.

Abstract: Memory is a central capability for LLM agents operating across long-horizon tasks. Existing memory benchmarks predominantly evaluate retention of personalized information in multi-turn chat scenarios, overlooking the dynamic memory formation that occurs during extended agent execution. Consequently, the memory systems they produce transfer poorly to realistic agentic environments, such as coding and web navigation. We present MemGym, a benchmark for agentic memory that unifies existing agent gyms and in-house memory-grounded pipelines behind one memory-reasoning interface. MemGym spans five evaluation tracks grouped into four agentic regimes: tool-use dialogue (tau2-bench), multi-turn deep-research search (MEMGYM-DR), coding (SWE-Gym and MEMGYM-CODEQA), and computer use (WebArena-Infinity). MemGym reports memory-isolated scores that decouple memory performance from reasoning, retrieval, and tool-use ability, so memory strategies can be ranked without those confounders. Our synthetic pipelines for MEMGYM-CODEQA and MEMGYM-DR are length-controllable, ablation-verified at every stage, and tightly aligned with downstream scenarios. To make evaluation on coding environments academically tractable, we train MemRM, a lightweight reward model (Qwen3-1.7B fine-tuned with QLoRA) that scores compression quality as a fast scalar read in place of full Docker rollouts.

</details>


### [42] [Terminal-World: Scaling Terminal-Agent Environments via Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20876)
*Zihao Cheng,Hongru Wang,Zeming Liu,Xinyi Wang,Xiangrong Zhu,Yuhang Guo,Wei Lin,Jeff Z. Pan,Yunhong Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Terminal-World是一个用于训练终端智能体的全自动化数据合成流水线。它以智能体技能为核心原语，联合合成任务指令、运行环境和支持轨迹，成功在仅使用1.2%训练数据的情况下超越了使用全部数据的Nemotron-Terminal-32B模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前终端智能体的发展受限于高质量训练数据的稀缺。现有方法从人类定义的种子或GitHub仓库等部分来源引导，导致任务局限于窄分布种子、环境与任务语义不一致、探索轨迹效率低下。这是终端智能体进一步发展的核心瓶颈。

Method: 提出Terminal-World全自动化流水线，以智能体技能作为核心综合原语。技能联合编码了"做什么"（任务目标）、"何时应用"（前置条件和环境状态）、"如何执行"（执行方式），从而实现任务指令、环境和教师轨迹的协同推导。该流水线还通过技能团队（skill teams）和技能图（skill graphs）进行组合，支持多角色和跨域任务合成。

Result: 构建了5,723个训练环境，训练了Terminal-World-8B/14B/32B三个规模的模型。在6个基准测试中一致优于终端智能体基线。仅使用1.2%的训练数据，Terminal-World-32B在Terminal-Bench 2.0上Pass@1达到31.5（比Nemotron-Terminal-32B提升4.5分），Pass@3达到43.8。

Conclusion: Terminal-World通过技能作为核心综合原语，成功构建了5,723个训练环境，训练出8B/14B/32B三种规模的模型。该方法在6个基准测试中一致优于基线方法，使用仅1.2%的训练数据即可超越Nemotron-Terminal-32B，在Terminal-Bench 2.0上达到31.5 Pass@1（提升4.5分）和43.8 Pass@3，证明了技能驱动联合合成的有效性。

Abstract: Terminal agents extend Large Language Models with the ability to execute tasks directly in command-line environments, but their progress is bottlenecked by the scarcity of high-quality training data. Existing approaches bootstrap from partial sources such as human-defined seeds or GitHub repositories to instantiate one component and then complete the rest, producing tasks confined to narrow seed distributions, environments misaligned with task semantics, and inefficient trajectories from unguided exploration. To address these limitations, we introduce Terminal-World, a fully automated pipeline that uses agent skills as the central synthesis primitive, which jointly encode what to accomplish, when to apply (preconditions and environment state), and how to execute, enabling task instructions, environments, and teacher trajectories to be co-derived. To further broaden the synthesis space, Terminal-World composes skills into skill teams and skill graphs for multi-role and cross-domain task synthesis. Using this pipeline, we construct 5,723 training environments and train Terminal-World-8B/14B/32B, evaluated across 6 benchmarks where the Terminal-World series consistently outperforms terminal-agent baselines. Notably, using the same teacher model and only 1.2% of the training data, Terminal-World-32B surpasses Nemotron-Terminal-32B on Terminal-Bench 2.0 by +4.5 Pass@1 (31.5) and achieves 43.8 Pass@3.

</details>


### [43] [Enhancing Scientific Discourse: Machine Translation for the Scientific Domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20912)
*Dimitris Roussis,Sokratis Sofianopoulos,Stelios Piperidis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了面向科学领域的多语言平行语料库和单语语料库的开发工作，涵盖西班牙语-英语、法语-英语和葡萄牙语-英语三个语言对。每个语言对包含一个通用科学大语料库和四个专业领域语料库（癌症研究、能源研究、神经科学、交通运输）。研究者利用这些语料库对通用神经机器翻译系统进行微调，并提供了详细的语料库构建流程和评估结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The increasing volume of scientific research creates a need for effective cross-language communication. Machine translation faces unique challenges in the scientific domain due to specialized vocabulary and complex sentence structures.

Method: Creation of parallel and monolingual corpora for three language pairs (Spanish-English, French-English, Portuguese-English), organized into a large general scientific corpus plus four domain-specific subcorpora (Cancer Research, Energy Research, Neuroscience, Transportation). These corpora are then used to fine-tune general-purpose NMT systems.

Result: The paper provides evaluation results showing that the domain-specific corpora can effectively improve NMT system performance for scientific translation tasks.

Conclusion: The evaluation results demonstrate the effectiveness of the domain-specific corpora for improving neural machine translation quality in the scientific domain.

Abstract: The increasing volume of scientific research necessitates effective communication across language barriers. Machine translation (MT) offers a promising solution for accessing international publications. However, the scientific domain presents unique challenges due to its specialized vocabulary and complex sentence structures. In this paper, we present the development of a collection of parallel and monolingual corpora for the scientific domain. The corpora target the language pairs Spanish-English, French-English, and Portuguese-English. For each language pair, we create a large general scientific corpus as well as four smaller corpora focused on the domains of: Cancer Research, Energy Research, Neuroscience, and Transportation research. To evaluate the quality of these corpora, we utilize them for fine-tuning general-purpose neural machine translation (NMT) systems. We provide details regarding the corpus creation process, the fine-tuning strategies employed, and we conclude with the evaluation results.

</details>


### [44] [Strategy-Induct: Task-Level Strategy Induction for Instruction Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20924)
*Po-Chun Chen,Hen-Hsen Huang,Hsin-Hsi Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Strategy-Induct框架，通过生成推理策略并与问题配对来诱导任务指令，使LLM能够在没有标注答案的情况下，仅凭少量示例问题设计有效的任务级提示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖输入-输出对获取标注答案，成本高昂或难以获取，限制了实际应用。

Method: 首先提示模型为每个问题生成显式推理策略，形成(strategy, question)对；然后利用这些配对诱导生成任务指令来指导后续推理。

Result: 在多个任务和模型规模上，Strategy-Induct在仅问题设置下优于现有最优方法。

Conclusion: Strategy-Induct框架仅需少量示例问题即可生成高质量任务指令，在无标注答案的场景下优于现有方法，且联合使用LLM和LRM可能带来进一步性能提升。

Abstract: Designing effective task-level prompts is crucial for improving the performance of Large Language Models (LLMs). While prior work on instruction induction demonstrates that LLMs can infer better instructions with limited examples, existing approaches often rely on input-output pairs, where obtaining labeled answers can be difficult or costly. To address this limitation, we propose Strategy-Induct, a framework that derives task-level instructions solely from a small set of example questions without requiring labeled answers. Our approach first prompts the model to generate explicit reasoning strategies for each question, forming (strategy, question) pairs. These pairs are then used to induce a task instruction that guides reasoning. Experiments across multiple tasks and model scales demonstrate that Strategy-Induct outperforms state-of-the-art methods in question-only settings. Furthermore, we observe that jointly utilizing LLMs and Large Reasoning Models across task instruction generation and inference may lead to further performance improvements.

</details>


### [45] [Thinking-while-speaking: A Controlled, Interleaved Reasoning Method for Real-Time Speech Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20946)
*Xuan Du,Qiangyu Yan,Wenshuo Li,Borui Jiang,Changming Xiao,Han Shu,Xinghao Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: InterRS addresses the thinking-while-speaking challenge by inserting reasoning steps during natural speech pauses. It uses a novel pipeline for aligned audio data generation and combines interleaved SFT with RL training (TA-Balance and Linguistic Quality rewards) to achieve 13% improvement on benchmarks while maintaining speech fluency and naturalness.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: A key challenge in making AI communication more human-like through thinking-while-speaking is maintaining fluent speech while performing deep reasoning. This requires high-quality data where reasoning and speech are precisely aligned with controlled length ratios.

Method: InterRS inserts reasoning steps only during natural speech generation. A novel pipeline generates seamlessly interleaved audio data with precisely aligned reasoning and speech under controlled length ratios. Training combines interleaved SFT with refined data and RL with two new rewards: TA-Balance Reward (manages timing and thinking-answer ratio) and Linguistic Quality Reward (refines expression).

Result: 13% better performance on mathematical and logic benchmarks; generates instant responses like spoken-language instruct models with fast CoT response; produces more natural and fluent answers than prior methods.

Conclusion: A novel InterRS method is proposed that inserts reasoning steps during natural speech generation, achieving 13% performance improvement on mathematical and logic benchmarks while maintaining instant response generation and more natural, fluent answers.

Abstract: The thinking-while-speaking paradigm aims to make AI communication more human. A key challenge is maintaining fluent speech while performing deep reasoning. Our method, InterRS, tackles this by inserting reasoning steps only during natural speech generation. This requires high-quality data where reasoning and speech are precisely aligned, and the length ratio are under controlled. We introduce a novel pipeline to generate such seamlessly interleaved audio data. To train our model, we combine interleaved SFT with refined data and reinforcement learning with two new rewards: a TA-Balance Reward to manage timing and thinking-answer ratio, and a Linguistic Quality Reward to refine expression. Experiments show our approach achieves 13% better performance on mathmatical and logic benchmarks while generating instant response like a spoken-language instruct model which outputs fast CoT response. Furthermore, our method generates more natural and fluent answers than prior methods.

</details>


### [46] [Memory Grafting: Scaling Language Model Pre-training via Offline Conditional Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20948)
*Runxi Cheng,Yuchen Guan,Yongxian Wei,Qianpu Sun,Qixiu Li,Sinan Du,Feng Xiong,Chun Yuan,Yan Lu,Yeyun Gong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Memory Grafting方法，通过利用冻结 grafting 模型的隐藏状态作为条件n-gram记忆来实现条件记忆扩展。该方法在离线运行grafting模型，将最终token的隐藏表示存储为记忆值，通过最长后缀匹配查找检索记忆，并使用轻量级投影和门控进行适配。实验表明，在2.8B规模下平均基准分数从51.95(MoE)和52.43(Engram)提升至53.86；在0.92B规模下所有grafting模型变体均超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有条件记忆扩展方法（如Engram）需要从头学习大规模记忆表，导致记忆扩展成本高且效果有限。

Method: Memory Grafting利用冻结grafting模型的隐藏状态作为条件n-gram记忆：1)离线运行grafting模型存储最终token隐藏表示；2)通过最长后缀匹配查找检索记忆；3)使用轻量级投影和门控适配检索结果；4)基于哈希的Engram作为未匹配上下文的回退机制。

Result: 在2.8B规模，平均基准分数从51.95(MoE)和52.43(Engram)提升至53.86。在0.92B规模，所有grafting模型变体均超越基线，Qwen3.5-35B-A3B表现最佳。

Conclusion: 预训练模型可作为外部潜在记忆的可重用构造器，为超越可训练参数扩展语言模型能力提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Scaling conditional memory offers a promising way to increase language-model capacity, but existing methods such as Engram learn large memory tables from scratch during pre-training, making memory scaling expensive and sometimes ineffective. We propose Memory Grafting, a conditional memory scaling method that utilizes frozen hidden states from a grafting model as conditional n-gram memory. Given frequent local n-grams, we run the grafting model offline, store final-token hidden representations as memory values, and let the recipient model retrieve them through exact longest-match suffix lookup. Retrieved memories are adapted by lightweight projections and gates, while a hash-based Engram fallback preserves coverage for unmatched contexts. Since the grafting model is only run offline and exact lookup has expected O(1) complexity with respect to memory-bank size, Memory Grafting expands external latent capacity with limited training and inference overhead. Experiments under matched recipient architectures and pre-training budgets show that Memory Grafting improves over both MoE and vanilla Engram baselines. In the 2.8B-scale setting, it improves the average benchmark score from 51.95 for MoE and 52.43 for vanilla Engram to 53.86. In the 0.92B-scale setting, all grafting-model variants improve over the baselines, with Qwen3.5-35B-A3B giving the strongest gains. These results suggest that pretrained models can serve as reusable constructors of external latent memory, providing a practical step toward scaling future language models beyond trainable parameters alone.

</details>


### [47] [ArPoMeme: An Annotated Arabic Multimodal Dataset for Political Ideology and Polarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20967)
*Wajdi Zaghouani,Kais Attia,Md. Rafiul Biswas,Fadhl Eryani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ArPoMeme，一个包含约7,300个阿拉伯语政治meme的大规模数据集，按意识形态倾向（左翼、伊斯兰主义、泛阿拉伯主义和讽刺）分类，通过半自动化数据收集管道构建，可用于分析政治对抗、动员和幽默的多模态特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Despite the centrality of memes to online political discourse in the Arab world, there is a lack of systematically curated resources for analyzing their multimodal and ideological dimensions in Arabic.

Method: Semi-automated data collection pipeline combining Playwright-based Facebook scraping with Google Drive synchronization, followed by Qwen2.5-VL-7B vision language model text extraction and manual annotation of three polarization dimensions via a custom Streamlit-based interface.

Result: Quantitative analysis reveals strong asymmetries in antagonistic framing across ideological groups, with Islamist and satirical memes exhibiting the highest levels of hostility and mobilization cues.

Conclusion: ArPoMeme is a reproducible and publicly available resource that advances the study of Arabic political discourse, multimodal ideology detection, and polarization dynamics.

Abstract: Memes have become a prominent medium of political communication in the Arab world, reflecting how humor, imagery, and text interact to express ideological and cultural positions. Despite the centrality of memes to online political discourse, there is a lack of systematically curated resources for analyzing their multimodal and ideological dimensions in Arabic. This paper presents ArPoMeme, a large-scale dataset of approximately 7,300 Arabic political memes categorized by ideological orientation, including Leftist, Islamist, Pan-Arabist, and Satirical perspectives. The dataset captures the diversity of Arabic meme ecosystems by grounding classification in the self-identification of public Facebook pages and groups that produce and disseminate these memes. To ensure both scale and accuracy, we designed a semi-automated data collection pipeline combining Playwright-based Facebook scraping with Google Drive synchronization, followed by text extraction using the Qwen2.5-VL-7B vision language model. The extracted text was manually verified and annotated for three polarization dimensions: Us vs. Them framing, Hostility toward out-groups, and Calls to action. Annotation was conducted through a custom Streamlit-based interface supporting distributed labeling, real-time tracking, and version control. The resulting dataset links visual content, textual messages, and ideological orientation, enabling fine-grained analysis of political antagonism, mobilization, and humor. Quantitative analysis of the annotated corpus reveals strong asymmetries in antagonistic framing across ideological groups, with Islamist and satirical memes exhibiting the highest levels of hostility and mobilization cues. The dataset and the annotation tool offers a reproducible and publicly available resource for studying Arabic political discourse, multimodal ideology detection, and polarization dynamics.

</details>


### [48] [Towards Context-Invariant Safety Alignment for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20994)
*Yixu Wang,Yang Yao,Xin Wang,Yifeng Gao,Yan Teng,Xingjun Ma,Yingchun Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出AIR方法，通过将可验证提示作为锚点，仅对开放式变体进行正则化，结合异构提示分组与GRPO训练，显著提升LLM安全对齐的上下文不变性，使模型对对抗性措辞保持鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current preference-based alignment makes safety behavior brittle - models may refuse harmful requests but comply when the same intent is wrapped in adversarial wording. Robust safety requires context-invariant alignment where behavior depends on underlying intent rather than surface form.

Method: Anchor Invariance Regularization (AIR) - treats verifiable prompts as anchors, uses stop-gradient target to regularize only open-ended variants toward anchor performance, implemented as plug-in auxiliary loss combined with group-based preference optimization (GRPO) via heterogeneous prompt grouping.

Result: Across Safety, Moral Reasoning, and Math benchmarks: in-distribution group accuracy improved by 12.71%, out-of-distribution consistency improved by 33.49%.

Conclusion: AIR is a plug-in auxiliary loss combined with GRPO via heterogeneous prompt grouping, improving in-distribution accuracy by 12.71% and out-of-distribution consistency by 33.49%, making safety constraints robust to adversarial framings.

Abstract: Preference-based post-training aligns LLMs with human intent, yet safety behavior often remains brittle. A model may refuse a harmful request in a standard prompt but comply when the same intent is wrapped in adversarial wording. We suggest that robust safety requires context-invariant alignment, where behavior depends on the underlying intent rather than surface form. Enforcing invariance is difficult in alignment because not all training signals are equally trustworthy; for some prompt variants we can obtain verifiable feedback (e.g., multiple-choice), while for open-ended variants we typically rely on noisy, gameable reward proxies (e.g., learned judges). As a result, standard symmetric invariance regularizers can reduce cross-context discrepancies by lowering performance on reliable variants instead of improving open-ended robustness. To address this, we introduce Anchor Invariance Regularization (AIR), which treats verifiable prompts as anchors and uses a stop-gradient target to regularize only the open-ended variants toward the anchor performance. AIR is implemented as a plug-in auxiliary loss and combined with group-based preference optimization (e.g., GRPO) via heterogeneous prompt grouping. Across Safety, Moral Reasoning, and Math, AIR improves context invariance, boosting in-distribution group accuracy by 12.71% and out-of-distribution consistency by 33.49%, making safety constraints robust to adversarial framings.

</details>


### [49] [Single-Pass, Depth-Selective Reading for Multi-Aspect Sentiment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20998)
*Yan Xia,Zhuangzhuang Pan,Amirrudin Kamsin,Chee Seng Chan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DABS框架解决多方面情感分析中的效率-表达力权衡问题。通过将句子编码与轻量级方面读取解耦，构建可重用的深度有序基质，实现单次编码多次查询，在保持性能的同时降低60%计算开销，特别适用于语言复杂的否定和对比场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型在处理多方面句子时面临效率与表达力的基本权衡：要么为每个方面重新编码句子导致计算冗余，要么静态使用深度表示导致适应性不足。

Method: 提出DABS框架，将句子编码与特定方面读取解耦。通过单次编码构建可重用的深度有序基质，每个方面通过查询机制选择性地读取相关token和抽象层级，无需重复编码。

Result: 在四个ATSA基准数据集上验证，DABS在保持竞争力的同时，多方面场景下端到端计算量减少60%；自适应深度查询对否定、对比等语言复杂场景最为有效。

Conclusion: DABS achieves competitive performance while reducing end-to-end computation by up to 60% in multi-aspect settings (M >= 2), with adaptive depth querying being most beneficial for linguistically complex cases.

Abstract: Aspect-Term Sentiment Analysis (ATSA) in multi-aspect sentences faces a fundamental tradeoff between efficiency and expressiveness. Existing models either re-encode the sentence for each aspect or rely on static use of deep representations, leading to redundant computation and limited adaptivity. We argue that Transformer depth is a costly, queryable resource, and propose DABS, a single-pass inference framework that encodes each sentence once to construct a reusable, depth-ordered substrate. Each aspect then queries this shared representation to selectively read relevant tokens and abstraction levels, without re-encoding. This decouples shared sentence encoding from lightweight, aspect-conditioned readout. Experiments on four ATSA benchmarks show that DABS achieves competitive performance while reducing end-to-end computation by up to 60% in multi-aspect settings (M >= 2). Further analyses indicate that adaptive depth querying is most beneficial for linguistically complex cases such as negation and contrast. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/panzhzh/acl-dabs

</details>


### [50] [APM: Evaluating Style Personalization in LLMs with Arbitrary Preference Mappings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21063)
*Philipp Spohn,Leander Girrbach,Zeynep Akata*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出APM基准测试，通过随机映射机制解耦用户属性与响应偏好以消除评估偏差。研究发现路由个性化方法最为可靠，而检索增强和软提示优化方法效果有限，表明在隐式偏好场景下实现有效个性化仍具挑战性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型响应往往遵循默认风格，但用户存在隐式偏好（语气、详细程度、正式程度）。现有评估面临三大挑战：用户仅提供提示而非参考响应、风格偏好难以事实验证、无参考LLM评判可能将个性化与通用质量混淆。

Method: 提出APM（任意偏好映射）基准测试，通过隐藏的随机映射矩阵C将用户属性与响应原则解耦。由于C无语义内容且每次运行重新采样，模型无法利用刻板关联，必须从对话历史中推断偏好。在Llama-3.1-8B和Qwen-3.5-27B上评估了检索增强、提示优化和路由三种个性化方法。

Result: 路由方法最为可靠；RAG仅在较强基础模型上改善；软提示优化相比非个性化基线无显著提升。

Conclusion: APM基准测试提供了一个无偏的评估框架。在该现实设置中，个性化仍然具有挑战性，但路由方法表现出前景，而RAG和软提示优化方法的效果有限。

Abstract: Typical LLM responses tend to follow a default style, even though users often have distinct preferences regarding tone, verbosity, and formality that they do not explicitly state in their prompts. Evaluating whether personalization methods can adapt to these implicit preferences is challenging, since users typically provide prompts rather than reference responses, style preferences are not factually verifiable, and reference-free LLM judges may conflate personalization with general response quality. To address these challenges, we introduce the Arbitrary Preference Mapping (APM) benchmark, which decouples user attributes (e.g. enthusiastic) from response principles (e.g. persuasive) via a hidden, randomized mapping $\mathbf{C}$ that maps user attributes to preferences about response traits. Because $\mathbf{C}$ carries no semantic content and is resampled across runs, models cannot exploit stereotypical associations and must infer preferences from conversation history. Using this unbiased evaluation methodology, we adapt retrieval-augmented, prompt-optimization, and routing personalization methods and evaluate them on Llama-3.1-8B and Qwen-3.5-27B. Our results show that routing is the most reliable approach, while RAG only improves with the stronger base LLM, and soft prompt optimization fails to improve significantly over a non-personalized baseline. Our extensive evaluation reveals that in this realistic setting, personalization remains challenging, but our adapted methods show promise.

</details>


### [51] [Fine-grained Claim-level RAG Benchmark for Law](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21071)
*Souvick Das,Sallam Abualhaija,Domenico Bianculli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了ClaimRAG-LAW数据集，用于评估法律RAG系统。该数据集支持法语和英语，涵盖专家和非专家用户，并包含多样化的现实问题类型。研究通过对先进法律RAG系统的细粒度评估，揭示了当前系统在检索、生成和声明级别分析方面存在的具体局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前法律RAG系统仍存在不同速率的幻觉问题，需要精细化评估；现有评估框架缺乏对检索和生成性能的分别细致分析能力；现有基准主要针对英语且聚焦法律专家查询，忽视了非专家用户的需求。

Method: 构建了一个多语言（法语和英语）、覆盖专家和非专家用户的法律RAG数据集，并应用细粒度评估框架对先进的法律RAG系统进行了系统评估。

Result: 该数据集支持法英双语、覆盖专家和非专家用户、包含多样化的现实场景问题类型；细粒度评估揭示了当前系统在检索、生成和声明级别分析方面的具体局限。

Conclusion: 通过ClaimRAG-LAW数据集揭示了当前法律RAG系统在检索、生成和声明级别分析方面的局限性，为法律领域RAG系统的改进提供了重要参考。

Abstract: The rapid progress of large language models (LLMs) is shifting semantic search toward a question-answering paradigm, where users ask questions and LLMs generate responses. In high-stake domains such as law, retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is commonly used to mitigate hallucinations in generated responses. Nonetheless, prior work shows that RAG systems, whether general-purpose or legal-specific, still hallucinate at varying rates, making fine-grained evaluation essential. Despite the need, existing evaluation frameworks for legal RAG systems lack the granularity required to provide detailed analysis of retrieval and generation performance separately. Moreover, current benchmarks are largely English-only and centered on legal expert queries, overlooking non-expert needs. We introduce ClaimRAG-LAW, a comprehensive dataset for legal RAG that supports French and English, targets both experts and non-experts, and includes diverse question types reflecting realistic scenarios. We further apply a fine-grained evaluation framework of state-of-the-art legal RAG systems, revealing limitations in retrieval, generation, and claim-level analysis in the legal domain.

</details>


### [52] [GradeLegal: Automated Grading for German Legal Cases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21076)
*Abdullah Al Zubaer,Lorenz Wendlinger,Simon Alexander Nonn,Michael Granitzer,Jelena Mitrovic*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探索LLM在德国法律考试（刑法和公法）自动评分中的可行性，评估27个模型及多种提示策略。结果显示：公法评分表现优异（QWK达0.91），刑法较难（0.60）；提示设计（样题+评分标准）和模型选择至关重要；集成方法可有效替代更强的闭源单模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 德国国家考试评分面临日益增长的评卷量和合格评分者短缺的双重压力，导致反馈延迟和瓶颈；同时作为高风险专家任务，考试成绩直接影响职业发展。然而，现有文献缺乏对法律考试评分有效方法的系统性研究。

Method: 对27个专有和开源LLM进行系统性评估，测试逐步增加任务相关信息的提示策略（包括样题答案和评分标准），使用二次加权kappa（QWK）衡量评分一致性。

Result: 推理导向型LLM在提供样题答案和评分标准后，公法评分与专家评分一致性可达0.91（QWK），而刑法仅0.60。集成模型比最佳单模型提升一致性达0.15。

Conclusion: 有效的提示设计（sample solution + grading rubric）和模型选择是实现可靠LLM法律考试评分的关键要素。集成方法可作为强大闭源单模型的替代方案，提升评分一致性。

Abstract: Grading German legal exam solutions faces growing volumes and a shortage of qualified graders, delaying feedback and creating a bottleneck. At the same time, it is a high-stakes expert task, since state exam grades strongly influence career outcomes in Germany. Despite this practical relevance, literature lacks systematic studies on effective methods for grading legal exams. To address this gap, we investigate whether large language models (LLMs) can support the automated grading of German legal case solutions in criminal and public law, thereby enabling scalable feedback and student self-testing. We present a systematic evaluation of 27 proprietary and open-source LLMs, benchmarking prompting strategies that incrementally add task-related information, such as a sample solution and a grading rubric. Using quadratic weighted kappa (QWK), reasoning-oriented LLMs can approximate expert grading in public law when given a sample solution and a grading rubric (up to 0.91), compared to 0.60 in criminal law, suggesting a harder grading task in criminal law. Beyond single-model grading, ensembling improves agreement by up to 0.15 over its best member and can offer an alternative to stronger closed-source single models. In addition, our findings suggest that effective prompt design and model selection are necessary for reliable LLM-based grading of legal exams.

</details>


### [53] [LoCar: Localization-Aware Evaluation of In-Vehicle Assistants through Fine-Grained Sociolinguistic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21086)
*Seogyeong Jeong,Kiwoong Park,Seyoung Song,Eunsu Kim,Ken E. Friedl,Jaeho Kim,Alice Oh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出针对韩语本地化的车载助手LLM评估框架。研究发现当前模型在韩语敬语控制和策略性对话能力上存在不足，建议汽车AI应注重精确语言适配和安全导向交互管理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLM）虽已广泛集成到车载对话系统中，但缺乏针对真实部署需求的领域特定评估标准，导致难以识别最优模型。

Method: 提出针对车载助手的评估框架，聚焦韩语本地化场景，通过细粒度分析评估模型的韩语敬语控制能力和策略性对话指标（如澄清、主动性）表现。

Result: （1）当前LLM的细粒度韩语敬语控制仍不稳定，在本地化场景中需要明确评估精确的语级实现；（2）模型在策略性对话指标（如澄清、主动性）上表现较弱，这源于此类任务的主观复杂性。

Conclusion: 汽车AI需要超越通用能力，向精确的语言适配和可靠的安全导向交互管理方向发展。框架采用保守评估策略以优先考虑可靠性。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly integrated into in-vehicle conversational systems, identifying the optimal model remains challenging due to the lack of domain-specific evaluation standards tailored to real-world deployment requirements. In this paper, we propose a novel evaluation framework for in-vehicle assistants, with a particular focus on Korean-language localization. Our empirical analysis reveals notable patterns in model behavior. First, fine-grained Korean honorific control remains unstable in current LLMs, indicating that precise speech-level realization must be explicitly evaluated in localization settings. Second, models exhibit weaker performance in strategic conversational metrics like clarification and proactivity. Our analysis suggests this stems from the inherent subjective complexity of these tasks, where our framework adopts a conservative evaluation stance to prioritize reliability. Together, our findings underscore that automotive AI must move beyond general competence toward precise linguistic tailoring and reliable, safety-oriented interaction management.

</details>


### [54] [WCXB: A Multi-Type Web Content Extraction Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21097)
*Murrough Foley*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: WCXB是一个包含2,008个网页的大规模基准数据集，涵盖7种页面类型，用于评估网页主内容提取系统。研究发现现有系统在文章类型上表现良好(F1=0.93)，但在结构化页面类型上性能显著下降(F1=0.41-0.84)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网页内容提取基准数据集存在三大局限：规模小(100-800页)、仅覆盖新闻文章、基于十余年前的网页。这些局限性阻碍了该领域的研究进展。

Method: 构建了WCXB数据集，包含1,497页开发集和511页测试集，涵盖7种页面类型(文章、论坛、产品、集合、列表、文档、服务页)。标注流程采用五阶段管道：LLM辅助起草→自动化验证→前沿模型四轮审查→片段与质量验证→人工审核。评估了13种提取系统(11种启发式、2种神经式)。

Result: 顶级系统在文章类型上表现优异(F1=0.93)，但在结构化页面类型上性能差异显著(F1=0.41-0.84)，揭示了现有基于文章-only基准测试无法发现的盲点。

Conclusion: WCXB数据集(CC-BY-4.0许可)提供了HTML源文件、标注、页面类型标签和基线结果，为网页内容提取研究提供了更全面、更贴近实际的评估框架。

Abstract: Web content extraction - isolating a page's main content from surrounding boilerplate - is a prerequisite for search indexing, retrieval-augmented generation, NLP dataset construction, and large language model training. Progress in this area has been constrained by the limitations of existing evaluation benchmarks, which are small (100-800 pages), restricted to news articles, or based on web pages from over a decade ago. We introduce the Web Content Extraction Benchmark (WCXB), a dataset of 2,008 web pages from 1,613 domains spanning seven structurally distinct page types: articles, forums, products, collections, listings, documentation, and service pages. The dataset includes a 1,497-page development set and a 511-page held-out test set with matched page type distributions. Ground truth annotations were produced through a five-stage pipeline: LLM-assisted drafting, automated verification, four-pass frontier model review, snippet and quality verification scripts, and human review. We evaluate 13 extraction systems - 11 heuristic and 2 neural - and find that while top systems converge on articles (F1 = 0.93), performance diverges sharply on structured page types (F1 = 0.41-0.84), revealing blind spots invisible to existing article-only benchmarks. The dataset is released under CC-BY-4.0 with HTML source files, ground truth annotations, page type labels, and baseline results.

</details>


### [55] [Smarter edits? Post-editing with error highlights and translation suggestions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21135)
*Fleur V. J. van Tellingen,Gautam Ranka,Dora Žugčić,Joyce van der Wal,Andrea Camasta,Livio Guerra,Alina Karakanta*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究探索了LLM生成的错误高亮和纠错建议在译后编辑中的实用性。对比常规PE、QE高亮和APE高亮+纠错建议三种条件，发现无生产力和质量提升，但APE高亮接受度更高，纠错建议改善用户体验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着MT质量提升，QE派生错误高亮等增强型译后编辑功能需求增长，但此类功能实用性的证据仍然有限。

Method: 对比实验：专业译员（英荷翻译方向）在三种条件下进行译后编辑——常规PE、QE错误高亮辅助PE、以及APE错误高亮+纠错建议辅助PE，评估生产力、翻译质量和用户体验。

Result: 三种条件在生产力或质量方面无显著差异；APE错误高亮比QE派生高亮更受青睐；纠错建议改善了整体用户体验。

Conclusion: LLM-derived APE错误高亮比QE方法更受译员欢迎，纠错建议能提升用户体验，但两种辅助方式均未能带来生产力或质量提升。研究结果提示错误高亮技术的实用性仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: As MT quality increases, interest in enhanced post-editing features such as QE-derived error highlights is growing, yet evidence for their usefulness remains limited. In this work, we explore the usefulness of LLM-derived error highlights and correction suggestions based on automatic post-editing (APE). We conduct a study where professional translators (En-Nl) post-edit translations using APE error highlights and correction suggestions and compare productivity, quality and user experience to regular PE and PE with QE-derived highlights. While no condition yielded productivity or quality gains compared to regular PE, APE highlights were better received than QE-derived highlights, and correction suggestions improved overall user experience.

</details>


### [56] [Automated ICD Classification of Psychiatric Diagnoses: From Classical NLP to Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21154)
*Fernando Ortega,Raúl Lara-Cabrera,Jorge Dueñas-Lerín,Alejandro de la Torre-Luque,Mercé Salvador Robert,Enrique Baca-García*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究利用NLP和ML技术自动将西班牙语精神科描述文本映射到ICD编码，使用145,513条数据集对比传统方法（BoW、TF-IDF）与LLM方法（e5_large、BioLORD、Llama-3-8B），e5_large微调后达最高F1_micro=0.866。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精神健康已成为全球优先议题，精神科诊断编码带来巨大的行政负担。需要自动化将自由文本描述映射至ICD编码以提高效率。

Method: 采用145,513条西班牙语精神科描述数据集，对比多种文本表示范式：传统频率模型（BoW、TF-IDF）与最新LLM（e5_large、BioLORD、Llama-3-8B），通过端到端微调进行评估。

Result: 基于Transformer的嵌入方法始终优于传统方法。e5_large模型通过端到端微调获得最高性能，F1_micro达到0.866。

Conclusion: Transformer嵌入能捕获隐含语义线索和细微医学术语。针对特定临床命名法调整LLM对于克服"长尾"标签分布和精神科话语固有歧义至关重要。

Abstract: Mental health has become a global priority, leading to a massive administrative burden in the coding of clinical diagnoses. This study proposes the automation of psychiatric diagnostic analysis by mapping free-text descriptions to the International Classification of Diseases (ICD) using Natural Language Processing (NLP) and Machine Learning (ML) techniques. Utilizing a specialized dataset of 145,513 Spanish psychiatric descriptions, various text representation paradigms were evaluated, ranging from classical frequency-based models (BoW, TF-IDF) to state-of-the-art Large Language Models (LLMs) such as e5\_large, BioLORD, and Llama-3-8B. Results indicate that transformer-based embeddings consistently outperform traditional methods by capturing implicit semantic cues and nuanced medical terminology. The e5\_large model, through end-to-end fine-tuning, achieved the highest performance with a $F1_{micro}$ score of 0.866. This research demonstrates that adapting LLMs to specific clinical nomenclature is essential for overcoming the challenges of ``long-tail'' label distributions and the inherent ambiguity of psychiatric discourse.

</details>


### [57] [Metaphors in Literary Post-Editing: Opening Pandora's Box?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21178)
*Aletta G. Dorst,Mayra O. Nas,Katinka Zeven*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究调查了译后编辑者对神经机器翻译（NMT）和大型语言模型（LLMs）在文学文本中隐喻翻译表现的反应。研究发现约三分之一的隐喻被编辑者修改，表明文学机器翻译中 figurative language（修辞性语言）的翻译确实存在问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文学文本中的隐喻翻译一直是机器翻译的难点，而随着NMT和LLMs的发展，其在文学翻译中的表现尚需评估。本研究旨在探究译后编辑者如何应对机器翻译的隐喻翻译质量，以期为文学机器翻译（LitMT）的改进提供实证依据。

Method: 通过分析译后编辑者对NMT和LLMs输出的隐喻翻译的修改行为及反馈，评估机器翻译在文学文本隐喻处理方面的表现。

Result: 约三分之一的隐喻被编辑者修改；编辑者对过度直译有所察觉（主要针对多词表达）；编辑者有时难以判断译文是否可接受；认为机器翻译整体质量较低。

Conclusion: 译后编辑工作比从零翻译更费时费力，这支持了已有研究观点——译后编辑限制了译者的创造力，削弱了译者对文本的归属感。文学机器翻译在处理 figurative language 方面仍有显著提升空间。

Abstract: This paper investigates how post-editors of literary texts react and respond to the way metaphors have been translated by Neu ral Machine Translation (NMT) and Large Language Models (LLMs). The results show that one in three metaphors in the output were changed by the post-editors, demonstrating that the translation of fig urative language is indeed problematic in literary MT (LitMT). The responses indi cate that the post-editors were aware of overly literal translations, though mostly for multiword expressions. Moreover, at times they found it difficult to determine whether solutions were acceptable. They rated the overall quality of the MT out put as quite poor and stated that the post editing was more work and more effort than it would have been translating from scratch. This supports previous studies ar guing that post-editing constrains transla tors in their creativity and diminishes their sense of text ownership.

</details>


### [58] [Manga109-v2026: Revisiting Manga109 Annotations for Modern Manga Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21182)
*Jeonghun Baek,Atsuyuki Miyai,Shota Onohara,Hikaru Ikuta,Kiyoharu Aizawa*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对漫画AI研究的基础数据集Manga109存在的问题进行修订，结合OCR检测与人工修订，构建了质量更高的Manga109-v2026版本，包含约29,000个修订后的对话标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Manga109数据集存在转录错误和粗粒度标注问题，无法很好地适应现代OCR和多模态漫画理解任务的需求。

Method: 识别了五类标注问题（转录错误、缺失文本区域、对话与拟声词重叠、气球分割不足），并结合OCR自动检测与人工修订两种方法对原数据集进行修正。

Result: 构建了Manga109-v2026数据集，修订了约29,000个对话标注，在保持漫画表达结构特征的同时，提升了数据集质量。

Conclusion: Manga109-v2026是一个改进的大规模漫画数据集，通过OCR检测与人工修订相结合的方式修订了约29,000个对话标注。该数据集更好地对齐现代OCR和多模态漫画理解系统，同时保留了漫画特有的表达结构，可作为漫画相关AI研究的新基准。

Abstract: Manga is a culturally distinctive multimodal medium and one of the most influential forms of Japanese popular culture. As AI systems increasingly target manga understanding, OCR, and translation, Manga109 has become a foundational dataset for manga-related AI research. However, the current Manga109 dataset contains transcription errors and coarse annotations, which do not align well with modern OCR and multimodal manga understanding tasks. In this work, we revisit the dialogue text annotations of Manga109 and identify five categories of annotation issues, including transcription errors, missing text regions, overlapping dialogue and onomatopoeia, and under-segmented speech balloons. To address these issues, we combine OCR-based issue detection and manual revision to construct Manga109-v2026, revising approximately 29,000 dialogue annotations. Our revisions better align Manga109 with modern OCR and multimodal manga understanding systems while preserving expressive structures characteristic of manga.

</details>


### [59] [LamPO: A Lambda Style Policy Optimization for Reasoning Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21235)
*Zhe Yuan,Yipeng Zhou,Jinghan Li,Xinyuan Chen,Bowen Deng,Zhiqian Chen,Liang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LamPO是一种Lambda风格策略优化方法，通过成对分解优势替代GRPO的标量统计优势，结合置信度加权机制提升稀疏奖励场景下的信用分配，并在有参考答案时引入ROUGE-L密集辅助奖励。实验证明其在数学推理任务上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GRPO等组相对目标方法使用标量统计量汇总采样组，丢弃了候选响应间的细粒度关系信息，在稀疏结果奖励下削弱了信用分配，特别是当多个生成解在推理质量上仅存在细微差异时问题尤为突出。

Method: 提出LamPO方法，核心是Pairwise Decomposed Advantage：用组内成对奖励差距的聚合替代标量统计量，并通过序列对数概率差异计算置信度权重来调制每次比较；同时保留PPO风格的无评论家和截断更新结构；当有参考答案时，额外引入ROUGE-L密集辅助奖励以缓解奖励稀疏。

Result: 在AIME24、AIME25、MATH-500和GPQA-Diamond上使用Qwen3-1.7B、Qwen3-4B和Phi-4-mini的实验表明，LamPO相比GRPO及近期RLVR变体持续提升性能，训练动态更稳定，样本效率更优。

Conclusion: LamPO通过成对优势分解和置信度加权机制，有效解决了GRPO在稀疏奖励场景下的细粒度关系信息丢失问题，在数学推理基准上实现了更稳定且高效的策略优化。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has become an effective paradigm for improving reasoning language models on tasks such as mathematics, coding, and scientific question answering. However, widely used group-relative objectives, such as GRPO, summarize each sampled group with scalar statistics and therefore discard fine-grained relational information among candidate responses. This weakens credit assignment under sparse outcome rewards, especially when multiple generated solutions differ only subtly in reasoning quality. We propose \textbf{LamPO}, a \textbf{Lambda-Style Policy Optimization} method that replaces scalar group advantages with a \emph{Pairwise Decomposed Advantage}. LamPO aggregates pairwise reward gaps within each response group and modulates each comparison by a confidence-aware weight computed from sequence log-probability differences, while retaining the critic-free and clipped-update structure of PPO-style optimization. When reference solutions are available, we further add a lightweight ROUGE-L-based dense auxiliary reward to reduce reward sparsity. Experiments on AIME24, AIME25, MATH-500, and GPQA-Diamond with Qwen3-1.7B, Qwen3-4B, and Phi-4-mini show that LamPO consistently improves over GRPO and recent RLVR variants, with more stable training dynamics and better sample efficiency.

</details>


### [60] [Reliable Automated Triage in Spanish Clinical Notes: A Hybrid Framework for Risk-Aware HIV Suspicion Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21256)
*Rodrigo Morales-Sánchez,Soto Montalvo,Raquel Martínez*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对临床NLP基准测试中确定性分类在模糊实例上产生虚假高分的问题，提出了一种风险感知混合选择性分类框架。该框架结合Mondrian共形预测（处理任意不确定性）和多质心马氏距离否决（处理认知不确定性），用于西班牙语临床记录中HIV早期怀疑识别。实验表明，标准不确定性度量在严格可靠性约束下会严重崩溃，而所提框架通过双重概率和几何保障成功隔离出高可信操作域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准临床NLP基准测试通过强制对模糊实例进行确定性分类，产生虚高的评估指标，掩盖了过度自信预测的临床风险。现有方法在安全关键的医学分诊场景中结构性不足，当面临严格可靠性约束时会出现严重的覆盖崩溃问题。

Method: 提出双验证方法：(1) 使用Mondrian共形预测显式解耦任意不确定性(aleatoric uncertainty)；(2) 采用多质心马氏距离(Multi-Centroid Mahalanobis Distance)否决机制处理认知不确定性(epistemic uncertainty)。该混合框架要求临床文本同时通过概率和几何双重保障验证。

Result: 实验评估表明，标准不确定性指标和基线分类器在安全医疗分诊场景下结构性不足，在严格可靠性约束下出现严重覆盖崩溃。提出的双验证框架成功隔离出高度可信的操作域。

Conclusion: 通过结合概率和几何两类不确定性建模，提出的风险感知混合选择性分类框架能够有效识别临床NLP中的不可靠预测，为安全关键的医学应用提供了更可靠的决策边界。

Abstract: Standard clinical Natural Language Processing (NLP) benchmarks often yield inflated metrics by forcing deterministic classification on ambiguous instances, thereby obscuring the clinical risks of overconfident predictions. To bridge this gap, we propose a risk-aware hybrid selective classification framework, evaluated on early Human Immunodeficiency Virus suspicion identification in Spanish clinical notes. Our dual-verification approach explicitly decouples aleatoric uncertainty through Mondrian conformal prediction and epistemic uncertainty using a Multi-Centroid Mahalanobis Distance veto. Empirical evaluations reveal that standard uncertainty metrics and baseline classifiers are structurally insufficient for safe medical triage, suffering severe coverage collapse when forced to operate under strict reliability constraints. In contrast, by demanding that clinical narratives pass both probabilistic and geometric safeguards, the proposed framework successfully isolates a highly trustworthy operational domain.

</details>


### [61] [Tracing the ongoing emergence of human-like reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21299)
*Paolo Morosi,Nikoleta Pantelidou,Fritz Günther,Elena Pagliarini,Evelina Leivada*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过对比25个大语言模型与100名人类在四种语言中的条件推理表现，发现LLM能够准确执行语义层面的逻辑运算，但普遍缺乏人类自然语言理解中的语用推理能力。模型表现与开放/闭源状态、训练方向或架构类型无关，表明务实推理仍是当前AI系统的弱项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类能够轻松超越字面意义进行推理，理解条件语句中的隐含意图（如"只有...才..."与"无论...都..."的区别）。尽管大语言模型在许多任务上表现出接近人类的水平，但尚不清楚它们是否像人类一样进行推理。

Method: 采用人口匹配实验设计，评估25个LLM在四种语言中计算条件推理的能力，并与每种语言25名人类参与者进行对比。研究通过测试不同类型的条件语句（如"如果你除草，我给你五十美元"vs"如果你饿了，烤箱里有披萨"）来区分语义推理与语用推理的表现。

Result: 研究结果呈现明显分化：一部分LLM完全遵循条件句的真值表但忽略语用推理；另一部分则偏离真值表，采用单一解释模式，反映出规则处理的准确性但非人类-like推理。总体而言，LLM是准确的语义操作者，但无法捕捉人类推理中典型的语用丰富化特征。

Conclusion: LLM的准确率既不能由开放/闭源状态、训练方向或架构类型预测，这表明务实推理仍然是人工智能系统认知工具包中的一项新兴能力。未来需要更多研究来弥合语义处理与人类自然语言理解之间的差距。

Abstract: Humans effortlessly go beyond literal meanings: If you mow the lawn, I will give you fifty dollars, is typically understood as implying that the speaker will pay only if the lawn is mowed, whereas If you are hungry, there is pizza in the oven implies that pizza is available regardless of the hearers hunger. Large Language Models - LLMs - show human-like performance on many tasks, yet it remains unclear whether they reason like humans. To address this, we conducted a population-matching experiment assessing how twentyfive LLMs compute conditional inferences across four languages, compared to an equal number of humans per language. We find that humans enrich logical reasoning through pragmatic inferences across languages. Model behavior is more variable. Some LLMs perfectly follow the truth-table of conditionals but they ignore pragmatic inferences, while others deviate from the truth-table, adhering to a single interpretation across the board, thus reflecting accurate rule-based processing but not human-like reasoning. Overall, LLMs are accurate semantic operators, but fail to capture the pragmatic enrichments characteristic of human reasoning. Crucially, LLM accuracy is neither predicted nor boosted by open vs. closed status, training orientation, or architecture type, suggesting that pragmatic reasoning is still an emerging ability in the cognitive toolkit of artificial systems.

</details>


### [62] [TextReg: Mitigating Prompt Distributional Overfitting via Regularized Text-Space Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21318)
*Lucheng Fu,Ye Yu,Yiyang Wang,Yiqiao Jin,Haibo Jin,B. Aditya Prakash,Haohan Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型（LLM）提示优化中的分布过拟合问题，提出通过正则化框架TextReg改善提示的分布外泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前提示优化方法（如TextGrad、REVOLVE等）通过迭代重写提示来优化LLM表现，但存在严重缺陷：优化后的提示趋向冗长、累积大量针对特定样本的规则，导致在训练分布外的泛化能力显著下降。这种"提示分布过拟合"问题反映了离散文本空间优化中表征控制能力的缺失。

Method: 本文提出"表征低效性"（Representational Inefficiency）这一双因子度量框架，将提示低效分解为容量成本（capacity cost）和范围狭窄度（scope narrowness）两个因素，并将分布过拟合归因于优化过程中二者的耦合增长。在此基础上，提出TextReg正则化框架，包含三个核心组件：双重证据梯度净化（Dual-Evidence Gradient Purification）、语义编辑正则化（Semantic Edit Regularization）和正则化引导的提示更新（Regularization-Guided Prompt Update）。

Result: 在多个推理基准测试中，TextReg显著提升了分布外泛化性能，相比TextGrad提升最高达+11.8%，相比REVOLVE提升最高达+16.5%。

Conclusion: 通过引入表征层面的正则化约束，TextReg有效抑制了提示优化过程中容量成本与范围狭窄度的耦合增长，为解决离散文本空间中的提示过拟合问题提供了新思路。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are highly sensitive to the prompts used to specify task objectives and behavioral constraints. Many recent prompt optimization methods iteratively rewrite prompts using LLM-generated feedback, but the resulting prompts often become longer, accumulate narrow sample-specific rules, and generalize poorly beyond the training distribution. We study this failure mode as prompt distributional overfitting and argue that it reflects a lack of representation control in discrete text-space optimization. We formalize this view through representational inefficiency, a dual-factor measure that decomposes prompt inefficiency into capacity cost and scope narrowness, attributing distributional prompt overfitting to their coupled growth during optimization. We propose TextReg, a regularization framework that realizes a soft-penalty objective through regularized textual gradients, combining Dual-Evidence Gradient Purification, Semantic Edit Regularization, and Regularization-Guided Prompt Update. Across multiple reasoning benchmarks, TextReg substantially improves out-of-distribution (OOD) generalization, with accuracy gains of up to +11.8% over TextGrad and +16.5% over REVOLVE.

</details>


### [63] [SymbolicLight V1: Spike-Gated Dual-Path Language Modeling with High Activation Sparsity and Sub-Billion-Scale Pre-Training Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21333)
*Ting Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SymbolicLight V1是一种新型脉冲门控双路径语言模型，通过二元LIF脉冲动力学与连续残差流结合，在194M参数规模下实现>89%激活稀疏度，语言建模性能逼近GPT-2水平，验证了时间积分机制而非单纯稀疏对性能的关键驱动作用，但当前密集硬件推理速度仍不及GPT-2，神经形态硬件部署为未来稀疏加速的机会。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原生脉冲语言模型难以同时实现Transformer级别的语言质量、稳健的多域预训练和高激活稀疏性这三个目标。

Method: 提出 SymbolicLight V1，一种脉冲门控双路径语言模型：采用二元LIF脉冲动力学结合连续残差流，Dual-Path SparseTCAM模块替代密集自注意力，包含指数衰减聚合路径（长程记忆）和脉冲门控局部注意力路径（短程精度），并配备动态上下文条件化解码头和双语分词器。

Result: 194M参数模型在3B token中英语料上训练，四个独立运行验证PPL为8.88-8.93，激活稀疏度>89%；相比GPT-2 201M PPL差距7.7%，但超越GPT-2 124M；消融实验表明脉冲门控局部注意力贡献最大，LIF动力学比确定性top-k掩码更能提升性能；0.8B参数规模扩展实验验证了优化和稀疏保持能力。

Conclusion: 当前密集硬件推理速度慢于GPT-2，神经形态硬件部署被视为未来稀疏驱动的机会而非已实现的硬件加速；Temporal integration（时间积分）而非单纯稀疏是性能的关键驱动因素。

Abstract: Natively trained spiking language models struggle to combine Transformer-like language quality, stable multi-domain pre-training, and high activation sparsity. We present SymbolicLight V1, a spike-gated dual-path language model that combines binary Leaky Integrate-and-Fire spike dynamics with a continuous residual stream. Its Dual-Path SparseTCAM module replaces dense self-attention with an exponential-decay aggregation path for long-range memory and a spike-gated local attention path for short-range precision, complemented by a dynamic context-conditioned decoding head and a bilingual tokenizer.
  A 194M-parameter SymbolicLight V1 model trained from scratch on a 3B-token Chinese-English corpus reaches held-out validation PPL 8.88-8.93 across four independent runs at >89% per-element activation sparsity. It trails GPT-2 201M by 7.7% in PPL while surpassing GPT-2 124M under the reported comparison. Component ablations at matched 0.5B-token training budgets show that the spike-gated local attention path is the largest contributor, and that replacing LIF dynamics with a deterministic top-k mask at matched sparsity causes a larger degradation, indicating that temporal integration rather than sparsity alone drives performance. We also report a 0.8B-parameter scale-up run trained on 48.8B tokens as evidence of optimization and sparsity preservation, not as a primary quality comparison. Current dense-hardware inference is slower than GPT-2, so neuromorphic deployment is presented as a future sparsity-driven opportunity rather than an achieved hardware speedup.

</details>


### [64] [Text Analytics Evaluation Framework: A Case Study on LLMs and Social Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21338)
*Yuefeng Shi,Nedjma Ousidhoum,Jose Camacho-Collados*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一个包含470个问题的评估框架，用于测试LLM在Twitter数据集上的语义理解和推理能力。研究发现LLM在处理大规模文本集合时存在严重性能下降，尤其在多标签、目标依赖场景及数值计算任务中，揭示了当前LLM架构在定量分析方面的关键瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs exhibit a notable gap in practical data analysis scenarios, particularly when processing long sequences of unstructured documents like social media posts.

Method: Question-based evaluation framework with 470 manually curated questions applied on diverse Twitter datasets covering sentiment analysis, hate speech detection, and emotion recognition.

Result: Performance heavily depends on input scale and data source complexity; declines in multi-label/target-dependent scenarios; drops progressively from basic semantic identification to comparison/counting/calculation; degrades substantially when input exceeds 500 instances, especially on numerical tasks.

Conclusion: Current LLMs have critical architectural bottlenecks for performing rigorous quantitative analysis over large text collections.

Abstract: LLMs have demonstrated exceptional proficiency in a wide range of NLP tasks. However, a notable gap remains in practical data analysis scenarios, particularly when LLMs are required to process long sequences of unstructured documents, such as news feeds or, as specifically addressed in this paper, social media posts. To empirically assess the effectiveness of LLMs in this setting, we introduce a question-based evaluation framework comprising 470 manually curated questions designed to evaluate LLMs' semantic understanding and reasoning abilities over aggregated text data. We apply our benchmark on diverse Twitter datasets covering various NLP tasks, including sentiment analysis, hate speech detection, and emotion recognition. Our results reveal that the performance depends heavily on input scale and the complexity of the data sources, declining noticeably in multi-label or target-dependent scenarios. In addition, as task complexity increases, performance drops progressively from basic semantic existence identification to more demanding operations such as comparison, counting, and calculation. Furthermore, as the input size grows beyond 500 instances, we identify a common limitation across LLMs, particularly Open-weights models: performance degrades substantially, especially on numerical tasks. These findings highlight critical architectural bottlenecks in current LLMs for performing rigorous quantitative analysis over large text collections.

</details>


### [65] [LASH: Adaptive Semantic Hybridization for Black-Box Jailbreaking of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21362)
*Abdullah Al Nomaan Nafi,Fnu Suya,Swarup Bhunia,Prabuddha Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LASH提出了一种自适应组合多种越狱攻击策略的黑盒框架，通过遗传优化器搜索种子子集和混合权重，在JailbreakBench上实现84.5%的攻击成功率，显著优于5个先进基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化越狱攻击方法仅采用单一攻击家族（单一 refinement loop、树搜索、变异空间或策略库），但不同方法在不同目标模型和危害类别上表现各异，没有单一方法能全面主导，表明组合多种异构攻击策略具有互补优势。

Method: LASH将多个基础攻击的输出作为可复用种子提示，通过搜索种子子集和softmax归一化混合权重进行自适应组合；组合模块合成单一候选提示；使用无导数遗传优化器结合黑盒目标反馈和两阶段适应度函数（基于关键词的拒绝检测 + LLM评判打分）更新权重。

Result: 在JailbreakBench（100个有害提示，10个类别）上对6个目标模型评估，LASH在基于关键词评估下达到84.5%攻击成功率，在两阶段评估下达到74.5%；仅需30次平均目标查询即可超越5个SOTA基线；在3种防御机制下仍具竞争力，并诱导更多类似成功的内部表征。

Conclusion: 跨异构越狱策略的自适应组合是黑盒红队测试的有前景方向。

Abstract: Jailbreak attacks expose a persistent gap between the intended safety behavior of aligned large language models and their behavior under adversarial prompting. Existing automated methods are increasingly effective but each commits to a single attack family (e.g., one refinement loop, one tree search, one mutation space, or one strategy library) and no single family dominates: the best-performing method shifts across target models and harm categories, suggesting complementary strengths that per-prompt composition could exploit. We introduce LASH (LLM Adaptive Semantic Hybridization), a black-box framework that treats outputs from multiple base attacks as reusable seed prompts and adaptively composes them for each target request. Given a seed pool, LASH searches over seed subsets and softmax-normalized mixture weights; a composition module synthesizes a single candidate prompt, and a derivative-free genetic optimizer updates the weights using black-box target feedback and a two-stage fitness function combining keyword-based refusal detection with LLM-judge scoring. On JailbreakBench, which contains 100 harmful prompts across 10 categories, we evaluate LASH on six common target models. LASH achieves an average attack success rate of 84.5% under keyword-based evaluation and 74.5% under two-stage evaluation, where responses are first filtered for refusals and then scored by an LLM judge for whether they substantively fulfill the original harmful request. LASH outperforms five state-of-the-art baselines on both metrics with only 30 mean target queries. LASH also remains competitive under three defense mechanisms and induces more success-like internal representations. These results suggest that adaptive composition across heterogeneous jailbreak strategies is a promising direction for black-box red-teaming.

</details>


### [66] ["I didn't Make the Micro Decisions": Measuring, Inducing, and Exposing Goal-Level AI Contributions in Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21363)
*Eunsu Kim,Jessica R. Mindel,Kyungjin Kim,Sherry Tongshuang Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) increasingly shape how users form, refine, and extend their goals, attributing contributions in human-AI collaboration becomes critical for users calibrating their own reliance and for evaluators assessing AI-assisted work. Yet existing methods focus on final artifacts, missing the process through which goals themselves are jointly shaped. We introduce a goal-level attribution framework, CoTrace, that decomposes explicit goals into verifiable requirements and traces both direct contributions and indirect influences across dialogue turns. Applying CoTrace to 638 real-world collaboration logs, we find that while models account for only 11-26% of goal-shaping contribution, they contribute substantially more on introducing lower-level concrete requirements, and make various kinds of indirect contributions. Through controlled simulations, we show that interaction design choices significantly affect model goal-shaping behavior. In a user study, exposing participants to goal-level analyses shifts their perceived contributions by nearly 2 points on a 5-point scale, revealing systematic miscalibration in how users understand their own AI-assisted work.

</details>


### [67] [Findings of the Fifth Shared Task on Multilingual Coreference Resolution: Expanding Datasets for Long-Range Entities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21369)
*Michal Novák,Miloslav Konopík,Anna Nedoluzhko,Martin Popel,Ondřej Pražák,Jakub Sido,Milan Straka,Zdeněk Žabokrtský,Daniel Zeman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Fifth edition of the Shared Task on Multilingual Coreference Resolution emphasizing long-range entities, expanded to five new datasets and two additional languages using CorefUD v1.4; ten systems participated with traditional approaches still leading but LLMs showing promise.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The task addresses the challenge of long-range entity resolution, which involves coreferential chains spanning significant distances across many words and sentences, and expands multilingual coverage.

Method: Systems were required to develop mention identification and identity-based coreference clustering capabilities across multiple languages and datasets.

Result: Ten systems participated (four LLM-based approaches including fine-tuned and few-shot approaches). Traditional systems maintained their lead, though LLMs showed significant potential.

Conclusion: LLMs demonstrated significant potential despite traditional systems maintaining the lead, suggesting they may soon challenge established approaches in future editions.

Abstract: This paper describes the fifth edition of the Shared Task on Multilingual Coreference Resolution, held in conjunction with the CODI-CRAC 2026 workshop. Building on previous iterations, the task required participants to develop systems capable of mention identification and identity-based coreference clustering.
  The 2026 edition specifically emphasizes long-range entities, defined as coreferential chains spanning significant distances, across many words and sentences.
  The task expanded its linguistic scope by incorporating five new datasets and two additional languages. These additions leverage version 1.4 of CorefUD, a harmonized multilingual collection comprising 27 datasets in 19 languages.
  In total, ten systems participated, including four LLM-based approaches (three fine-tuned models and one few-shot approach). While traditional systems still maintained their lead, LLMs demonstrated significant potential, suggesting they may soon challenge established approaches in future editions.

</details>

*Xiaoqiang Wang,Chao Wang,Hadi Nekoei,Christopher Pal,Alexandre Lacoste,Spandana Gella,Bang Liu,Perouz Taslakian*

Main category: cs.CL

Abstract: We present Mem-$π$, a framework for adaptive memory in large language model (LLM) agents, where useful guidance is generated on demand rather than retrieved from external memory stores. Existing memory-augmented agents typically rely on similarity-based retrieval from episodic memory banks or skill libraries, returning static entries that often misalign with the current context. In contrast, Mem-$π$ uses a dedicated language or vision-language model with its own parameters, separate from the downstream agent, to generate context-specific guidance for complex tasks. Conditioned on the current agent context, the model jointly decides when to produce guidance and what guidance to produce. We train it with a decision-content decoupled reinforcement learning (RL) objective, enabling it to abstain when generation would not help and otherwise produce concise, useful guidance. Across diverse agentic benchmarks spanning web navigation, terminal-based tool use, and text-based embodied interaction, Mem-$π$ consistently outperforms retrieval-based and prior RL-optimized memory baselines, achieving over 30% relative improvement on web navigation tasks.

</details>

### [69] [Efficient Banzhaf-Based Data Valuation for $k$-Nearest Neighbors Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21033)
*Guangyi Zhang,Lutz Oettershagen,Lixu Wang,Aristides Gionis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对kNN分类器中的Banzhaf值计算难题，证明其#P难解性，并利用kNN局部性特征提出动态规划框架，实现加权场景O(Wkn²)和非加权场景O(nk²)的高效算法，同时提供蒙特卡洛估计方法，实验验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据估值是机器学习中的基础问题，博弈论方法（如Banzhaf值）虽然提供公平的数据估值原则框架，但存在指数级计算复杂度，难以应用于实际。

Method: 利用kNN分类器的局部性特征，设计动态规划框架：加权kNN的伪多项式算法O(Wkn²)，以及针对非加权kNN的O(nk²)线性算法，并提供蒙特卡洛估计方法。

Result: 加权kNN伪多项式算法复杂度O(Wkn²)，非加权kNN达线性复杂度O(nk²)，显著优于传统指数级方法。

Conclusion: 提出了高效的动态规划框架，为kNN分类器提供了实用的精确计算算法和蒙特卡洛估计方法，在真实数据集上验证了计算效率和有效性。

Abstract: Data valuation, the task of quantifying the contribution of individual data points to model performance, has emerged as a fundamental challenge in machine learning. Game-theoretic approaches, such as the Banzhaf value, offer principled frameworks for fair data valuation; however, they suffer from exponential computational complexity. We address this challenge by developing efficient algorithms specifically tailored for computing Banzhaf values in $k$-nearest neighbor ($k$NN) classifiers. We first establish the theoretical hardness of the problem by proving that it is \#P-hard. Despite this intractability, we exploit the locality properties of $k$NN classifiers to develop practical exact algorithms. Our main contribution is a dynamic programming framework that achieves significant computational improvements: we present a pseudo-polynomial algorithm with $O(Wkn^2)$ time complexity for weighted $k$NN classifiers, where $W$ is the maximum sum of top-$k$ weights, and a specialized algorithm for unweighted $k$NN that achieves $O(nk^2)$ time complexity, that is, linear in the number of data points. We also offer efficient Monte Carlo estimation methods. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets demonstrate the practical efficiency of our approach and its effectiveness in data valuation applications.

</details>


### [70] [Dynamic Shapley Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20620)
*Xuan Yang,Hsi-Wen Chen,Ming-Syan Chen,Jian Pei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: D-Shap通过将Shapley值表示为矩阵并利用数据中的局部性结构，将动态数据估值转化为高效的矩阵维护问题，实现了毫秒级任务更新和三个数量级的玩家更新加速，同时通过self-valuation消除了对预定义任务的需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Shapley数据估值方法将计算视为一次性过程，产生聚合分数而非可复用结构，无法应对动态场景中任务和训练玩家的变化，导致每次变更都需完全重算，计算成本过高。

Method: 提出D-Shap框架，将Shapley值表示为玩家×任务矩阵，利用utility locality（相似任务产生相似估值）和coalition locality（每任务仅依赖少量训练玩家）实现增量更新：新增任务通过结构感知插值推断，新增玩家仅影响局部矩阵块。同时引入self-valuation机制，基于可扩展子集复用和覆盖感知锚点选择直接从训练数据构建初始矩阵，避免预定义评估任务。

Result: D-Shap在多种模型上验证：任务更新达到毫秒级，玩家更新成本降低最高三个数量级，同时估值质量与完整重计算相当。

Conclusion: D-Shap将动态Shapley数据估值问题转化为结构化矩阵维护问题，通过利用utility locality和coalition locality实现了高效的增量更新。Self-valuation机制进一步消除了对预定义评估任务的依赖，使框架可直接从训练数据构建初始矩阵。实验表明该方法在保持估值质量与完整重计算相当的同时，任务更新达到毫秒级，玩家更新成本降低三个数量级。

Abstract: Shapley-based data valuation provides a principled way to quantify the contribution of training data, but its high computational cost makes it impractical in dynamic settings where tasks and training players evolve. Existing methods treat Shapley computation as a one-shot process and collapse contributions into aggregated scores, preventing reuse and requiring recomputation under any change. We introduce a new perspective that represents Shapley values as a player-by-task matrix and formulates dynamic valuation as a structured matrix maintenance problem. We exploit the fact that each task depends on a small subset of training players and that similar tasks yield similar valuations, leading to utility locality and coalition locality. Based on these insights, we propose D-Shap, a dynamic valuation framework that enables efficient updates by modifying only a small portion of the matrix: new task valuations are inferred via structure-aware interpolation, while updates induced by new players are confined to affected local matrix blocks. To eliminate the need for pre-specified evaluation tasks, we introduce self-valuation, which constructs the initial matrix directly from training data, supported by scalable subset reuse and coverage-aware anchor selection. Experiments across diverse models show that D-Shap performs task updates in milliseconds and reduces the cost of player updates by up to three orders of magnitude, while achieving valuation quality competitive with full recomputation.

</details>


### [71] [GraphDiffMed: Knowledge-Constrained Differential Attention with Pharmacological Graph Priors for Medication Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20188)
*Krati Saxena,Tomohiro Shibata*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GraphDiffMed是一种知识约束的药物推荐框架，采用双重尺度差分注意力机制在就诊内和就诊间层级过滤噪声，并整合药理学约束。实验证明该方法能生成更可靠、临床意义更强的药物推荐，同时在安全性和有效性之间取得更好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有药物推荐方法通常在时序建模或药理学知识整合方面表现良好，但难以同时兼顾两者并有效抑制电子健康记录中的噪声干扰。患者轨迹具有长时序、高噪声和临床异质性的特点，增加了推荐难度。

Method: GraphDiffMed采用双重尺度差分注意力（Differential Attention v2）机制，分别在就诊内（intra-visit）和就诊间（inter-visit）层级过滤噪声信号，同时在学习过程中整合药理学约束（如药物-药物相互作用，DDI）。框架基于知识约束构建。

Result: 实验结果表明，该设计在MIMIC-III数据集和消融实验中持续提升推荐质量与排序性能，同时实现了更优的安全性平衡。最佳配置仅使用人口统计学辅助特征即可达到最优表现。

Conclusion: GraphDiffMed通过将噪声感知注意机制与药理学约束相结合，验证了该方法能够产生更可靠且具有临床意义的药物推荐。该框架在MIMIC-III数据集上的实验表明其在推荐质量、排序性能和安全性方面均优于强基线模型，且仅使用人口统计学辅助特征时表现最佳。

Abstract: Recommending safe and effective medication combinations from electronic health records (EHRs) is a core clinical AI problem, yet it remains difficult because patient trajectories are long, noisy, and clinically heterogeneous. Existing methods typically excel at either temporal modeling across visits or pharmacological knowledge integration (e.g., drug-drug interactions, DDIs), but rarely achieve both while robustly suppressing noise. We present GraphDiffMed, a knowledge-constrained medication recommendation framework built on dual-scale Differential Attention v2. Differential attention is applied at both intra-visit and inter-visit levels to filter spurious signals within encounters and across longitudinal history, while pharmacological constraints are incorporated during learning. Experiments on MIMIC-III and ablation studies show that this design consistently improves recommendation quality and ranking over strong baselines while achieving a more favorable safety performance balance. We further find that the strongest-performing configuration uses only demographic auxiliary features under our experimental setting. Overall, GraphDiffMed demonstrates that combining noise-aware attention with pharmacological constraints yields more reliable and clinically meaningful medication recommendation. We open-source our code at https://github.com/saxenakrati09/GraphDiffMed.

</details>


### [72] [Polynomial-Time Robust Multiclass Linear Classification under Gaussian Marginals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21428)
*Ilias Diakonikolas,Giannis Iakovidis,Mingchen Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: This paper addresses agnostic learning of multiclass linear classifiers under Gaussian distribution. The authors reveal that standard multiclass perceptron fails even in clean settings, and develop polynomial-time robust learners achieving error Õ(k^(3/2)√opt) for general k, O(opt) for k=3, and poly(k)·opt for geometrically regular classifiers.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The binary case (k=2) of agnostic learning under Gaussian distribution has well-developed algorithmic theory, but for k≥3, prior robust algorithms incur exponential complexity. Even for k=3, robust learning remained computationally intractable.

Method: Developed new structural results for multiclass linear classifiers, including pairwise improper-learning framework and sharper localization-based framework to achieve efficient learning.

Result: 1) Standard multiclass perceptron requires super-polynomially many samples even with clean labels; 2) Pairwise improper-learning yields error O(k^(3/2)√opt)+ε; 3) Localization-based framework achieves error O(opt)+ε for k=3 and poly(k)·opt+ε for geometrically regular classifiers.

Conclusion: Successfully developed fully polynomial-time robust learners for multiclass linear classification with dimension-independent error guarantees, overcoming the exponential complexity barrier of prior methods.

Abstract: We study the task of agnostic learning of multiclass linear classifiers under the Gaussian distribution. Given labeled examples $(x, y)$ from a distribution over $\mathbb{R}^d \times [k]$, with Gaussian $x$-marginal, the goal is to output a hypothesis whose error is comparable to that of the best $k$-class linear classifier. While the binary case $k=2$ has a well-developed algorithmic theory, much less is known for $k \ge 3$.
