Even for $k=3$, prior robust algorithms incur exponential dependence on the inverse of the desired accuracy in both complexity and representation size. In this work, we develop new structural results for multiclass linear classifiers and use them to design fully polynomial-time robust learners with dimension-independent error guarantees. Our first result shows that the standard multiclass perceptron algorithm requires super-polynomially many samples and updates, even with clean labels and Gaussian marginals, revealing a basic obstruction absent in the binary case. Our main positive result is a pairwise improper-learning framework which yields an efficient learner with error $\widetilde O(k^{3/2}\sqrt{\mathrm{opt}})+ε$ for general $k$. Additionally, we develop a sharper localization-based framework which leads to error $O(\mathrm{opt})+ε$ for $k=3$, and error $\mathrm{poly}(k)\mathrm{opt}+ε$ for geometrically regular $k$-class linear classifiers.

</details>


### [73] [TabPFN-MT: A Natively Multitask In-Context Learner for Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20234)
*Cormac Cureton,Narges Armanfard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TabPFN-MT是一种多任务表格学习模型，通过扩展y编码器和共享解码器头实现多任务上下文学习，在保持单任务竞争力的同时将推理成本从O(T)降至O(1)，在344个数据集上达到深度表格多任务学习新SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PFNs专为单任务推理设计，处理多目标预测需重复前向传播且无法实现任务间信息共享，存在计算效率低下和缺乏依赖关系建模的问题。

Method: 提出TabPFN-MT模型，采用扩展的多目标合成先验训练，通过扩展的y编码器和共享解码器头实现多任务上下文学习，支持同时推理。模型依赖上下文学习而非传统梯度训练进行小到中型数据集预测。

Result: 在344个数据集上的广泛评估表明，TabPFN-MT在深度表格多任务学习中建立新SOTA；在多任务数据集上实现平均准确率排名4.89，为所有测试模型中最高平均排名；同时保持与最新单任务集成方法的强竞争力。

Conclusion: TabPFN-MT成功实现了高效的多任务表格学习，在保持竞争力的同时将T个任务的推理成本从O(T)降至O(1)，为小型至中型数据集的多目标表格应用带来显著的计算效率提升。

Abstract: Prior-Data Fitted networks (PFNs) have been very successful in tabular contexts, handling prediction tasks in context. However, they are designed for single-task inference, meaning that predicting several target values within a context requires repeated forward calls and precludes inter-task information sharing. We propose TabPFN-MT, which is trained on an expanded multi-target synthetic prior to capture inter-task dependencies in context. This model uses an expanded $y$-encoder and a shared decoder head to enable multitask in-context learning and simultaneous inference. The model is uniquely specialized for small-to-medium datasets by relying on in-context learning rather than traditional gradient-based training. Within this regime (averaging fewer than 1,000 samples), extensive evaluations across 344 datasets demonstrate that TabPFN-MT establishes a new state-of-the-art for deep tabular multitask learning. Furthermore, despite the inherent compute asymmetry of joint optimization, our model remains highly competitive with the latest state-of-the-art single-task ensembles. Notably, on multitask datasets it achieves an overall Accuracy rank of 4.89, the highest average rank among all models tested. Crucially, TabPFN-MT delivers this highly competitive performance while reducing the inference cost for $T$ tasks from $O(T)$ to $O(1)$ forward passes, offering a massive computational efficiency improvement for multi-target tabular applications.

</details>


### [74] [Provably Learning Diffusion Models under the Manifold Hypothesis: Collapse and Refine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20235)
*Wei Huang,Andi Han,Mingyuan Bai,Huanjian Zhou,Qixin Zhang,Taiji Suzuki,Kenji Fukumizu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了扩散模型中score函数几何驱动的"坍缩-精炼"学习机制，并提出SiLD框架，通过单一去噪分数匹配目标统一流形学习与密度估计，实现样本复杂度仅依赖数据内在维度的理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型虽能生成高质量高维数据，但其训练如何高效学习score函数、避开维度诅咒（当数据位于低维流形上时）的机理尚缺乏理论解释。现有VAE-based潜在扩散模型的KL正则化缺乏理论依据。

Method: 本文提出Score-induced Latent Diffusion (SiLD)框架，核心是"坍缩-精炼"机制。该机制由score函数几何特性驱动：小噪声尺度下，score函数的奇异性导致去噪映射快速坍缩到数据流形投影；中等噪声尺度下，训练过程精炼学习流形上的内在密度。采用两阶段架构：流形学习与密度估计均从单一去噪分数匹配目标中涌现。

Result: 实验验证：在Stacked MNIST、CelebA变体及分子生成基准上，SiLD在生成质量上匹配或超越VAE-based LDMs，且重建效果显著改善，证实了理论预测的准确性。

Conclusion: SiLD成功将流形学习和密度估计统一在去噪分数匹配框架下，在保持高质量生成的同时显著提升重建效果，且样本复杂度仅依赖内在维度而非环境维度，为扩散模型的机理理解提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Diffusion models generate high-dimensional data with remarkable quality, yet how their training efficiently learns the score function, bypassing the curse of dimensionality when data is supported on low-dimensional manifolds, remains theoretically unexplained. We identify a collapse-and-refine mechanism driven by the geometry of the score function itself: at small noise scales, the diverging singularity of the score drives a rapid dimensional collapse of the induced denoising map onto the data manifold projection; at moderate noise scales, training refines the intrinsic density on the learned manifold. We instantiate this principle as Score-induced Latent Diffusion (SiLD), a two-stage framework in which both manifold learning and density estimation emerge from a single denoising score matching objective, replacing the heuristic KL regularization of VAE-based latent diffusion models. We prove that the resulting sample complexity depends on the intrinsic dimension rather than the ambient dimension. Experiments on Stacked MNIST, CelebA variants, and molecular generation benchmarks show that SiLD matches or outperforms VAE-based LDMs in generation quality and consistently improves reconstruction, validating our theoretical predictions.

</details>


### [75] [MagBridge-Battery: A Synthetic Bridge Dataset for Li-ion Magnetometry and State-of-Health Diagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20240)
*Sakthi Prabhu Gunasekar,Prasanna Kumar Rangarajan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发布了MagBridge-Battery v1.0数据集，通过桥接真实磁形态数据与电池SOH标签，创建了6,760个磁场签名样本用于电池健康诊断研究。数据集包含有根据样本、合成异常样本和低电压外推样本三部分，并定义了回归、分类和异常检测三项基准任务。标签混淆实验验证了数据集的有效性，代码和数据均公开可用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电池健康诊断主要依赖电池端子处的电化学信号测量。另一研究脉络表明磁传感可以解析端子测量无法获取的信息，但方法开发受限于公开磁测量数据集的缺失。目前缺乏配对有标签的电池磁测量数据集，制约了该领域的发展。

Method: 数据集构建采用桥接策略：从Mohammadi-Jerschow开放科学框架档案提取真实磁形态数据，从PulseBat数据集获取SOH标签，通过条件生成方法创建6,760个配对样本。数据集包含三类样本：(1)5,600个PulseBat条件化的有根据样本，(2)600个源自干净父样本的合成传感器异常样本，(3)560个低电压Regime-B外推样本。基准分割采用细胞级别的不重叠设计，确保零重叠细胞、零跨分割父子对、零样本ID重叠。

Result: 数据集规模：6,760个磁场签名样本。基准验证：细胞不重叠分割通过验证，零重叠细胞、零跨分割父子对、零样本ID重叠。三项基准任务定义完成。消融实验：受控标签混淆使SOH回归R²从约0.77降至约0，证实桥接方法有效编码输入SOH信息而非产生标签对齐伪影。

Conclusion: 提供了磁传感电池诊断的公共基准数据集。MagBridge-Battery v1.0在Zenodo上以CC-BY-4.0许可发布，代码在Apache-2.0许可下发布。该数据集填补了磁传感电池诊断领域缺乏公开基准的空白，为该研究方向提供了重要的基础设施。标签混淆消融实验验证了数据集本身编码了有意义的SOH信息，而非简单的标签伪影。

Abstract: Battery health diagnostics today rely overwhelmingly on electrochemical signals measured at the cell terminals. A parallel literature has shown that magnetic sensing can resolve information that terminal-only measurements miss, but method development is limited by the absence, to the best of our knowledge, of public battery magnetic-measurement datasets paired with degradation labels. We release MagBridge-Battery v1.0, a synthetic dataset of 6,760 magnetic-field signatures that bridges real magnetic morphology from the Mohammadi-Jerschow Open Science Framework (OSF) archive with state-of-health (SOH) labels from the PulseBat dataset. The release contains 5,600 PulseBat-conditioned grounded samples, 600 synthetic sensor-anomaly samples derived from clean parents, and 560 low-voltage Regime-B extrapolation samples. A cell-disjoint, parent-child-leakage-free primary benchmark split is verified to contain zero overlapping cells, zero cross-split parent-child pairs, and zero sample-ID overlap. We define three primary benchmark tasks: SOH regression, second-life classification, and anomaly detection, plus an auxiliary anomaly-subtype classification task. A controlled label-shuffle ablation collapses SOH regression from R^2 approximately 0.77 to approximately 0, confirming that the bridge encodes input SOH non-trivially rather than producing label-aligned artifacts. The dataset is released on Zenodo under CC-BY-4.0, and the bridge code and benchmark suite are released under Apache-2.0. This work provides a public benchmark for magnetic-sensing battery diagnostics while paired magnetic-electrochemical measurements remain scarce.

</details>


### [76] [Geometry-Lite: Interpretable Safety Probing via Layer-Wise Margin Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20241)
*Woo Seob Sim,Yu Rang Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究引入Geometry-Lite探针分析LLM安全检测的层级几何结构，发现安全证据主要体现在持久的边界位置几何而非层级间运动信号，最终/极值边际和不安全侧层级占用主导检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前安全探测虽能实现良好的平均检测性能，但无法解释安全/不安全提示分离的几何结构。具体而言：安全证据如何跨层形成？哪些几何特征支持低假阳性决策？在基准转移下哪些几何偏置保持稳定？

Method: 提出Geometry-Lite方法：将每层最终提示-token表示映射到带符号边际，使用三类读出器（质心、局部邻域、监督线性边界），然后通过边界位置、层级变化和粗略形状三个维度总结边际轮廓。在9个指令微调主干模型（1.2B-70B）和7个安全基准上验证。

Result: 安全证据主要通过持久边界位置几何表达：最终边际/极值边际和不安全侧层级占用主导聚合检测性能。有限差分漂移和结构总结对AUROC贡献很小，但漂移可在低FPR阈值下提供小规模召回导向修正。基准转移时，优化线性边界在训练混合上锐利，而类别条件均值几何在预定义困难留出子集上更可靠保留分离。

Conclusion: 安全证据本质上是持久的层级边际几何结构，而非层级间的运动信号。其有用组件和读出级偏置在决策关键区域变得可见。Geometry-Lite作为紧凑分析工具，在提升安全检测性能的同时，揭示了优化线性边界在训练混合上表现锐利，而类别条件均值几何在困难留出子集上更可靠保留分离能力。

Abstract: Prompt-level safety probes for large language models use hidden-state representations to separate safe from unsafe prompts, but strong average detection performance does not explain the geometry of this separation. In particular, it remains unclear how safety evidence is formed across layers, which aspects of that layer-wise geometry support low-false-positive decisions, and which geometric biases remain stable under benchmark shift. We study this as an empirical decomposition problem and introduce Geometry-Lite, a compact prompt-level probe that maps each layer's final prompt-token representation to signed margins under centroid, local-neighborhood, and supervised linear-boundary readouts, then summarizes the resulting margin profiles by boundary position, layer-to-layer change, and coarse shape. Across nine instruction-tuned backbones ($1.2$B--$70$B) and seven safety benchmarks, Geometry-Lite improves over single-layer probes while remaining close to raw multi-layer score stacking, making it a useful instrument for analyzing the multi-layer safety signal. The decomposition shows that safety evidence is expressed primarily through persistent boundary-position geometry: final or extremal margins and unsafe-side layer occupancy dominate aggregate detection performance. In contrast, finite-difference drift and structural summaries add little to pooled AUROC, although drift can provide small recall-oriented corrections under shifted low-FPR thresholds. Under benchmark shift, optimized linear boundaries are sharp on the training mixture, whereas class-conditional mean geometry retains separation more reliably on a predefined hard held-out subset. Overall, prompt-level safety evidence is not primarily a layer-to-layer motion signal, but a persistent layer-wise margin geometry whose useful components and readout-level biases become visible in decision-critical regimes.

</details>


### [77] [LEAP: A closed-loop framework for perovskite precursor additive discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20242)
*Xin-De Wang,Zhi-Rui Chen,Ze-Feng Gao,Peng-Jie Guo,Cheng Mu,Zhong-Yi Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LEAP是一个结合领域专用大语言模型与贝叶斯优化的闭环框架，用于钙钛矿太阳能电池添加剂的智能优先级排序。该框架从文献中提取机理相关知识并生成可解释分子描述符，在低数据条件下进行不确定性感知筛选。实验验证表明该方法可将器件效率从19.25%提升至21.32%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The vast chemical space for precursor additives makes trial-and-error screening prohibitively inefficient for improving perovskite solar cell performance; there's a need for intelligent prioritization that leverages accumulated scientific knowledge.

Method: Domain-specialized LLM extracts mechanism-relevant knowledge from perovskite additive literature and generates interpretable molecular descriptors; these descriptors integrate into a Bayesian optimization workflow for uncertainty-aware prioritization under low-data conditions; expert-in-the-loop review ensures feasibility assessment.

Result: Domain-specialized model outperforms general-purpose LLMs in mechanism-consistent reasoning on unseen literature; experimental validation across 3 screening rounds demonstrates improved additive prioritization with average PCEs reaching 20.13% (6-CDQ) and 20.87% (2-CNA) versus 19.25% control, achieving a champion device PCE of 21.32%.

Conclusion: Literature-grounded mechanistic descriptors coupled with Bayesian optimization and expert review can effectively support mechanism-aware additive prioritization; achieved champion PCE of 21.32% with 2-CNA treatment, representing meaningful improvement over 19.25% control baseline.

Abstract: Efficient discovery of precursor additives is essential for improving the performance of perovskite solar cells, yet the large chemical space makes conventional trial-and-error screening inefficient. We develop LEAP(LLM-driven Exploration via Active Learning for Perovskites), an expert-in-the-loop closed framework that couples a domain-specialized large language model(LLM) with active learning for iterative additive prioritization. The LLM is trained to extract mechanism-relevant knowledge from the perovskite additive literature and to represent candidate molecules through interpretable descriptors, which are further integrated into a Bayesian optimization workflow for uncertainty-aware prioritization under low-data conditions. Benchmark results on unseen literature show that the domain-specialized model outperforms general-purpose models in mechanism-consistent reasoning. Experimental validation in an expert-in-the-loop proof-of-concept study suggests improved additive prioritization across three screening rounds, leading to average device PCEs of 20.13% and 20.87% for the later-round 6-CDQ- and 2-CNA-treated devices, respectively, compared with 19.25% for the control, with a champion PCE of 21.32%. These results provide preliminary evidence that literature-grounded mechanistic descriptors, when coupled with Bayesian optimization and expert feasibility review, can support mechanism-aware additive prioritization in perovskite photovoltaics.

</details>


### [78] [GROW: Aligning GRPO with State-Action Modeling for Open-World VLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20246)
*Xiongbin Wu,Zhihao Luo,Shanzhe Lei,Lechao Zhang,Xuhong Wang,Jie Yang,Zhonglong Zheng,Yuanjie Zheng,Xin Tan,Wei Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GROW是一种用于开放世界VLM智能体的强化学习框架，通过将轨迹分解为状态-动作样本并在其间计算优势来克服标准GRPO的局限性，在800多个Minecraft任务上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM智能体依赖监督微调（SFT），而先进的强化学习算法GRPO因需完整轨迹导致上下文过长和噪声问题，尚未被有效应用于多轮强化学习。

Method: 提出GROW框架，将收集的轨迹分解为状态-动作样本，在样本间而非整条轨迹上计算优势；并提供替代损失函数分析，证明在简化假设下可保留GRPO的相对策略优化核心信号。

Result: 在800多个Minecraft任务上达到SOTA性能，验证了该RL框架对开放世界VLM智能体的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过轨迹分解策略，GROW成功将GRPO应用于多轮VLM智能体训练，为开放世界视觉-语言智能体的强化学习提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Recently, vision-language model (VLM) agents have shown promising progress in open-world tasks, where successful task completion often requires multiple turns of visual perception and action execution. However, existing methods still rely primarily on Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) with expert demonstrations, while the advanced reinforcement learning (RL) algorithm, specifically Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), has not been effectively employed for multi-turn RL in these tasks because standard GRPO requires full trajectories as training samples which leads to excessively long context and noise. To address this issue, we propose GROW, a RL framework for open-world VLM agents that decomposes collected trajectories into state-action samples, and computes advantages between these samples rather than treating a full trajectory as a single entity. We further provide a surrogate analysis indicating that, even though the grouped samples are conditioned on different local states rather than an identical prompt context, the objective can preserve the core relative policy optimization signal of GRPO under simplifying assumptions. Experiments on more than 800 Minecraft tasks show that our method achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance, demonstrating the effectiveness of our proposed RL framework for open-world VLM agents.

</details>


### [79] [A Deployment Audit of Release-Side Risk in Conformal Triage under Prevalence Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20956)
*Chengze Li,Xiao Liu,Hanrong Zhang,Haiyang Peng,Yanghao Ruan,Huanhuan Ma,Chunyu Miao,Qichao Zhou,Xiangrong Qi,Philip Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对共形分诊中的部署审计问题，提出一种泄漏感知审计框架，通过将目标对象分配到三个非重叠角色（患病率校正、共形校准、留出发布安全评估）来评估在高患病率偏移下，是否有事件阳性患者被无审查释放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有边际覆盖率和工作审查率等指标无法捕捉安全性关键问题：当患病率发生偏移时，真正经历目标事件（如癌症复发）的患者是否被释放而未经审查。传统的评估方法可能掩盖危险信号。

Method: 提出泄漏感知部署审计框架，将目标受试者分配到三个非重叠角色：(1) 患病率校正，(2) 共形校准，(3) 留出发布安全评估。该方法通过分离这三个角色，直接评估发布安全性：有多少事件阳性患者被清除而未审查、试点是否有足够事件标签进行校准、以及安全-审查权衡如何变化。

Result: 在NSCLC（非小细胞肺癌）回顾性试点研究中，该审计揭示了降低审查率的误导性：经患病率校正后，合并共形分支通过释放更多患者降低了审查率，但其中部分患者为事件阳性。类别级分支作为稀缺性诊断工具显示：试点中事件标签不足，无法认证安全的低审查率发布。

Conclusion: 该审计框架能够识别传统指标无法发现的安全隐患；证实了在事件标签稀缺的情况下，低审查率发布存在风险；为共形分诊的部署安全性提供了系统性的评估工具。

Abstract: Conformal triage converts predictive scores into deployment actions that either release a case, flag it for urgent attention, or defer it to human review. Under prevalence shift, however, the usual summaries of marginal coverage and human-review rate can miss the safety-critical question of whether patients who truly experience the target event are released without review. To address this gap, we introduce a leakage-aware deployment audit for release-side conformal triage. It first assigns target subjects to three non-overlapping roles: prevalence correction, conformal calibration, and held-out release-safety evaluation. This separation then lets the audit evaluate release directly: how many event-positive patients are cleared without review, whether the pilot has enough event labels for calibration, and how the safety-review trade-off shifts. Applying this audit to a retrospective NSCLC pilot shows why lower review can be misleading: after prevalence correction, the pooled conformal branch lowers review by releasing more patients, some of whom are event-positive. Within the audit, the classwise branch acts as a scarcity diagnostic: the pilot has too few event labels to certify safe low-review release.

</details>


### [80] [CP-MoE: Consistency-Preserving Mixture-of-Experts for Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20247)
*Yang Liu,Toan Nguyen,Flora D. Salim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CP-MoE框架，通过引入瞬态专家捕获早期任务特定更新并引导其整合到稳定专家中，结合一致性保持路由偏置和正则化机制，解决了MoE架构中持续学习的灾难性遗忘问题，在语言和多模态任务上均验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LoRA的MoE持续学习方法面临根本性权衡：专家隔离过于激进限制知识跨任务迁移，或任务特定更新覆盖重要现有参数导致严重遗忘。

Method: 提出CP-MoO框架，包含两个核心机制：1) 一致性保持路由偏置，利用瞬态专家估计与稳定专家的表示相似性，引导路由选择更兼容的专家；2) 瞬态专家引导的正则化机制，在合并时选择性保护重要的历史参数。两者协同作用减少参数干扰和遗忘，同时保持跨任务知识迁移。

Result: 在SuperNI基准测试中达到最优性能并展现更强的零样本迁移能力；在VQA v2数据集上有效扩展到多模态视觉推理，持续减少遗忘并优于强基线。

Conclusion: CP-MoE通过瞬态专家机制和双重策略有效解决了LoRA-based MoE持续学习中的参数干扰和遗忘问题，在语言和视觉-语言任务上均达到最优性能。

Abstract: Catastrophic forgetting remains a major obstacle to continual learning in large language models (LLMs) and vision--language models (VLMs). Although Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures offer an efficient path to scaling, existing LoRA-based MoE continual learning methods still face a fundamental trade-off: they either isolate experts too aggressively, limiting knowledge transfer across tasks, or allow task-specific updates to overwrite important existing parameters, leading to severe forgetting. To address this, we propose CP-MoE, a continual learning framework built around a transient expert that captures early task-specific updates and guides their integration into stable experts. CP-MoE introduces a consistency-preserving routing bias, which uses the transient expert to estimate representation similarity with stable experts and steer routing towards more compatible expert selection, and a transient expert-guided regularisation mechanism, which selectively protects important historical parameters during merging. Together, these components reduce parameter interference and forgetting while preserving cross-task knowledge transfer. We validate CP-MoE on both unimodal and multimodal continual learning benchmarks with LLM-based and VLM-based MoE models. On SuperNI benchmark, spanning diverse sequential language tasks, CP-MoE achieves state-of-the-art performance and stronger zero-shot transfer to unseen tasks. On VQA v2 dataset, it scales effectively to multimodal visual reasoning, consistently reduces forgetting, and outperforms strong MoE baselines.

</details>


### [81] [Automated Kernel Discovery Towards Understanding High-dimensional Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20249)
*Taeyoung Yun,Woocheol Shin,Inhyuck Song,Jaewoo Lee,Jinkyoo Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Kernel Discovery，一种LLM驱动的进化框架，用于高维贝叶斯优化中的核函数自动设计。核心创新包括：①两阶段方法（数学形式→代码）避免直接生成代码的语义重复问题；②突破传统搜索空间限制；③使用LOO-CRPS准则防止过拟合。在5个高维BO任务中，该方法在17种方法中达到平均排名1.2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维贝叶斯优化（BO）中，GP核函数设计是关键但困难的任务。现有自动化方法存在两个瓶颈：①核函数搜索空间被限制在基础核函数的加法和乘法组合；②基于LLM的方法需要处理原始观测数据，受限于上下文长度且难以提取有效模式。这两个限制导致现有方法在高维场景下性能不佳。

Method: 1. **两阶段LLM生成方法**：第一阶段让LLM提出新颖的数学形式，第二阶段将数学形式转换为可执行的验证代码，避免直接生成代码导致的语法多样但功能重复问题。

2. **扩展核函数搜索空间**：突破传统加法和乘法基础核函数的限制，允许更自由的核函数形式搜索。

3. **LOO-CRPS选择准则**：留一法连续排名概率分数作为核函数选择标准，通过惩罚过拟合来选择更优的核函数。

4. **进化框架**：结合LLM生成能力和进化算法思想，迭代优化核函数设计。

Result: 在5个高维BO基准测试中，Kernel Discovery在17种方法中取得平均排名1.2的优异性能，显著优于竞争性基线方法。研究还发现了在高维BO中表现优异的核函数特征。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了Kernel Discovery框架，通过LLM驱动的进化方法自动化设计高维贝叶斯优化中的GP核函数。实验表明该方法在17种方法中平均排名1.2，显著优于基线方法，并发现了适合高维BO的关键核函数特征。

【核心贡献】
1. 提出两阶段LLM方法解决上下文长度限制问题
2. 定义更广阔的核函数搜索空间（突破预设组合规则）
3. 设计LOO-CRPS准则防止核函数过拟合
4. 通过代码生成+数学形式分离的方式确保生成的核函数有效且可执行

【关键发现】
- 直接让LLM生成核函数代码会导致语法多样但功能相同的问题
- 两阶段方法（数学形式→代码）可有效解决此问题
- 发现了适合高维BO的核函数特征

Abstract: Gaussian Process (GP) kernels are central to Bayesian optimization (BO), yet designing effective kernels for high-dimensional problems still relies on extensive manual engineering. Existing automated approaches struggle in high dimensions for two bottlenecks: their kernel search space is limited to additions and multiplications of base kernels, and LLM-based approaches require conditioning on raw observations, which becomes infeasible due to context-length limits and the difficulty of extracting meaningful patterns. We introduce \textbf{Kernel Discovery}, a LLM-driven evolutionary framework for high-dimensional BO that searches a broader kernel space beyond predefined composition rules and does not require conditioning on observations. Motivated by the observation that directly prompting an LLM to generate kernel code yields syntactically varied but functionally identical kernels, we adopt a two-stage approach: an LLM first proposes novel mathematical forms, then a second LLM call converts each form into validated, executable code. We also propose a leave-one-out continuous ranked probability score (LOO-CRPS) as a selection criterion that penalizes overfitted kernels. On five high-dimensional BO benchmarks, our method achieves an average rank of \textbf{1.2 out of 17}, outperforming competitive baselines.
  We further analyze the discovered kernels to identify which kernels lead to improvements in high-dimensional BO.

</details>


### [82] [LOSCAR-SGD: Local SGD with Communication-Computation Overlap and Delay-Corrected Sparse Model Averaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20866)
*Yassine Maziane,Ammar Mahran,Artavazd Maranjyan,Peter Richtárik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LOSCAR-SGD是一种针对异构计算环境的分布式学习方法，结合了稀疏通信、本地SGD和通信计算重叠，通过延迟校正合并规则处理异步更新，首次为这三项技术的组合提供了理论收敛保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式学习（尤其是联邦学习）中通信是主要瓶颈，现有的组合三种通信减少技术（压缩、本地训练、通信计算重叠）缺乏理论支撑，且异构环境中不同worker可能执行不同数量的本地步骤，现有方法难以有效处理。

Method: LOSCAR-SGD方法采用稀疏通信（仅传输模型坐标子集）、本地步骤优化和延迟校正合并规则，能够在通信进行中继续优化，并将延迟的同步信息纳入更新而不丢弃重叠阶段的进展。

Result: 给出了光滑非凸目标函数的收敛保证，明确了稀疏性、重叠和worker异构性对收敛率的影响。

Conclusion: LOSCAR-SGD通过结合稀疏通信、局部训练和延迟校正合并规则，在异构计算环境下实现了有效收敛，实验验证了通信计算重叠可减少训练时间，延迟校正合并优于简单的覆盖策略。

Abstract: Communication is a major bottleneck in distributed learning, especially in large-scale settings and in federated learning environments with slow links. Three standard ways to reduce this cost are communication compression, local training, and communication-computation overlap. Methods that combine these ingredients are used in practice and have been found to be effective for large-scale training, but there is little theory for methods that combine all three. We study a heterogeneous-compute setting in which different workers may take different numbers of local steps, and we propose LOSCAR-SGD, a Local SGD method that communicates only a sparse subset of model coordinates and continues optimizing while communication is in flight. A key ingredient is a delay-corrected merge rule that incorporates delayed synchronized information without discarding the progress made during the overlap phase. We give convergence guarantees for smooth non-convex objectives and show how sparsity, overlap, and worker heterogeneity affect the rate. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first theory for this combination of ingredients. Experiments further show that communication-computation overlap reduces training time and that the delay-corrected merge outperforms naive overwriting.

</details>


### [83] [Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Safe Autonomous Driving Under Pedestrian Behavioral Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20255)
*Prakash Aryan,Kaushik Raghupathruni,Timo Kehrer,Sebastiano Panichella*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出使用多智能体强化学习（MARL）共同训练自动驾驶汽车（SDC）与行人，相比固定策略的规则基准产生更真实的交互场景。实验表明，共同训练使SDC目标达成率从35%提升至78%，碰撞率从33%降至14%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于仿真的自动驾驶测试依赖脚本化或简化的行人模型，无法捕捉真实人类过街行为的异质性和不确定性，尤其在由潜在人格特质支配的乱穿马路场景中，限制了安全评估的真实性。

Method: 构建MARL环境，使用MAPPO算法共同训练1辆SDC与12个行人。行人采用脚本化Dijkstra路径规划，RL策略控制高层决策（走/停），乱穿马路概率由每集开始时采样的隐藏人格特质决定。

Result: 500集评估中，MARL训练的SDC达78%目标、14%碰撞率，最佳规则基准仅35%目标、33%碰撞率。速度差分指标显示SDC在乱穿马路者附近（0-3m）比在人行横道用户附近快2.65m/s，表明乱穿马路场景未被预见。乱穿马路占13%过街事件但导致62%碰撞。

Conclusion: MARL共同训练相比单智能体RL减少30%碰撞，行人学会在SDC高速接近时等待。乱穿马路虽占比低但造成大多数碰撞，可从轨迹数据直接测量可预测与不可预测过街的行为差距。

Abstract: Simulation-based testing of self-driving cars (SDCs) typically relies on scripted or simplified pedestrian models that do not capture the heterogeneity and uncertainty of real human crossing behavior. This limits the realism of safety assessments, especially in scenarios involving jaywalking, which is governed by latent personality traits that the vehicle cannot observe. We hypothesize that jointly training pedestrians and the SDC with multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) produces more realistic interaction scenarios than training the SDC against fixed pedestrian policies, and that the resulting behavior gap between predictable and unpredictable crossings can be measured directly from trajectories. This paper describes a MARL environment in which an SDC and 12 pedestrians are co-trained using Multi-Agent Proximal Policy Optimization (MAPPO). Pedestrian locomotion follows scripted Dijkstra pathfinding, while an RL policy controls high-level go/wait decisions. Jaywalking probability depends on a per-pedestrian personality trait sampled at episode start and hidden from the SDC. In 500-episode evaluations, the co-trained SDC reached 78% of goals with a 14% collision rate, compared to 35% goals and 33% collisions for the best rule-based baseline. A speed differential metric shows that the SDC traveled 2.65 m/s faster near jaywalkers than near crosswalk users at close range (0-3 m), indicating that jaywalking encounters were not anticipated. Jaywalking accounted for 13% of crossing events but was associated with 62% of collisions. Co-training with MARL pedestrians reduced collisions by 30% relative to single-agent RL, as pedestrians learned to wait when the SDC approached at speed.

</details>


### [84] [FBOS-RL: Feedback-Driven Bi-Objective Synergistic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20256)
*Xikai Zhang,Yongzhi Li,Likang Xiao,Yingze Zhang,Yanhua Cheng,Quan Chen,Peng Jiang,Wenjun Wu,Liu Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FBOS-RL框架，通过反馈驱动的双目标协同机制解决GRPO在超出模型能力边界任务上的训练停滞问题，EPA和ECC形成正向飞轮效应，显著提升训练效率和最终性能上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前GRPO及其变体采用简单的采样策略，对所有rollout使用相同的原始prompt。当任务超出策略模型的当前能力时，这种采样方案难以产生高质量rollout，导致策略模型在参数更新时缺乏有意义的梯度方向，从而造成训练停滞。

Method: 提出了反馈驱动的双目标协同强化学习框架FBOS-RL，包含两个相互增强的训练目标：EPA（利用导向的策略对齐）和ECC（探索导向的能力培养）。核心创新包括：1）基于环境反馈的引导式探索增强机制；2）EPA与ECC的协同训练框架，形成正向飞轮效应。

Result: 实验结果表明：1）在相同的rollout数量下，FBOS-RL比GRPO和基于反馈的基线方法学习速度显著更快；2）最终达到更高的性能上限；3）整个训练过程中展现出更高的策略熵和更低的梯度范数。

Conclusion: FBOS-RL通过EPA和ECC的相互强化形成了正向飞轮效应，在相同数量的rollouts下，显著提升了训练效率和最终性能上限，同时展现出更高的策略熵和更低的梯度范数。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has become a cornerstone for aligning and unlocking the reasoning capabilities of large-scale models. At its core, the training loop of GRPO and its variants alternates between rollout sampling and policy update. Unlike supervised learning, where each gradient step is anchored to an explicit ground-truth target, the optimal gradient direction for updating model parameters in this setting is not known a priori; the high-quality rollouts drawn during the sampling stage therefore act as the implicit "teacher" that guides every parameter update. However, GRPO adopt a simple sampling scheme that conditions all rollouts on the same original prompt. When a task lies beyond the policy model's current capability, this sampling scheme rarely yields a high-quality rollout, leaving the policy model without a meaningful gradient direction when updating its parameters, which causes training to stall. To address this issue, we propose FBOS-RL, a Feedback-Driven Bi-Objective Synergistic reinforcement learning framework. Specifically, we let the model perform Feedback-Guided Exploration Enhancement based on the feedback provided by the environment, and on top of this we design two mutually reinforcing training objectives: Exploitation-oriented Policy Alignment(EPA) and Exploration-oriented Capability Cultivation(ECC). Extensive experiments demonstrate that EPA and ECC can mutually reinforce each other, forming a positive flywheel effect that significantly improves both the training efficiency and the final performance ceiling of reinforcement learning. Specifically, under an identical number of rollouts, FBOS-RL learns substantially faster than GRPO and feedback-based baselines and ultimately attains a higher performance ceiling, while exhibiting higher policy entropy and lower gradient norms throughout training.

</details>


### [85] [Instance Discrimination for Link Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20257)
*Valentin Cuzin-Rambaud,Mathieu Lefort,Rémy Cazabet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对图自监督学习中的链接预测任务，提出基于链接表示的新模型L-GRACE和L-BGRL，并证明其性能与现有监督/自监督方法相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的实例判别自监督学习方法在图像领域已取得成功，在图领域也被应用于节点分类，但较少有工作专门针对链接预测任务进行研究。

Method: 1) 系统评估了现有自监督模型在链接预测上的表现，发现性能主要取决于数据增强过程；2) 提出基于社区结构的结构性增强方法；3) 提出基于链接表示而非节点表示的新模型L-GRACE和L-BGRL。

Result: 所提出的L-GRACE和L-BGRL模型在无属性图上表现优异，性能可与当前最优的监督和自监督方法相媲美。

Conclusion: 基于链接表示的图自监督学习方法在链接预测任务上具有竞争力，数据增强策略对性能有重要影响，社区结构是有效的增强信号。

Abstract: Recently, instance discrimination models have emerged as a major solution for self-supervised learning. Having already demonstrated its effectiveness in the image domain, instance discrimination learning is now proving equally convincing in the graph domain, in particular for node classification. However, fewer contributions have tackled the link prediction task. In this contribution, we propose to adapt existing methods to this context. We first provide a rigorous evaluation of existing self-supervised models in the field of link prediction, showing that the main performance depends on the augmentation process (like in computer vision). We then propose a new structural augmentation based on the community structure that is relevant for link prediction. Our main contribution introduces two new models, L-GRACE and L-BGRL, based on link representations instead of node representations, which improve the performance of the existing methods, especially on unattributed graphs, and we show that they perform on par with the state of the art, both in supervised and self-supervised contexts.

</details>


### [86] [Residual Paving: Diagnosing the Routing Bottleneck in Selective Refusal Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20262)
*Bryce Hinkley,Peyman Najafirad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 Residual Paving 方法解决选择性拒绝编辑问题，通过将路由选择性（是否干预）与残差编辑能力（如何编辑）解耦，在冻结的指令微调transformer上实现三向控制。实验表明该方法显著优于单方向 steering 控制，且学习到的路由选择性是主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Address selective refusal editing as a three-way control problem: induce non-refusal on designated edit prompts while preserving benign behavior and harmful refusals outside the edit set. Current one-direction steering methods are insufficient for this multi-constraint task.

Method: Routed residual editing with decomposed architecture: early-layer router predicts scalar gate and expert mixture, and when active, prompt-conditioned bottleneck residual experts apply later-layer residual updates while leaving the backbone unchanged. Includes oracle-routing diagnostic replacing learned gate with held-out edit/keep labels.

Result: On Gemma-3-4B-IT: edit refusal reduced from 88.6% to 4.0%, with 95.5% benign distribution preservation and 87.3% harmful distribution preservation. Oracle routing improves keep-side diagnostic on every reported row across six backbones (median gain +12.9pp).

Conclusion: Residual Paving significantly outperforms one-direction steering controls (edit refusal 4.0% vs 86.8%/78.9%). Learned route selectivity is identified as the main bottleneck via oracle-routing diagnostics (median gain +12.9pp). Trajectory analysis confirms directed movement toward edit-target continuations rather than generic refusal suppression.

Abstract: We study selective refusal editing as a three-way control problem: induce non-refusal on designated edit prompts while preserving benign behavior and harmful refusals outside the edit set. We introduce Residual Paving, a routed residual editing method for frozen instruction-tuned transformers that separates route selectivity, whether to intervene, from residual-edit capacity, what edit to apply. An early-layer router predicts a scalar gate and expert mixture; when active, prompt-conditioned bottleneck residual experts apply later-layer residual updates while leaving the backbone unchanged. This decomposition supports an oracle-routing diagnostic where only the learned scalar gate is replaced with the held-out edit/keep label, leaving the residual editor and frozen backbone fixed. On the primary Gemma-3-4B-IT held-out split, learned Residual Paving reduces edit refusal from 88.6% to 4.0%, with 95.5% benign distribution preservation and 87.3% harmful distribution preservation. Same-protocol one-direction steering controls are much weaker on edit success, leaving edit refusal at 86.8% for Edit-target ActAdd and 78.9% for DIM-style refusal steering. The remaining failure is off-target harmful-keep degradation: harmful refusal remains below the frozen-base rate, 65.3% vs. 81.6%. Across six backbones, oracle routing improves the keep-side diagnostic score on every reported row, with median gain +12.9 pp, supporting the interpretation that learned route selectivity is the main observed bottleneck. Trajectory diagnostics on two backbones further suggest directed movement toward edit-target continuations rather than generic refusal suppression.

</details>


### [87] [Catching a Moving Subspace: Low-Rank Bandits Beyond Stationarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20269)
*Hamed Khosravi,Xiaoming Huo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究分段平稳低秩线性上下文老虎机问题，在潜在子空间低维且发生漂移的场景下，首次同时刻画了可辨识边界并实现了仅依赖固有秩r而非环境维d的动态遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有老虎机研究在处理低维潜在子空间和子空间漂移时存在割裂：低秩方法利用低维结构但无法适应变化，非平稳方法可适应漂移但需付出O(d√T)的环境维代价。实际应用（推荐、临床给药、广告定向）同时具备这两个特征，但现有方法无法兼顾。

Method: 提出SPSC算法，通过各向同性探测与窗控投影岭UCB开发交替进行，并引入CUSUM变体在线发现分段边界。理论上基于奖励二次泛函建立可辨识边界。

Result: 当且仅当三个条件同时满足（已知噪声方差、有界状态-噪声耦合、全维探测支撑），移动子空间才可恢复。动态遗憾为O(r̃√T)+O(T^(2/3))+O(W·V_in)，将环境维d替换为固有秩r。

Conclusion: 首次在该设置下刻画了可辨识边界并达到固有秩动态遗憾率。实验覆盖11个基准测试，涵盖合成数据、UCI/MovieLens、半合成临床及ZOZOTOWN生产日志数据，当d-r≳T^(1/6)时SPSC表现优于基线。

Abstract: Many bandit deployments (recommendation, clinical dosing, ad targeting) share two facts prior work handles only in isolation: rewards live on a low-dimensional latent subspace, and that subspace drifts. Stationary low-rank bandits exploit rank but break under subspace change; non-stationary linear bandits adapt to drift but pay ambient rate $\widetilde{O}(d\sqrt{T})$. We study piecewise-stationary low-rank linear contextual bandits with scalar feedback: $θ_t = B_k^\star w_t$ with rank-$r$ factor $B_k^\star\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times r}$ constant within each of $K$ unknown segments and able to shift at boundaries. Our results are tight along three axes. (i) Identification boundary. With single-play scalar rewards, the moving subspace is recoverable through quadratic functionals of rewards iff three probe-side conditions hold: known noise variance, bounded state-noise coupling, and full-dimensional probe support. Each is necessary in the unrestricted-second-moment problem, and jointly they are sufficient, characterizing the boundary of the solvable region. (ii) Algorithm and dynamic regret. SPSC interleaves isotropic probes with windowed projected ridge-UCB exploitation inside the learned $r$-dimensional subspace; a CUSUM-style variant discovers segment boundaries online. The costed dynamic regret is $\widetilde{O}(r\sqrt{T})+\widetilde{O}(T^{2/3})+O(W\,V_{\mathrm{in}})$, replacing the ambient $d\sqrt{T}$ rate with the intrinsic rank. (iii) Empirics. On eleven benchmarks spanning synthetic, UCI/MovieLens, semi-synthetic clinical, and ZOZOTOWN production-log data, SPSC outperforms non-stationary and low-rank baselines whenever $d-r\gtrsim T^{1/6}$, matching the analytical crossover. To our knowledge, this is the first work to characterize the identification boundary and attain the intrinsic-rank dynamic-regret rate in this setting.

</details>


### [88] [Conformal Selective Acting: Anytime-Valid Risk Control for RLVR-Trained LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20270)
*Hamed Khosravi,Xiaoming Huo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CSA是一种针对本地LLM部署的安全性认证包装器，通过在Bonferroni网格上维护Ville型e过程来填补"选择性风险+任意时间路径有效性"的方法空白，在480+160+10,300轮实验验证中成为唯一同时满足路径有效性和全量部署的方法，为无法使用前沿API的受监管组织提供模型无关的部署侧安全保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有包装器无法在自适应在线更新流上提供每轮安全证书。离线保角方法需要可交换性；在线保角方法仅绑定长期均值；非可交换扩展仅边际有效；最近的anytime包装器A-RCPS控制边际风险而非选择性风险。部署要求导致一个空缺：需要同时满足e-process per阈值、选择性风险、任意时间路径有效性、最优认证阈值规则这四个条件的方案。

Method: CSA构建了一个Bonferroni网格，为每个阈值维护Ville型e过程，并在RLVR滤波下进行评估。通过可预测更新和等渗校准的单调风险假设，建立了三个理论保证：(i) 任意时间路径选择性风险上界 R_T^act ≤ α + O(N_T^{-1/2})；(ii) 速率最优的认证性能 Θ(η̄^{-2}log(1/δ))；(iii)  horizon无关的发布率差距。

Result: 在480条专家基准流、160条对抗分布偏移流和10,300轮实时专家迭代RLVR实验（含四种基础模型、三种架构家族、在线LoRA微调）中，CSA在十种对比方法中唯一同时满足路径有效性和非拒绝部署。

Conclusion: CSA是唯一在所有实验场景中同时满足路径有效性保证和非拒绝部署的方法，填补了"e-process + 选择性风险 + 任意时间路径有效性 + 最大认证阈值规则"这一空白。它作为模型无关的部署侧补充工具，面向无法使用前沿API的受监管组织中的RLVR微调本地LLM部署场景。

Abstract: A local specialist LLM, fine-tuned with reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR) on operator-local data, is installed in a regulated organization with per-deployment error budget $α$. The operator needs a safety certificate for this deployment's stream at every round: no pooling across deployments, no waiting for a long-run average. Existing wrappers cannot deliver this on adaptive, online-updated streams: offline conformal-risk methods require exchangeability; online-conformal methods bound only long-run averages; non-exchangeable extensions are marginally valid; and the closest anytime wrapper, A-RCPS, controls marginal rather than selective risk. Using a (test statistic, validity guarantee, deployment rule) framework, we identify one empty cell forced by deployment requirements: e-process per threshold, selective risk, anytime-pathwise validity, max-certified-threshold rule. Conformal Selective Acting (CSA) fills it as a per-round wrapper maintaining a Ville-type e-process per threshold on a Bonferroni grid, evaluated against the RLVR filtration. Under predictable updates and isotonic-calibrated monotone risk we prove (i) an anytime-pathwise selective-risk bound $R_T^{\mathrm{act}}\leα+O(N_T^{-1/2})$, (ii) rate-optimal certification matching $Θ(\barη^{-2}\log(1/δ))$, and (iii) a horizon-independent release-rate gap. Across eight specialist benchmarks ($480$ streams), sixteen adversarial distribution-shift cells ($160$ streams), and five live Expert-Iteration RLVR cells with online LoRA over four base models in three architecture families ($10{,}300$ rounds), CSA is the only method among ten compared that satisfies pathwise validity and non-refusing deployment on every cell. We do not propose a new LLM, training algorithm, or policy class; CSA is the deployment-side complement, orthogonal to the model, for operators who cannot use a frontier API.

</details>


### [89] [Smaller Abstract State Spaces Enable Cross-Scale Generalization in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20272)
*Nasehatul Mustakim,Lucas Lehnert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个RL智能体中实现分布外（OOD）泛化的理论模型，通过扩展POMDPs中的状态抽象框架，定义了一种新的继任者加权模型约简方法，并推导了OOD测试性能的边界条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类能够将抽象概念泛化到更复杂或更大规模的任务中，但构建具有这种能力的强化学习系统仍然困难重重。本文旨在解决RL智能体如何实现OOD泛化的理论问题。

Method: 1. 将现有状态抽象框架和证明技术扩展到POMDPs；2. 定义继任者加权模型约简（successor-weighted model reduction），允许压缩到比先前定义更小的抽象空间；3. 推导OOD测试性能边界。

Result: 性能损失分解为近似误差和估计误差两部分，揭示了缩小抽象状态空间如何改善测试性能和OOD泛化能力。

Conclusion: 约束智能体在有限小规模抽象状态集上运作对于实现向更复杂任务的泛化是必要的；该研究为学习跨不同复杂度任务扩展的RL架构提供了理论基础。

Abstract: While humans readily generalize abstract concepts to more complex or larger tasks, building Reinforcement Learning (RL) systems with this ability remains elusive. Here, we present the first theoretical model of how such Out-of-Distribution (OOD) generalization can be achieved in RL agents. Our approach considers Partially Observable Markov Decision Processes (POMDPs) and assumes that an intelligent agent uses an abstraction function to determine which experiences can be treated as equivalent and which must be distinguished. First, we extend the existing state abstraction framework and proof techniques to POMDPs. Then, we define a successor-weighted model reduction, a model reduction variant that enables compression into smaller abstract spaces than prior definitions allow. We derive a bound on the agent's OOD test performance, thereby defining the conditions under which OOD generalization is achievable. This bound decomposes an agent's performance loss into approximation and estimation errors, revealing how reducing an agent's abstract state space size improves test performance and OOD generalization. Our analysis suggests that constraining an agent to operate over a small, finite set of abstract states is necessary for achieving generalization to more complex tasks. Our results motivate further research into learning RL architectures that scale across tasks of varying complexity levels.

</details>


### [90] [Modality-Decoupled Online Recursive Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20273)
*Siyuan Li,Youyuan Zhang,Fangming Liu,Jing Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: M-ORE是一种针对多模态大语言模型(MLLMs)的在线模型编辑方法，通过解耦模态更新和正交低秩编辑子空间来解决跨模态冲突和长期干扰问题。该方法在多个MLLM主干和基准上验证了其在可靠性、泛化性和局部性方面的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对纯文本LLM的编辑方法在MLLMs上表现退化，原因包括：1) 视觉主导的激活值扭曲了更新统计量，导致跨模态冲突；2) 序列编辑在共享编辑空间中纠缠，放大长期干扰，造成编辑间干扰。在线模型编辑需要在严格计算和内存预算下处理持续的修正流。

Method: M-ORE通过以下设计解决上述问题：1) 基于统一近端投影(proximal-projection)公式推导；2) 采用Sherman-Morrison递归实现闭合形式更新，达到常数级单次编辑开销；3) 为文本栈和视觉投影器维护模块级局部统计量，避免视觉主导的更新塑形；4) 在固定正交低秩编辑子空间中进行持续更新，通过Sherman-Morrison递归缓解长期干扰。

Result: 实验在多个MLLM主干和在线编辑基准上进行，结果表明M-ORE方法在可靠性、泛化性和局部性方面一致优于强基线方法，同时实现了良好的质量-效率权衡。

Conclusion: M-ORE成功实现了针对多模态场景优化的在线持续编辑能力，通过模态解耦和正交子空间约束有效解决了跨模态冲突和编辑间干扰问题，为MLLMs的终身适应提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Online model editing for multimodal large language models (MLLMs) requires assimilating a stream of corrections under tight compute and memory budgets. Yet editors developed for text-only LLMs often degrade on MLLMs: visually dominant activations skew the statistics that shape updates, causing cross-modal conflict, while sequential writes become entangled in a shared edit space and amplify long-horizon interference, causing inter-edit interference. To address these, we propose M-ORE, a modality-decoupled online recursive editor for lifelong MLLM adaptation. M-ORE is derived from a unified proximal-projection formulation and admits a closed-form update with a Sherman-Morrison recursion, yielding constant per-edit overhead. It maintains module-wise locality statistics for the text stack and the visual projector to avoid visually dominated update shaping and performs continual updates in a fixed orthogonal low-rank edit subspace via a Sherman-Morrison recursion to mitigate long-horizon interference. Experiments on multiple MLLM backbones and online editing benchmarks show that our M-ORE method consistently improves reliability, generality, and locality over strong baselines, while achieving favorable quality-efficiency scaling. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/lab-klc/M-ORE.

</details>


### [91] [Domain-Adaptable Reinforcement Learning for Code Generation with Dense Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21180)
*Erfan Aghadavoodi Jolfaei,Daniel Maninger,Abhinav Anand,Mert Tiftikci,Mira Mezini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于PPO的强化学习框架，通过可定制的执行感知奖励和token级信用分配机制微调代码生成LLM，显著提升了通用代码生成（MBPP/MBPP+）和机器人程序合成（RoboEval）的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在自动化代码生成方面潜力巨大，但缺乏对正确性、质量、安全性和领域特定约束的保证。在机器人等领域，环境感知和物理约束认知至关重要。

Method: 1) 使用PPO算法微调预训练LLM；2) 设计可定制的执行感知奖励公式，涵盖语法、功能正确性、代码风格、安全性和模拟器可执行性；3) 提出token级奖励映射机制，实现从执行结果到生成token的有效信用分配。

Result: 1) MBPP上pass@1提升19%；2) RoboEval上执行失败率降低51%；3) 功能正确性和模拟器可执行性显著改善。

Conclusion: RL框架能有效对齐LLM与正确程序生成及领域特定需求，实验结果验证了执行感知奖励和token级信用分配的有效性。

Abstract: Large language models show strong potential for automated code generation, but lack guarantees for correctness, quality, safety, and domain-specific constraints. For instance in robotics, where code generation is increasingly being used for planning and executing actions, awareness of the environment and physical constraints is critical. To facilitate the adaption of code-generating LLMs to diverse requirements, including domain-specific ones, we present a reinforcement learning framework that fine-tunes pre-trained LLMs using proximal policy optimization. Our customizable execution-aware reward formula captures and optimizes syntax, functional correctness, code style, security, and simulator executability. A token-level reward mapping mechanism enables effective credit assignment from execution outcomes to generated tokens. The framework is evaluated on general-purpose code generation (MBPP/MBPP+) and robotic program synthesis (RoboEval). The results show substantial improvements in functional correctness and simulator executability, including an absolute pass@1 increase of 19% on MBPP and a reduction in execution failures by 51% on RoboEval. These findings demonstrate that structured reinforcement learning can effectively align language models to correct program generation and domain-specific requirements.

</details>


### [92] [OmniISR: A Unified Framework for Centralized and Federated Learning via Intermediate Supervision and Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20276)
*Wei-Bin Kou,Guangxu Zhu,Ming Tang,Chen Zhang,Lisheng Wu,Lei Zhou,Yujiu Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OmniISR是一个统一框架，通过在多个隐藏层引入中间监督和正则化信号，融合集中式学习(CL)、联邦学习(FL)和混合CL-FL三种训练模式。该框架使用互信息(MI)对齐内部协变量偏移和客户端漂移表征，使用负熵(NE)避免过度自信预测和设备特定坍缩。理论部分给出统一的O(1/√T)收敛界、联邦漂移界、梯度对齐保证和逃逸时间界。实验表明该方法在两种范式下均提升性能，CL-FL差距减少22.60%，在48个配对指标中获得37个胜出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前全球部署的边缘智能面临异构法律框架：部分地区允许通过云端数据聚合进行集中式学习(CL)，而其他地区强制执行严格的数据本地化，需要联邦学习(FL)。这种操作二分法导致两种不兼容的优化机制——CL产生无偏全局梯度但存在内部协变量偏移，FL产生有偏且易漂移的本地更新。任何简单整合这两种方法都缺乏严格的理论保证。

Method: 提出OmniISR框架，核心是在多个隐藏层配备中间监督和正则化(ISR)信号。具体包括：(i)使用互信息(MI)作为中间监督，对齐CL中的内部协变量偏移和FL中的客户端漂移表征；(ii)采用负熵(NE)作为中间正则化器，惩罚过度自信预测，保留表征不确定性，避免设备特定坍缩。

Result: 理论层面推导出：(i)统一的、ISR无关的、非渐近的O(1/√T)收敛界，表明ISR不破坏标准SGD收敛；(ii)量化ISR减少客户端漂移的联邦漂移界；(iii)在温和偏差条件下确保CL和FL更新不冲突的梯度对齐保证；(iv)表明CL-FL混合扩大有效随机性并加速从严格鞍点逃逸的显式逃逸时间界。实验层面：OmniISR在集中式和联邦式范式中一致提升模型性能，CL-FL差距减少22.60%，在多个FL算法中取得48个配对指标中的37个胜出。

Conclusion: OmniISR成功构建了一个兼顾法律合规性（支持CL/FL/混合模式切换）和理论严谨性的统一学习框架。ISR机制（MI监督+NE正则化）有效解决了两种范式各自的核心问题（内部协变量偏移和客户端漂移），为跨异构法律管辖区的边缘智能部署提供了可行的技术方案。

Abstract: The global deployment of edge intelligence operates across heterogeneous legal frameworks. While some regions permit centralized learning (CL) via cloud data aggregation, others enforce strict data localization, necessitating federated learning (FL). This operational dichotomy introduces two incompatible optimization regimes (i.e., unbiased global gradients yet coupled with internal covariate shift in CL versus biased, drift-prone local updates in FL), resulting in that any naive integration of the two lacks rigorous theoretical guarantees. To fill this gap, we propose OmniISR, a unified framework that fuses pure CL, pure FL, and hybrid CL-FL training modes via equipping intermediate supervision and regularization (ISR) signals at multiple hidden layers. Specifically, we propose (i) to use mutual-information (MI) as intermediate supervision to align shifting internal covariate in CL and client-drifting representations in FL, and (ii) to adopt negative-entropy (NE) as intermediate regularizer to penalize overconfident prediction, preserve representational uncertainty, and avoid device-specific collapse. On the theory side, we derive (i) a unified, ISR-agnostic, and non-asymptotic O(1/sqrt(T)) convergence bound that shows the introduced ISR does not violate standard SGD convergence, (ii) a federated drift-bound that quantifies the ISR-reduced client drift, (iii) a gradient-alignment guarantee that ensures non-conflicting CL and FL updates under mild bias, and (iv) an explicit escape-time bound that indicates that CL-FL hybrid mixing enlarges effective stochasticity and accelerates escape from strict saddles. Extensive experiments demonstrate that OmniISR consistently improves model performance in both centralized and federated paradigms, reduces the CL-FL gap by 22.60%, and yields 37/48 paired metric wins across multiple FL algorithms.

</details>


### [93] [ClaimDiff-RL: Fine-Grained Caption Reinforcement Learning through Visual Claim Comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20278)
*Tianle Li,Xuyang Shen,Yan Ma,Rongxin Guo,Shaoxiang Chen,Jiacheng Chen,Haochen Wang,Hongyang Tang,Yucong Zhou,Yu Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ClaimDiff-RL框架，通过将图像描述的奖励粒度从序列级别细化到"原子声明差异"级别，解决强化学习在长图像描述任务中的奖励粒度问题。该方法使用多模态法官枚举视觉差异、验证差异、分配错误类型和严重程度，从而分别可测量和调整幻觉与遗漏事实。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL方法使用成对偏好、基于参考的指标或整体标量奖励，这些方法将局部错误压缩为序列级信号，掩盖了事实性与覆盖率之间的权衡。例如，使用整体标量奖励可以减少幻觉，但同时会增加遗漏事实的问题。

Method: 给定图像、Actor描述和参考描述，多模态法官：(1)枚举视觉 grounded 的差异；(2)验证每条差异与图像的符合性；(3)分配开放词汇错误类型和严重程度；(4)生成每条差异的统计数据用于奖励组合。

Result: ClaimDiff-RL能够暴露事实性与覆盖率之间的权衡，并在160张人工标注的诊断基准、公开描述基准和VQA基准上表现优异。在物体计数、空间关系和场景识别等细粒度能力上甚至超越Gemini-3-Pro-Preview。

Conclusion: Typed, verifiable claim differences是一种有效的细粒度和可诊断的描述RL奖励单元，能够实现更平衡的幻觉-遗漏事实权衡点。

Abstract: Long-form image captioning exposes a reward granularity problem in RL: captions are judged as whole sequences, while the important errors occur at the level of individual visual claims. A good dense caption should be both faithful and informative, avoiding hallucination without omitting salient details. Yet pairwise preferences, reference-based metrics, and holistic scalar rewards compress these local errors into a single sequence-level signal, obscuring the tradeoff between factuality and coverage. We introduce ClaimDiff-RL, a framework that uses reference-conditioned atomic claim differences as the reward unit for caption RL. Given an image, an actor caption, and a reference caption, a multimodal judge enumerates visually grounded differences, verifies each difference against the image, assigns open-vocabulary error types and severity levels, and produces per-difference statistics for reward composition. This makes hallucinated claims and omitted salient facts separately measurable and tunable. Experiments show that holistic scalar rewards can reduce hallucination by increasing missing facts, while ClaimDiff-RL exposes this faithfulness and coverage tradeoff and enables more balanced operating points. On a 160-image human-labeled diagnostic benchmark, public captioning benchmarks, and VQA benchmarks, ClaimDiff-RL improves the hallucination--missing-fact balance, preserves general capability, and even surpasses Gemini-3-Pro-Preview on several fine-grained Capability dimensions such as object counting, spatial relations, and scene recognition. These results suggest that typed, verifiable claim differences are an effective reward unit for fine-grained and diagnosable caption RL.

</details>


### [94] [Introspective X Training: Feedback Conditioning Improves Scaling Across all LLM Training Stages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20285)
*Brandon Cui,Ximing Lu,Jaehun Jung,Syeda Nahida Akter,Hyunwoo Kim,Yuxiao Qu,David Acuna,Shrimai Prabhumoye,Yejin Choi,Prithviraj Ammanabrolu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出内省训练(IXT)，通过思考奖励模型生成批判性反馈并以前缀条件化方式实现质量感知训练，使7.5-12B模型实现2.8倍计算效率提升，并解锁数学和代码领域的新性能水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM训练流程包含多个不断增长的阶段（如预训练、后训练等），如何在这些阶段中更高效地扩展成为一个关键问题。核心观察是后续阶段的动态信息可用于指导早期阶段训练。

Method: 提出内省训练(IXT)方法，使用"思考奖励模型"对数据进行自然语言批判性反馈标注，然后通过前缀条件化方式训练模型，使模型在训练早期阶段即能区分不同质量的内容。

Result: 在7.5-12B参数的Transformer密集型LLM上（最高训练至18万亿token）验证表明：计算效率提升达2.8倍；在数学和代码领域达到此前无法实现的性能水平。

Conclusion: IXT通过在训练早期引入质量感知机制，实现了高达2.8倍的计算效率提升，并在数学和代码领域达到了此前无法达到的性能水平，证明了利用后续阶段信息指导早期训练的有效性。

Abstract: We tackle the question of how to scale more efficiently across the many, ever-growing stages of current LLM training pipelines. Our guiding intuition stems from the fact that the dynamics of later stages of the pipeline, e.g. post-training, can be used to inform earlier stages such as pre-training. To this end, we propose Introspective Training (or IXT), inspired by offline reward-conditioned reinforcement learning and applicable to any stage of training. IXT uses a thinking reward model to annotate data with natural language critique based feedback, enabling quality aware training from the earliest stages of the pipeline. Models are then trained by prefix-conditioning the data with the generated feedback -- ensuring that not all tokens are treated equally starting much earlier in training than usual. Comprehensive experiments on 7.5-12B transformer-based dense LLMs trained from scratch all the way up to 18 Trillion tokens seen show that our method: bends scaling curves resulting in up to 2.8x more compute efficiency generally; and reaches performance levels unachievable for models trained otherwise in domains such as math and code.

</details>


### [95] [Plug-and-Play Spiking Operators: Breaking the Nonlinearity Bottleneck in Spiking Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20289)
*Xinzhe Yuan,Xiang Peng,Bin Gu,Huan Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种即插即用的框架，通过LIF神经元群体实现除法、指数和L2范数三种基本运算，实现Transformer中非线性算子（Softmax、SiLU、LayerNorm等）的脉冲友好近似，支持现有ANN-to-SNN转换流程，在多种LLM Transformer上精度损失小于1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前ANN-to-SNN转换流程主要关注线性代数运算的脉冲实现，对关键非线性算子支持有限。然而，神经形态硬件执行约束要求非线性运算（如除法、指数、归一化）需通过标准LIF神经元动态实现，但现有方法存在兼容性问题。

Method: 将非线性计算分解为三种基本原语——除法、指数和ℓ2范数，通过LIF神经元群体进行群体计算实现，并结合轻量级移位缩放避免浮点运算。将这些原语组合为模块化算子块，形成即插即用的框架。

Result: 在多种LLM Transformer上评估，选择性替换目标非线性算子后，所有任务的精度损失均小于1%。

Conclusion: 该框架无需微调即可支持常见Transformer非线性算子，为大规模脉冲语言模型的训练free转换提供了实用方案。

Abstract: ANN-to-SNN conversion offers a practical, training-free route to spiking large language models. However, current pipelines primarily focus on spike-driven realizations for Transformer linear-algebra operations, while providing limited support for key nonlinear operators. This gap limits compatibility with neuromorphic-style execution constraints, where such nonlinearities typically require division, exponentiation, or norm computations that are not naturally supported by standard leaky integrate-and-fire dynamics. To solve this problem, we propose a plug-and-play framework that implements spike-friendly approximations for Transformer nonlinearities and integrates into existing ANN-to-SNN pipelines. Our method decomposes these nonlinear computations into three recurring primitives -- division, exponentiation, and $\ell_2$ norms -- and realizes them via population computation using LIF neuron groups, combined with lightweight bit-shift scaling to avoid floating-point arithmetic. By composing these primitives as modular operator blocks, our framework supports common Transformer nonlinearities (e.g., Softmax, SiLU, and normalization) without any fine-tuning. Experiments on a range of LLMs Transformers show that selectively replacing the targeted nonlinear operators incurs less than a $1\%$ accuracy drop across all evaluated tasks.

</details>


### [96] [TreeText-CTS: Compact, Source-Traceable Tree-Path Evidence for Irregular Clinical Time-Series Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20292)
*Kwanhyung Lee,Juhwan Choi,Jongheon Kim,Joohyung Lee,Hyeongwon Jang,Eunho Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TreeText-CTS将不规则电子健康记录轨迹转换为可读、可溯源的树路径证据单元，通过XGBoost模型和语言模型组合实现高性能临床预测，同时保持证据的透明性和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数值时间序列模型不提供可读证据；现有文本接口存在序列化冗长或摘要难以溯源的问题，亟需一种可解释、可溯源的临床时间序列表示方法。

Method: 提出TreeText-CTS框架，将不规则EHR轨迹通过冻结的XGBoost模型路由多尺度窗口摘要，verbalize激活的树路径为确定性的、可溯源的阈值条件证据单元，由证据选择器筛选信息性子集，最终由语言模型编码器整合进行预测，无需患者级摘要或推理时自回归解码。

Result: 在PhysioNet 2012死亡预测、MIMIC-III死亡预测和PhysioNet 2019脓毒症预测三项任务中，TreeText-CTS在文本类EHR接口中取得最佳AUROC和AUPRC，相比最强文本接口AUPRC提升6.0-9.7个百分点，且与数值时间序列模型相当。消融实验验证了树路径证据构建、证据选择和语言模型组合各环节的贡献。

Conclusion: TreeText-CTS通过将每个传递给语言模型编码器的文本片段都建立在激活的树路径阈值条件之上，实现了最终预测器所提供证据的可检查性和可溯源性，为临床决策支持提供了透明、可解释的时间序列分析工具。

Abstract: Numerical time-series models can effectively process irregular electronic health record (EHR) trajectories, but they do not naturally expose the measurements and temporal patterns supporting each risk estimate as readable evidence. Existing text-based interfaces improve readability, but typically rely on either raw serialization, which is lengthy and redundant, or patient-level free-form summaries, which are difficult to trace to source measurements and time windows. To bridge this gap, we introduce TreeText-CTS (Clinical Time-Series), which converts irregular EHR trajectories into human-readable, compact, source-traceable tree-path evidence units without patient-level summarization or inference-time autoregressive decoding. TreeText-CTS routes multi-scale window summaries through frozen XGBoost models and verbalizes activated tree paths as deterministic, source-traceable evidence units composed of threshold conditions. An evidence selector assembles an informative subset of these units, which a language-model encoder then integrates for prediction. Across PhysioNet 2012 mortality, MIMIC-III mortality, and PhysioNet 2019 sepsis-onset forecasting, TreeText-CTS achieves the best AUROC and AUPRC among evaluated text-based EHR time-series interfaces, improving AUPRC by 6.0 to 9.7 absolute percentage points over the strongest prior text-based interface while remaining competitive with numerical time-series models. Ablations show that tree-path evidence construction, evidence selection, and language-model composition each contribute to performance. Because every span passed to the language-model encoder is constructed from activated tree-path threshold conditions, TreeText-CTS makes the evidence supplied to the final predictor inspectable and source-traceable.

</details>


### [97] [Quant.npu: Enabling Efficient Mobile NPU Inference for on-device LLMs via Fully Static Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20295)
*Jinghe Zhang,Daliang Xu,Chenghua Wang,Weikai Xie,Tao Qi,Yun Ma,Mengwei Xu,Gang Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Quant.npu，一个面向NPU硬件的integer-only全静态量化框架，通过可学习的量化参数和旋转矩阵实现低比特激活-权重量化。核心技术包括针对不同激活profile的旋转-and-bit-width-aware初始化、针对旋转/非旋转张量的distribution-aware选择性优化两阶段流程，以及sensitivity-guided自适应混合精度策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动端NPUs要求完全静态量化，但现有PTQ方法依赖动态激活量化，无法兼容NPU硬件约束，存在高保真度PTQ与NPU约束推理之间的gap。

Method: 提出旋转-and-bit-width-aware初始化策略应对不同激活分布；采用distribution-aware selective optimization的两阶段量化流程分别处理旋转和非旋转张量；引入sensitivity-guided adaptive mixed-precision平衡精度与效率。

Result: 在真实移动NPU上实现与SOTA方法相当的精度，推理延迟降低最多15.1%。

Conclusion: Quant.npu 通过整数-only全静态量化框架，解决了NPU部署的兼容性问题，在保持精度的同时将推理延迟降低15.1%。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed on mobile devices, where Neural Processing Units (NPUs) necessitate fully static quantization for optimal inference efficiency. However, existing post-training quantization (PTQ) methods predominantly rely on dynamic activation quantization, rendering them incompatible with NPU hardware constraints. To bridge the gap between high-fidelity PTQ and NPU-constrained inference, we propose Quant.npu, a integer-only fully static quantization framework. It incorporates learnable quantization parameters and rotation matrices, enabling low-bit activation-weight quantization without runtime quantization parameters re-computation. Crucially, we identify that initialization and selective optimization of quantization parameters is pivotal for optimization stability, as improper initialization and naive joint optimization induce gradient instability that disrupts the optimization of rotation matrices. To address this, we propose a rotation-and-bit-width-aware initialization tailored to diverse activation profiles and a distribution-aware selective optimization (two-stage quantization pipeline) tailored to rotated and unrotated tensors. Furthermore, we introduce a sensitivity-guided adaptive mixed-precision scheme to balance accuracy with inference efficiency. Extensive experiments on real-world mobile NPUs demonstrate that Quant.npu achieves comparable accuracy to state-of-the-art methods, while reducing inference latency by up to 15.1%.

</details>


### [98] [Spectral Unforgetting: Post-Hoc Recovery of Damaged Capabilities Without Retraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20296)
*Aarash Abro,Muhammad Tahir*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DG-Hard，一种仅使用预训练检查点和微调后检查点的后验修复方法，通过对权重增量矩阵进行Donoho-Gavish硬奇异值阈值来保留高能量结构、移除光谱噪声，从而在恢复微调损坏能力的同时保留目标任务收益和有益的held-out改进。实验在多个设置中验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型在目标任务上进行微调时，会系统性地损害预训练数据中从未明确训练过的能力，即灾难性遗忘。当前研究不足之处在于：1) 现有方法难以在不访问原始预训练数据的情况下修复；2) 评估指标不完善——平均准确率掩盖基准间的个体失败，朴素恢复分数反而奖励简单回退到基础模型的行为。

Method: DG-Hard将微调更新Δ = W_ft - W_base视为低秩任务对齐信号，嵌入IID-like噪声残差中（梯度下降无动机移除），对每个权重-增量矩阵应用Donoho-Gavish硬奇异值阈值，仅保留更新中的结构化高能量部分，移除光谱Bulk，转化为闭合形式的SVD滤波步骤。

Result: DG-Hard在14个(model, task)设置和9个跨域held-out基准上实现了最强的均衡修复效果；在三个独立安全维度上恢复被良性微调降低的安全对齐（且未使用任何对齐数据）；证明了微调导致的能力损失部分是权重更新中可移除的光谱残差。

Conclusion: DG-Hard在14个(model, task)设置和9个跨域held-out基准上实现了最强的均衡修复效果，并能在三个独立安全维度上恢复被微调降低的安全对齐，表明微调导致的能力损失部分源于权重更新中的可移除光谱残差，而非专业化的必然代价。

Abstract: Fine-tuning a language model for a target task routinely degrades capabilities the training data never explicitly threatened. We study this phenomenon, known as catastrophic forgetting, and propose a post-hoc repair solution that uses only the pretrained checkpoint $W_{\mathrm{base}}$ and its fine-tuned descendant $W_{\mathrm{ft}}$. The goal is not merely to revert the model toward the base checkpoint, but to recover capabilities damaged by fine-tuning while preserving both the target-task gains and any beneficial held-out improvements. We introduce DG-Hard, a checkpoint-only spectral repair method for the fine-tuning update $Δ= W_{\mathrm{ft}} - W_{\mathrm{base}}$. DG-Hard treats $Δ$ as a low-rank task-aligned signal embedded in an IID-like noise residual that gradient descent has no incentive to remove, and applies the Donoho-Gavish hard singular-value threshold to each weight-delta matrix, keeping the structured high-energy part of the update and removing the spectral bulk. This reduces repair to a closed-form SVD filtering step requiring no data-dependent tuning. A central difficulty is evaluation: average accuracy hides per-benchmark failures, while naive recovery scores reward models that simply revert toward the base. We therefore introduce a partition-conditional metric that separately tracks healing, preservation, non-damage, and target-task retention. Across $14$ (model, task) settings and nine cross-domain held-out benchmarks, DG-Hard achieves the strongest balanced repair among post-hoc baselines. DG-Hard also restores safety alignment degraded by benign fine-tuning on three independent safety axes, despite using no alignment data. These results suggest that part of fine-tuning-induced capability loss is not an unavoidable consequence of specialization, but a removable spectral residue in the weight update itself.

</details>


### [99] [Mechanisms of Misgeneralization in Physical Sequence Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20299)
*Kento Nishi,Raphael Tang,Karun Kumar,Core Francisco Park,Hidenori Tanaka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究生成式序列模型在物理规划任务中的"物理误泛化"问题：当个体轨迹看起来合理时，其在物理量（如距离、能量）上的聚合分布却与训练意图不符。通过合成任务实验，作者揭示这是模型典型局部误差通过物理测量传播所致，并提出用"数据偏差核"预测分布偏移，最终给出核引导的干预策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程师在构建训练数据集时会精心选择演示样本以控制物理量的分布（如均匀覆盖行程距离范围），期望约束智能体的预期功耗等特性。但发现标准深度学习模型会违反这一设计意图——生成的个体轨迹看似合理，聚合后的物理量分布却出现系统性偏差。这种现象被称为"物理误泛化"，其机制尚不明确。

Method: 1. 构建受控的合成任务以隔离研究物理误泛化现象；2. 提出"数据偏差核"（data deviation kernel）用于估计模型误差；3. 分析误差如何通过物理测量传播并导致分布偏移；4. 在三个不同复杂度的任务上验证：合成运动任务、迷宫导航、 双摆运动。

Result: 物理误泛化确实存在且可复现；数据偏差核能够预测哪些物理量会获得或失去概率质量；该机制解释在不同任务中分布偏移的方向和程度；基于核的干预措施能有效缓解物理误泛化。

Conclusion: 物理误泛化是深度学习模型在物理规划任务中的系统性缺陷，源于模型局部误差与物理测量动态的耦合。数据偏差核为诊断和预测该现象提供了有效工具，机械论解释为设计针对性的缓解策略奠定了基础。

Abstract: Generative sequence models are often trained to plan motion in physical domains, from robotics to mechanical simulations. When constructing a dataset to train such a model, engineers may curate demonstrations to specify how trajectories should be distributed over a physical quantity like travel distance or mechanical energy. For example, a roboticist building a maze navigation agent might choose demonstrations whose travel distances cover a fixed range uniformly, hoping to constrain the agent's expected power usage. We find that standard deep learning can violate this intent: each generated trajectory can seem plausible on its own, but the aggregate distribution over the physical quantity is wrong. We call this failure physical misgeneralization, and develop an account of its mechanism. Using controlled synthetic tasks, we show that physical misgeneralization arises when local errors typical of the model class propagate through the physical measurement to shift the recovered distribution. We estimate these errors with a data deviation kernel, and we use it to predict which physical quantities gain or lose mass in both our synthetic and more applied maze navigation and double-pendulum motion tasks. Finally, our mechanistic interpretation helps identify which mitigation strategies are structurally promising, and we use it to propose a kernel-informed intervention.

</details>


### [100] [Robust Subspace-Constrained Quadratic Models for Low-Dimensional Structure Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20300)
*Zheng Zhai,Xiaohui Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种鲁棒子空间约束二次模型（SCQM），通过整合子空间约束与灵活噪声建模，实现了对重尾和轻尾噪声的统一处理。开发了带回溯线搜索的梯度优化算法，并通过敏感度分析对比了ℓ_p^p和ℓ_2损失函数的特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统低维结构学习方法在处理复杂噪声时鲁棒性不足，需要一种能够同时应对重尾和轻尾噪声的统一框架。

Method: 提出了鲁棒子空间约束二次模型（SCQM），基于子空间约束二次矩阵分解（SQMF）框架，能够适配广义高斯和径向拉普拉斯等多样噪声分布；设计了带回溯线搜索的梯度算法以高效求解非凸优化问题。

Result: 所提方法在多样数据环境下均展现了优异的鲁棒性和重建精度，显著优于现有方法；敏感度分析阐明了ℓ_p^p和ℓ_2损失函数在不同噪声特征下的行为差异。

Conclusion: 所提出的SCQM方法通过结合子空间约束与灵活噪声建模，在鲁棒性和重建精度方面均优于现有方法，特别是在处理复杂噪声环境时展现出显著优势。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a robust subspace-constrained quadratic model (SCQM) for learning low-dimensional structure from high-dimensional data. Building upon the subspace-constrained quadratic matrix factorization (SQMF) framework, the proposed model accommodates a broad class of noise distributions, including generalized Gaussian and radial Laplace models. This generalization enables reliable performance under both heavy-tailed and light-tailed noise, thereby substantially enhancing robustness across diverse data regimes. To efficiently address the resulting nonconvex optimization problem, we develop a gradient-based algorithm equipped with a backtracking line-search strategy that ensures stable and efficient convergence. In addition, we present a sensitivity analysis of the $\ell_p^p$ and $\ell_2$ loss functions, elucidating their distinct behaviors under varying noise characteristics. Extensive numerical experiments corroborate the theoretical analysis and demonstrate that the proposed approach consistently outperforms existing methods in terms of robustness and reconstruction accuracy.

</details>


### [101] [Neural Collapse by Design: Learning Class Prototypes on the Hypersphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20302)
*Panagiotis Koromilas,Theodoros Giannakopoulos,Mihalis A. Nicolaou,Yannis Panagakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示交叉熵和监督对比学习是单位超球面上原型对比的两种表现形式，分别通过归一化损失（NTCE/NONL）和固定原型SCL修复各自的NC失败点，实现监督学习向原型学习的范式转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两个主流监督分类范式（交叉熵CE和监督对比学习SCL）均未能达到理论最优解Neural Collapse（NC）：CE留下径向自由度未约束并收敛到退化几何结构；SCL在预训练中驱动特征接近NC但在后续线性探测阶段丢弃了这种结构。

Method: （1）针对CE：提出NTCE和NONL两种归一化损失函数，引入对比优化的缺失成分（大规模有效负样本集、解耦的对齐性和一致性项）；（2）针对SCL：证明SCL目标在训练全程已优化了以类均值嵌入为权重的原则性分类器，使线性探测变得冗余且有害，从而直接使用固定原型进行分类。

Result: NTCE和NONL超越CE准确率，紧密逼近NC（≥95%），在不到7.5%的迭代次数内匹配CE收敛后的NC（5项指标中的4项）；带固定原型的SCL无需数小时分类器训练即可匹配线性探测效果；学习到的几何结构在迁移学习中获得+5.5%平均相对提升，类别严重不平衡时达+8.7%，ImageNet-C上mCE更低。

Conclusion: 两个范式（CE和SCL）实际上都是单位超球面上原型对比的不同表现形式，通过针对性修复各自的失败点可以达成NC；该工作将监督学习重新定义为超球面上的原型学习，在两条路径上都通过设计达到了NC。

Abstract: Supervised classification has a theoretical optimum, Neural Collapse (NC), yet neither of its two dominant paradigms reaches it in practice. Cross entropy (CE) leaves radial degrees of freedom unconstrained and converges to a degenerate geometry, while supervised contrastive learning (SCL) drives features toward NC during pretraining but discards this structure in a post hoc linear probing phase. We show that both paradigms are different appearances of the same method, prototype contrast on the unit hypersphere, and that closing the gap requires fixing each at its specific point of failure. From the CE side, we propose NTCE and NONL, two normalized losses that import contrastive optimization's missing ingredients into classifier learning: a large effective negative set and decoupled alignment and uniformity terms. From the SCL side, we prove that SCL's objective already optimizes throughout training for a principled classifier whose weights are the class mean embeddings, making linear probing both redundant and harmful. Empirically, on four benchmarks including ImageNet-1K, NTCE and NONL surpass CE accuracy, closely approximate NC ($\geq 95\%$), and match CE's converged NC on 4/5 metrics in under $7.5\%$ of its iterations, while SCL with fixed prototypes matches linear probing without the hours-long classifier training phase. The learned geometry yields $+5.5\%$ mean relative improvement in transfer learning, up to $+8.7\%$ under severe class imbalance, and lower mCE on ImageNet-C, recasting supervised learning as prototype learning on the hypersphere, with NC reached by design on both paths.

</details>


### [102] [AirfoilGen: A valid-by-construction and performance-aware latent diffusion model for airfoil generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20303)
*Zhijie Yang,Min Tang,Qiang Zou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AirfoilGen，一种基于潜在扩散模型的翼型生成方法。通过引入圆扫描表示法确保几何有效性，使用Transformer编码器结合条件扩散模型实现气动性能（升力/阻力系数）的显式控制。实验表明该方法可生成几何有效的翼型，并达到98.41%的性能条件化准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度生成方法在翼型设计中存在两大关键局限：几何有效性不足（生成无效几何形状）和物理可控性欠缺（无法有效条件化气动性能），这直接制约了其在实际工程中的应用价值。

Method: 提出AirfoilGen，一种基于潜在扩散模型的翼型生成方法。核心创新包括：(1) 圆扫描表示法（Circular Sweeping Representation）——一种新型翼型表示方案，通过约束生成过程确保输出形状符合翼型的基本几何特征；(2) 在学习到的潜在空间中进行操作，使用Transformer将翼型形状编码为向量嵌入，随后条件扩散模型在给定目标气动性能（如升力系数、阻力系数）条件下对高斯噪声去噪生成潜在嵌入；(3) 构建了包含超过20万个翼型的新数据集，规模远超UIUC数据集（1,650个翼型）。

Result: AirfoilGen实现了98.41%的平均性能条件化准确率，能够生成具有优异几何有效性和气动性能可控性的翼型形状。

Conclusion: AirfoilGen实现了显著更高的几何有效性和气动性能可控性，平均性能条件化准确率达到98.41%，显著优于先前方法。

Abstract: Airfoil shape design is a fundamental task in aerospace engineering, with a direct impact on flight stability and fuel consumption. Deep learning has recently emerged as a promising tool for this task, but existing deep generative approaches remain limited in both geometric validity and physical controllability. They offer little control over the generated shapes, yielding invalid geometries, and they typically do not condition effectively on aerodynamic performance. To address these issues, this paper proposes AirfoilGen, a valid-by-construction and performance-aware latent diffusion model for airfoil. It first introduces a novel airfoil representation scheme, the circle sweeping representation, to constrain the generative process so that output shapes respect essential airfoil characteristics. It then enables explicit control over aerodynamic performance (e.g., lift and drag coefficients) by operating in a learned latent space: a transformer model encodes airfoil shapes into vector embeddings, and a conditional diffusion model denoises Gaussian noise into these latent embeddings while incorporating target aerodynamic performance. In addition, this paper presents a new dataset of over 200,000 airfoils, which is substantially larger than the widely used UIUC airfoil dataset (1,650 airfoils) and more suitable for training modern deep generative models. Experiments demonstrate that AirfoilGen enables airfoil generation with far greater geometric validity and aerodynamic performance controllability than previously achievable, with an average performance-conditioning accuracy of 98.41%.

</details>


### [103] [Less Data, Faster Training: repeating smaller datasets speeds up learning via sampling biases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20314)
*Jingwen Liu,Ezra Edelman,Surbhi Goel,Bingbin Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 这篇论文研究了"小样本重复训练 vs 大样本训练"的现象，发现用更少的数据进行更多轮次的重复训练反而能节省计算资源。这种优势源于采样偏差引起的逐层增长效应，不仅适用于数据稀缺的场景，还可以作为有利的归纳偏置来优化训练过程，尤其是在推理任务中表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Prior theory fails to explain why training on fewer samples with more repetitions can be more compute-efficient than using larger datasets. This "small-vs-large gap" is consistently observed across algorithmic tasks, architectures, and optimizers, but lacks theoretical justification.

Method: Both theoretical analysis and empirical interventions were conducted across various tasks, model architectures, and optimizers to investigate the mechanism behind the observed phenomenon.

Result: The compute efficiency gains originate from "layer-wise growth" that is enabled by sampling biases. This effect is more pronounced when working with smaller datasets, leading to unexpected training speedups.

Conclusion: Using smaller datasets with more repetitions is not merely a fallback for data scarcity, but a proactive optimization strategy—particularly beneficial for reasoning tasks—as it provides favorable inductive biases.

Abstract: This work investigates the ``small-vs-large gap'', where repeating on fewer samples can lead to compute saving during training compared to using a larger dataset. This is observed across algorithmic tasks, architectures and optimizers and cannot be explained using prior theory. We argue that the speedup comes from appropriate layer-wise growth enabled by sampling biases, which is more pronounced when the dataset size is smaller. We provide both theoretical analysis and empirical evidence from various interventions. Our results suggest that using a smaller dataset with more repetitions is not just a fallback strategy under data scarcity, but can be proactively leveraged as a favorable inductive biases for optimization, particularly in reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [104] [Symmetrization of Loss Functions for Robust Training of Neural Networks in the Presence of Noisy Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20347)
*Alexandre Lemire Paquin,Brahim Chaib-Draa,Philippe Giguère*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究标签噪声下的鲁棒损失函数设计。通过将多类损失对称化，推导出多类unhinged损失是唯一的凸多类对称损失，并证明其在任意对称损失线性近似中的基础性作用。进一步提出SGCE和alpha-MAE两种可控制平滑性的损失函数，在噪声标签任务中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标注训练集成本高昂且易出错，因此设计能够抵抗标签噪声的鲁棒损失函数具有重要意义。

Method: 基于多类损失函数的唯一分解（对称分量与类无关项），提出对称化方法；将交叉熵损失对称化得到多类unhinged损失的线性扩展；在假设条件下证明多类unhinged损失是唯一的凸多类对称损失；引入SGCE和alpha-MAE两种可在多类unhinged损失与MAE之间插值且可控制平滑性的损失函数。

Result: （1）多类unhinged损失是唯一的凸多类对称损失；（2）任意对称损失在得分向量分量相等处的线性近似等价于多类unhinged损失；（3）SGCE和alpha-MAE在标准噪声标签基准测试中具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 提出的SGCE和alpha-MAE损失函数能够在多类unhinged损失和MAE之间插值，并允许控制β-平滑性，在标准噪声标签基准测试中展现出具有竞争力的性能。

Abstract: Labeling a training set is often expensive and susceptible to errors, making the design of robust loss functions for label noise an important problem. The symmetry condition provides theoretical guarantees for robustness to such noise. In this work, we study a symmetrization method arising from the unique decomposition of any multi-class loss function into a symmetric component and a class-insensitive term. In particular, symmetrizing the cross-entropy loss leads to a linear multi-class extension of the unhinged loss. Unlike in the binary case, the multi-class version must have specific coefficients in order to satisfy the symmetry condition. Under suitable assumptions, we show that this multi-class unhinged loss is the unique convex multi-class symmetric loss. We also show that it has a fundamental local role: the linear approximation of any symmetric loss around score vectors with equal components is equivalent to the multi-class unhinged loss. We then introduce SGCE and alpha-MAE, two loss functions that interpolate between the multi-class unhinged loss and the Mean Absolute Error while allowing control of the beta-smoothness of the loss. Experiments on standard noisy-label benchmarks show competitive performance compared with existing robust loss functions.

</details>


### [105] [Consistently Informative Soft-Label Temperature for Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20357)
*Hoang-Chau Luong,Nghia Van Vo,Kaiqi Zhao,Lingwei Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CIST解决标准知识蒸馏中固定温度的问题，通过为教师和学生分别分配样本级自适应温度，产生一致信息性的软标签，同时放宽师生对数几率尺度对齐约束，并在理论上证明教师标签熵主要由最大对数几率与温度的比值决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Standard knowledge distillation uses a fixed global temperature for all samples, which is inherently sample-agnostic. Since samples differ in logit scale and learning difficulty, a single temperature causes: (1) some predictions to remain overly sharp (limited inter-class information) and (2) others to become over-smoothed (lost class-discriminative information). Additionally, sharing the same temperature between teacher and student imposes rigid logit-scale alignment despite their capacity mismatch.

Method: CIST assigns separate sample-wise adaptive temperatures to teacher and student networks. The method: (1) produces consistently informative teacher soft labels via adaptive smoothing based on the ratio between max teacher logit and temperature, (2) relaxes rigid teacher-student logit-scale alignment through separate temperature control, and (3) reweights the distillation objective based on teacher confidence and student learning difficulty.
