Result: Experiments on vision and language distillation tasks demonstrate consistent improvements over standard KD and strong baselines with negligible computational overhead.

Conclusion: CIST achieves consistent improvements over standard KD and strong baselines across vision and language tasks with negligible computational overhead.

Abstract: Knowledge distillation (KD) transfers knowledge from a high-capacity teacher to a compact student by matching their predictive distributions, with temperature scaling serving as a central mechanism for smoothing teacher predictions and exposing informative "dark knowledge" beyond the hard label. However, the standard fixed-temperature design is inherently sample-agnostic. Since samples differ in logit scale and learning difficulty, a single global temperature produces teacher soft labels with highly inconsistent entropy: some predictions remain overly sharp and provide limited inter-class information, whereas others become over-smoothed and lose class-discriminative information. Moreover, sharing the same temperature between teacher and student further imposes rigid logit-scale alignment despite their capacity mismatch. To address these limitations, we propose CIST (Consistently Informative Soft-label Temperature), which assigns separate sample-wise adaptive temperatures to the teacher and student. This design produces consistently informative teacher soft labels while relaxing rigid teacher--student logit-scale matching. It also reweights the distillation objective according to teacher confidence and student learning difficulty. Theoretically, we show that teacher-label entropy is largely governed by the ratio between the maximum teacher logit and the temperature, providing a principled basis for adaptive smoothing. Empirically, CIST mitigates the inconsistency induced by fixed temperature, and experiments on both vision and language distillation tasks show consistent improvements over standard KD and strong baselines with negligible computational overhead.

</details>


### [106] [Nonlocal operator learning for fMRI encoding and decoding tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20389)
*Andreas Kramer,Saugat Acharya,Alice Giola,Emanuele Zappala*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于神经积分算子的潜在变量框架用于fMRI数据的编码和解码任务，系统研究了时空上下文窗口大小对模型性能的影响，发现更大的时间窗口和更广泛的脑区覆盖能够提升预测性能并产生更结构化的潜在表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: fMRI数据具有高维时空结构，传统方法难以有效捕获其非局部时空依赖关系；此外，解码（从脑活动预测刺激）和编码（从刺激预测脑活动）任务都面临挑战，需要新的模型架构来捕获分布式非局部结构。

Method: 提出潜在神经积分算子框架，通过在辅助空间中进行不动点迭代来学习表示，然后通过解码器进行分类和刺激预测；在两个开源fMRI数据集上系统比较不同时空窗口（短/长时窗、视觉皮层/全脑）和解码/编码任务的性能。

Result: 在解码任务中，学习的潜在空间比原始数据提供更清晰的类别分离；在编码任务中，虽然绝对性能适中（任务难度所致），但较长时空窗口仍带来一致的性能提升；整体而言，更大的时空窗口通常改善结果并产生更结构化的学习表示。

Conclusion: 神经积分算子为建模fMRI动力学提供了有前景的框架，更广泛的时空上下文对预测和表示学习均有益处；捕获脑动力学中的分布式非局部结构需要专门设计的模型架构。

Abstract: Functional MRI data exhibit high-dimensional spatiotemporal structure, making both prediction and decoding challenging. In this work, we investigate neural integral-operator-based models for encoding and decoding tasks in fMRI, with particular emphasis on the role of nonlocal spatiotemporal context. We implement a latent neural integral operator framework that performs fixed point iterations in an auxiliary space from which classification and stimuli prediction is performed via a decoder. We evaluate our model on two open-source fMRI datasets.
  Our experiments examine both decoding of stimuli from fMRI recordings and encoding of fMRI dynamics from stimulus representations. A main focus is the effect of spatiotemporal context: we systematically compare short and long temporal windows, as well as the use of visual cortex vs whole brain recordings, and analyze their influence on performance and latent-space geometry. Across tasks and datasets, larger temporal windows generally improve results and produce more structured learned representations. In decoding experiments, the learned latent space often provides clearer class separation than the raw data. In encoding experiments, although absolute performance remains moderate due to the difficulty of the task, longer temporal windows still yield consistent gains.
  These findings suggest that neural integral operators provide a promising framework for modeling fMRI dynamics and that broader spatiotemporal context can be beneficial for both prediction and representation learning. More broadly, the results indicate that exploiting distributed nonlocal structure in brain dynamics requires model architectures specifically designed to capture such dependencies.

</details>


### [107] [Score-Based Causal Discovery of Latent Variable Causal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20396)
*Ignavier Ng,Xinshuai Dong,Haoyue Dai,Biwei Huang,Peter Spirtes,Kun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于评分的方法来处理含有潜在变量的因果结构发现难题。通过设计适当的评分函数，实现了评分等价性和一致性，并提供了精确和连续两种评分方法。该工作为多种现有约束方法提供了统一视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Constraint-based methods face issues like testing-order dependency, error propagation, and significance level selection when identifying causal structures with latent variables.

Method: Formulated proper scoring functions achieving score equivalence and consistency for latent variable causal models; characterized degrees of freedom for marginal distributions under various structural assumptions.

Result: Score-based methods successfully identify causally-related latent variables; experimental results validate the proposed approaches.

Conclusion: Developed exact and continuous score-based methods with identifiability guarantees, offering a unified view of existing constraint-based methods.

Abstract: Identifying latent variables and the causal structure involving them is essential across various scientific fields. While many existing works fall under the category of constraint-based methods (with e.g. conditional independence or rank deficiency tests), they may face empirical challenges such as testing-order dependency, error propagation, and choosing an appropriate significance level. These issues can potentially be mitigated by properly designed score-based methods, such as Greedy Equivalence Search (GES) (Chickering, 2002) in the specific setting without latent variables. Yet, formulating score-based methods with latent variables is highly challenging. In this work, we develop score-based methods that are capable of identifying causal structures containing causally-related latent variables with identifiability guarantees. Specifically, we show that a properly formulated scoring function can achieve score equivalence and consistency for structure learning of latent variable causal models. We further provide a characterization of the degrees of freedom for the marginal over the observed variables under multiple structural assumptions considered in the literature, and accordingly develop both exact and continuous score-based methods. This offers a unified view of several existing constraint-based methods with different structural assumptions. Experimental results validate the effectiveness of the proposed methods.

</details>


### [108] [Decomposing MXFP4 quantization error for LLM reinforcement learning: reducible bias, recoverable deadzone, and an irreducible floor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20402)
*Xiaocan Li,Shiliang Wu,Zheng Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MXFP4量化可加速LLM强化学习后训练，但量化误差导致精度下降。本文将量化误差分解为三个成分：scale bias、deadzone truncation和grid noise，分别影响梯度精度、rollout质量和策略熵。针对性修正包括Macro-block scaling、Outlier Fallback和AQN，在Qwen模型上恢复至BF16精度的0.7%和3.0%以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MXFP4量化可显著加速LLM强化学习后训练，但量化误差造成严重精度损失。现有方法将量化误差视为整体噪声，未能区分其对训练的不同影响机制。

Method: 1) 理论证明量化误差的三向分解；2) 将MXFP4量化误差分解为三个加性成分：scale bias（幂二取整）、deadzone truncation（零化小值）、grid noise（最近4-bit网格取整）；3) 针对每个成分设计针对性修正方案。

Result: Scale bias通过反向传播乘法累积影响梯度精度；Deadzone truncation降低rollout质量；Grid noise增加策略熵。三种修正方案Macro-block scaling、Outlier Fallback和AQN可有效缓解各失败模式。

Conclusion: 在Qwen2.5-3B密集模型和Qwen3-30B-A3B-Base MoE模型上，针对性修正方案分别恢复至BF16精度的0.7%和3.0%以内，验证了误差分解理论的有效性。

Abstract: MXFP4 arithmetic can dramatically accelerate reinforcement learning (RL) post-training of large language models (LLMs), yet the quantization error introduces severe accuracy degradation. Existing work treats the quantization error as a monolithic noise term, missing the distinct mechanisms upon interpreting how quantization error damages training. We prove an exact three-way decomposition of quantization error and show how each component dominates a distinct RL training pathway. Our theoretical and empirical analysis decomposes the MXFP4 quantization error into three additive components: "scale bias" from power-of-two rounding, "deadzone truncation" from zeroing small values, and "grid noise" from rounding to the nearest 4-bit grid. Each component dominates a distinct RL failure mode: scale bias accumulates multiplicatively through the backward pass, affecting gradient accuracy; deadzone truncation degrades rollout quality; and grid noise raises the policy's entropy. We combine corrections that are RL failure mode-targeted but not component-exclusive: Macro-block scaling to reduce scale bias, Outlier Fallback recovers deadzone entries, but also partially reduces scale bias induced error, and Adaptive Quantization Noise (AQN) for controlling the policy entropy. On Qwen2.5-3B dense and Qwen3-30B-A3B-Base mixture-of-experts model, the targeted corrections recover BF16 accuracy to within 0.7% and 3.0% respectively.

</details>


### [109] [Spectral Souping: A Unified Framework for Online Preference Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20408)
*Yinlam Chow,Guy Tennenholtz,Ted Yun,James Harrison,Arthur Gretton,Andre Barreto,Bo Dai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Spectral Souping框架，利用LLM中发现的通用频谱表示进行模型合并，实现无需再训练的在线个性化偏好对齐，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLHF能对齐LLM与聚合人类偏好，但无法满足不同用户的多样化且冲突的个体需求——这是当前对齐方法的根本局限。

Method: 提出两阶段方法：离线阶段学习专注于不同细粒度偏好维度的专业化策略基；在线阶段通过频谱表示在推理时高效合并这些策略（输出合并或参数合并）。核心发现是LLM中存在高度适于模型合并的通用频谱表示。

Result: 在在线偏好对齐基准测试中显著优于现有最优方法，验证了该框架在性能和计算效率上的优势。

Conclusion: Spectral Souping通过在LLM内部发现一个通用的频谱表示，能够高效地合并专业化策略。该方法在推理时动态混合不同策略的参数或输出，无需针对个体用户偏好进行昂贵的在线再训练，实现了可扩展且计算高效的个性化LLM适应方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) effectively aligns Large Language Models (LLMs) with aggregate human preferences but often fails to address the diverse and conflicting needs of individual users. To overcome this issue, we introduce Spectral Souping, a unified framework for efficient, online preference alignment. Our contribution is the discovery of a universal spectral representation within LLMs, which is proven to be highly amenable to model merging. This theoretical insight enables a two-phase methodology: we first learn a basis of specialized policies offline, each focused on a distinct, fine-grained preference dimension. An online adaptation algorithm then efficiently ``soups'' these policies at inference time, either by merging their outputs or parameters, enabling rapid model adaptation without the need for costly online retraining w.r.t. tailored preference rewards. Experiments on online preference alignment benchmarks demonstrate that our method achieves significant performance improvements over existing state-of-the-art approaches, presenting a scalable and computationally efficient solution for dynamically adapting LLMs to individual user preferences.

</details>


### [110] [Supervised Latent Restructuring for Small-Data Quantum Learning in Plant Phenomics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20413)
*Alakananda Mitra,David H. Fleisher,Vangimalla Reddy,Chittaranjan Ray*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究针对高维小样本生物数据的分类问题，提出结合PCA压缩与LDA监督重构的混合工作流。实验表明监督潜在重构显著提升了几何可分性，但对不同分类器存在差异化影响，且QKA在约束预算下仍具挑战，揭示了压缩表征中恢复非线性判别结构的困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维生物数据通常存在特征维度与样本量之间的严重不匹配问题，在极小数据场景下使可靠分类变得困难。核方法在潜在压缩未能保留类别分离结构时可能失去判别能力。

Method: 提出混合工作流：(1)将1280维深度图像嵌入压缩至64维PCA空间；(2)使用线性判别分析(LDA)将其重构为11维监督潜在空间；(3)在NVIDIA L40S硬件上进行GPU加速的量子核对齐(QKA)。

Result: 监督潜在重构显著改善了压缩表示的几何可分性，轮廓系数从原始嵌入空间的0.003和PCA-64的-0.006提升至监督LDA-11空间的0.197。下游经典评估显示明显的压缩权衡：Linear SVM和XGBoost在重构潜在空间中性能提升，而RBF-SVM和随机森林在相同11维瓶颈下性能下降。

Conclusion: 该研究将表示几何定位为小数据量子学习中的核心设计变量，并揭示了从激进压缩的生物表征中恢复非线性判别结构的实践困难。QKA在约束优化预算下仍具挑战性，表明仅靠潜在几何结构不足以实现强性能的可训练量子模型。

Abstract: High-dimensional biological data often exhibit a severe mismatch between feature dimensionality and sample size, making reliable classification difficult in extremely small-data regimes. In these settings, kernel methods can lose discriminative power when latent compression fails to preserve class-separating structure. We study this problem in fine-grained plant phenomics and propose a hybrid workflow that compresses 1280-dimensional deep image embeddings into a 64-dimensional PCA space and then restructures them into an 11-dimensional supervised latent space using Linear Discriminant Analysis (LDA), followed by GPU-accelerated Quantum Kernel Alignment (QKA) on NVIDIA L40S hardware. Empirically, supervised latent restructuring substantially improves the geometric separability of the compressed representation, increasing the Silhouette coefficient from 0.003 in the raw embedding space and -0.006 in PCA-64 to 0.197 in the supervised LDA-11 space. However, downstream classical evaluation reveals a clear compression trade-off: Linear SVM and XGBoost improve in the restructured latent space, whereas RBF-SVM and Random Forest degrade under the same 11-dimensional bottleneck. Under a constrained optimization budget, QKA in this regime remains challenging, indicating that latent geometry alone is not sufficient for strong trainable quantum performance. These findings position representation geometry as a central design variable in small-data quantum learning and expose the practical difficulty of recovering nonlinear discriminative structure from aggressively compressed biological representations.

</details>


### [111] [Miller-Index-Based Latent Crystallographic Fracture Plane Reasoning with Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20416)
*Qinwu Xu,Yifan Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究多模态大语言模型(MLLMs)能否利用Miller指数作为潜在表征来推理断裂几何，发现模型在理想化设置中表现良好，并能拒绝物理条件不满足的情况，表明MLLMs可作为物理感知的推理系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索MLLMs是否能够利用晶体学Miller指数作为结构化潜在表征，对断裂几何进行物理感知推理，并评估模型在何时应接受或拒绝这种表征的适用性。

Method: 将Miller指数z=(h,k,l)形式化为控制理想平面断裂的潜在变量，通过三种实验设置评估两个核心能力：(i)潜在推理——从视觉观察到符合物理条件的晶面假设；(ii)潜在适用性评估——判断表征对给定断裂图像是否有意义。实验涵盖合成数据、受控2D-3D几何对以及跨陶瓷、玻璃、金属、混凝土等多种材料类别的真实断裂图像。

Result: MLLMs在理想化设置中能可靠执行潜在推理，关键的是，当底层物理不支持时，模型能够拒绝潜在表征。实验结果跨越合成数据、受控2D-3D几何对以及多种材料类别的真实断裂图像。

Conclusion: MLLMs可作为物理感知推理系统，以结构化潜在先验为条件，但需明确建模其有效性领域。该研究为AI辅助材料科学推理提供了新的方法论框架。

Abstract: We study whether multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can leverage crystallographic plane indices (Miller indices) as a structured latent representation for reasoning about fracture geometry. We formulate Miller indices $z = (h,k,l)$ as a latent variable governing idealized planar fracture and evaluate two complementary capabilities: (i) latent inference, where the model maps visual observations to plane hypotheses under physically valid conditions, and (ii) latent applicability assessment, where the model determines whether such a representation is meaningful for a given fracture image.
  Through extensive experiments spanning synthetic data, controlled 2D--3D geometric pairs, and real-world fracture images across multiple material classes -- including ceramics, glass, metals, and concrete -- we show that MLLMs can reliably perform latent inference in idealized settings and, critically, can reject the latent representation when the underlying physics does not support it. These results suggest that MLLMs can act as physics-aware reasoning systems conditioned on structured latent priors, provided that the domain of validity is explicitly modeled.

</details>


### [112] [Can Conversational XAI Improve User Performance? An Experimental Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20439)
*Sven Kruschel,Julian Rosenberger,Lasse Bohlen,Mathias Kraus,Patrick Zschech*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出一种实验设计，评估对话式可解释人工智能(XAI)助手对用户预测准确率、模型理解和错误识别的辅助效果。对比对话式与问答式两种辅助方式，初步结果显示(N=42)两种条件下用户均显著优于模型本身，但辅助类型间无显著差异，参与度较为一般。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前XAI技术往往未能达到提升用户表现的预期，对话式XAI助手虽有望克服这些局限，但其在客观性能指标上的实证证据仍然不足。

Method: 设计实验评估解释辅助效果，采用可解释设计预测模型创造条件使用户能通过识别和补偿系统性误差来超越模型性能。对比对话式辅助与问答式辅助两种方式。

Result: 两种辅助处理的参与者(N=42)均显著优于模型，但辅助类型间无性能差异，整体参与度较为一般。

Conclusion: 研究结果为完整研究提供改进方向：需加强参与度干预措施，并深入探究驱动预测改善的作用机制。

Abstract: Explainable AI (XAI) techniques aim to provide insights into predictive models and enhance user performance, yet they often fall short of these expectations. Conversational XAI assistants promise to overcome such limitations, but empirical evidence on their impact on objective performance measures remains limited. We propose an experimental design for evaluating explanation assistance through prediction accuracy, model understanding, and error identification. Using an explainable-by-design prediction model, we create conditions where users can outperform the model by identifying and compensating for systematic errors. We compare conversational assistance against Q&A-based assistance to assess which better supports users in working with model explanations. Preliminary results from testing our experimental design show that participants (N=42) in both treatments significantly outperformed the model but reveal no performance differences between assistance types and modest engagement overall. These findings inform refinements for our planned full study, including enhanced engagement interventions and investigation of the mechanisms driving improved predictions.

</details>


### [113] [LLM Pretraining Shapes a Generalizable Manifold: Insights into Cross-Modal Transfer to Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20449)
*Alexis Roger,Prateek Humane,Zhenghan Tai,Gwen Legate,Andrei Mircea,Vasilii Feofanov,Irina Rish*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了大型语言模型（LLM）预训练如何以及为何能够迁移到时序预测任务。核心发现是：语言预训练构建了一个可复用的流形结构，而微调过程本质上是将该数值动态流形低秩对齐到任务相关方向。实验证据包括：冻结模型的线性探测可解码真实轨迹、检索式预测竞争力强、预训练产生相干梯度与各向异性损失景观、微调呈现低秩更新且共享周期/趋势/重复等特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索语言预训练的Transformer模型能否有效迁移到时序预测任务，以及这种迁移的内在机理是什么。

Method: 通过线性探测、检索实验、梯度分析、损失景观可视化、低秩更新分析、子空间对齐和特征归因等多种技术手段，系统性地验证了LLM到时序数据的跨模态迁移机制。

Result: 发现语言预训练构建了一个可复用的流形结构：1) 冻结LLM的隐状态通过线性探测即可解码出逼真的时序轨迹；2) 检索该投影空间可产生有竞争力的预测；3) 预训练改善了优化过程，产生相干梯度和高度各向异性的损失景观；4) 微调表现为低秩对齐，重用已有方向而非从头学习时序原语。

Conclusion: 语言预训练构建了流形结构，而微调则将该流形投影到任务相关的方向上，形成一种几何化的跨模态迁移机制。

Abstract: Can language-pretrained transformers become effective time-series forecasters, and why? In this paper, we show that cross-modal transfer arises because language pretraining preconditions time series training with a reusable manifold. A linear probe on frozen LLM states decodes realistic time-series trajectories without paired supervision, and retrieval in this projected space yields competitive forecasts, showing that structure and dynamics exist before finetuning. Pretrained initialization also improves optimization, producing coherent gradients and a highly anisotropic loss landscape unlike random initialization. Finetuning then acts as low-dimensional alignment, reusing existing directions rather than learning temporal primitives from scratch, as evidenced by low-rank updates, subspace alignment, and shared features for periodicity, trend, and repetition. Together, these results support a geometric account of LLM-to-time-series transfer: language pretraining builds the manifold, and finetuning projects numerical dynamics onto task-relevant directions.

</details>


### [114] [CASCADE Conformal Prediction: Uncertainty-Adaptive Prediction Intervals for Two-Stage Clinical Decision Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20468)
*Ricardo Diaz-Rincon,Muxuan Liang,Adolfo Ramirez-Zamora,Benjamin Shickel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CASCADE是一种新型卷积预测框架，通过从药物变更需求筛选分类器传播认知不确定性来动态调整药物剂量预测区间。相比传统卷积方法，该框架利用Venn-Abers多概率不确定性映射实现连续风险适应，在帕金森病药物管理中为高置信患者提供更窄预测区间，同时为不确定案例自动扩展覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 帕金森病药物管理面临异质性疾病进展、可变患者反应和药物副作用等挑战；现有AI预测模型的标准不确定性量化无法有效传达预测可靠性，对高低置信度临床决策一视同仁。

Method: 该方法通过Venn-Abers多概率不确定性直接映射到非一致性分数，实现连续风险适应；采用卷积预测框架，将分类任务的认知不确定性动态缩放回归任务的预测区间。

Result: 该"级联效应"为置信患者生成高效区间（比标准卷积基线缩小38.9%），同时自动扩展不确定情况的区间以确保稳健覆盖率。

Conclusion: CASCADE框架通过从筛选分类器传播认知不确定性到下游预测任务，在高置信度患者中实现预测区间缩小38.9%，同时对不确定情况自动扩展区间以确保稳健覆盖率，有效弥合了离散临床决策与连续剂量预测之间的差距。

Abstract: Effective medication management in Parkinson's Disease (PD) is challenging due to heterogeneous disease progression, variable patient response, and medication side effects. While AI models can forecast levodopa equivalent daily dose (LEDD) as a measure of medication needs, standard uncertainty quantification often fails to communicate the reliability of these predictions, treating high and low confidence clinical decisions identically. We introduce CASCADE (Calibrated Adaptive Scaling via Conformal And Distributional Estimation), a novel conformal prediction framework that propagates epistemic uncertainty from a screening classifier to adapt downstream predictions. Unlike standard conformal methods that rely on auxiliary residual regression, we leverage epistemic uncertainty from a primary classification task (identifying whether a medication change is needed) to dynamically scale the prediction intervals of a secondary regression task (predicting how much change). By mapping Venn-Abers multi-probabilistic uncertainty directly to non-conformity scores, our framework achieves continuous risk adaptation. We demonstrate that this ``cascade effect'' produces highly efficient intervals for confident patients (38.9% narrower than standard conformal baselines) while automatically expanding intervals to ensure robust coverage for uncertain cases, bridging the gap between discrete clinical decision-making and continuous dose forecasting in PD.

</details>


### [115] [Training Language Agents to Learn from Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20477)
*Yuval Shalev,Zifeng Ding,Mateja Jamnik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出In-context Training (ICT)任务框架，解决现有反射式方法无法跨任务泛化自我改进的问题。通过RL训练流程，模型可从经验中学习生成系统提示以提升未来未见任务的性能，在ALFWorld和MiniHack上验证了方法有效性，并开源了MetaGym库支持后续研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于反射的语言智能体只能在同一任务实例内进行自我纠正，无法将经验提炼为可重用的"教训"以提升未来未见任务的性能。这一局限性阻碍了智能体的持续自我改进能力的发展。

Method: 1) 提出ICT任务框架：反射模型观察行动模型收集的轨迹，生成系统提示以改进未来任务表现；2) 采用RL训练流程直接从未标注经验中学习反射，无需人工标注示例；3) 在ALFWorld和MiniHack环境中验证

Result: 训练后的反射模型在大多数保留任务族上优于未训练基线，证明"从经验中学习"的能力是可学习的。部分情况下观察到跨基准泛化能力，即反射器能在训练基准之外的不同环境中有效工作。

Conclusion: 跨任务自我改进能力可通过学习获得。ICT框架和MetaGym库的提出为自我改进语言智能体的未来研究提供了评估标准和基础设施支持。

Abstract: Language agents can adapt from experience in interactive environments, but current reflection-based methods can only self-correct within a single task instance. Whether such experience can be distilled into reusable lessons that improve performance on future unseen tasks remains unclear. We address this problem by introducing the In-context Training (ICT) task, a framework for evaluating cross-task self-improvement in language agents. In ICT, a reflector model observes trajectories collected by an actor model and generates system prompts intended to improve the actor's performance on future unseen tasks. We then propose an RL-based training pipeline for learning such reflections directly from experience, without human-provided examples. Across ALFWorld and MiniHack, our trained reflectors outperform an untrained baseline on most held-out task families, showing that the ability to learn from experience can itself be learned. In some cases, we observe generalisation beyond the benchmark on which the reflector was trained, to substantially different environments. Finally, we introduce MetaGym, a generic Python library for constructing meta-environments, enabling future research on self-improving language agents.

</details>


### [116] [Quadratic Characterizations for Reachability Analysis of Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20482)
*Elias Khalife,Mazen Farhood,Pierre-Loic Garoche*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: This paper develops a framework for constructing verified quadratic characterizations of scalar relations by combining local convex optimization with global SOS verification, providing domain-dependent alternatives to generic sector/slope descriptions for neural network reachability analysis with reduced conservatism.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Generic analytical quadratic characterizations of nonlinearities and uncertainties can be conservative on bounded domains.

Method: Local generation via convex QP with sample points; global verification using SOS certificates over semialgebraic or relaxed polynomial descriptions; compatible with existing QC/IQC frameworks for reachability analysis.

Result: Domain-dependent quadratic characterizations for smooth activations; reduced conservatism for ReLU networks via neuron dependencies and tighter local bounds; applicability beyond neural networks demonstrated.

Conclusion: Improved reachability results for smooth activations, reduced conservatism for ReLU networks, and applicability beyond neural networks through saturation example.

Abstract: Quadratic constraints (QCs) are widely used to characterize nonlinearities and uncertainties, but generic analytical characterizations can be conservative on bounded domains. This paper develops a framework for constructing verified quadratic characterizations of scalar relations in the two-dimensional real plane. Candidate quadratic inequalities are locally generated by solving convex quadratic programs using samples from the relation and exterior sample points. They are then verified globally using sum-of-squares certificates over an exact semialgebraic description or, in the case of nonpolynomial relations, over relaxed polynomial descriptions. The resulting verified constraints define a sound overapproximation of the scalar relations over the considered domains. These constraints are directly compatible with existing analysis frameworks based on QCs and pointwise integral quadratic constraints (IQCs) for static nonlinearities and uncertainties, and they can also be embedded in QC-based semidefinite programs for reachability and safety analysis of feedforward neural networks. For smooth activations such as $\tanh$, the method yields domain-dependent quadratic characterizations that constitute an alternative to generic sector- or slope-based descriptions. For ReLU networks, we give methods to reduce conservatism in QC-based reachability analysis of feedforward networks by exploiting dependencies between neurons and tighter local bounds. Numerical examples demonstrate improved reachability results for smooth activations, reduced conservatism for ReLU networks, and applicability beyond neural networks through an example involving saturation.

</details>


### [117] [ZEBRA: Zero-shot Budgeted Resource Allocation for LLM Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20485)
*May Hamri,Inbal Talgam-Cohen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ZEBRA是一种零样本框架，通过将多阶段预算分配问题简化为连续非线性背包问题，在LLM控制器估计每阶段效用曲线后，使用拉格朗日乘子的水填充搜索返回各阶段分配方案，在APPS和HotpotQA基准上显著优于LLM-direct方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着自主智能体在固定预算下执行端到端任务，如何有效分配预算成为关键问题。现有方法仅在单个智能体内逐步控制推理步骤，或通过强化学习学习资源分配策略，但均未解决如何在推理时将预算分配到多智能体流水线的各个阶段。

Method: 提出ZEBRA框架，将多阶段预算分配问题转化为连续非线性背包问题：LLM控制器估计每阶段效用曲线，通过对拉格朗日乘子进行水填充搜索返回各阶段预算分配。加法和乘法聚合方案统一在同一求解器下实现。

Result: 在150个任务的APPS编码基准上，ZEBRA在所有聚合指标上均优于LLM-direct。在α=0.5的预算下，ZEBRA恢复94.4%的无约束质量，而LLM-direct仅为88.1%。在3阶段HotpotQA管道上，ZEBRA超越LLM-direct达14.3个百分点，且分配方案对曲线估计噪声具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: ZEBRA能够根据流水线结构自适应调整预算分配（APPS偏向细化阶段，HotpotQA接近均衡分配），表明推理时的轻量级算法指导可改善自主多智能体系统的经济行为。

Abstract: As autonomous agents increasingly execute end-to-end tasks under fixed monetary budgets, the pressing open question shifts from whether the budget is respected, to how to spend it effectively. Existing budget-aware methods typically control reasoning step-by-step within a single agent, or learn resource allocation policies via RL. None address how to split a budget across the composing phases of a multi-agent pipeline at inference time. We propose ZEBRA, a zero-shot framework that reduces multi-phase budget allocation to a continuous nonlinear knapsack problem: an LLM controller estimates per-phase utility curves, and a water-filling search on the Lagrange multiplier returns the per-phase split. Additive and multiplicative aggregations are unified under the same solver. On a $150$-task APPS coding benchmark, both ZEBRA variants outperform LLM-direct (budget allocation directly by an LLM) on every aggregate metric. At a budget of $α= 0.5$ of the unconstrained spend, ZEBRA recovers $94.4\%$ of unconstrained quality, versus $88.1\%$ for LLM-direct. The advantage is statistically significant and transfers beyond coding: on a $3$-phase HotpotQA pipeline, ZEBRA beats LLM-direct by $14.3$pp, with allocations empirically robust to curve-estimation noise. On HotpotQA, ZEBRA arrives at a different budget split (near-balanced) compared to the APPS one (skewed towards a refinement phase), showing adaptation to the pipeline structure. More broadly, we show that lightweight algorithmic guidance at inference time can improve the economic behavior of autonomous multi-agent systems.

</details>


### [118] [Tippett-minimum Fusion of Representation-space Diffusion Models for Multi-Encoder Out-of-Distribution Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20502)
*Neelkamal Bhuyan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出EncMin2L框架，通过多编码器融合与双层min门结构实现全谱系分布偏移的OOD检测，无需OOD标签即可有效校准各编码器专长，在保持高检测性能的同时大幅降低参数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OOD检测方法难以同时应对全谱系分布偏移——包括全局域变化、语义偏移、纹理差异和协变量腐蚀。单一编码器对不同偏移类型的敏感性不同，需要有效融合多编码器表示。

Method: 提出EncMin2L框架，一种编码器无关的双层min门结构，融合多个编码器的表示空间扩散模型(RDM)似然检测器。通过η²(类别条件F检验)和Δμ(合成腐蚀下的对数似然偏移)两种ID数据诊断指标量化编码器专长，并采用Tippett最小p值组合将各编码器分数聚合为校准稳定的OOD信号。

Result: 实验结果表明，EncMin2L在全部四类偏移上均达到≥0.94 AUROC，显著优于现有state-of-the-art的表示空间扩散OOD检测器，且参数量减少2.3倍。

Conclusion: EncMin2L在参数成本降低2.3倍的条件下，于所有四类分布偏移上同时达到≥0.94 AUROC，超越了现有最优的表示空间扩散OOD检测方法。

Abstract: We address out-of-distribution (OOD) detection across the full spectrum of distribution shifts -- global domain changes, semantic divergence, texture differences, and covariate corruptions -- through a multi-encoder fusion of per-encoder representation-space diffusion models (RDMs). We statistically identify each encoder's sensitivity to specific shift types from ID data alone and introduce EncMin2L -- an encoder-agnostic two-level $\min(\cdot)$-gate that combines and calibrates per-encoder diffusion-based likelihood detectors without OOD labels, outperforming monolithic multi-encoder baselines at $2.3\times$ lower parameter cost. Two ID-data diagnostics: $η^2$ (class-conditional F-test) and $Δμ$ (log-likelihood shift under synthetic corruptions) -- quantify encoder specialization, while a Tippett minimum $p$-value combination aggregates per-encoder scores into a single, calibration-stable OOD signal. EncMin2L achieves $\geq 0.94$ AUROC across all four shift types simultaneously, outperforming the state-of-the-art representation-space diffusion OOD detectors across overlapping benchmarks.

</details>


### [119] [Reinforcing Human Behavior Simulation via Verbal Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20506)
*Weiwei Sun,Xuhui Zhou,Jiarui Liu,Weihua Du,Haojia Sun,Yiqing Xie,Qianou Ma,Sihao Chen,Mengting Wan,Longqi Yang,Pei Zhou,Sherry Wu,Sean Welleck,Graham Neubig,Yiming Yang,Maarten Sap*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DITTO是一种通过强化学习将口头反馈作为一等信号进行训练的模型，旨在使LLM能够模拟人类行为。模型在每次rollout后接收口头反馈并生成基于反馈的改进版本，通过GRPO联合优化两个输出，从而将口头指导提炼到基础策略中，无需在测试时提供反馈。在包含10个任务的SOUL基准测试上，DITTO平均提升36%，在6/10任务上超越GPT-5.4。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM主要在代码、数学等可验证领域学习RL反馈，但LLM越来越多地被用于模拟人类角色（用户、患者、学生等），这需要一种根本不同的信号：主观的、多维度的口头反馈。现有方法忽视了这种verbal feedback的价值。

Method: 1) DITTO采用GRPO算法，在每次rollout后接收口头反馈，生成feedback-conditioned改进rollout，两个输出联合优化；2) 提出SOUL基准，包含6大类10个任务：心理理论、角色扮演、社交技能、学习者模拟、用户模拟和 persona模拟。

Result: DITTO相比基线模型平均提升36%，在SOUL的10个基准中，有6个超越GPT-5.4。

Conclusion: 使用口头反馈的强化学习是训练LLM模拟人类行为的有效方向，DITTO展示了将主观、多维度口头指导蒸馏到策略模型中的可行性。

Abstract: Humans learn social norms and behaviors from verbal feedback (e.g., a parent saying "that was rude" or a friend explaining "here's why that hurt"). Yet, learning from feedback for LLMs has largely focused on domains like code and math, where RL rewards are directly verifiable and condensed into scalar values. As LLMs are increasingly used to simulate human behavior, e.g., standing in for users, patients, students, and other personas, there is a pressing need to make them more human-like, which requires embracing a fundamentally different kind of signal: feedback that is verbal, subjective, and multi-faceted. We present DITTO, a model trained by treating verbal feedback as a first-class signal in reinforcement learning. After each rollout, DITTO receives verbal feedback and generates a feedback-conditioned improved rollout; both outputs are jointly optimized with GRPO, distilling verbal guidance into the base policy without requiring feedback at test time. We also introduce SOUL (Simulation gym Of hUman-Like behavior), a unified benchmark and training data suite spanning 10 tasks across six categories: Theory of Mind, character role play, social skill, learner simulation, user simulation, and persona simulation. DITTO achieves an average 36% improvement over the base model and exceeds GPT-5.4 on 6 of 10 SOUL benchmarks, demonstrating that RL with verbal feedback is a promising direction for training LLMs to simulate human behavior.

</details>


### [120] [Online Conformal Prediction with Corrupted Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20515)
*Bowen Wang,Matteo Zecchin,Osvaldo Simeone*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在线共形预测(OCP)在反馈损坏环境下的鲁棒性问题。提出两种鲁棒方法：基于过滤的鲁棒OCP和基于主动补偿的鲁棒OCP，并提供显式的错误覆盖保证。实验验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代AI系统需要可靠的校准不确定性估计。传统OCP虽然能提供确定性的长期错误覆盖保证，但这些保证依赖于完美反馈的假设。在实际场景中，反馈可能因噪声、通信故障或对抗性攻击而损坏，导致校准性能严重下降。因此需要研究反馈损坏下的鲁棒OCP方法。

Method: 1) 将反馈损坏建模为任意二进制翻转序列；2) 分析标准OCP在反馈损坏下的性能退化；3) 提出两种鲁棒方案：过滤法（利用预测阈值结构过滤损坏反馈）和主动补偿法（引入主动补偿机制抵消损坏影响）；4) 针对独立随机翻转模型和带记忆的错误模型提供专门的理论保证。

Result: 建立了两种鲁棒方法的显式错误覆盖保证，理论上证明了在反馈损坏环境下仍能维持校准性能。实验表明相比基线OCP方法，所提方法具有更好的校准效果和更小的预测集。

Conclusion: 本文为OCP在不可靠反馈环境中的应用提供了理论支撑和实用方法，两种鲁棒方案能有效应对反馈损坏问题，为实际部署中的鲁棒不确定性估计奠定了基础。

Abstract: Modern artificial intelligence systems require calibrated uncertainty estimates that remain reliable in sequential and non-stationary environments. Online conformal prediction (OCP) addresses this challenge through adaptively updated prediction sets that provide deterministic long-run miscoverage guarantees. These guarantees, however, hinge on the assumption of perfect feedback about the coverage of past prediction sets. In practice, the observed miscoverage indicator may be corrupted by noise, communication failures, or adversarial manipulation, which can severely degrade OCP's calibration guarantees. In this paper, we study OCP under corrupted feedback. We first model feedback corruption as an arbitrary binary flip sequence, and analyze how feedback corruption affects and degrades the miscoverage performance of standard OCP. We then propose two robust schemes: robust OCP via filtering, which leverages the structural properties of the predicted threshold to filter corrupted feedback, and robust OCP via active compensation, which incorporates an active compensation mechanism to mitigate the effect of corrupted feedback. For both methods, we establish explicit miscoverage guarantees, which are further specialized for an independent stochastic flip model and for an arbitrary error model with memory bounds. Experiments on real-world datasets validate the proposed approach, showing markedly improved calibration and significantly smaller prediction sets compared with baseline OCP methods under corrupted feedback.

</details>


### [121] [An exponential mechanism based on quadratic approximations for fine-tuning machine learning models with privacy guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20521)
*Hoang Tran,Jorge Ramirez,Jiayi Wang,Alberto Bocchinfuso,Christopher Stanley,M. Paul Laiu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于指数机制的差分隐私微调算法，通过构造结合局部二次近似与新数据的效用函数，实现多变量正态分布的闭式精确采样，并引入随机投影策略处理高维模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微调预训练模型时存在隐私泄露风险，模型可能记忆敏感数据点，容易受到试图提取敏感信息的对手攻击。需要一种在保护隐私的同时保持模型效用的微调方法。

Method: 基于指数机制的随机化差分隐私微调算法。核心创新是构造结合预训练模型局部二次近似与新数据集信息的效用函数，通过随机投影策略实现高维模型的可扩展性。

Result: 理论与实验验证了方法的有效性：建立了隐私保证、敏感性边界和精度估计的理论框架；在MNIST基准测试和MIMIC临床数据集上展示了对现有差分隐私微调技术的竞争力。

Conclusion: 通过在多变量正态分布中进行精确采样，实现了可扩展的差分隐私微调。在MNIST和MIMIC临床数据集上的实验表明，该方法在隐私保护与效用之间取得了良好的平衡。

Abstract: Fine-tuning adapts a pretrained machine learning model to a small, sensitive dataset, but this process risks memorizing individual new data points, making the model vulnerable to adversaries who seek to extract sensitive information. In this work, we develop a randomized algorithm based on the exponential mechanism for fine-tuning while ensuring differential privacy. Our key idea is to construct a simple utility function that combines a local quadratic approximation of the pretrained model with information from the new dataset. The resulting exponential mechanism admits exact sampling from a multivariate normal distribution in closed form. We establish theoretical privacy guarantees, sensitivity bounds, and accuracy estimations for our method. We further introduce a random-projection strategy that makes the approach scalable to high-dimensional models. Numerical experiments on the MNIST benchmark and the MIMIC clinical dataset demonstrate competitive performance against existing differentially private fine-tuning techniques.

</details>


### [122] [Ada2MS: A Hybrid Optimization Algorithm Based on Exponential Mixing of Elementwise and Global Second-Moment Estimates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20533)
*Meng Zhu,Quan Xiao,Weidong Min*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Ada2MS是一种新型优化器，通过连续指数插值融合AdamW和Momentum SGD优势，在视觉任务中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AdamW具有稳定性和鲁棒性但泛化性能有时较弱；Momentum SGD泛化性能更好但对超参数敏感，两种范式各有优劣。

Method: 通过在逐元素二阶矩估计和全局二阶矩估计之间进行连续指数插值，实现AdamW类行为与Momentum SGD类行为之间的平滑过渡。

Result: 在统一优化器对比协议下的视觉任务评估中，Ada2MS取得了具有竞争力的结果。

Conclusion: Ada2MS成功融合了AdamW的稳定性和Momentum SGD的泛化能力，在视觉任务上取得了具有竞争力的结果。

Abstract: Optimization algorithms are core methods by which machine learning models iteratively minimize loss functions, update parameters, learn from data, and improve performance. Momentum SGD and AdamW represent two important optimization paradigms. AdamW produces stable updates and usually has strong robustness across training scenarios, but its generalization performance is sometimes weaker than that of momentum methods. Momentum SGD can often obtain better generalization after careful tuning, but it is more sensitive to gradient-scale variation and hyperparameter settings. To balance the strengths and weaknesses of the two paradigms, this paper proposes Ada2MS, an optimization algorithm that achieves a smooth transition between AdamW-like behavior and momentum-SGD-like behavior through continuous exponential interpolation between elementwise second-moment estimates and global second-moment estimates. On the visual tasks evaluated in this study, Ada2MS obtains competitive results under a unified optimizer-comparison protocol. The code will be released at https://github.com/mengzhu0308/Ada2MS

</details>


### [123] [Axiomatizing Neural Networks via Pursuit of Subspaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20534)
*Mehmet Yamac,Mert Duman,Ugur Akpinar,Felix Rojas Casadiego,Serkan Kiranyaz,Marcel van Gerven,Moncef Gabbouj*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: This paper proposes the Pursuit of Subspaces (PoS) hypothesis—an axiomatic geometric framework to demystify deep neural networks. By formulating neural behavior through geometric postulates, the work aims to provide theoretical grounding for understanding representation, computation, and generalization in both shallow and deep networks, addressing the fundamental "black box" problem in deep learning.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Deep neural networks achieve strong empirical results but lack interpretability—their mechanisms remain "black boxes," creating a gap analogous to pre-axiomatic classical geometry.

Method: Axiomatic/geometric framework using set-theoretic postulates to model neural network behavior.

Result: The framework unifies representation, computation, and generalization across shallow and deep architectures through geometric principles.

Conclusion: PoS framework offers geometric explanations for representation structure, architectural mechanisms, and generalization behavior, advancing a coherent theoretical foundation for deep learning.

Abstract: While deep neural networks have achieved remarkable success across a wide range of domains, their underlying mechanisms remain poorly understood, and they are often regarded as black boxes. This gap between empirical performance and theoretical understanding poses a challenge analogous to the pre-axiomatic stage of classical geometry. In this work, we introduce the Pursuit of Subspaces (PoS) hypothesis, an axiomatic framework that formulates neural network behavior through a set of geometric postulates. These axioms, together with their derived consequences, provide a unified perspective on representation, computation, and generalization in both shallow and deep architectures. We show that this framework yields geometric explanations for fundamental questions in deep learning, including representation structure, architectural mechanisms, and generalization behavior, offering a principled step toward a coherent theoretical foundation.

</details>


### [124] [OpenSeisML: Open Large-Scale Real Seismic and well-log Dataset for Generative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20539)
*Ipsita Bhar,Huseyin Tuna Erdinc,Thales Souza,Charles Jones,Felix J. Herrmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OpenSeisML是一个面向地震反演生成式AI工作流程的真实地震数据集集合，来源于英国国家数据仓库的公开勘探数据，通过checkshot建立时深关系并插值构建速度模型，实现时间深度转换。目标是训练生成模型捕捉地下属性的统计分布，用于合成多个统计一致的实现进行不确定性量化，作为地震反演的先验知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统迭代地震反演方法计算成本高昂，而机器学习和计算机视觉虽能加速反演工作流程，但其发展和评估受限于真实速度模型的稀缺性，因为高质量数据大多由油气公司私人拥有。

Method: OpenSeisML从英国国家数据仓库（NDR）的公开地震勘探数据中提取真实数据集。当地震数据处于时间域而井数据处于深度域时，使用checkshot数据建立时深关系，通过插值构建速度模型，实现后叠地震数据的时间深度转换。开发了自动化数据管理流程以确保数据准备的可复现性。

Result: 构建了OpenSeisML数据集和配套的自动化数据管理pipeline。

Conclusion: OpenSeisML提供了一个可复现的自动化数据管理流程，为训练生成模型奠定基础，该模型可捕捉地下属性的统计分布，合成多个统计一致的实现，用于不确定性量化，可作为地震反演的先验知识。

Abstract: The advent of machine learning (ML) and computer vision has significantly accelerated seismic inversion workflows by reducing the computational cost of traditionally expensive iterative methods. However, the development and evaluation of ML methods remain limited by the scarcity of realistic velocity models, as most high-quality data are privately owned by oil and gas companies. To address this gap, we present OpenSeisML, a collection of real seismic datasets designed to support generative AI (Gen-AI) workflows for seismic inversion. The datasets are curated from publicly available surveys in the UK National Data Repository (NDR). When seismic volumes are in the time domain and wells are in depth, a time-to-depth conversion is required. We use checkshot data to establish the time-depth relationship and construct a velocity model through interpolation for accurate conversion of post-stack seismic data. Here, we present an automated data curation pipeline that enables seismic data preparation while ensuring reproducibility. The objective is to train a generative model that captures the statistical distribution of subsurface properties, enabling the synthesis of multiple statistically consistent realizations for uncertainty quantification which can act as a prior for seismic inversion.

</details>


### [125] [Latent Process Generator Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20547)
*Lukas Billera,Hedwig Nora Nordlinder,Ben Murrell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"潜在过程生成器匹配"框架，将观测生成状态视为 tractable Markov 过程的确定性映射，从而将 Generator Matching 从静态潜在变量推广到时变潜在条件过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 flow-matching 和扩散模型依赖辅助随机动力学，但辅助状态在生成时难以采样或并非期望输出。当前 Generator Matching 理论仅能处理静态潜在变量，无法处理动态辅助过程。需要统一理论框架来连接辅助动力学与目标生成分布。

Method: 提出 latent process generator matching 框架，将观测生成状态 X_t = Φ(Y_t) 定义为 tractable Markov 过程 Y_t 的确定性映射。在此设置下，学习图像空间上与投影过程具有相同一维边缘分布的随机过程生成器。

Result: 该框架统一并推广了现有离散潜在过程结果，将 Generator Matching 从静态潜在变量扩展到丰富的时变潜在条件过程族。

Conclusion: 本研究提供了连接辅助动力学与图像空间生成分布的统一理论框架，证明了可以在不显式模拟潜在过程的情况下，仅通过投影关系实现分布匹配。

Abstract: Many recent flow-matching and diffusion-style generative models rely on auxiliary stochastic dynamics during training: a richer process is simulated to define conditional targets, but the auxiliary state is either intractable to sample at generation time or simply not part of the desired output. Existing Generator Matching theory formalises conditioning on static latent random variables, and several recent papers prove special cases of projection results for particular augmented-state constructions. We introduce latent process generator matching, a general framework that treats the observed generative state as a deterministic image $X_t=Φ(Y_t)$ of a tractable Markov process $Y_t$. We show that in this setting one may learn the generator of a stochastic process on the image space which has the same one-time marginal distributions as the projected process. This generalizes and subsumes the discrete latent process results from the literature, and extends Generator Matching from static latent variables to a rich family of time-dependent latent conditional processes.

</details>


### [126] [TriForces: Augmenting Atomistic GNNs for Transferable Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20581)
*Ali Ramlaoui,Alexandre Duval,Hannah Bull,Victor Schmidt,Hugues Talbot,Fragkiskos D. Malliaros,Joseph Musielewicz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TriForces是一个模型无关的三流框架，通过分离成分和结构信息，结合自监督学习来保持可迁移表示，显著提升MLIP在小数据集场景下的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current MLIPs transfer inconsistently across domains, with representations often losing accessible composition and structure information when adapted to target chemistries using small task-specific datasets.

Method: Model-agnostic three-stream framework separating composition and structure information, combined with self-supervised learning to preserve transferable representations.

Result: On MatBench and QM9: outperforms baselines without needing DFT labels; enables efficient similar structure retrieval. On OMat24: reduces energy MAE by 57% at 20K samples and improves force MAE across all sample sizes.

Conclusion: Pretrained TriForces variants across multiple MLIP architectures released with code for reproducibility and extension.

Abstract: Machine learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) achieve excellent accuracy when trained on large Density Functional Theory (DFT) data. To be useful in practice, they must often be adapted to target chemistries using small and expensive task-specific datasets. However, MLIPs transfer inconsistently across domains, with representations that often loose accessible composition and structure information. To address this, we present TriForces, a model-agnostic three-stream framework that separates composition and structure information, combined with self-supervised learning to preserve transferable representations. TriForces improves performance on MatBench and QM9 over baselines without needing DFT labels and enables efficient similar structure retrieval through its learned latent space. On OMat24, in limited-data training regime, TriForces reduces energy MAE by 57% at 20K samples only and improves force MAE across sample sizes. We release pretrained TriForces variants across multiple MLIP architectures with code at https://github.com/Ramlaoui/triforces.

</details>


### [127] [ReversedQ: Opportunities for Faster Q-Learning in Episodic Online Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20592)
*Sofia R. Miskala-Dinc,Aviva Prins*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对有限Horizon эпизодических MDP中的无模型Q学习问题，基于Wang等人的RandomizedQ方法，通过调整值函数更新顺序、更新频率和值函数初始化三个方面的改进，提出ReversedQ方法。实验表明，该方法在BDCL任务中奖励提升从9.53%增至78.78%，链式MDP中从21.76%增至61.81%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无模型后验采样方法在证明理论保证时依赖于延迟学习，导致学习速度受限。这是当前模型-free posterior-sampling研究中的一个核心问题，限制了方法的实用性。

Method: 以RandomizedQ为基础，从三个方面进行改进：(i) 值函数更新顺序，(ii) 更新频率，(iii) 值函数初始化。通过消融实验验证各改进的单独及累积效果，最终提出改进方法ReversedQ。

Result: 结合三项修改后，ReversedQ在BDCL任务中 mean cumulative reward 提升从9.53%增至78.78%，链式MDP中从21.76%增至61.81%。消融实验表明三项改进具有协同效应。

Conclusion: 通过调整更新顺序、频率和初始化三个因素，可以有效加速无模型后验采样方法的学习进程。三项改进的组合效果显著优于单独使用任一改进。

Abstract: We study model-free Q-learning in finite-horizon episodic Markov Decision Processes (MDPs) with stationary dynamics across episodes. We identify a central issue in nascent model-free posterior-sampling works: the reliance on delayed learning in order to prove theoretical guarantees. In particular, we identify three opportunities for faster learning - (i) value-function update order, (ii) update frequencies, and (iii) value-function initialization. Using Wang et al.'s RandomizedQ as a basis, we illustrate these changes and their individual (as well as cumulative) impact in multiple empirical studies. We find that our combined modifications, termed ReversedQ, improve scaled mean cumulative reward compared to RandomizedQ, from 9.53% to 78.78% in the Bidirectional Diabolical Combination Lock (BDCL), and from 21.76% to 61.81% in a chain MDP.

</details>


### [128] [Unsupervised clustering and classification of upper limb EMG signals during functional movements: a data-driven](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20599)
*L. F. Salazar Álvarez,D. Escobar-Saltarén,M. B. Salazar Sánchez,S. C. Henao-Aguirre*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究针对上肢表面肌电信号设计了一个完整的处理pipeline（预处理→特征提取→手势聚类筛选→分类评估），在NINAPRO DB4数据集上验证了方法的有效性，最终以5个关键特征实现6种代表性手势的高效分类，为假肢控制提供了可扩展的低延迟方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表面肌电信号（sEMG）可用于控制上肢假肢，但多通道信号的噪声处理、特征选择和实时分类仍是挑战，需要一个完整的pipeline实现低延迟、高精度的手势识别。

Method: 该研究采用四阶段pipeline：信号预处理（四阶低通滤波、希尔伯特包络变换）、特征提取（26个时域/频域特征）、层次聚类手势筛选（马氏距离）、分类器比较评估（PyCaret自动对比+独立训练验证）。

Result: 从26个候选特征中筛选出5个关键特征，层次聚类将52种手势压缩为6种代表性动作，200ms窗口被确认为最优分割长度。Extra Trees和ANN分类器表现最佳，其中ANN展现渐进学习能力，ET保持稳健一致的性能。

Conclusion: 研究提出了一个用于表面肌电信号分类的综合pipeline，成功从52种手势中筛选出6种代表性动作，最终使用5个关键特征实现高效分类。Extra Trees和ANN分类器表现最优，证明了该方法在假肢控制系统中的可行性和可扩展性，为实时低延迟控制提供了技术支撑。

Abstract: This study presents a comprehensive approach for the clustering and classification of upper-limb surface electromyography (sEMG) signals during functional reach and grasp movements. The methodology was applied to the NINAPRO DB4 dataset, which provides multichannel EMG recordings of 52 gestures. A four-stage pipeline was designed, including signal preprocessing, fea-ture extraction, gesture selection via hierarchical clustering, and comparative model evaluation. Preprocessing involved a fourth-order low-pass filter (0.6 Hz) and Hilbert envelope transformation, effectively reducing noise and enhancing signal clarity. Feature extraction yielded 26 temporal and frequency-domain met-rics, which were later refined using visual analysis, mutual information, principal component analysis, and decision tree importance scores. A final subset of five key features was selected for classification tasks. Gesture selection was per-formed through hierarchical clustering using Mahalanobis distance, resulting in six representative movements that balanced biomechanical diversity and compu-tational efficiency. A 200 ms window was identified as optimal for temporal seg-mentation based on stability and physiological plausibility. Classifier models were evaluated in two stages. Automated comparison using PyCaret identified Extra Trees (ET) and Artificial Neural Networks (ANN) as top performers. Sub-sequent independent training confirmed their stability and generalization capac-ity, with ANN showing progressive learning and ET maintaining robust, con-sistent results. The findings support the implementation of adaptive, low-latency control strategies for myoelectric prostheses and provide a scalable pipeline for future real-time applications.

</details>


### [129] [Compositional Transduction with Latent Analogies for Offline Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20609)
*Junseok Kim,Dohyeong Kim,Mineui Hong,Songhwai Oh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对离线目标条件强化学习中的组合泛化问题，提出类比转导框架，通过任务内生类比与上下文的组合合成新计划。核心贡献是一种捕捉最优执行变化且对上下文不变的新类比表示，以及使类比转导泛化到未见类比-上下文对的方法。实验在OGBench上验证了方法有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通过时序连续片段进行轨迹缝合来追求组合泛化，这限制了在不同上下文间组合行为的能力，难以应对离线目标条件强化学习中未见目标与新上下文变化的挑战。

Method: 提出了一种新颖的类比表示方法，该表示捕捉最优任务执行下的变化部分，对上下文变化保持不变，且足以实现最优目标达成；并进一步引入一种新方法使类比转导能够泛化到未见过的类比-上下文组合。

Result: 在OGBench操作环境中显著优于不进行类比转导的先前方法。

Conclusion: 提出了类比转导（analogy transduction）方法，在OGBench操作环境中显著优于不进行类比转导的先前方法。

Abstract: Compositional generalization is essential for reaching unseen goals under novel contextual variations in offline goal-conditioned reinforcement learning (GCRL), where a generalist goal-reaching agent must be learned from limited data. Most prior approaches pursue this via trajectory stitching over temporally contiguous segments, which limits composing behaviors across varying contexts. To overcome this limitation, we formalize analogy transduction as synthesizing new plans by composing task-endogenous analogies with given contexts and propose a novel analogy representation tailored for it. Grounded in our theory, this analogy representation captures what changes under optimal task execution, remains invariant to contextual variations, and is sufficient for optimal goal reaching. We further contend that generalization to unseen analogy-context pairs is a practical obstacle in analogy transduction, and introduce a new approach for offline GCRL that enables analogy transduction beyond seen pairs to unseen combinations. We empirically demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach on OGBench manipulation environments, substantially outperforming prior methods that do not perform analogy transduction. Project page: https://rllab-snu.github.io/projects/CTA/

</details>


### [130] [Matryoshka Concept Bottleneck Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20612)
*Ziye Chen,Hongbin Lin,Xinyue Xu,Jie Li,Lijie Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MCBM通过嵌套概念层次结构解决CBM高干预成本问题，成本从线性降至对数阶，兼顾可解释性与效率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CBM方法面临两难：静态概念集需穷尽标注导致成本过高，多模型方案则产生计算和运维开销

Method: 借鉴Matryoshka表征学习，将概念组织为嵌套层次结构（基于最大相关性+最小冗余），支持多粒度推理无需重训练

Result: 理论：干预成本从O(K)降至O(log K)；实验：性能匹配独立训练模型，实现动态高效人机交互

Conclusion: MCBM通过嵌套层次结构设计平衡了模型可解释性与干预效率，为可解释深度学习提供了统一实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Concept Bottleneck Models (CBMs) have emerged as a prominent paradigm for interpretable deep learning, learning by grounding predictions in human-understandable concepts. However, their practical deployment is hindered by the high cost of test-time intervention, as correcting model errors typically requires human experts to manually inspect and verify a large set of predicted concepts. Existing approaches suffer from a fundamental structural limitation: they either adopt a single static concept set, forcing experts to exhaustively annotate concepts and incurring prohibitive intervention costs, or train multiple models tailored to different concept budgets, resulting in substantial computational and maintenance overhead. To address this challenge, we propose the Matryoshka Concept Bottleneck Model (MCBM), a unified architecture that enables adaptive concept utilization within a single model. Inspired by Matryoshka Representation Learning, MCBM organizes concepts into a nested hierarchy based on maximum relevance and minimum redundancy, allowing inference at multiple levels of conceptual granularity without retraining. Theoretically, we show that MCBM reduces the expected intervention costs from linear to logarithmic order, $O(\log K)$, while guaranteeing monotonic performance improvement. Empirically, extensive experiments demonstrate that MCBM matches the performance of independently trained models while enabling dynamic and efficient expert interaction.

</details>


### [131] [SURF: Steering the Scalarization Weight to Uniformly Traverse the Pareto Front](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20619)
*Liuyuan Jiang,Chentong Huang,Lisha Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SURF解决了一个关键问题：在多目标优化中，均匀采样标量化权重不能产生均匀的帕累托前沿覆盖。SURF通过分析标量化路径的几何性质（遍历速度），构建弧长累积分布函数（CDF），通过CDF反演得到PF-aware的权重采样规则，实现均匀的帕累托前沿覆盖，并在理论和实验上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The core problem is that uniformly sampling scalarization weights does NOT yield uniform coverage of the Pareto front, due to varying traversal speeds as weights change. This mismatch causes biased sampling in preference-based multi-objective optimization applications.

Method: SURF employs a two-stage approach: (1) geometric analysis of scalarization path traversal speeds to derive arc-length CDFs, and (2) CDF inversion to determine PF-aware weight sampling rules. For structured problems (e.g., bi-objective bandits), closed-form solutions are derived; for general problems, SURF alternates between CDF reconstruction and weight sampling to progressively refine coverage uniformity.

Result: SURF achieves more uniform PF coverage than baselines, with linear convergence to a finite-sampling floor under provable conditions.

Conclusion: SURF provides a principled, theoretically grounded solution for achieving uniform Pareto front coverage in multi-objective optimization, with strong empirical validation across bandits, multi-objective-gymnasium, and multi-objective LLM alignment domains.

Abstract: Scalarization is widely used in multi-objective optimization owing to its simplicity and scalability. In many applications, the goal is to generate solutions that represent diverse user preferences, ideally with uniform coverage of the Pareto front (PF). However, uniformly sampling scalarization weights usually induces non-uniform coverage of the PF. We explain this mismatch through a geometric analysis of the scalarization path. As the scalarization weight varies, the corresponding solutions trace the PF with a generally non-uniform traversal speed. This speed induces an arc-length cumulative distribution function (CDF); inverting this CDF map yields a principled rule for selecting weights that produce uniform PF coverage. Building on this insight, we propose SURF (Sampling Uniformly along the PaReto Front). For structured problems, including bi-objective bandits, we derive closed-form expressions for this CDF map and the resulting PF-aware weight sampling rule. For general problems, SURF alternates between CDF reconstruction and weight sampling. Theoretically, we show that under provable conditions, SURF converges linearly to an unavoidable finite-sampling floor. Empirically, experiments on bandits, multi-objective-gymnasium, and multi-objective LLM alignment demonstrate that SURF efficiently achieves more uniform PF coverage than baselines.

</details>


### [132] [Same Target, Different Basins: Hard vs. Soft Labels for Annotator Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20642)
*Mirerfan Gheibi,Gashin Ghazizadeh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究标注者分歧下的标签传递策略，比较硬标签方法（多遍和随机标签采样）与软标签训练。在CIFAR-10H上的实验表明，在标注稀缺场景下硬标签方法表现更优，且收敛到更平坦的盆地带增强泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当标注者存在分歧时，这种分歧可能反映认知不确定性而非简单标签噪声。现有方法通常将投票压缩为单一标签或直接在经验软标签分布上训练，但作者认为硬标签传递是另一种可行的选择。

Method: 在CIFAR-10H上对比两种硬标签方法（多遍和随机标签采样）与软标签训练；以确定性控制作为多遍的消融实验，以打乱的SLS作为破坏样本-分布匹配的对照；通过OOD检测（SVHN和CIFAR-100）评估模型收敛到平坦盆地的特性。

Result: 当每个样本仅有少量标注时，硬标签传递优于软标签训练，且稀疏经验目标与完整标注者分布差距越大时优势越明显；当完整标注者分布可用时，两种硬标签方法均与软标签训练持平；硬标签传递收敛至更平坦的损失盆地。

Conclusion: 多遍（Multipass）在有原始投票计数时是实用的默认选择；当只有少量投票时，随机标签采样（SLS）是一种轻量级且具有竞争力的替代方案，且当完整标注者分布可用时与软标签训练性能相当。

Abstract: When annotators disagree, that disagreement can reflect epistemic uncertainty rather than simple label noise. We study hard-label delivery as an alternative to the usual choices of collapsing votes to a single label or training directly on the empirical soft-label distribution. We focus on two primary hard-label methods: multipass, which cycles through observed votes while keeping the dataset size fixed, and stochastic label sampling (SLS), which samples one label per example at the start of each epoch. On CIFAR-10H, we find that when only a small number of annotations per example is available, hard-label delivery improves over soft-label training, with larger improvements where the sparse empirical target is farther from the full annotator distribution. When full annotator distributions are available, both hard-label methods match soft-label training. We use deterministic control as an ablation of multipass and shuffled SLS as a control that breaks the example-to-distribution match. We also show that SLS and soft-label cross-entropy optimize the same expected objective. Hard-label delivery also converges to flatter basins, with supporting descriptive evidence from OOD detection on SVHN and CIFAR-100. Overall, these results suggest that multipass is a strong practical default when raw vote counts are available, while SLS offers a lightweight alternative that remains competitive when only a few votes per example are available and matches soft-label training when full annotator distributions are available.

</details>


### [133] [AVSD: Adaptive-View Self-Distillation by Balancing Consensus and Teacher-Specific Privileged Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20643)
*Duy Nguyen,Hanqi Xiao,Archiki Prasad,Zaid Khan,Anirban Das,Austin Zhang,Sambit Sahu,Hyunji Lee,Elias Stengel-Eskin,Mohit Bansal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AVSD提出了一种自适应多视角自蒸馏方法，通过分离跨视角共识信号与视角特定残差信号来解决自蒸馏中的两个核心问题：教师-学生信息不对称和单一视角选择的困难。在数学竞赛（AIME24/25, HMMT25）和代码生成（Codeforces, LiveCodeBench）基准上，AVSD相比单视角自蒸馏和GRPO方法取得了一致的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自蒸馏面临两个关键挑战：(1) 教师使用学生无法访问的特权信息，导致推理时信息不对称；(2) 最优特权信息类型通常与任务相关，难以选择单一视角作为教师。

Method: AVSD通过以下机制解决上述挑战：分离跨视角共识信号（提供可靠更新方向）与视角特定残差信号（调整更新幅度），仅当残差信号与共识方向一致且与共识信号成比例时才予以添加。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B和Qwen3-4B上，AVSD相比最强基线分别获得3.1%和2.2%的Avg@8提升；在代码生成任务上，Qwen3-8B平均提升2.4%。

Conclusion: 多视角自适应自蒸馏能够有效结合不同特权信息的优势，通过共识机制筛选可靠的监督信号，为语言模型的自训练提供了一种更稳健的策略。

Abstract: Self-distillation enables language models to learn on-policy from their own trajectories by using the same model as both student and teacher, with the teacher being conditioned on privileged information unavailable to the student. Such information can come in different types or views, such as solutions, demonstrations, feedback, or final answers. This setup provides dense token-level feedback without relying on a separate external model, but creates a fundamental asymmetry: the teacher may rely on view-specific information that the student cannot access at inference time. Moreover, the best type of privileged information is often task-dependent, making it difficult to choose a single teacher view. In this work, we address both these challenges jointly by introducing AVSD (Adaptive-View Self-Distillation), a novel method of self-distillation with multiple privileged-information views, which reconstructs token-level supervision by separating stable cross-view consensus from view-specific residual signals. AVSD identifies the consensus signal shared across views, which provides a reliable update direction, and then selectively adds the view-specific residual signal to adjust the update magnitude when it both aligns with the consensus direction and remains proportionate to the consensus signal. Experiments on math competition benchmarks (AIME24, AIME25, and HMMT25) show that AVSD consistently outperforms both single-view self-distillation baselines and GRPO, achieving average Avg@8 gains of 3.1% and 2.2% over the strongest baselines on Qwen3-8B and Qwen3-4B, respectively. Moreover, on code-generation benchmarks (Codeforces, LiveCodeBench v6) using Qwen3-8B, AVSD outperforms the single-view self-distillation baseline by 2.4% on average.

</details>


### [134] [Design for Manufacturing: A Manufacturability Knowledge-Integrated Reinforcement Learning Framework for Free-Form Pipe Routing in Aeroengines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20644)
*Caicheng Wang,Zili Wang,Shuyou Zhang,Yongzhe Xiang,Zheyi Li,Liangyou Li,Jianrong Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FPRO框架，通过Frenet标架下的曲率/挠率参数化表示和制造约束集成，结合PPO强化学习算法，实现航空发动机复杂管路的自主设计与可制造优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航空发动机复杂管路设计仍采用与传统制造脱钩的方法，导致设计环节需要大量试错迭代，难以保证可制造性。

Method: 提出FPRO框架，将管路路径优化问题表述为Frenet标架下的边值问题，采用立方Hermite插值生成曲率和挠率分布曲线；通过将制造知识嵌入为曲率/挠率可行域约束实现设计与制造集成；使用近端策略优化(PPO)算法进行路径优化，配合随机探索和阶段引导奖励机制；通过统一映射公式将优化路径转化为六轴自由弯曲机的运动轨迹。

Result: 实验表明FPRO能持续生成无碰撞、可制造的平滑路径；相比笛卡尔方法具有更优几何特性；相比先进RL基准算法具有更快收敛速度，并在终端对齐、路径长度、障碍避让和可制造性方面表现更优；实际制造验证确认了数字设计与实体管路的高度几何一致性。

Conclusion: FPRO成功实现了设计与制造环节的有效集成，验证了该框架在实际工程应用中的可行性。

Abstract: Design for manufacturing plays a critical role in advanced aeroengine development, where complex components necessitate careful consideration of manufacturability. However, current practices in pipe routing remain largely decoupled from down-stream manufacturing, leading to labor-intensive, trial-and-error iterations to achieve manufacturable designs. To address this problem, this study proposes the Frenet-based pipe routing optimization (FPRO) framework, a manufacturability knowledge-integrated reinforcement learning approach for free-form pipe design in aeroengines. FPRO formulates the routing problem as a boundary value problem in the Frenet frame. In this framework, the pipe path is represented by curvature and torsion profiles, which are generated using cubic Hermite interpolation. To integrate design and manufacturing, domain-specific manufacturing knowledge is embedded as constraints on the permissible ranges of curvature and torsion. The path optimization is performed using the proximal policy optimization algorithm with stochastic exploration and a stage-guided reward mechanism. A unified mapping formulation then translates the optimized path into motion trajectories for the bending die, enabling direct fabrication on a six-axis free-bending machine. Experimental results demonstrate that FPRO consistently generates collision-free, manufacturable paths with smoother geometric profiles compared to Cartesian-based methods. It also achieves faster convergence and superior performance in terminal alignment, path length, obstacle avoidance, and manufacturability compared to state-of-the-art reinforcement learning baselines. Real-world validation confirms the close geometric correspondence between the manufactured pipe and its digital design, validating the practical feasibility of FPRO.

</details>


### [135] [LT2: Linear-Time Looped Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20670)
*Chunyuan Deng,Yizhe Zhang,Rui-Jie Zhu,Yuanyuan Xu,Jiarui Liu,T. S. Eugene Ng,Hanjie Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LT2是一种将循环Transformer中的二次复杂度softmax注意力替换为线性或稀疏注意力的高效架构，通过循环机制使线性注意力获得迭代记忆精炼能力，使稀疏注意力获得逐步扩展的感受野，在保持质量的同时大幅提升效率，其中Ouro-hybrid-1.4B以线性时间成本实现了超越业界1B并匹敌4B模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统循环Transformer（LT）配对全注意力保持二次复杂度，计算成本高且速度慢，限制了其在规模化和小模型场景的应用。

Method: 提出LT2（线性时间循环Transformer）系列，通过用线性时间和稀疏注意力替代二次复杂度的softmax注意力来实现；开发了LT2-linear（线性注意力）、LT2-sparse（稀疏注意力）和LT2-hybrid（混合注意力）三种变体；实现了预训练模型转换方法，可将预训练的LT转换为LT2-hybrid。

Result: 循环机制与线性/稀疏注意力协同增效：迭代记忆精炼使线性注意力增强，逐步扩展稀疏注意力的有效感受野；在召回、状态追踪和语言建模任务中取得一致提升；LT2-hybrid (GDN+DSA) 以全线性时间成本匹配标准LT质量；LT2-hybrid (Full+GDN) 以少量全注意力层超越标准LT的性能和效率；转换后的Ouro-hybrid-1.4B超越业界1B模型，匹敌4B模型。

Conclusion: 提出了LT2-hybrid模型，其中Ouro-hybrid-1.4B在约1B tokens训练下，性能超越业界1B模型并匹敌4B模型，同时保持线性时间注意力的速度优势，为循环transformer的大规模化和小型语言模型的高效化提供了清晰路径。

Abstract: Looped Transformers (LT) have emerged as a powerful architecture by iterating their layers multiple times before decoding the final token. However, pairing them with full attention retains quadratic complexity, making them computationally expensive and slow. We introduce LT2 (Linear-Time Looped Transformers), a family of looped architectures that replace quadratic softmax attention with subquadratic, linear-time attention. We study two variants: LT2-linear with linear attention and LT2-sparse with sparse attention. We find that looping uniquely synergizes with these variants: it enables iterative memory refinement in linear attention and progressively expands the effective receptive field in sparse attention. We formalize these benefits theoretically and demonstrate consistent empirical gains across controlled recall, state-tracking, and language modeling tasks. We then explore LT2-hybrid, which combines different attention variants in a looped setting. Two variants are especially promising: LT2-hybrid (GDN+DSA), which interleaves linear and sparse attention to maximize efficiency and matches the standard looped transformer's quality at fully linear-time cost; and LT2-hybrid (Full+GDN), which interleaves GDN with a small fraction of full attention layers to maximize quality, surpassing the standard looped transformer in both performance and efficiency. We also show how to convert a pre-trained LT into an LT2-hybrid model. With about 1B tokens of training, our converted model, Ouro-hybrid-1.4B, outperforms industry-level 1B models and is competitive with industry-level 4B models while retaining the speed benefits of linear-time attention. Together, these results show a clear path toward making looped transformers more scalable and advancing efficient, capable small language models.

</details>


### [136] [Dynamic TMoE: A Drift-Aware Dynamic Mixture of Experts Framework for Non-Stationary Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20678)
*Jiawen Zhu,Shuhan Liu,Di Weng,Yingcai Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Dynamic TMoE是一种面向非平稳时间序列预测的动态专家混合框架，通过MMD检测分布漂移实现专家的动态实例化与剪枝，并利用时序记忆路由器进行上下文感知的稳定路由，无需测试时更新即可有效适应突发 regime 转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE方法受限于固定专家池和内存-less路由，难以适应突发 regime 转换，无法有效捕捉非平稳时间序列中的演化分布漂移。

Method: 提出Dynamic TMoE框架，通过MMD检测分布漂移，动态实例化和剪枝专家以优化容量；引入时序记忆路由器，利用循环状态和异常仓库实现稳定、上下文感知的专家选择，无需测试时更新。

Result: 在9个基准数据集上达到最优性能，MSE降低10.4%，MAE降低7.8%。

Conclusion: Dynamic TMoE 在9个基准数据集上实现了最优性能，MSE降低10.4%，MAE降低7.8%，验证了动态专家池与时序记忆路由在处理非平稳时间序列预测中的有效性。

Abstract: Non-stationary time series forecasting is challenged by evolving distribution shifts that static models struggle to capture. While Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures offer a promising paradigm for decoupling complex drift patterns, existing approaches are limited by fixed expert pools and memoryless routing, hampering their ability to adapt to abrupt regime shifts. To address this, we propose Dynamic TMoE, a framework that unifies architectural evolution with temporal continuity during learning phase. By detecting distribution shifts via Maximum Mean Discrepancy (MMD), we dynamically instantiate heterogeneous experts and prune redundant ones to optimize capacity. Additionally, a temporal memory router leverages recurrent states and an anomaly repository to ensure stable, context-aware expert selection without requiring test-time updates. Experiments on nine benchmarks demonstrate state-of-the-art performance, reducing MSE by 10.4% and MAE by 7.8%. Code is available at https://github.com/andone-07/Dynamic-TMoE.

</details>


### [137] [Distributed Direct Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20696)
*Zhanhong Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 首个分析分布式环境中DPO收敛性和时间复杂度的理论工作，涵盖联邦和去中心化两种架构，通过建模个性化离线RL和偏好分布，量化了异构偏好和通信约束对优化动态的影响，并在标准对齐基准上验证了理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏好-based强化学习(RL)是对齐策略与人类判断的关键范式，但偏好数据分散在异构用户中的分布式场景下其理论行为尚不清楚。DPO避免显式奖励建模，但在联邦和去中心化训练下缺乏收敛保证。

Method: 基于用户特定偏好分布建模个性化离线RL，通过谱连通性分析在一般通信图上建立收敛性理论。

Result: 为联邦DPO推导出量化客户端漂移、通信频率和偏好异质性影响的收敛速率；为去中心化DPO建立一般通信图上的收敛性，并证明谱连通性支配优化速度和一致性。

Conclusion: 为分布式偏好优化提供了首个完整的理论分析框架，量化了客户端漂移、通信频率和偏好异质性的影响，并通过实验验证了方法的有效性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Preference-based reinforcement learning (RL) is a key paradigm for aligning policies with human judgments, yet its theoretical behavior in distributed settings where preference data are fragmented across heterogeneous users remains poorly understood. Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) avoids explicit reward modeling but lacks convergence guarantees under federated and decentralized training, where communication constraints and non-IID preferences fundamentally alter optimization dynamics. We provide the first convergence and time-complexity analysis of DPO in distributed environments. Modeling personalized offline RL with user-specific preference distributions, we characterize the induced global optimization landscape. For federated DPO, we derive convergence rates that quantify the impact of client drift, communication frequency, and preference heterogeneity; for decentralized DPO, we establish convergence over general communication graphs and show how spectral connectivity governs optimization speed and consensus. Empirically, we corroborate our theoretical insights on standard alignment benchmarks, demonstrating that our proposed methods not only enjoy strong theoretical guarantees but also deliver robust and scalable performance in practice. The code base is available here.

</details>


### [138] [Decision-Path Patterns as Tree Reliability Signals: Path-based Adaptive Weighting for Random Forest Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20716)
*Youngjoon Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于决策路径拓扑模式的新型随机森林加权方法，通过类条件比率对树进行样本级重加权，在保证零类别偏差的前提下显著提升分类性能，在30个二分类数据集上验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统随机森林对所有树给予等权重投票，忽视了不同决策树对不同样本的可靠性差异，具有改进空间。

Method: 利用每棵树根到叶决策路径上主导类别标签翻转的拓扑模式作为树可靠性信号，采用类条件比率(class-conditional ratio)加权进行样本重加权，保证构造上零期望类别偏差。在30个二分类基准数据集上，以共享森林-共享分裂协议进行30次重复实验进行验证。

Result: 在四种对比方案（RF、weighted RF、KNORA-Eliminate、KNORA-Union）中，该方法是唯一在统计上显著提升准确率的方案(p=0.018)，其余三种方案均未达到统计显著(p>0.5)；该方法也是唯一不存在多数类recall回归的方案，少数类recall回归仅在3/30数据集上出现；在100-1000棵树的森林规模下增益均稳健。

Conclusion: 提出的类条件比率加权方法CCR是无偏的，是唯一在统计上显著提升随机森林准确率的方法( Wilcoxon's p=0.018)，且对多数类recall无回归，少数类recall回归仅在3/30数据集上出现，增益在100-1000棵树的森林规模下均稳健。

Abstract: Random forests aggregate tree votes by simple majority, treating all trees as equally informative. We observe that the topological pattern along each tree's root-to-leaf decision path -- where and how often the dominant class label flips along it -- carries a signal of tree reliability that is exploitable for per-sample reweighting. The naive use of this signal is structurally confounded with the predicted class, so we propose a class-conditional ratio weighting that guarantees zero expected class bias by construction. On 30 binary classification benchmarks under a shared-forest, shared-split protocol with 30 repeats, the proposed method is the only one among four compared schemes -- RF, weighted RF, KNORA-Eliminate, KNORA-Union -- to yield a statistically significant accuracy improvement over RF (Wilcoxon p = 0.018), while the three alternatives all fail to do so (p > 0.5). It is also the only scheme without majority-recall regressions, with minority-recall regressions limited to 3/30 datasets -- a one-sided loss to which classical dynamic ensemble selection methods are susceptible. The gain is robust across forest sizes from 100 to 1000 trees.

</details>


### [139] [The Hidden Signal of Verifier Strictness: Controlling and Improving Step-Wise Verification via Selective Latent Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20745)
*Yefan Zhou,Yilun Zhou,Austin Xu,Soroush Vosoughi,Shafiq Joty,Jiang Gui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究生成式验证器中的严格度控制问题，提出VerifySteer方法，通过隐藏状态 steering在段落边界进行样本级路由，在错误检测和正确性认证之间取得平衡，实验表明该方法优于提示优化和激活干预基线，且与自洽方法相当但计算量减少4-7倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式验证器在逐步验证中常表现出严格度失衡问题：过于宽容会漏检错误步骤，过于严格会误拒正确推理。这种"验证器严格度"问题限制了验证系统的可靠性。

Method: 1. 发现验证特定隐藏状态信号：验证器对解步骤的接受/拒绝倾向编码在对应验证段落的边界附近；2. 提出VerifySteer方法：利用潜在正确性信号进行样本级路由，选择性地在段落边界进行干预；3. 该方法无需微调，直接通过隐藏状态 steering 调制验证器严格度。

Result: 在ProcessBench和Hard2Verify上的实验表明：(1) VerifySteer优于提示优化和激活干预基线；(2) 与自洽方法性能相当，但推理计算量减少4-7倍；(3) 可与验证微调互补，在微调验证器基础上进一步提升性能。

Conclusion: 隐藏状态干预可有效控制验证器严格度，但统一干预会在错误检测与正确性认证间产生权衡。VerifySteer通过样本级路由和选择性边界干预解决了这一trade-off问题，且具有良好的可扩展性（可与微调方法结合）。

Abstract: Generative verifiers have emerged as a promising paradigm for step-wise verification, but their verification behavior is often poorly calibrated: they may be under-critical and miss erroneous steps, or over-critical and reject correct reasoning. We refer to this tendency to be overly lenient or overly critical as verifier strictness. In this work, we study whether verifier strictness can be controlled through hidden-state intervention. We uncover a verification-specific hidden-state signal: in step-wise verification, a verifier's tendency to accept or reject a solution step is encoded near the boundary of the corresponding verification paragraph. Exploiting this signal, we show that hidden-state steering can directly modulate verifier strictness without fine-tuning. However, uniform steering induces a trade-off between error detection and correctness certification. To address this, we propose VerifySteer, which exploits latent correctness signals for sample-level routing and selectively intervenes on paragraph boundaries. Experiments on ProcessBench and Hard2Verify show that VerifySteer outperforms prompt optimization and activation steering baselines, and is competitive with self-consistency while requiring 4-7x less inference compute. VerifySteer is also complementary to verification fine-tuning, providing further gains on top of fine-tuned verifiers. The code is available at https://github.com/YefanZhou/VerifySteer.

</details>


### [140] [Hack-Verifiable Environments: Towards Evaluating Reward Hacking at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20744)
*Amit Roth,Ankur Samanta,Matan Halevy,Yoav Levine,Yonathan Efroni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的评估范式，通过在环境中嵌入可检测的奖励黑客机会，实现自动化、可验证的测量方法，并开源了Hack-Verifiable TextArena测试平台用于大规模分析语言模型的奖励黑客行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奖励黑客是AI领域的核心挑战，但现有方法难以大规模可靠测量奖励黑客行为。

Method: 在环境中直接嵌入可检测的奖励黑客机会，使漏洞利用可通过设计验证，实现自动化确定性测量。

Result: 建立了可验证的奖励黑客基准，能在多样化环境中分析语言模型的奖励黑客行为。

Conclusion: 开源了Hack-Verifiable TextArena测试平台，为可扩展地测量奖励黑客行为提供了可靠方法。

Abstract: Aligning autonomous agents with human intent remains a central challenge in modern AI. A key manifestation of this challenge is reward hacking, whereby agents appear successful under the evaluation signal while violating the intended objective. Reward hacking has been observed across a wide range of settings, yet methods for reliably measuring it at scale remain lacking. In this work, we introduce a new evaluation paradigm for measuring reward hacking. Whereas prior studies have primarily analyzed it post hoc by inspecting agent trajectories, we instead embed detectable reward hacking opportunities directly into environments. This makes their exploitation verifiable by design, enabling deterministic and automated measurement of whether and how agents exploit such vulnerabilities. We instantiate this approach in $\textit{TextArena}$ and release $\textit{Hack-Verifiable TextArena}$, a testbed in which reward hacking can be measured reliably. Using this benchmark, we analyze reward hacking behavior across language models in diverse environments and settings. We open source the code at https://github.com/MajoRoth/hack-verifiable-environments/.

</details>


### [141] [Most Transformer Modifications Still Do Not Transfer at 1-3B: A 2020-2026 Update to Narang et al. (2021) with Downstream Evaluation and a Noise Floor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20798)
*Yang Zhao,Jiahao Lu,Bin Huang,Guhua Zhang,Jie Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable
