Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Narang et al. (2021) evaluated 40+ Transformer modifications at T5-base scale and concluded that most did not transfer. Five years later, the typical working regime has moved to 1-3B parameters, downstream evaluation has replaced pretraining perplexity, and a substantially different catalogue of modifications has emerged. We revisit their question by testing 20 post-2021 Transformer modifications at 1.2B and 3B under strict iso-data, iso-compute, iso-recipe control, with a multi-seed baseline noise floor and CLIMB-12 downstream evaluation as the primary metric. The central finding reproduces theirs at this curated set: most modifications do not transfer. Of the 20 modifications, only two clear Bonferroni correction at 1.2B; one of those two further fails to train stably at 3B under the shared recipe. We also find that the loss-downstream gap reported by Tay et al. (2023) enlarges several-fold for attention-output modifications: two significant failures converge to within 2-3% of baseline validation loss yet drop 6-16 CLIMB-points. We conclude that noise-floor reporting, downstream evaluation, and cross-scale stability testing are now prerequisites for architecture comparisons at 1-3B.

</details>


### [142] [DASH: Fast Differentiable Architecture Search for Hybrid Attention in Minutes on a Single GPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20936)
*Weizhe Chen,Miao Zhang,Junpeng Jiang,Yaping Li,Weili Guan,Liqiang Nie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DASH是一种快速的差异化搜索框架，用于混合注意力架构设计。它将离散的层级注意力算子分配松弛为连续的架构logits，并使用冻结模型权重的架构搜索，显著提升了搜索效率。仅需12.3M tokens（20分钟）即可超越200B tokens的Jet-Nemotron搜索效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing hybrid attention architecture designs rely on manual empirical rules or proxy-based selector signals, which are suboptimal and time-consuming. While NAS-style systems like Jet-Nemotron show promise for automated search, their PostNAS stages alone consume 200B tokens, making such approaches impractical as routine design methods.

Method: DASH introduces three key innovations: (1) relaxes discrete layer-wise attention operator placement into continuous architecture logits for differentiable optimization; (2) prepares reusable teacher-aligned linear candidates; (3) performs architecture-only search with model and operator weights frozen to significantly enhance search efficiency. Each search run uses only 12.3M tokens and completes in ~20 minutes on a single RTX Pro 6000 GPU.

Result: On Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct, DASH consistently outperforms comprehensive baseline suites of selector-style hybrid attention designs. It achieves stronger RULER performance than released Jet-Nemotron models while remaining competitive on overlapping short-context and general benchmarks. Search efficiency is dramatic: only 0.006% of the tokens required by Jet-Nemotron's PostNAS stage.

Conclusion: High-quality hybrid attention architectures can be obtained through minutes-level differentiable search, providing a promising direction for hybrid architecture design.

Abstract: Hybrid attention architectures are becoming an increasingly important paradigm for improving LLM inference efficiency while preserving model quality, making hybrid architecture design a central problem. Existing designs often rely on manual empirical rules or proxy-based selector signals for layer-wise operator allocation. Recent NAS-style systems such as Jet-Nemotron demonstrate the promise of automated hybrid architecture search. However, Jet-Nemotron's PostNAS search stages alone use 200B tokens, making such search pipelines difficult to use as routine methods for hybrid architecture design. We introduce DASH, a fast differentiable search framework for hybrid attention architecture design, which relaxes discrete layer-wise attention operator placement into continuous architecture logits, prepares reusable teacher-aligned linear candidates, and performs architecture-only search with model and operator weights frozen to significantly enhance search efficiency. On Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct, DASH consistently outperforms a comprehensive suite of existing selector-style hybrid attention design baselines, showing that direct differentiable search can discover stronger hybrid architectures. Moreover, DASH achieves stronger RULER performance than released Jet-Nemotron models while remaining competitive on overlapping short-context and general benchmarks. Notably, each DASH search run uses only 12.3M tokens and takes about 20 minutes on a single RTX Pro 6000 GPU, corresponding to merely 0.006% of the PostNAS search tokens reported by Jet-Nemotron. These results suggest that high-quality hybrid attention architectures can be obtained through minutes-level differentiable search, providing a promising direction for hybrid architecture design.

</details>


### [143] [Correcting Stochastic Update Bias in Preconditioned Language Model Optimizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20756)
*Nikhil Nayak,Julia White,Urchade Zaratiana,Kelton Zhang,Henrijs Princis,Dhruv Atreja,Henry Fawcett,Matthew Thomas,George Hurn-Maloney,Ash Lewis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文识别了语言模型预训练中预条件优化器的两类有限样本偏差，并提出统一的双偏差校正框架，通过交叉拟合预条件估计和方差校正求逆，在多种优化器(AdamW、Sophia、Shampoo)上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预条件优化器是语言模型训练的核心，但现有研究将随机更新规则视为对总体预条件下降的直接近似，忽视了两个关键的有限样本偏差来源，导致优化器更新存在系统性偏差。

Method: 提出单批次偏差校正框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 交叉拟合预条件估计——将小批次分为独立微批次组分别估计梯度和预条件器，消除梯度-预条件器耦合偏差；(2) 方差校正求逆——利用微批次变异性减去Delta方法的主要偏差项，校正非线性求逆带来的偏差。该框架可应用于对角矩估计、对角曲率估计和矩阵预条件方法。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-0.5B上，偏差校正分别降低AdamW、Sophia、Shampoo的保留集预训练损失0.15、0.07、0.11 nats；混合质量预训练和下游指令微调任务上效果一致为中性至正向。

Conclusion: 偏差校正是一个实用且有效的机制，可减少有限样本更新偏差并提升预条件优化器性能，为优化器设计提供了理论基础和实践指导。

Abstract: Preconditioned optimizers are central to language model training, but their stochastic update rules are usually treated as direct approximations to population preconditioned descent. We show that this view misses two finite-sample biases. First, the gradient and preconditioner are typically estimated from the same minibatch, introducing gradient--preconditioner coupling bias. Second, even when the preconditioner estimate is unbiased, its inverse or inverse-root is generally biased because inversion is nonlinear. We propose a single-batch bias-correction framework that addresses both effects: cross-fitted preconditioning estimates the numerator and preconditioner from independent microbatch groups, while variance-corrected inversion uses microbatch variability to subtract the leading delta-method bias term. The framework applies to diagonal moment, diagonal curvature, and matrix preconditioning methods, instantiated in AdamW, Sophia, and Shampoo. Bias correction reduces held-out pretraining loss on Qwen2.5-0.5B by $0.15$, $0.07$, and $0.11$ nats, respectively; the effects on mixed-quality pretraining and downstream instruction tuning are consistently neutral-to-positive. Together, these results establish bias correction as a practical mechanism for reducing finite-sample update bias and improving the performance of preconditioned optimizers.

</details>


### [144] [Cumulative Meta-Learning from Active Learning Queries for Robustness to Spurious Correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20771)
*Kin Whye Chew,Jingxian Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CAML通过在主动学习框架中引入累积归纳偏置来解决虚假相关性问题。与传统方法仅更新似然项不同，CAML利用元学习捕捉主动学习轮次间的序列依赖关系，在多个虚假相关基准数据集上实现了最高27.8%~29.9%的少数群体准确率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型易受虚假相关(spurious correlations)影响，在虚假相关基准下表现不佳。标准主动学习方法仅追加查询样本更新似然项，在过参数化模型中这些信息性样本的影响会被稀释。同时，现有元学习方法将任务视为独立同分布，忽略了主动学习轮次间的时序依赖关系。

Method: 提出累积主动元学习(CAML)框架：将每轮主动学习建模为元学习任务，当前标注集作为元训练数据进行模型适配，新查询批次作为元测试数据评估泛化能力。通过维护并逐步细化的累积归纳偏置(Cumulative Inductive Bias)捕捉轮次间的序列依赖。理论上证明该累积形式引入了交互项，将早期元学习归纳偏置与后期查询目标耦合。

Result: 在Dominoes、Waterbirds、SpuCo和CivilComments四个虚假相关基准数据集上，CAML均优于基线方法。少数群体准确率提升分别为：Dominoes 27.8%、Waterbirds 29.9%、SpuCo 14.3%、CivilComments 24.0%。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模主动学习轮次间的序列依赖，CAML能有效引导模型关注核心特征而非虚假相关模式。该框架为解决分布外泛化和公平性问题提供了一种新的主动学习范式。

Abstract: Spurious correlations in real-world datasets cause machine learning models to rely on irrelevant patterns, undermining reliability, generalization, and fairness. Active learning offers a promising way to address this failure mode by querying informative samples that distinguish core features from spurious ones. However, standard active-learning methods simply append queried examples to the labeled set, effectively updating only the likelihood term. In deep learning regimes, the influence of these informative samples can be diluted by the larger labeled set and memorized by overparameterized models. We propose Cumulative Active Meta-Learning (CAML), an active-learning framework that uses queried examples to meta-learn the prior, or inductive bias, governing how the model adapts. CAML casts each active-learning round as a meta-learning task: the current labeled set serves as meta-train data for adaptation, while the newly queried batch serves as meta-test data for evaluating generalization. Unlike conventional meta-learning, which treats tasks as independent and identically distributed, CAML exploits the sequential dependence between active-learning rounds by maintaining a cumulative inductive bias that is progressively refined. Theoretically, we show that this cumulative formulation introduces interaction terms that couple earlier meta-learned inductive biases with later query-induced objectives, capturing dependencies absent from standard meta-learning. Empirically, CAML improves minority-group accuracy across spurious-correlation benchmarks and acquisition strategies, with gains of up to 27.8% on Dominoes, 29.9% on Waterbirds, 14.3% on SpuCo, and 24.0% on CivilComments.

</details>


### [145] [Learning to Think in Physics: Breaking Shortcut Learning in Scientific Diffusion via Representation Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20780)
*Haozhe Jia,Pengyu Yin,Wenshuo Chen,Shaofeng Liang,Lei Wang,Bowen Tian,Xiucheng Wang,Nanqian Jia,Yutao Yue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: REPA-P是一种无教师、架构无关的框架，通过在中间层附加轻量投影头并施加PDE残差损失，实现物理约束从输出层向中间层的扩展，显著提升收敛速度、物理保真度和分布外鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理信息扩散模型仅对最终输出施加PDE约束，导致中间表示缺乏约束，在边界条件偏移时容易产生捷径学习（shortcut learning）。

Method: REPA-P采用teacher-free、architecture-agnostic的架构，通过在选定层附加轻量级1×1投影头将隐藏激活解码为物理量，并在训练时施加PDE残差损失；推理时丢弃这些投影头，实现零开销。

Result: 在Darcy流、拓扑优化、静电势和湍流通道流四种PDE任务中，REPA-P实现最高2倍收敛加速、66.4%物理残差 reduction和49.3%分布外鲁棒性提升，对U-Net和Diffusion Transformer backbone均有效。消融实验表明，仅监督少量中间层即可获得大部分收益。

Conclusion: REPA-P通过轻量化投影头实现中间层监督，在四种PDE任务上均表现优异，且对U-Net和Diffusion Transformerbackbone均有效。代码已开源于GitHub。

Abstract: Physics-informed diffusion models typically enforce PDE constraints only on final outputs, leaving intermediate representations unconstrained and prone to shortcut learning under shifted boundary conditions. We introduce **REPA-P**, a teacher-free, architecture-agnostic framework that aligns intermediate features with physical states using first-principles residuals. REPA-P attaches lightweight $1{\times}1$ projection heads to selected layers, decodes hidden activations into physical quantities, and applies PDE residual losses during training. These heads are discarded at inference, introducing **zero overhead**. Across four PDE tasks, including Darcy flow, topology optimization, electrostatic potential, and turbulent channel flow, REPA-P accelerates convergence by up to $2{\times}$, reduces physics residuals by up to $66.4\%$, and improves out-of-distribution robustness by up to $49.3\%$, with consistent gains on both U-Net and Diffusion Transformer backbones. Ablations show that supervising a small set of intermediate layers captures most benefits and complements output-level physics losses. Code is available at [https://github.com/Hxxxz0/REPA-P](https://github.com/Hxxxz0/REPA-P).

</details>


### [146] [You Only Need Minimal RLVR Training: Extrapolating LLMs via Rank-1 Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21468)
*Zhepei Wei,Xinyu Zhu,Wei-Lin Chen,Chengsong Huang,Jiaxin Huang,Yu Meng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现RLVR权重轨迹具有极低秩且高度可预测的特性，90%以上的性能提升可由秩-1近似捕获。提出RELEX方法，通过观察窗口估计秩-1子空间并线性外推，以15%训练步骤达到完整RLVR效果，并能外推10-20倍于观察窗口的范围。成功源于秩-1投影的去噪效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR已成为提升LLM推理能力的主流范式，但其权重轨迹的几何特性未被充分探索。

Method: RELEX：利用短观察窗口估计秩-1子空间，通过线性回归外推未来检查点，无需训练模型。

Result: RELEX在三个模型（Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B、Qwen3-4B-Base、Qwen3-8B-Base）的域内和域外基准测试中均达到或超越RLVR性能，仅需15%训练步骤即可。

Conclusion: RELEX通过仅需15%的RLVR训练步骤即可达到相当甚至更好的性能，且能外推10-20倍于观察窗口的范围。其成功归因于秩-1投影的"去噪"效应。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has become a dominant paradigm for improving reasoning in large language models (LLMs), yet the underlying geometry of the resulting parameter trajectories remains underexplored. In this work, we demonstrate that RLVR weight trajectories are extremely low-rank and highly predictable. Specifically, we find that the majority of downstream performance gains are captured by a rank-1 approximation of the parameter deltas, where the magnitude of this projection evolves near-linearly with training steps. Motivated by this, we propose a simple and compute-efficient method RELEX (REinforcement Learning EXtrapolation), which estimates the rank-1 subspace from a short observation window and extrapolates future checkpoints via linear regression, with no learned model required. Across three models (i.e., Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B, Qwen3-4B-Base, and Qwen3-8B-Base), RELEX produces checkpoints that match or exceed RLVR performance on both in-domain and out-of-domain benchmarks, requiring as few as 15% steps of full RLVR training. Remarkably, RELEX is able to extrapolate far beyond the observation window at no training cost, predicting checkpoints up to 10-20$\times$ beyond the observed prefix with continued improvement (e.g., observe only the first 50 steps and extrapolate to 1000 steps). Our ablation analysis confirms the minimalist sufficiency of RELEX: neither increasing the subspace rank nor employing non-linear modeling yields further gains in extrapolation. Finally, we show that RELEX's success stems from a "denoising" effect: by projecting updates onto the rank-1 subspace, the model discards stochastic optimization noise that would otherwise degrade performance during extrapolation. Our code is available at https://github.com/weizhepei/RELEX.

</details>


### [147] [Causal Machine Learning Is Not a Panacea: A Roadmap for Observational Causal Inference in Health](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20782)
*Donna Tjandra,Trenton Chang,Sonali Parbhoo,Rajesh Ranganath,Andre Kurepa Waschka,William Mitchell,Maggie Makar,Shalmali Joshi,Finale Doshi-Velez,Leo Anthony Celi,Jenna Wiens*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将因果机器学习应用于观察性临床数据的路线图，强调因果假设验证的重要性，并指出因果ML的局限性常被低估，需谨慎建模以避免偏倚结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机对照试验（RCT）面临实施困难，而大规模观察性临床数据集日益丰富，研究者热衷于用法因果ML进行因果推断，但其局限性尚未得到广泛认可，可能导致偏倚或误导性结果。

Method: 概述了在观察性数据中应用因果ML的流程，强调需在可用数据内评估因果假设的有效性，并为临床专家和缺乏临床经验的ML从业者分别提供指导。

Result: 指出尽管因果ML取得进展，但其局限性在不同学科中仍普遍被低估，这种知识差距可能影响研究结果的有效性。

Conclusion: 因果ML可成为生成因果假设的有力工具；文章提供模板以增强因果分析的严谨性和可解释性，但必须满足因果假设并合理说明建模选择。

Abstract: Objective: The growing availability of large-scale observational clinical datasets and challenges in conducting randomized controlled trials have spurred enthusiasm in using causal machine learning (ML) for causal inference in observational data. We present a roadmap for applying causal ML to observational data. Materials and methods: We outline the importance of assessing validity assumptions within available data and applying causal ML responsibly for clinical experts using causal ML and ML practitioners with limited clinical expertise. Observations: Despite advances in causal ML, its limitations remain largely under-appreciated across disciplines. This gap in shared knowledge may impact the validity of findings. Discussion: Causal assumptions must be satisfied and modeling choices justified. Otherwise, these approaches risk producing biased or misleading results, with consequences for clinical research and patient care. Conclusion: Causal ML can be a powerful tool for generating causal hypotheses. We provide a template to strengthen the rigor and interpretability of causal analyses.

</details>


### [148] [Beyond Numerical Features: CNN-Driven Algorithm Selection via Contour Plots for Continuous Black-Box Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20797)
*Yiliang Yuan,Xiang Shi,Mustafa Misir*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于轮廓图可视化的实例级算法选择新方法，利用CNN回归器从探测景观的等高线图中学习预测各求解器的性能表现，在BBOB基准上显著优于单最优求解器，并与特征基线方法具有竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有连续优化算法选择主要依赖数值特征（如ELA特征和DeepELA嵌入），本文探索一种互补的视觉表征方法，即通过探测景观的等高线图来捕捉空间结构信息，为算法选择提供新的表征视角。

Method: 构建包含两个关键技术环节：（1）生成实例特定的轮廓图可视化；（2）设计CNN回归模型接收多视图轮廓图（堆叠或逐视图编码后聚合），预测各求解器的性能，通过预测最优值实现算法选择。在BBOB 2009单目标协议上评估，并扩展至DeepELA设置下的双目标场景。

Result: 在BBOB 2009单目标基准上，提出的选择器显著优于单最优求解器（SBS），并与基于特征的基线方法具有竞争力。DeepELA设置下的双目标评估进一步表明，图像化方法在使用窗口化轮廓视图时同样具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 简单的视觉模型能够利用探测景观中的空间结构进行算法选择，无需手工设计的ELA特征，验证了轮廓图作为一种互补表征在算法选择问题上的有效性。

Abstract: The present paper introduces a new representation-driven approach to per-instance algorithm selection, applied to black-box optimization, for automatically choosing the most promising solver from a fixed portfolio. Prior work in continuous optimization largely relies on numerical descriptors, including Exploratory Landscape Analysis features and learned embeddings such as Deep-ELA. This work studies a complementary representation: contour-map visualizations of probed landscapes. A CNN regressor takes multiple instance-specific contour views (stacked or encoded per view and aggregated) and predicts per-solver performance, enabling selection by the predicted best value. On the standard BBOB 2009 single-objective protocol, the resulting selectors significantly outperform the single best solver (SBS) and are competitive with feature-based baselines. A subsequent bi-objective evaluation under the DeepELA setting further indicates that the same image-based principle can be competitive when using windowed contour views. Overall, the results suggest that simple vision models can exploit spatial structure in probed landscapes for algorithm selection without handcrafted ELA features.

</details>


### [149] [Tunable MAGMAX: Preference-Aware Model Merging for Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20803)
*Kei Hiroshima,Kento Uchida,Shinichi Shirakawa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Tunable MAGMAX，一个基于模型合并的持续学习框架，通过引入偏好向量实现任务性能的可控调节，使模型能够适应不同的部署环境和用户偏好需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续学习方法主要关注所有任务的平均性能，缺乏对不同部署环境或用户偏好的灵活适配能力。用户在不同场景下可能需要不同任务性能权重，但现有方法无法提供这种细粒度控制。

Method: 提出Tunable MAGMAX框架，核心创新包括：(1) 引入偏好向量控制从各任务向量中选择元素的权重，实现任务性能的动态调节；(2) 提出基于少量目标环境数据和训练任务数据集自动构建偏好向量的方法，无需人工指定偏好参数。

Result: 在持续学习基准任务上的实验表明，Tunable MAGMAX能够有效控制任务级性能，并成功适配多种目标部署环境。在性能上达到或超越基线方法，验证了框架的实用性和有效性。

Conclusion: Tunable MAGMAX为持续学习模型的部署提供了一种灵活实用的解决方案，能够根据不同环境和偏好需求调整模型性能，无需重新训练即可实现任务性能的动态权衡。

Abstract: Continual learning (CL) aims to train models sequentially on multiple tasks while mitigating catastrophic forgetting of previously learned knowledge. Recent advances in large pre-trained models (LPMs) and model merging techniques, such as MAGMAX, have demonstrated effective CL performance by combining task-specific parameters. However, existing methods primarily focus on average performance across all tasks and do not adequately address how to construct models accommodating different deployment environments or varying user preferences. This paper proposes a model merging framework, termed Tunable MAGMAX, which enables preference-aware control of task-specific performance in CL. Our method introduces a preference vector that controls the number of elements selected from each task vector during model merging, allowing us to adjust the merged model performance according to their deployment needs. We further propose a method for automatically constructing appropriate preference vectors by leveraging small amounts of target environment data and datasets from model training tasks, thereby eliminating the need for manual specification. The experimental result on CL benchmark tasks demonstrates that Tunable MAGMAX effectively controls task-wise performance and successfully adapts merged models to various target environments. The proposed Tunable MAGMAX achieves superior or comparable performance to baseline methods, making it a practical solution for deploying CL models to various environments where the preferences of each task performance differ.

</details>


### [150] [Markovian Circuit Tracing for Transformer State Dynamic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20824)
*Abdullah X*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出了MCT（马尔可夫电路追踪）方法，用于测试transformer激活是否包含粗粒度状态转换结构。通过使用已知真实值的合成HMM任务基准测试，发现tiny causal transformers能学习接近贝叶斯最优的预测器，残差激活包含部分贝叶斯信念信息，状态抽象可恢复粗粒度转换信号，且状态修补显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Many sequence computations are better understood as movement through internal states rather than isolated local circuits. There is a need to test whether transformer activations contain coarse state-transition structure, which requires controlled benchmarks with known ground truth for evaluation.

Method: Markovian Circuit Tracing (MCT): a diagnostic pipeline that uses synthetic Hidden Markov Model (HMM) tasks with known ground truth (latent states, transition matrices, Bayesian belief vectors, Bayes-optimal predictions, and forced-state counterfactual targets). State abstractions are extracted from residual activations and tested via state forcing with recovered-state centroid patching.

Result: 1. Tiny causal transformers achieve near-Bayes-optimal performance (mean excess loss: 0.0138); 2. Residual activations contain partial Bayesian belief information; 3. State abstractions recover coarse transition signal, strongest in persistent and lower-state regimes, weaker in ambiguous-emission and six-state regimes; 4. State forcing via recovered-state centroid patching reduces KL divergence to exact HMM counterfactual from 0.1957 to 0.0532, outperforming all control conditions.

Conclusion: MCT provides a controlled benchmark and evaluation framework for transformer state-dynamics interpretability, serving as a simple reference pipeline for studying whether transformer activations contain coarse state-transition structure.

Abstract: Many sequence computations are easier to study as movement through internal states than as isolated local circuits. We introduce Markovian Circuit Tracing (MCT), a diagnostic pipeline for testing whether transformer activations contain coarse state-transition structure. The benchmark uses synthetic Hidden Markov Model (HMM) tasks where latent states, transition matrices, Bayesian belief vectors, Bayes-optimal predictions, and forced-state counterfactual targets are known exactly. Across six HMM families and three seeds per family, tiny causal transformers learn near-Bayes next-token predictors, with mean excess loss over Bayes of 0.0138. Residual activations contain partial Bayesian belief information in this controlled synthetic benchmark. State abstractions extracted from these activations recover coarse transition signal, strongest in persistent and lower-state regimes, and weaker in ambiguous-emission and six-state regimes. The clearest result comes from state forcing. Patching a recovered-state centroid reduces KL to the exact HMM counterfactual target from 0.1957 in the unpatched model to 0.0532 on average, beating wrong-state, mean-activation, random-activation, and shuffled-label controls. The contribution is a controlled benchmark and evaluation framework for transformer state-dynamics interpretability, with MCT as a simple reference pipeline

</details>


### [151] [Finite-Time Regret Analysis of Retry-Aware Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20854)
*Bingkui Tong,Junpei Komiyama,Soichiro Nishimori,Paavo Parmas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了针对retry-aware目标优化的随机老虎机算法ReMax。该算法选择最大化M次虚拟抽取后验期望最大奖励的采样分布。对于M=2的高斯奖励场景，论文证明了首个次线性遗憾界，并揭示了欠估计效应这一ReMax特有的现象——这解释了为何ReMax比Thompson采样更具利用性且分析更复杂。实验验证了欠估计程度与性能的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将强化学习中用于不确定性下探索的retry-aware目标（pass@k, max@k）应用于多臂老虎机问题，验证其在随机老虎机中是否具有良好 regret 性质。

Method: 对于M=2的高斯奖励场景，通过期望改进平衡条件刻画最优ReMax分布；引入饱和行为与欠估计效应的分离分析框架。关键证明技术需处理欠估计导致的采样偏差问题。

Result: 证明了首个ReMax的次线性遗憾上界；发现ReMax存在特有的欠估计效应（最优臂在不利估计后被采样过少）；实证表明轻度欠估计下ReMax优于KL-UCB和TS，后验方差缩放可缓解严重欠估计。

Conclusion: ReMax可被证明实现次线性遗憾，且分析揭示了“欠估计效应”——最优臂在不利估计后可能被采样过少，这解释了为何ReMax比Thompson采样更具利用性且其遗憾分析技术上更复杂。实验表明，在轻度欠估计条件下ReMax性能优于KL-UCB和Thompson采样，后验方差缩放可缓解严重欠估计。

Abstract: We study a stochastic bandit algorithm motivated by retry-aware objectives that value the best outcome among multiple attempts, such as pass@$k$ and max@$k$. Given a posterior over arm values, ReMax chooses a sampling distribution that maximizes the posterior expected maximum reward over $M$ virtual draws. Although this objective was introduced in reinforcement learning as an exploration mechanism under uncertainty, its regret properties in bandit problems have remained unclear. For Gaussian rewards and the first nontrivial case $M=2$, we characterize the optimal ReMax distribution through an expected-improvement balance condition and prove the first sublinear regret bound for ReMax. Our analysis separates the usual saturation behavior of suboptimal arms from a ReMax-specific underestimation effect, in which the optimal arm may be sampled too rarely after an unfavorable estimate. This explains why ReMax can be more exploitative than Thompson sampling (TS) and why its regret analysis is technically delicate. Experiments support this picture: ReMax often outperforms KL-UCB and Thompson sampling under mild underestimation, while posterior-variance scaling empirically mitigates severe underestimation.

</details>


### [152] [Runtime-Certified Bounded-Error Quantized Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20868)
*Dean Calver*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种分层KV缓存架构，通过在GPU内存存储INT8键和INT4值，同时在系统RAM保留FP16原值，结合在线计算的逐头逐步误差界和自适应精度选择，实现运行时可认证的注意力计算，保证在需要时精确恢复到FP16参考输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KV缓存量化可降低长上下文LLM推理的内存成本，但引入近似误差，现有系统仅依赖经验性验证，缺乏运行时检测或故障恢复机制，无法保证计算正确性。

Method: 设计分层KV缓存架构：GPU内存存储INT8键和INT4值，系统RAM保留FP16原值用于确定性回退。采用两项误差分解方法，计算键量化导致的注意力分布畸变和值重建误差的逐头逐步界。基于这些在线计算的误差界驱动自适应精度选择和多级回退阶梯，保证在必要时精确恢复到密集FP16注意力输出。

Result: 在LLaMA 3.1-8B、128K上下文长度的PG-19、NIAH和RULER基准上，语言建模和检索任务的质量与密集FP16 KV相当，同时恢复了朴素INT8/INT4基线中的灾难性故障。短上下文值敏感任务存在压缩保真度的可控权衡，可通过更严格的值容差或FP16值回退消除。

Conclusion: 该方法将KV缓存量化重新定位为运行时验证的计算而非固定近似。认证是局部的（逐头、逐步），不保证端到端模型正确性，但确保每次注意力计算要么有界（相对于FP16参考），要么通过回退精确恢复。目标不是追求原始加速，而是实现在严格质量约束下安全部署激进KV压缩。

Abstract: KV cache quantization reduces the memory cost of long-context LLM inference, but introduces approximation error that is typically validated only empirically. Existing systems rely on average-case robustness, with no mechanism to detect or recover from failures at runtime. We present a tiered KV cache architecture that enables runtime-certified attention: INT8 keys and INT4 values are stored in GPU memory, while FP16 originals are retained in system RAM for deterministic fallback. A two-term error decomposition yields per-head, per-step bounds on (i) attention distribution distortion from key quantization and (ii) value reconstruction error. These bounds are computed online and used to drive adaptive precision selection and a multi-stage fallback ladder, which guarantees recovery to the exact dense attention output when required. Across PG-19, NIAH, and RULER benchmarks on LLaMA~3.1-8B with contexts up to 128K, the system matches dense FP16 KV quality within noise for language modelling and retrieval tasks, while recovering catastrophic failures observed in naive INT8/INT4 baselines. Value-sensitive tasks at short context expose a controlled trade-off between compression and fidelity, which can be eliminated via tighter value tolerances or FP16-value fallback. The certification is local (per-head, per-step) and does not guarantee end-to-end model correctness, but ensures that each attention computation is either bounded relative to an FP16 reference or exactly recovered via fallback. This reframes KV cache quantization as a runtime-verified computation rather than a fixed approximation. The goal is not raw speedups, but enabling safe deployment of aggressive KV compression under strict quality constraints.

</details>


### [153] [CIG: Exploration via Conditional Information Gain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20878)
*Tim Joseph,Marcus Fechner,Philipp Stegmaier,Karam Daaboul,J. Marius Zöllner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出条件信息增益(CIG)奖励，通过对数行列式目标和集成disagreement kernel的Cholesky分解，解决了强化学习中探索奖励的不同条件集组合问题，在12个任务中验证了其优越性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有探索奖励方法存在局限性：终身奖励忽略回合内冗余，阶段性奖励忽略终身进步，混合方法需要高斯过程动态模型无法扩展到高维状态空间，且轨迹级信息增益对深度模型不可处理。

Method: 推导出CIG奖励作为可处理替代方案：基于集成 disagreement kernel 的对数行列式目标，其Cholesky分解产生因果每步奖励，同时保留两种条件集（重放缓冲区和回合前缀），可扩展到高维状态空间。在基于模型的设置中实例化。

Result: 在12个任务（离散MiniGrid和连续控制OGBench）中，在干净和随机干扰设置下，CIG优于或匹配先前的探索方法。

Conclusion: CIG通过Cholesky分解实现因果每步奖励，在高维状态空间中保持可扩展性，在离散(MiniGrid)和连续控制(OGBench)共12个任务中优于或匹配先前的探索方法，并展现出对随机干扰物的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Intrinsic rewards for exploration in reinforcement learning condition on different contexts: lifelong rewards score each transition against accumulated experience but ignore within-rollout redundancy; episodic rewards penalize intra-trajectory repetition but discard lifetime progress. Hybrid methods combine both signals through heuristic weights or require Gaussian-process dynamics that do not scale beyond low-dimensional state spaces. Trajectory-level information gain decomposes into per-step terms that condition on the replay buffer and rollout prefix simultaneously, but remains intractable for deep models. We derive the Conditional Information Gain (CIG) reward as a tractable surrogate: a log-determinant objective over an ensemble disagreement kernel whose Cholesky factorization yields causal per-step rewards that retain both conditioning sets while scaling to high-dimensional state spaces. We instantiate CIG in a model-based setting, where rollouts are short and within-rollout corrections remain largely unexplored. Across twelve tasks spanning discrete (MiniGrid) and continuous control (OGBench), in both clean and stochastic-distractor settings, CIG outperforms or matches prior exploration methods while remaining robust to stochastic distractors.

</details>


### [154] [NeighborDiv: Training-free Zero-shot Generalist Graph Anomaly Detection via Neighbor Diversity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20879)
*Kaifeng Wei,Teng Liu,Liang Dong,Xiubo Liang,Yuke Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NeighborDiv proposes a novel Neighbor-to-Neighbor Diversity Paradigm for Generalist Graph Anomaly Detection (GGAD), replacing the dominant Node-to-Neighbor Consistency approach with a training-free method that uses inter-neighbor feature similarity variance as the anomaly signal. It achieves state-of-the-art performance with zero training dependency and zero performance volatility.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing Generalist GAD (GGAD) methods predominantly rely on the Node-to-Neighbor Consistency Paradigm for anomaly quantification, which suffers from: (1) complex training pipelines, (2) heavy training data dependency, (3) high computational costs, and (4) unstable cross-domain generalization.

Method: NeighborDiv shifts from the Node-to-Neighbor Consistency Paradigm to the Neighbor-to-Neighbor Diversity Paradigm. It quantifies neighbor diversity via the variance of inter-neighbor feature similarities, capturing how a node organizes its local graph environment. As a training-free framework, it operates independently of conventional node-to-neighbor consistency mechanisms.

Result: Under two standard GGAD evaluation paradigms, NeighborDiv achieves state-of-the-art performance: (1) SDIT setting: 10.25% AUC improvement and 17.78% AP improvement over the second-best baseline; (2) UMDT setting: 6.89% AUC and 9.58% AP improvement. Notably, it yields zero performance volatility across all datasets.

Conclusion: NeighborDiv establishes a lightweight, highly practical GGAD framework that eliminates training-set dependency. It achieves zero performance volatility across all datasets, offering a robust alternative to training-dependent methods.

Abstract: Graph Anomaly Detection (GAD) is increasingly shifting to Generalist GAD (GGAD) for cross-domain "one-for-all" detection, but existing GGAD methods predominantly rely on the neighbor consistency principle, falling into the \textbf{Node-to-Neighbor Consistency Paradigm} for anomaly quantification. These methods suffer from complex training pipelines, heavy training data dependency, high computational costs, and unstable cross-domain generalization. To address these limitations, we propose NeighborDiv, a training-free generalist graph anomaly detection framework based on neighbor diversity. Departing from the dominant Node-to-Neighbor Consistency Paradigm, we shift the focus to the \textbf{Neighbor-to-Neighbor Diversity Paradigm}, and uncover that the internal structural dispersion of a node's neighbor set is a powerful, independently discriminative anomaly signal. We quantify neighbor diversity via the variance of inter-neighbor feature similarities, which captures how a node organizes its local graph environment, and operates independently of conventional node-to-neighbor consistency frameworks. Extensive experiments under two standard GGAD evaluation paradigms show NeighborDiv achieves state-of-the-art performance, with relative gains of 10.25% in average AUC and 17.78% in average AP over the second-best baseline under Single-Domain Independent Training (SDIT), and 6.89%/9.58% in AUC/AP under Unified Multi-Domain Training (UMDT), respectively. Notably, NeighborDiv yields zero performance volatility across all datasets, eliminating training-set dependency and establishing a lightweight and highly practical GGAD framework.

</details>


### [155] [Learning fMRI activations dictionaries across individual geometries via optimal transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20883)
*Sonia Mazelet,Rémi Flamary,Bertrand Thirion*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Dictionary learning is a powerful tool for creating interpretable representations. When applied to functional magnetic resonance imaging (fMRI) data, the resulting patterns of brain activity can be used for various downstream tasks, such as brain state classification or population-level analysis. However, a major challenge is the variability in brain geometry across individuals. This is usually addressed by projecting each individual brain geometry onto a common template, which removes subject-specific information. In this work, we introduce a novel approach to dictionary learning on fMRI data that explicitly accounts for this variability. We use the optimal transport-based Fused Gromov-Wasserstein (FGW) distance to compare graphs with different geometries and features. To address the challenge of computing multiple FGW distances for large graphs such as those arising from fMRI data, we rely on amortized optimization to learn a neural network that predicts an approximation of the optimal transport plans, which substantially reduces the computational cost. Additionally, we learn dictionary atoms that depend on the FGW trade-off parameter, which controls the balance between feature alignment and structural consistency. Numerical experiments on the HCP dataset demonstrate that the proposed approach captures different levels of geometric variability in the data and provides representations that preserve essential information.

</details>


### [156] [Training distribution determines the ceiling of drug-blind cancer sensitivity prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20885)
*Taekyung Heo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究揭示精准肿瘤学中药物敏感性预测停滞的真正原因——标准评估指标(global Pearson r)存在缺陷，而非药物特征表示不足。通过重新设计评估指标(per-drug Pearson r)并进行对照实验，证明将作用机制(MoA)作为训练分布约束而非特征，可有效提升预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精准肿瘤学中药物盲敏感性预测的性能停滞不前，研究者普遍认为是药物表示不够复杂所致，但本文质疑这一假设，试图找出性能停滞的真正原因。

Method: 1) 在四个独立数据集上比较全局Pearson r与药物内Pearson r指标；2) 设计对照实验，将MoA身份作为药物特征或训练分布约束两种方式进行比较；3) 测试机制分层训练和响应匹配两种部署策略。

Result: 全局Pearson r被药物效力差异主导，简单的药物均值预测器即可捕获；per-drug Pearson r显示现有药物编码均不优于仅使用细胞特征的模型；MoA作为特征几乎无效，但作为训练分布约束可显著提升靶向激酶抑制剂的预测性能。

Conclusion: 药物盲敏感性预测的性能瓶颈源于评估指标的人为因素，而非特征表示不足。机制分层训练和响应匹配是恢复预测能力提升的可行策略。

Abstract: Precision oncology requires predicting which drugs will suppress a specific tumor from its molecular profile, but drug-blind sensitivity prediction has plateaued despite increasingly complex drug representations. Here we show that this stagnation reflects a metric artifact rather than a representational bottleneck. The standard benchmark, global Pearson r, is dominated by between-drug potency differences that a trivial drug-mean predictor captures without any cell-specific learning. Per-drug Pearson r, which isolates within-drug cell ranking, reveals that no drug encoding improves over cell-only features across four independent datasets. A controlled experiment channeling mechanism-of-action identity as either a drug feature or a training-distribution constraint identifies the cause. Supplying MoA as a feature yields negligible benefit, whereas using it to stratify training raises per-drug r substantially for targeted kinase inhibitors, because pan-cancer co-training suppresses pathway-specific sensitivity signals. Mechanism-stratified training and response matching from pilot observations provide two deployable strategies that together recover the principal sources of predictive gain in drug-blind sensitivity prediction.

</details>


### [157] [Choose Wisely and Privately: Proactive Client Selection for Fair and Efficient Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20975)
*Adda Akram Bendoukha,Heber Hwang Arcolezi,Nesrine Kaaniche,Aymen Boudguiga*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种主动式客户端选择框架，通过差分隐私 contingency 表计算互信息来量化跨特征相关性，在训练前寻找数据效用和公平性达到最优平衡的客户端联盟。实验表明该方法在四个基准数据集上优于均匀采样和最先进的自适应聚合策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于平均的联邦学习受非独立同分布(non-IID)数据限制，导致收敛速度慢和模型精度下降。现有方法存在效率低下问题——噪声或高度异构数据客户端的梯度计算被丢弃或大幅降权，造成计算资源浪费、更多通信轮次和不必要的隐私暴露。传统方法是被动的，本文认为应主动在训练前选择最优客户端组合。

Method: 1. 利用差分隐私 contingency 表计算互信息，量化联合数据集中跨特征相关性；2. 提出潜在联邦损失(Potential Federation Loss, PFL)函数，同时最大化数据效用并确保公平的跨特征相关性以防止群体不公平；3. 将客户端选择表述为 PFL 目标上的最优子集搜索问题；4. 使用模拟退火算法在强差分隐私保证下求解。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上的实验表明，通过最优联邦训练的模型具有更快的收敛速度、更公平的跨特征相关性和更高的准确率，优于均匀采样以及先进的自适应聚合或采样策略。

Conclusion: 通过主动的客户端选择而非被动的聚合权重调整，可以更高效地构建联邦学习系统，在保证差分隐私的前提下实现更快的训练、更公平的模型和更高的准确率。该框架为非IID数据环境下的联邦学习提供了一种有前景的解决方案。

Abstract: Federated Learning enables collaborative model training across decentralized data sources without data transfer. Averaging-based FL is limited by the presence of non-IID data, which negatively impacts convergence speed and final model accuracy. Conventional alternatives suffer from significant inefficiency. Clients with noisy or highly heterogeneous data contribute expensive gradient computations that are either discarded or heavily down-weighted before aggregation. These reactive approaches waste computational resources, require more communication rounds and result in unnecessary privacy exposure. In this paper, we propose a proactive client selection framework that aims to find an optimal federation of clients whose combined data match utility and fairness requirements before training begins. Our method relies on mutual information computed from differentially private contingency tables to quantify the relevance of cross-feature correlations in the union dataset. We introduce a Potential Federation Loss (PFL) over the set of fixed-size federations, which balances two objectives. Maximizing collective data utility while ensuring fair cross-features correlations to prevent group unfairness. Client selection is expressed as an optimal subset search problem over the PFL objective, which we solve using simulated annealing under strong differential privacy guarantees for clients' local statistics. Experimental results on four benchmarks show faster, fairer, and more accurate models trained on optimally found federations, compared to uniform sampling, even when state-of-the-art adaptive aggregation or sampling strategies are employed.

</details>


### [158] [A Sharper Picture of Generalization in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20988)
*Paul Lintilhac,Sair Shaikh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从PAC-Bayes理论角度研究Transformer在布尔函数上的泛化行为，证明稀疏低阶谱函数可构造平坦最小值并获得非平凡泛化界，并通过经验评估和机械可解释性研究验证理论构建的现实性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究（Edelman等2022; Trauger和Tewari 2024）使用Rademacher复杂度推导泛化界，存在一定局限性。本文探索通过PAC-Bayes理论获得泛化界的新路径，以更深入理解Transformer的泛化机制。

Method: 1. 证明稀疏谱（集中在低阶分量）可实现平坦最小值构造；2. 对理想化低锐度学习器应用PAC-Bayes界；3. 进行经验评估和机械可解释性研究验证理论构建在真实Transformer中的现实性。

Result: 成功获得非平凡（non-vacuous）的泛化界，验证了稀疏谱函数与良好泛化性能之间的关联。

Conclusion: PAC-Bayes理论可有效用于分析Transformer在布尔域上的泛化行为，稀疏谱集中于低阶分量是实现平坦最小值和良好泛化的关键因素。

Abstract: We study transformers' generalization behavior on boolean domains from the perspective of the Fourier Spectra of their target functions. In contrast to prior work (Edelman et al., 2022; Trauger and Tewari, 2024), which derived generalization bounds from Rademacher complexity, we investigate the feasibility of obtaining generalization bounds via PAC-Bayes theory. We show that sparse spectra concentrated on low-degree components enable low-sharpness constructions with good generalization properties. Our idea is to show the existence of flat minima implementing any boolean function of sparsity no greater than the context length, and then apply a PAC-Bayes bound to an idealized low-sharpness learner, resulting in a non-vacuous generalization bound. We evaluate predictions empirically and conduct a mechanistic interpretability study to support the realism of our theoretical construction in real transformers.

</details>


### [159] [Modeling Temporal scRNA-seq Data with Latent Gaussian Process and Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20989)
*Mehmet Yigit Balik,Harri Lähdesmäki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究针对单细胞RNA测序数据中时间进程推断的挑战，提出了一种结合异方差高斯过程、最优传输和细胞特异性时间建模的生成式框架，有效解决了现有方法的过拟合问题，并实现了最先进的轨迹推断性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从单细胞RNA测序的静态快照数据推断时间进程是一个基础性挑战，当前基于神经微分方程和流的方法对过拟合敏感，且缺乏对生物变异性的仔细考虑。

Method: 提出生成式框架，使用Hilbert空间方法近似潜在异方差高斯过程(GP)建模人群趋势；利用最优传输(OT)目标对齐生成与观测的人群分布；通过整合细胞特异性潜在时间和细胞类型条件来捕获生物异质性，从而解耦时间异步性和到不同细胞类型的轨迹。

Result: 在复杂插值和外推基准测试中展示了最先进的性能，并引入了一种新颖的基于梯度的策略用于推断扰动轨迹。

Conclusion: 该方法成功解决了过拟合问题，并在复杂插值和外推基准测试中展示了最先进的性能，同时引入了用于推断扰动轨迹的梯度策略，为单细胞时间进程推断提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Single-cell RNA sequencing provides insights into gene expression at single-cell resolution, yet inferring temporal processes from these static snapshot measurements remains a fundamental challenge. Current approaches utilizing neural differential equations and flows are sensitive to overfitting and lack careful considerations of biological variability. In this work, we propose a generative framework that models population trends using a latent heteroscedastic Gaussian process (GP) approximated by Hilbert space methods. To address the absence of genuine cell trajectories, we leverage an optimal transport (OT) objective that aligns generated and observed population distributions. Our method explicitly captures biological heterogeneity by incorporating cell-specific latent time and cell type conditioning to disentangle temporal asynchrony and trajectories to different cell types. We demonstrate state-of-the-art performance on complex interpolation and extrapolation benchmarks and introduce a novel gradient-based strategy for inferring perturbation trajectories.

</details>


### [160] [Beyond the Bellman Recursion: A Pontryagin-Guided Framework for Non-Exponential Discounting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20996)
*Hojin Ko,Jeonggyu Huh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对非指数折扣场景下Bellman递归失效的问题，提出PG-DPO框架，通过变分方法结合Pontryagin最大值原理和Monte Carlo rollout，实现有效策略优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Bellman-style递归在非指数折扣（如人类偏好和生存过程中的双曲折扣）下会失效。问题根源在于指数折扣依赖于乘性性和时间齐次性的脆弱平衡，破坏任一特性都会导致标准动态规划崩溃。

Method: 提出了Pontryagin引导的直接策略优化(PG-DPO)方法，将变分框架与Pontryagin最大值原理相结合，通过Monte Carlo rollout和Adjoint-MC投影实现逐点Hamiltonian最大化，摒弃了传统的Bellman递归结构。

Result: 在多维双曲折扣和生存折扣基准测试中，PG-DPO相比方程驱动求解器和基于critic的基线方法，在准确性和稳定性方面均有显著提升。

Conclusion: PG-DPO demonstrates superior performance on multi-dimensional hyperbolic and survival-discount benchmarks, showing improved accuracy and stability where equation-driven solvers and critic-based baselines fail to converge.

Abstract: Most value-based and actor--critic reinforcement learning methods rely on Bellman-style recursions, yet these recursions collapse under non-exponential discounting common in human preferences and survival processes. We show the breakdown is structural: exponential discounting sits at a fragile intersection of multiplicativity and time homogeneity, and violating either property breaks standard dynamic programming. To overcome this, we propose Pontryagin-Guided Direct Policy Optimization (PG-DPO), a variational framework that abandons recursion and couples the Pontryagin Maximum Principle with Monte Carlo rollouts via an Adjoint-MC projection enforcing pointwise Hamiltonian maximization. Across multi-dimensional hyperbolic and survival-discount benchmarks, PG-DPO improves accuracy and stability where equation-driven solvers and critic-based baselines diverge.

</details>


### [161] [A Dialogue between Causal and Traditional Representation Learning: Toward Mutual Benefits in a Unified Formulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21058)
*Yan Li,Yuewen Sun,Shaoan Xie,Gongxu Luo,Yunlong Deng,Kun Zhang,Guangyi Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个统一的形式化框架，将表征学习解构为任务组件和约束组件，以弥合因果表征学习与传统表征学习之间的鸿沟，实现双向优势互补，并通过对CausalVerse数据集的实验验证了任务设计对因果约束效果的关键影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果表征学习(CRL)与传统表征学习因发展轨迹不同而存在鸿沟：传统方法由应用驱动、关注经验目标，而CRL侧重理论问题尤其是可辨识性。这种关注点差异导致术语、问题形式化和评估方法的分歧，限制了跨领域交流并造成工作重复或脱节。

Method: 提出统一形式化框架，将表征学习分解为任务组件（指定学习表征需保留的信息）和约束组件（在潜在空间施加的结构），从而在统一框架下整合两种学习范式。

Result: 在CausalVerse数据集上的实验表明，不同任务组件与结构化约束的组合对CRL方法的行为产生显著影响，因果约束的有效性高度依赖于其配套任务的设计。

Conclusion: 提出了统一框架来弥合因果表征学习与传统表征学习之间的鸿沟，通过任务组件和约束组件的统一形式化，实现了双向互利——因果表征学习提供理论工具理解结构化潜在约束的价值，传统表征学习则提供任务设计和目标选择的实践洞察。实验结果表明因果约束的有效性高度依赖于其配套任务的选择。

Abstract: Causal representation learning (CRL) and traditional representation learning have largely developed along different trajectories. Traditional representation learning has been driven mainly by applications and empirical objectives, whereas CRL has focused more on theoretical questions, particularly identifiability. This difference in emphasis has created a gap between the two fields in terminology, problem formulation, and evaluation, limiting communication and sometimes leading to disconnected or redundant efforts. In this paper, we argue that these two fields should be brought into dialogue rather than treated as separate paradigms. To this end, we introduce a unified formulation in which the representation learning is characterized by two components: a task component, which specifies what information the learned representation is required to preserve, and a constraint component, which specifies what structure is imposed on the latent space. Under this formulation, the benefits run in both directions. CRL provides theoretical tools for understanding when structured latent constraints are useful or necessary, while traditional representation learning offers practical insights on task design and objective choice that can improve the development of CRL methods. To illustrate this interaction, we experimentally study how different task components affect the behavior of CRL methods under different structured constraints. Results on CausalVerse show that the effectiveness of causal constraints depends strongly on the tasks with which they are paired.

</details>


### [162] [Divide et Calibra: Multiclass Local Calibration via Vector Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21060)
*Cesare Barbera,Lorenzo Perini,Giovanni De Toni,Andrea Passerini,Andrea Pugnana*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于向量量化的组合校准方法，通过结构化划分潜在空间和跨区域参数共享，解决多分类模型校准中异质性误差捕获与信息保留的难题，实现局部校准性能提升而不牺牲全局性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多类校准方法存在局限性：全局方法假设校准误差在潜在空间中均匀分布，而局部方法通常依赖潜在空间降维，导致信息丢失。因此需要一种既能捕捉异质性校准误差又能避免信息损失的方法。

Method: 通过向量量化(VQ)诱导表示空间的结构化划分，结合基于码字的Dirichlet浓度参数化实现跨区域参数共享，构建区域特定的校准映射。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在局部校准方面显著提升，同时在全局校准和预测性能上保持竞争力，即使在潜在空间稀疏区域也有良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: The proposed VQ-based compositional calibration approach achieves significant improvements in local calibration while maintaining competitive global calibration and predictive performance across benchmark datasets.

Abstract: Accurate and well-calibrated Machine Learning (ML) models are mandatory in high-stakes settings, yet effective multiclass calibration remains challenging: global approaches assume calibration errors are homogeneous across the latent space, while local methods often rely on latent-space dimensionality reduction, which leads to information loss. To address these issues, we propose a compositional approach to multiclass calibration, where region-specific calibration maps are constructed from shared codeword-dependent factors. We instantiate this idea via Vector Quantization (VQ), which induces a structured partition of the representation space, and an indexed parameterization of Dirichlet concentrations that enables parameter sharing across regions. Our approach learns heterogeneous calibration maps that generalize well even to sparse regions of the latent space. Experiments on benchmark datasets show significant improvements in local calibration while maintaining competitive global calibration and predictive performance.

</details>


### [163] [Robust Personalized Recommendation under Hidden Confounding in MNAR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21066)
*Zongyu Li,Wanting Su,Tianyu Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Recommender systems often rely on observational user--item interaction data, which is prone to selection bias due to users' selective interactions with items. Inverse propensity weighting and doubly robust estimators effectively mitigate selection bias under observed confounding, but are unreliable in the presence of hidden confounders. Existing approaches relying on randomized controlled trials (RCTs) or global sensitivity bounds are constrained in practice: RCTs demand costly experimental data, while global sensitivity bounds presume a uniformly bounded effect of unmeasured confounders on propensities through sensitivity analysis, thereby neglecting heterogeneity across user--item interactions. To overcome this limitation, we propose a novel framework, which estimates user--item level sensitivity bounds, thereby substantially relaxing the homogeneity assumption inherent in global sensitivity bounds named Personalized Unobserved-Confounding-aware Interaction Deconfounder (PUID). To ensure both robustness and predictive accuracy, we further develop an adversarial optimization strategy and propose a benchmark-guided variant (BPUID) that incorporates pre-trained models as stabilizing references. Extensive experiments on three real-world datasets demonstrate that our approach significantly outperforms global methods under hidden confounding, without requiring RCT data.

</details>


### [164] [Towards Understanding Self-Pretraining for Sequence Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21070)
*Omar Coser,Loredana Zollo,Paolo Soda,Antonio Orvieto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究复制并系统消融了Amos等人(2024)的自预训练(SPT)方法，发现SPT的核心改进源于让模型学习位置邻近交互，而非深度或泛化本身；在简化理论设置中进一步证明，标签监督对某些注意力分数方向存在局部盲区，而掩码重建可以检测到这些方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Amos等人(2024)展示了通过自预训练(SPT)可提升Transformer在序列分类任务上的准确率，但未解释其机制。本研究旨在回答两个核心问题：SPT如何驱动优化到更好的解？标准监督训练为何会在Transformer中失败？

Method: 复制Amos等人(2024)的实验并进行系统性消融实验，使用最小化设置来隔离关键因素。

Result: 核心瓶颈不是深度或泛化本身，而是标签监督从随机初始化学习有效的query-key注意力模式的能力。通过最小化设置发现，将绝对位置编码转化为邻近偏差注意力分数是SPP改进的关键来源。

Conclusion: 标签监督对某些注意力分数方向存在局部盲区，而掩码重建可以检测到这些方向，解释了SPT相比标准监督训练的优势。

Abstract: Amos et al. (2024) showed that the accuracy of Transformer models in sequence classification can be significantly improved by first pretraining with a masked token prediction objective without external data or augmentation, a procedure referred to as self-pretraining (SPT). While the primary objective of Amos et al. (2024) was to showcase that Transformers can achieve strong performance on the Long-Range Arena (LRA), their pipeline raises more fundamental questions: How does SPT drive optimization to better solutions? Why can standard supervised training fail in Transformers? To better understand this, we replicate and systematically ablate the findings of Amos et al. (2024). Our ablations suggest that a central bottleneck in the studied settings is not depth or generalization alone, but the ability of label supervision to learn useful query-key Attention patterns from random initialization. With a minimal setup, we identify learning proximity interactions - turning absolute positional encodings into proximity-biased Attention scores - as a key source of the improvements brought by SPT. Finally, in a simplified theoretical setup, we show that label supervision can be locally blind to certain Attention-score directions that are instead detectable through masked reconstruction.

</details>


### [165] [Reviving Error Correction in Modern Deep Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21088)
*Minh Hoang Nguyen,Dai Do,Huu Hiep Nguyen,Dung Nguyen,Kien Do,Hung Le*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对深度时间序列预测中自回归推理的误差累积问题，提出一种架构无关、免重训练的通用误差校正模型UEC-STD，通过季节性-趋势分解分别校正各分量，在多种模型和数据集上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代深度学习模型在时间序列预测中面临长期预测性能下降的问题，根本原因在于自回归推理中的误差累积。传统统计方法中的误差校正机制（ECM）难以有效应用于深度学习模型。

Method: 提出UEC-STD方法，通过将预测分解为趋势和季节性分量，分别对各分量进行校正，实现误差修正。

Result: 在4种 backbone 和10个数据集上显著提升了校正精度和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: UEC-STD能够有效提升多种深度预测模型的长期预测精度，同时保持架构无关性和免重训练的特性。

Abstract: Modern deep-learning models have achieved remarkable success in time-series forecasting. Yet, their performance degrades in long-term prediction due to error accumulation in autoregressive inference, where predictions are recursively used as inputs. While classical error correction mechanisms (ECMs) have long been used in statistical methods, their applicability to deep learning models remains limited or ineffective. In this work, we revisit the error accumulation problem in deep time-series forecasting and investigate the role and necessity of ECMs in this new context. We propose a simple, architecture-agnostic error correction model that can be integrated with any existing forecaster without requiring retraining. By explicitly decomposing predictions into trend and seasonal components and training the corrector to adjust each separately, we introduce the Universal Error Corrector with Seasonal-Trend Decomposition (UEC-STD), which significantly improves correction accuracy and robustness across 4 backbones and 10 datasets. Our findings provide a practical tool for enhancing forecasts while offering new insights into mitigating autoregressive errors in deep time-series models. Code is available at https://github.com/DA2I2-SLM/UEC-STD.

</details>


### [166] [UOTIP: Unbalanced Optimal Transport Map for Unpaired Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21094)
*Donggyu Lee,Taekyung Lee,Jaewoong Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出UOTIP方法，通过非平衡最优传输学习从噪声测量分布到干净信号分布的映射，用于解决非配对图像逆问题。该方法通过放松边际约束获得对多级噪声的鲁棒性和类别不平衡的自适应性，理论上证明二次代价项可确保传输映射的存在唯一性，实验表明其性能达到最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决非配对图像逆问题这一具有挑战性的场景，其中训练时只有独立的非配对噪声测量集和干净目标信号集可用。

Method: 通过融入基于似然的代价函数，将重建任务（从噪声测量预测干净信号）表述为学习从噪声测量分布到干净信号分布的UOT映射，并通过放松精确边际约束来处理问题。

Result: UOTIP在非配对图像逆问题基准测试中达到了最优性能，适用于线性和非线性逆问题；UOT框架提供了对多级观测噪声的鲁棒性、对噪声和干净数据集之间类别不平衡的自适应能力，以及对多样化噪声类型场景的泛化能力。

Conclusion: UOTIP在非配对图像逆问题基准测试中实现了最先进的性能，覆盖线性和非线性逆问题场景。

Abstract: We investigate unpaired image inverse problems, a challenging setting where only independent, non-paired sets of noisy measurements and clean target signals are available for training. We propose a novel inverse problem solver based on Unbalanced Optimal Transport, called Unbalanced Optimal Transport Map for Inverse Problems (UOTIP). Our method formulates the reconstruction task, predicting clean target signals from noisy measurements, as learning a UOT Map from noisy measurement distribution to clean signal distribution by incorporating a likelihood-based cost function. By relaxing the exact marginal constraint, the UOT framework provides key advantages to our model: robustness to multi-level observation noise, adaptability to class imbalance between noisy and clean datasets, and generalizability to diverse noise-type scenarios. Furthermore, we theoretically demonstrate that incorporating a quadratic cost term ensures the existence and uniqueness of the transport map by satisfying the twist condition, even for ill-posed inverse problems. Our experiments demonstrate that UOTIP achieves state-of-the-art performance on unpaired image inverse problem benchmarks, across linear and nonlinear inverse problems.

</details>


### [167] [A Typed Tensor Language for Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21103)
*Theofilos Mailis,Kalliopi-Christina Despotidou,Konstantinos Filippopolitis,Yannis Foufoulas,Thanasis-Michail Karampatsis,Andreas Ktenidis,Evdokia Mailli,Theodore Papamarkou,Yannis Ioannidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于联邦学习的带类型张量语言，通过区分客户端分区的联邦张量与全局共享张量，形式化了一轮程序的编码-合并-解码结构。核心贡献是共享状态分解理论：所有可分解程序都等价于通过固定维度共享状态进行通信的协议，且维度与客户端数量和记录数无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习和分析协议通常被描述为独立协议集合，缺乏统一的形式化框架。论文旨在为这些协议建立统一的类型化数学基础，解决通信复杂性与表达能力的理论刻画问题。

Method: 引入带类型的张量语言，区分沿记录轴分区的联邦张量（federated tensors）与全局共享张量（shared tensors）；通过虚拟全局张量定义语义；建立共享状态分解理论，论证typed one-round程序可分解为客户端本地张量表达式与跨客户端合并操作的组合。

Result: 证明了typed one-round程序可通过固定维度共享状态进行分解，共享状态维度与客户端数量和记录数无关；建立了逆表示定理，表明满足表达性条件的分解都对应于typed one-round程序；开发了可微分片段，支持服务器端梯度下降和共享线性代数二阶更新。

Conclusion: 该框架为"可经由固定维度共享状态进行通信"的一类广泛联邦学习计算提供了完整的形式化刻画，统一了联邦学习中的多种协议，并揭示了通信结构与表达能力之间的数学联系。

Abstract: Federated learning and analytics are often described as collections of separate protocols, even when they share the same mathematical form: client-local tensor computation, mergeable aggregation into shared state, and shared-only post-processing. We introduce a typed tensor language that formalizes this structure. The language distinguishes federated tensors, whose records are partitioned across clients along a tracked record axis, from shared tensors, which are available globally. Its semantics are defined by comparison with a virtual global tensor, used only as a reference object. The main result is a shared-state factorization theory. We show that typed one-round programs factor through fixed-dimensional shared state whose size is independent of the number of clients and records, computed from client-local tensor expressions and merged across clients. We also prove a converse representability result; factorizations whose encoders and decoders are expressible in the language are realized by typed one-round programs, and the correspondence extends to iterative programs whose cross-round state is shared. This gives a formal account of the computations in the language that can be expressed as encode, merge, and decode procedures. We then develop a differentiable fragment for learning. If a per-record loss and its per-record gradient are represented by client-local tensor expressions, the global gradient is represented by record-axis summation of the federated gradient tensor. This yields typed iterative programs for server-side gradient descent and shared-linear-algebra second-order updates. The framework characterizes a broad class of federated learning computations whose communication passes through fixed-dimensional shared state.

</details>


### [168] [Improved Guarantees for Constrained Online Convex Optimization via Self-Contraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21107)
*Dhruv Sarkar,Abhishek Sinha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了约束在线凸优化(COCO)问题，针对强凸和凸损失函数分别提出了新的投影算法，在保持最优regret的同时，将约束违反(CCV)从$O(\sqrt{T \log T})$和$O(\sqrt{T} \log T)$分别大幅降低至$O(\log T)$和$O(\sqrt{T})$，其中对强凸损失函数实现了指数级改进。核心创新在于利用了自收缩曲线的几何性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有COCO算法在regret和CCV之间存在权衡瓶颈：强凸损失下CCV为$O(\sqrt{T \log T})$，凸损失下CCV为$O(\sqrt{T} \log T)$。论文旨在打破这一限制，同时获得最优regret和更小的CCV。

Method: 提出一种简单的基于投影的算法。关键创新是引入自收缩曲线(self-contracted curves)的几何结果，用于分析和设计算法。

Result: 强凸损失：$O(\log T)$ regret + $O(\log T)$ CCV（CCV指数级改进）；凸损失：$O(\sqrt{T})$ regret + $O(\sqrt{T})$ CCV（去除对数因子）。

Conclusion: 该工作首次在对强凸损失函数实现指数级CCV改进，核心贡献——自收缩曲线的几何结果——可能具有独立的理论价值。

Abstract: We consider Constrained Online Convex Optimization (COCO) with adversarially chosen constraints. At each round, the learner chooses an action before observing the loss and constraint function for that round. The goal is to achieve small static regret against the best point satisfying all constraints while also controlling cumulative constraint violation ($\mathsf{CCV}$). For strongly convex losses, state-of-the-art algorithms achieve $O(\log T)$ regret and $O(\sqrt{T \log T})$ $\mathsf{CCV}.$ The corresponding best-known bounds for convex losses is $O(\sqrt{T})$ regret and $O(\sqrt{T} \log T)$ $\mathsf{CCV}$. In this paper, we give a simple projection-based algorithm that simultaneously achieves $O(\log T)$ regret and $O(\log T)$ $\mathsf{CCV}$ for strongly-convex losses, yielding an exponential improvement in the $\mathsf{CCV}$. For the convex losses, our algorithm improves the $\mathsf{CCV}$ to $O(\sqrt{T})$ while maintaining the optimal $O(\sqrt{T})$ regret. The key to our improvement is a recent geometric result for self-contracted curves, which may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [169] [A Unified Framework for Uncertainty-Aware Explainable Artificial Intelligence: A Case Study in Power Quality Disturbance Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21114)
*Yinsong Chen,Samson S. Yu,Zhong Li,Chee Peng Lim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: This paper formalizes "explanation distribution" for Bayesian neural networks and proposes UA-RAO, a family of uncertainty-aware attribution operators. The framework transforms BNN posterior through Lipschitz attribution operators, enabling principled uncertainty quantification in explainable AI. Evaluated on power quality disturbance classification, results show that deep ensembles with mean UA-RAO enhance localization, while other summaries expose uncertainty patterns invisible to deterministic methods.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Post-hoc XAI methods produce deterministic attribution maps, but Bayesian neural networks induce a distribution over explanations. Capturing variability of this distribution is crucial for uncertainty-aware decision-making, yet this aspect remains underexplored in existing literature.

Method: Formalizes explanation distribution as push-forward measure of BNN posterior through Lipschitz-continuous attribution operators; proposes UA-RAO family (mean, variance, coefficient of variation, quantiles, set-theoretic aggregation); provides Monte Carlo accessibility and Wasserstein approximation bounds; evaluates on 15-class PQD classification benchmark comparing 3 BNN approximations × 3 attribution operators using relevance mass accuracy and IoU metrics.

Result: Deep ensembles with mean UA-RAO improve localization over deterministic baselines; other UA-RAO summaries reveal uncertainty patterns absent from point-estimate attributions; qualitative results on measured signals suggest generalization beyond synthetic training distribution.

Conclusion: Deep ensembles with mean UA-RAO improves localization over deterministic baselines; other UA-RAO summaries reveal uncertainty patterns not visible in point-estimate attributions; framework is domain-agnostic and applicable to any BNN with Lipschitz-continuous attribution operators.

Abstract: Post-hoc explainable AI (XAI) methods typically produce deterministic attribution maps, whereas Bayesian neural networks (BNNs) induce a distribution over explanations. Capturing the variability of this distribution is important for uncertainty-aware decision-making. This paper formalises the \emph{explanation distribution} as the push-forward measure of the BNN posterior through any Lipschitz-continuous attribution operator. It further proposes the uncertainty-aware relevance attribution operator (UA-RAO), a general family of operators that summarises the explanation distribution using the mean, variance, coefficient of variation, quantiles, and set-theoretic aggregation measures. Theoretical support is provided through Monte Carlo accessibility and Wasserstein approximation bounds. The framework is evaluated on a 15-class power quality disturbance (PQD) classification benchmark, comparing three BNN approximations paired with three attribution operators using relevance mass accuracy and intersection-over-union as localisation metrics. Results show that deep ensembles with the mean UA-RAO improve localisation over the deterministic baseline, while other UA-RAO summaries reveal uncertainty patterns absent from point-estimate attributions. Qualitative results on measured signals further suggest that these patterns generalise beyond the synthetic training distribution. The framework is domain-agnostic and can be applied to any BNN paired with a Lipschitz-continuous attribution operator.

</details>


### [170] [Advantage Collapse in Group Relative Policy Optimization: Diagnosis and Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21125)
*Xixiang He,Qiyao Sun,Ao Cheng,Xingming Li,Xuanyu Ji,Hailun Lu,Runke Huang,Qingyong Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GRPO算法存在优势崩塌问题（同类奖励组导致梯度消失），本文提出ACR指标量化这一问题，并设计AVSPO方法通过注入虚拟奖励样本解决优势崩塌，在0.5B-14B模型上均取得4-6%的精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO suffers from advantage collapse where homogeneous rewards within a group produce near-zero advantages and vanishing gradients, leading to training stagnation.

Method: AVSPO injects virtual reward samples guided by real-time ACR monitoring to enable learning from homogeneous groups without additional model rollouts.

Result: ACR strongly predicts training stagnation and final performance. AVSPO reduces advantage collapse by 58-63% relative to GRPO and yields consistent accuracy gains of 4-6 percentage points.

Conclusion: AVSPO significantly reduces advantage collapse by 58-63% and improves accuracy by 4-6 percentage points across all model scales (0.5B-14B), while maintaining generalization to out-of-domain tasks.

Abstract: Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), a prominent algorithm within the Reinforcement Learning from Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) framework, has achieved strong results in improving the reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs). However, GRPO is prone to advantage collapse, a failure mode where homogeneous rewards within a group (e.g., all correct or all incorrect answers) yield near-zero advantages and vanishing gradients. To address this, we introduce the Advantage Collapse Rate (ACR), the first diagnostic metric quantifying the proportion of training batches with ineffective gradients. Across models from 0.5B to 14B parameters on mathematical reasoning benchmarks, we show that ACR strongly predicts training stagnation and final performance. We then propose Adaptive Virtual Sample Policy Optimization (AVSPO), a lightweight extension of GRPO that injects virtual reward samples, guided by real-time ACR monitoring, to enable learning from homogeneous groups without additional model rollouts. AVSPO reduces advantage collapse by 58-63% relative to GRPO and yields consistent accuracy gains of 4-6 percentage points across all model scales, while maintaining generalization on the evaluated out-of-domain task. Code and datasets are available at https://qingyonghu.github.io/AVSPO.

</details>


### [171] [PREFINE: Preference-Based Implicit Reward and Cost Fine-Tuning for Safety Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21225)
*Richa Verma,Bavish Kulur,Sanjay Chawla,Balaraman Ravindran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PREFINE enables safety alignment of pre-trained RL policies using preference feedback without retraining. By adapting DPO from LLM alignment to trajectory-level preferences in continuous control, it achieves 60%+ reduction in constraint violations while preserving reward performance, with superior efficiency over offline RL or imitation learning.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current approaches to making pre-trained RL policies safety-aware typically require full retraining or numerical cost encoding. A more general setting exists where safety constraints are provided as preferences (preferred low-cost vs dispreferred high-cost trajectories) rather than explicit numerical costs. There is a need for methods that can adapt language model alignment techniques like DPO to continuous control domains with trajectory-level preferences.

Method: PREFINE (Preference-based Implicit Reward and Cost Fine-Tuning) adapts Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) to sequential decision-making. The method constructs policy-sampled counterfactual trajectories to establish meaningful preference contrasts and jointly optimizes for reward retention and safety alignment. Unlike standard RLHF which operates at response level, this approach handles trajectory-level preferences in continuous control environments.

Result: Empirically validates that PREFINE reduces constraint violations and catastrophic failures by over 60% while maintaining original reward behavior. The method achieves low-cost, high-reward performance with significantly improved data and computational efficiency compared to full offline RL or imitation learning baselines.

Conclusion: PREFINE bridges preference alignment and safe policy adaptation in continuous domains, enabling efficient safety alignment without full retraining. Results show 60%+ reduction in constraint violations while maintaining reward performance, with significant improvements in data and computational efficiency over offline RL or imitation learning approaches.

Abstract: We address the problem of making a pre-trained reinforcement learning (RL) policy safety-aware by incorporating cost constraints without retraining it from scratch. While costs could be numerically encoded, we assume a more general setting is when costs are provided as preferences. Given a reward-optimized policy and a small dataset of preferred (low-cost) and dispreferred (high-cost) trajectories, our goal is to fine-tune the policy to generate low-cost behaviors while retaining high rewards. Unlike standard RLHF in language models, where preferences are defined over responses to the same prompt, our setting involves trajectory-level preferences in continuous control environments. We introduce PREFINE: Preference-based Implicit Reward and Cost Fine-Tuning for Safety Alignment which is a preference-based fine-tuning method that adapts Direct Preference Optimization (DPO), which is now widely used for LLM fine-tuning, to the sequential decision making setting. PREFINE constructs policy-sampled counterfactual trajectories to establish meaningful preference contrasts and jointly optimizes for reward retention and safety alignment. Empirically, PREFINE reduces constraint violations and catastrophic failures by over 60% while maintaining original reward behavior. PREFINE produces policies that achieve low-cost, high-reward performance with significantly improved data and computational efficiency compared to full offline RL or imitation learning, bridging preference alignment and safe policy adaptation in continuous domains.

</details>


### [172] [Reasoning-Trace Collapse: Evaluating the Loss of Explicit Reasoning During Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21127)
*Lukas Twist,Helen Yannakoudakis,Jie M. Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究表明，推理模型在微调后可能失去有效的推理过程（推理轨迹崩溃），但答案正确率可能保持不变，导致评估指标掩盖真实问题。通过结构化评估框架发现标准微调会快速抑制有效推理轨迹，但简单的损失掩码策略可有效缓解此问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前推理模型的训练与微调存在数据不匹配问题：显式推理模型在包含中间推理步骤的数据上训练，但下游微调常使用普通问答数据（不含推理轨迹）。这种不匹配可能导致推理轨迹崩溃，即模型输出正确答案但失去了结构有效的显式推理能力，而现有答案级指标无法检测此问题。

Method: 提出结构化评估框架，将答案正确性与推理轨迹有效性分离，测量有效、空缺、缺失和截断四类推理轨迹，同时评估基于推理的条件化任务表现。研究对四种开源推理模型进行实验分析，并探索损失掩码策略作为缓解方案。

Result: 标准监督微调会快速抑制有效推理轨迹的产生；在多种设置下，基于有效推理的条件性能保持较高，但有效推理率急剧下降，说明答案指标可能掩盖推理失败；简单的损失掩码策略可显著缓解推理轨迹崩溃问题。

Conclusion: 微调推理模型的评估应报告结构化推理可靠性指标，而非仅关注最终答案性能，尤其当适应数据不含显式推理轨迹时。损失掩码等简单策略可有效维持推理能力，无需依赖教师生成的推理轨迹。

Abstract: Explicit reasoning models are trained to produce intermediate reasoning traces before final answers, but downstream fine-tuning is often performed on ordinary instruction-response data that contains no such traces. We show that this mismatch can induce reasoning-trace collapse: a fine-tuned model continues to produce plausible final answers while losing the structurally valid explicit reasoning traces that made it a reasoning model in the first place. We introduce a structural evaluation framework that separates answer correctness from reasoning-trace validity, measuring valid, empty, missing, and truncated reasoning alongside reasoning-conditioned task performance. Using this framework, we study four open-weight reasoning models and find that standard supervised fine-tuning can rapidly suppress valid reasoning traces, and that answer-only metrics can substantially obscure this failure: in several settings, performance conditional on valid reasoning remains high while the rate of valid reasoning falls sharply. We further show that simple loss-masking strategies can substantially mitigate collapse without requiring teacher-generated reasoning traces. These results suggest that evaluations of fine-tuned reasoning models should report structural reasoning reliability metrics in addition to final-answer performance, especially when adaptation data does not contain explicit reasoning traces.

</details>


### [173] [OCTOPUS: Optimized KV Cache for Transformers via Octahedral Parametrization Under optimal Squared error quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21226)
*Mark Boss,Vikram Voleti,Simon Donné,Shimon Vainer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OCTOPUS是一种针对KV缓存的旋转预条件联合量化编解码器，通过八面体参数化对坐标三元组进行三维联合量化实现非均匀比特分配，在多种模态上达到最优性能，且支持零额外延迟的融合解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文自回归推理中，键值缓存主导了内存带宽和占用空间，需要高效压缩。

Method: 该方法基于旋转预条件框架，对旋转后的三维坐标进行联合量化。每个三元组的方向通过八面体参数化映射到正方形，对两个角度坐标和三元组范数分别进行Lloyd-Max量化，依据优化后的每三元组均方误差分配非均匀比特数。编解码器数据无关、在线且确定性（给定种子）。

Result: 在文本、视频、音频任务中，OCTOPUS在所有报告的比特宽度和指标上匹配或超越所有先前的旋转编解码器，且在极端压缩场景下优势增大。

Conclusion: OCTOPUS通过八面体参数化对旋转后的三维坐标进行联合量化，实现非均匀比特分配，在文本、视频、音频任务上超越所有先前的旋转编解码器。其Triton融合实现可在不解压缩的情况下重构键值，零额外延迟。

Abstract: The key-value (KV) cache dominates memory bandwidth and footprint in long-context autoregressive inference. Recent rotation-preconditioned codecs (TurboQuant, PolarQuant) show that a structured random rotation followed by a per-coordinate scalar quantizer matched to an analytically tractable marginal is a near-optimal recipe for KV compression. OCTOPUS advances this paradigm through joint quantization of rotated coordinate triplets. Each triplet's direction is mapped to a square via an octahedral parameterization, and the two resulting coordinates and the triplet norm are Lloyd-Max quantized against implementation-matched marginals. Optimizing the per-triplet squared error gives a strictly non-uniform bit allocation depending only on the total dimensionality of the keys. We find the finite-dimensional quality optimum with sweeps to be constant on every real decoder we test. The codec is data-oblivious, online, and deterministic given a seed. Across text, video, and audio, OCTOPUS matches or beats every prior rotation codec at every reported bit width and metric, with a lead that grows as bits drop for extreme compression. Furthermore, a fused Triton implementation reconstructs keys on the fly without materializing the uncompressed key, so the codec adds no decode-time bandwidth or latency over the existing dequantization. Project Page: https://octopus-quant.github.io/

</details>


### [174] [Learning First Integrals via Backward-Generated Data and Guided Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21160)
*Jingfeng Zhong,Zhengxiang Liu,Zhijie Wang,Shuai Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FISolver是一个基于大语言模型的首次积分求解器，通过后向生成算法解决训练数据稀缺问题，并结合监督微调和强化学习优化。在保持低计算成本的同时，显著超越大型数学LLM和商业求解器（如Mathematica），为首次积分的自动化发现提供了新的数据驱动路线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 首次积分发现对理解动力系统守恒定律具有基础科学意义，但现有符号计算工具和大语言模型受限于高质量训练数据稀缺和数学直觉依赖，难以有效求解此类问题。

Method: 提出三大核心技术：(1) 后向生成算法，从积分出发反向构建微分方程数据集；(2) 监督微调结合基于Levenshtein距离的强化学习塑形奖励；(3) 数据合成与混合策略支持稀疏样本到困难问题族的迁移。

Result: 实验表明FISolver在显著降低计算成本的同时，性能显著优于更大规模的数学LLM和Mathematica等商业求解器。

Conclusion: FISolver 为首次积分的自动化发现提供了一条新的数据驱动路线，证明了小而精的专用模型配合高质量合成数据策略，在特定数学任务上可超越通用大模型和商业软件。

Abstract: The discovery of first integrals is of fundamental scientific importance for understanding conservation laws in dynamical systems. However, existing symbolic computation tools and Large Language Models (LLMs) remain limited on this task because high-quality training data are scarce and successful solutions often depend on mathematical intuition. This paper presents FISolver, an LLM-based solver developed to address this challenge. First, we introduce a "Backward Generation" algorithm that systematically builds large-scale datasets of (differential equation, first integral) pairs by deriving differential equations from sampled integrals, thereby alleviating the data scarcity bottleneck. Second, we apply supervised fine-tuning to a compact mathematical model and further improve its performance through reinforcement learning with a Levenshtein Distance-based shaped reward. In addition, we design data synthesis and blending strategies that support effective adaptation to difficult problem families from sparse examples. Experiments show that FISolver, while requiring substantially lower computational cost, significantly outperforms larger mathematical LLMs and commercial solvers such as Mathematica on challenging benchmarks, indicating a new data-driven route for automated discovery of first integrals.

</details>


### [175] [How Much Online RL is Enough? Informative Rollouts for Offline Preference Optimization in RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21266)
*Richa Verma,Balaraman Ravindran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: G2D通过短期GRPO预热+静态偏好数据构建+离线DPO微调的三阶段流程，在大幅降低计算成本的同时实现超越完全在线GRPO的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO等在线RLVR方法需要持续生成rollout，计算成本高且难以扩展；DPO作为离线替代方案从冷SFT策略开始训练时通常性能落后于在线方法，导致离线-在线差距问题。

Method: 提出G2D三阶段流程：1) 短期GRPO预热生成多样化rollout；2) 从预热后的策略构建静态偏好数据集；3) 使用DPO进行离线微调。通过改变GRPO在线步数K，在两个7B规模模型上进行了系统性实验对比。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-7B上，G2D (K=150) 达到62.4% MATH-500精度，超出GRPO (51.6%) 10.8%，计算量仅为后者的1/4；在Llama-3.1-8B上，G2D (K=500) 同样超越GRPO。性能取决于偏好数据的信息量而非数量：适度预热产生校准良好的不确定性提供强对比信号，过度预热则导致过度自信策略和信息量下降。

Conclusion: 通过适度的在线RL预热和困难度校准的离线DPO微调，可以实现比完全在线RL更高效且性能相当甚至更优的推理训练方案，性能差距主要源于数据信息量而非偏好对数量。
