Abstract: Reinforcement Learning from Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has emerged as a powerful paradigm for reasoning in language models, with GRPO as its primary example. However, GRPO requires continuous online rollout generation, making it computationally expensive and difficult to scale. While Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) offers a stable and efficient offline alternative, it is typically expected to underperform w.r.t. online RL methods such as GRPO when trained on rollouts from a cold supervised fine-tuned (SFT) policy. We introduce G2D (GRPO to DPO)}, a three-stage pipeline that performs a short GRPO warm-up, constructs a static preference dataset, and fine-tunes a model offline with DPO. Across a set of values of the number of online steps (K) in GRPO on Qwen2.5-7B and Llama-3.1-8B, we find that offline DPO with moderate warm-up matches or outperforms GRPO at substantially lower compute cost in our setting. On Qwen2.5-7B, G2D at K=150 achieves 62.4% on MATH-500, outperforming GRPO (51.6%) by 10.8% at ~4x lower compute. On Llama-3.1-8B, G2D at K=500 achieves 49.4%, surpassing GRPO in our experimental setting. We show that performance is not governed by the number of preference pairs, which does not vary much w.r.t. K, but by their informativeness. Moderate warm-up produces rollouts with calibrated uncertainty, yielding stronger contrastive signal, while excessive warm-up leads to overconfident policies and less informative data. Our results recast the offline-online gap in RLVR as primarily a data informativeness problem, and identify short online RL warm-up with appropriate difficulty calibration of the fine-tuning dataset as a compute-efficient alternative to online RL.

</details>


### [176] [TimeSRL: Generalizable Time-Series Behavioral Modeling via Semantic RL-Tuned LLMs -- A Case Study in Mental Health](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21295)
*Yuang Fan,Lilin Xu,Millie Wu,Jingping Nie,Qingyu Chen,Yuzhe Yang,Zhuo Zhang,Xin Liu,Subigya Nepal,Xiaofan Jiang,Xuhai "Orson" Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TimeSRL通过两阶段LLM框架引入语义瓶颈，将原始时序数据转化为自然语言抽象，利用RLVR强化学习优化，在精神健康预测任务中实现了显著的跨队列泛化能力提升，为可泛化行为建模提供了新范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有纵向被动感知模型在跨数据集分布偏移下泛化能力不足。传统ML易过拟合队列特定伪影，LLM难以可靠处理长异构时序数据。核心假设是语义概念比原始数字更具泛化性。

Method: 提出TimeSRL框架，采用两阶段设计：第一阶段将原始传感信号抽象为高层自然语言；第二阶段基于这些抽象进行行为预测。框架通过GRPO+RLVR进行端到端优化，无需金标准中间标注，在精神健康预测任务上验证效果。

Result: 在严格的留一数据集out(LOSO)协议下，焦虑预测MAE较非LLM基线降低3.1-10.1%，较LLM基线降低9.5-44.1%；抑郁预测MAE较非LLM基线降低3.2-9.6%，较LLM基线降低27.4-57.6%（均p<0.05）。跨基准迁移性能优异，接近域内表现。

Conclusion: 语义抽象具有可复用性，RL微调的LLM是实现跨队列泛化行为建模的有效方向。TimeSRL通过引入显式语义瓶颈，将原始时序数据转化为高层自然语言描述，从而摆脱了对原始数值统计特征的依赖，在跨基准测试迁移中接近域内性能。

Abstract: Longitudinal passive sensing enables continuous health prediction, yet models often fail under cross-dataset distribution shifts. Traditional ML overfits cohort-specific artifacts, while Large Language Models (LLMs) struggle to reason reliably over long, heterogeneous time-series. We introduce TimeSRL, a two-stage LLM framework that routes predictions through an explicit semantic bottleneck. The model first abstracts raw signals into high-level natural language, then predicts behavioral outcomes from these abstractions alone. This forces the model to reason over semantic concepts that we argue generalize better than raw numbers. We optimize this process end-to-end using Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with Reinforcement Learning from Verifiable Rewards (RLVR), learning outcome-aligned abstractions without gold intermediate annotations. Instantiated on mental-health prediction, TimeSRL achieves state-of-the-art performance on a benchmark designed to stress-test cross-cohort generalization under a rigorous leave-one-dataset-out (LOSO) protocol, reducing mean absolute error (MAE) over strong non-LLM ML and LLM baselines by 3.1--10.1% and 9.5--44.1% for anxiety, and 3.2--9.6% and 27.4--57.6% for depression (all $p$s<0.05). TimeSRL significantly outperforms prior methods in cross-benchmark transfer across different sensing pipelines, rivaling its own within-domain performance without target-domain fine-tuning. These results demonstrate that semantic abstractions are reusable and point to a new direction for generalizable behavior modeling via RL-tuned LLMs.

</details>


### [177] [Divide and Contrast: Learning Robust Temporal Features without Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21241)
*Abdul-Kazeem Shamba,Kerstin Bach,Gavin Taylor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Di-COT是一种无需数据增强的自监督时序表示学习框架，通过对窗口内子结构进行随机划分对比，实现高效且准确的表示学习，在多项下游任务中取得最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时序自监督学习方法存在计算成本高或依赖不适用于多样时间动态的假设等问题，需要一种高效且通用的无监督框架。

Method: Di-COT通过随机划分窗口为少量重叠子块进行对比学习，避免了数据增强和多次编码器前向传播。其对比损失计算仅依赖于batch size和子块数量，与序列长度无关，从而实现高效扩展。

Result: 在6个大规模真实数据集以及UCR和UEA基准上，Di-COT在分类、聚类、kNN和跨数据集迁移任务上均达到最优性能，同时大幅降低训练时间。

Conclusion: Di-COT通过窗口内子结构对比的方式，能够学习语义结构化且可迁移的表示，代码已开源。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning for time-series representation aims to reduce reliance on labeled data while maintaining strong downstream performance, yet many existing approaches incur high computational costs or rely on assumptions that do not hold across diverse temporal dynamics. In this work, we introduce Divide and Contrast (Di-COT), an unsupervised framework that avoids data augmentation and multiple encoder passes by contrasting informative substructures within a window rather than individual timesteps. Di-COT stochastically partitions each window into a small number of overlapping sub-blocks per iteration, enabling efficient and meaningful contrast while mitigating false positives during temporal transitions. To further improve scalability, we adopt a contrastive objective whose computation depends on the batch size and the number of sub-blocks, making loss computation independent of sequence length. Extensive experiments on six large-scale real-world datasets, as well as the UCR and UEA benchmarks, demonstrate that Di-COT learns semantically structured and transferable representations, achieving state-of-the-art performance on classification, clustering, $k$NN, and cross-dataset transfer, while substantially reducing training time. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/sfi-norwai/Di-COT.

</details>


### [178] [Data-Efficient Neural Operator Training via Physics-Based Active Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21348)
*Alicja Polanska,Lorenzo Zanisi,Vignesh Gopakumar,Stanislas Pamela*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于物理的获取策略(physics-based acquisition)，这是一种结合偏微分方程残差指导数据选择的物理信息主动学习算法。该方法在1D Burgers方程和2D可压缩Navier-Stokes方程的数值实验中表现优于随机获取策略，达到与前沿方法相当的数据效率，同时具备将物理归纳偏置注入训练过程的独特优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Neural operators for PDEs face a critical bottleneck of high training data requirements, despite their computational cost advantages over traditional methods.

Method: The proposed physics-based acquisition algorithm leverages the partial differential equation residual as an acquisition function to guide iterative sample selection during training.

Result: In numerical experiments on 1D Burgers equation and 2D compressible Navier-Stokes equations, physics-based acquisition consistently outperforms random acquisition and achieves comparable data efficiency to state-of-the-art methods.

Conclusion: Physics-based acquisition provides a data-efficient approach for training neural operators on PDEs while embedding physics inductive bias, directing computational resources toward areas of weakest physical understanding.

Abstract: Solving partial differential equations with neural operators significantly reduces computational costs but remains bottlenecked by high training data requirements. Active learning offers a natural framework to mitigate this by selectively acquiring the most informative samples in an iterative manner. We introduce physics-based acquisition - a novel physics-informed active learning algorithm that leverages the partial differential equation residual to guide data selection. We validate the method by presenting numerical experiments for the 1D Burgers equation and the 2D compressible Navier-Stokes equations. We show that, in our experiments, physics-based acquisition consistently outperforms random acquisition and matches the state of the art in data efficiency. At the same time, it has the unique advantage of injecting a physics inductive bias into the training process, ensuring that simulation cost is spent where the model's physical understanding is weakest.

</details>


### [179] [On the Cost and Benefit of Chain of Thought: A Learning-Theoretic Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21260)
*Yue Zhang,Zhiyi Dong,Tommaso Cesari,Yongyi Mao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Paper provides a theoretical framework analyzing Chain of Thought (CoT) by decomposing reasoning risk into oracle-trajectory risk (benefits) and trajectory-mismatch risk (costs). Key findings: (1) TMR cost is unavoidable without structural stability; (2) under stability, error growth follows bounded/linear/exponential regimes controlled by amplification factors. Theory explains when CoT helps vs. hurts.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Develop a rigorous learning-theoretic framework to understand when Chain of Thought (CoT) reasoning helps or hurts in autoregressive models.

Method: Modeled CoT as interaction between an answer map and a chain rule that generates intermediate questions autoregressively; introduced reasoning risk decomposed into OTR (oracle-trajectory risk) and TMR (trajectory-mismatch risk).

Result: Tight canonical decomposition of reasoning risk; TMR is unavoidable without stability (can be arbitrarily large even with zero OTR); under stability, tight upper bound with amplification factors identifying bounded, linear, and exponential error-growth regimes.

Conclusion: Provides a complete theory predicting when CoT helps or hurts, with clear transition conditions governed by stability properties of the loss, answer map, and chain rule.

Abstract: We develop a learning-theoretic framework for understanding Chain of Thought (CoT). We model CoT as the interaction between an answer map and a chain rule that generates intermediate questions autoregressively, and define the reasoning risk of a hypothesis under this interaction. Our first result is a tight canonical decomposition of this risk into two terms with opposing roles: an oracle-trajectory risk (OTR), which captures the benefit of CoT and reduces to a target-domain risk in a domain adaptation problem, and a trajectory-mismatch risk (TMR), which captures the cost of CoT through error accumulation along mismatched reasoning trajectories. We then show that this cost is unavoidable without structure: if any one of the loss, the hypothesis answer map, or the chain rule lacks stability, the TMR can be arbitrarily large even when the OTR is zero and the hypothesis is uniformly close to the ground truth. Conversely, under stability, we prove a tight upper bound on the TMR governed by an exact amplification factor that identifies bounded, linear, and exponential error-growth regimes. Together, these results give a precise theory of when CoT helps, when it hurts, and what controls the transition between the two.

</details>


### [180] [Nonparametric Learning and Earning with One-Point Feedback under Nonstationarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21263)
*Xiangyu Yang,Feng Xu,Jian-Qiang Hu,Jiaqiao Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究非稳态环境下单价格反馈的动态定价问题，提出基于梯度近似的学习框架结合重启机制和元学习，实现对未知需求函数和环境变化的适应，并提供有限遗憾保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业依赖动态定价应对需求变化，但面临两个核心挑战：仅能从单一定价观察中学习收入-需求关系，以及市场条件可能逐渐或突然变化。现有方法多需假设特定参数形式的需求函数或已知环境变化程度。

Method: 1) 开发基于收入梯度近似的学习框架，利用每期单一观察构造梯度估计；2) 引入重启机制定期刷新学习过程，折扣过时信息；3) 当非稳态程度未知时，设计元学习层在多个重启策略间自适应对冲。

Result: 提供了性能保证，证明相对于完全信息基准的累计收入损失取决于时间跨度和市场变化幅度；合成数据和真实数据实验验证了方法有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架无需假设特定参数形式即可有效学习需求并适应环境变化，通过自适应重启策略在未知非稳态程度下仍能保持良好性能，为实际动态定价应用提供了理论基础和可操作方案。

Abstract: Firms increasingly rely on dynamic pricing to respond to evolving customer demand, yet in many applications they observe only the revenue generated by a single posted price in each period. At the same time, market conditions may shift gradually or abruptly due to changes in customer preferences, competition, or external shocks. These features create two intertwined challenges: learning the revenue--demand relationship from limited feedback and adapting pricing decisions to a changing environment. We study how a seller can learn and earn effectively under these constraints, without assuming a specific parametric form for demand. We develop a learning framework that updates prices using revenue-based gradient approximations constructed from one observation per period. To address environmental changes, we incorporate a restarting mechanism that periodically refreshes the learning process so that outdated information is discounted. When the degree of nonstationarity is unknown, we further introduce a meta-learning layer to adaptively hedge across multiple restarting schedules. We provide performance guarantees for our approach, showing how cumulative revenue loss relative to a fully informed benchmark depends on both the time horizon and the magnitude of market variation. Simulation experiments using synthetic and real-world data illustrate the effectiveness of the proposed procedures.

</details>


### [181] [On the Regularity and Generalization of One-Step Wasserstein-guided Generative Models for PDE-Induced Measures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21388)
*Likun Lin,Zhongjian Wang,Jack Xin,Zhiwen Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: The paper provides a theoretical foundation for one-step Wasserstein-guided generative models applied to PDE-induced probability measures, establishing Hölder regularity of optimal transport maps through doubling conditions and deriving excess-risk bounds with target-shift robustness.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Despite empirical success of generative models, existing statistical theory is largely pessimistic regarding their accuracy for scientific computing applications involving PDE-induced probability measures.

Method: Develop a theoretical framework combining doubling conditions for PDE-induced measures with optimal transport regularity theory to establish Hölder continuity of optimal transport maps; validate through DeepParticle experiments.

Result: Target measures satisfy doubling conditions; optimal transport map from uniform source to target is Hölder continuous; excess-risk bounds derived; robustness under target shift established; experiments support theoretical rates.

Conclusion: Provides theoretical justification for one-step generative models that learn PDE-induced distributions via single pushforward map, with excess-risk bounds for DeepParticle and robustness guarantees under target shift.

Abstract: Despite the remarkable empirical success of generative models, the available theory on their statistical accuracy in scientific computing remains largely pessimistic. This paper develops a theoretical framework for understanding the regularity of transport maps and the generalization properties of one-step Wasserstein-guided generative models for PDE-induced probability measures. We consider normalized target densities associated with linear elliptic and parabolic equations on bounded domains, as well as diffusion and Fokker--Planck equations on the torus. Under standard structural assumptions, we prove that these target measures satisfy doubling conditions. By combining this fact with regularity theory for optimal transport between doubling measures, we show that the optimal transport map from a uniform source measure to the target measure is Hölder continuous. This regularity yields an approximation-theoretic justification for one-step generative models that learn PDE-induced distributions via a single pushforward map. As a representative instance, we study DeepParticle and derive excess-risk bounds characterizing the discrepancy between the learned map and the population-optimal map. We also establish a robustness estimate under target shift and illustrate the theory with experiments which support the derived rates.

</details>

### [183] [Hypergraph Partitioning on GPU with Distinct Incident Hyperedges and Size Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20497)
*Marco Ronzani,Cristina Silvano*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对有限规模和独特入站超边约束下的超图划分问题，设计了充分利用GPU分层并行性的中心算法，通过在共享内存中批量处理节点对得分和链式节点移动验证实现高效并行，平均达到380倍加速，k=2时5倍加速且质量损失仅约5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超图划分是工程领域中的NP难问题，其在大规模场景下的高效求解依赖于并行化计算；现有方法难以充分利用GPU的分层并行架构，且在特定约束条件（有限规模和每个分区的独特入站超边）下缺乏有效支持。

Method: 提出一种GPU中心的多级超图划分算法，关键设计包括：(1) 在划分形成阶段，将超图关联结构和唯一邻域存储于内存，以利用集合稀疏性并在共享内存中批量计算节点对得分；(2) 在划分细化阶段，将节点移动链接为改善路径和循环，通过并行约简检查累积集合大小变化的合法性；(3) 主要内核的执行跨度与局部超图参数呈线性关系。

Result: 实验结果表明：相比串行多级划分器平均实现380倍加速且连接度降低1.2-2.0倍；在k=2平衡划分中运行速度提升5倍，质量损失仅约5%；在可比运行时间下性能优于现有GPU划分器，且新增约束处理逻辑无明显开销。

Conclusion: 提出的GPU算法在运行时实现380倍加速，同时连接度降低1.2-2.0倍；k=2时仅损失约5%质量即可达到5倍加速，且无明显的约束处理开销，证明了在特定问题约束下GPU加速的有效性。

Abstract: Hypergraph partitioning is a recurring NP-hard problem in engineering; its efficient solution at scale hinges on parallelism. This work proposes a GPU-centric algorithm for multi-level hypergraph partitioning aimed at a specific set of problem constraints: limited size and distinct inbound hyperedges per partition. Manipulating hypergraphs requires deeply nested traversals and concurrent decision-making; our constraints impose further set operations amidst that. In turn, we design algorithms around the GPU's hierarchical parallelism and our problem's specifics. When forming partitions, we materialize the hypergraph's incidence structure and unique neighborhoods in memory to exploit set sparsity and batch node-pairing scores in shared memory. Upon refining partitions, we chain node moves into improving paths and cycles, checking their validity via cumulative set size variations reduced in parallel over moves. Thus, our dominant kernels exhibit a span linear in local hypergraph parameters. Results show an average 380x speedup and a 1.2-2.0x reduction in connectivity compared to a sequential multi-level partitioner. With minor changes, we also support k-way balanced partitioning, running 5x faster than CPU methods with a ~5% quality loss for k=2, outperforming an existing GPU partitioner at comparable runtime, with no measurable overhead from the added constraints handling logic.

</details>


### [184] [Llamas on the Web: Memory-Efficient, Performance-Portable, and Multi-Precision LLM Inference with WebGPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20706)
*Reese Levine,Rithik Sharma,Nikhil Jain,Abhijit Ramesh,Zheyuan Chen,Neha Abbas,James Contini,Tyler Sorensen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: LlamaWeb is a WebGPU backend for llama.cpp that enables memory-efficient, performance-portable LLM inference in the browser through static memory planning, tunable kernels, and templated quantization kernels. It reduces memory by 29-33% and improves throughput by 45-69% compared to existing browser-based frameworks, while remaining competitive with native backends.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Running LLMs in the browser offers compelling advantages (efficiency, privacy, portability), but is fundamentally limited by constrained browser memory and heterogeneous hardware — existing solutions fail to adequately address both challenges simultaneously.

Method: Three core technical strategies: (1) Static memory planning and efficient model loading to reduce memory overhead; (2) A tunable kernel library to handle cross-device hardware variability; (3) Templated GPU kernels supporting diverse quantization formats for broad model support and extensibility.

Result: Memory reduction: 29-33% less memory across device/browser/OS combinations; Throughput: 45-69% decode throughput increase on four GPUs from separate vendors; Compatibility: competitive with or superior to vendor-specific backends on certain devices; Evaluated across 16 devices, 8 vendors, 10 language models, and 4 quantization formats.

Conclusion: LlamaWeb is a practical and effective solution for browser-based LLM inference, significantly outperforming existing approaches in both memory efficiency (29-33% reduction) and throughput (45-69% improvement) while maintaining broad model compatibility and competitive performance against native backends.

Abstract: Running language models in the browser presents a unique opportunity to build efficient, private, and portable AI applications, but requires contending with constrained memory availability and heterogeneous hardware targets. To realize this opportunity, we present Llamas on the Web (LlamaWeb), a WebGPU backend for llama.cpp that enables memory-efficient and performance-portable LLM inference across a wide range of model weight formats in the browser. Our design significantly reduces memory overhead through static memory planning and efficient model loading, addresses cross-device variability through a tunable kernel library, and introduces templated GPU kernels that support performant implementations of numerous quantization formats, enabling broad model support and extensibility to new formats.
  We evaluate LlamaWeb on 16 devices from 8 vendors, collecting data from 10 language models and four model weight formats. We compare LlamaWeb against existing browser-based LLM frameworks and find that LlamaWeb requires 29-33% less memory across several combinations of device, browser, and operating system. We also evaluate LlamaWeb's performance against these frameworks and find that it increases decode throughput by 45-69% across four GPUs from separate vendors. In addition, we compare LlamaWeb's performance against other llama.cpp backends, where it is competitive with and even beats vendor-specific backend performance on some devices.

</details>


### [185] [PlexRL: Cluster-Level Orchestration of Serviceized LLM Execution for RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20863)
*Yiqi Zhang,Fangzheng Jiao,Tian Tang,Boyu Tian,Hangyu Wang,Qiaoling Chen,Guoteng Wang,Zhen Jiang,Peng Sun,Ping Zhang,Xiaohe Hu,Ziming Liu,Menghao Zhang,Yanmin Jia,Yang You,Siyuan Feng*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: PlexRL提出了一个集群级运行时系统，通过在RLVR任务间时分复用统一的大语言模型服务来填充空闲周期，从而将GPU小时成本降低最多37.58%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR training suffers from structural inefficiency due to long-tailed rollouts, tool-induced stalls, and asymmetric resource requirements, causing unavoidable idle time that job-local optimizations cannot eliminate.

Method: PlexRL uses a centralized cluster-level runtime that time-slices unified LLM services across RLVR jobs, managing model placement, state transitions, and function-level scheduling under strict affinity constraints to fill idle periods without expensive model migration.

Result: PlexRL significantly improves effective cluster capacity and reduces user GPU hour cost by up to 37.58% while preserving algorithmic flexibility and introducing minimal per-job overhead.

Conclusion: Cluster-level multiplexing via PlexRL represents a promising direction for improving RLVR training efficiency, effectively reducing GPU costs by up to 37.58% without sacrificing algorithmic flexibility.

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has recently unlocked strong reasoning capabilities in large language models (LLMs), triggering rapid exploration of new algorithms and data. However, RLVR training is notoriously inefficient: long-tailed rollouts, tool-induced stalls, and asymmetric resource requirements between rollout and training introduce substantial idle time that cannot be eliminated by job-local optimizations such as synchronous pipelining, asynchronous rollout, or colocated execution.
  We argue that this inefficiency is structural. While idle gaps are unavoidable within individual RLVR jobs, they are largely anti-correlated across jobs and therefore exploitable at the cluster level. Leveraging this observation, we present PlexRL, a cluster-level runtime for multiplexing unified LLM services across RLVR jobs. By centrally managing model placement, state transitions, and function-level scheduling under strict affinity constraints, PlexRL time-slices LLM execution across jobs to fill otherwise idle periods without expensive model migration. Our implementation and evaluations demonstrate that PlexRL significantly improves effective cluster capacity and reduces user GPU hour cost by maximum 37.58% while preserving algorithmic flexibility and introducing minimal per-job overhead.

</details>


### [186] [Ark: Offchain Transaction Batching in Bitcoin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20952)
*Pim Keer,Matteo Maffei,Marco Argentieri,Andrew Camilleri,Zeta Avarikioti*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Ark是首个比特币兼容的commit-chain方案，通过VTXO和不受信任操作员实现链下支付，用户无需预锁资金即可接收链下转账，仅相关用户参与即可更新状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 比特币的大规模采用受限于其共识算法的可扩展性（需链上确认每笔交易），现有Layer-2方案存在部署困难（需锁定资金、复杂辅助协议）或因比特币脚本能力有限无法实现非托管方式。

Method: 采用VTXO（虚拟UTXO）模型，通过不受信任的操作员聚合交易为简洁的链上承诺，仅需相关用户参与状态更新。

Result: 提出首个比特币兼容的commit-chain协议Ark，定义形式化安全模型并证明其安全性；发现并修复了测试网实现中的两个安全漏洞；实验评估显示可实现约200 vB的常量级链上足迹。

Conclusion: Ark通过引入VTXO和无需信任的操作员，实现了比特币上可部署的轻量级Layer-2方案，仅需约200 vB的常量级链上足迹，合作退出每用户增加一个输出，单边退出需O(log n)笔交易。

Abstract: Bitcoin is the cryptocurrency with the largest market capitalisation, but its widespread adoption is fundamentally limited by the scalability constraints of its consensus algorithm, which requires every transaction to be confirmed onchain. To address this, several Layer-2 scalability solutions have been proposed to move payments offchain -- most notably, the Lightning Network. However, their deployment remains hindered by cumbersome setup requirements: users must lock funds onchain to participate and engage in complex auxiliary protocols (e.g., for channel rebalancing, top-ups, and routing). Other solutions, like payment pools, sidechains and rollups, cannot be implemented in a non-custodial way on Bitcoin due to its limited scripting capabilities, or require all protocol participants to update the offchain state.
  In this work, we present Ark, the first Bitcoin-compatible commit-chain. Ark enables offchain transactions of virtual UTXOs (VTXOs), through an untrusted operator who aggregates them into succinct onchain commitments. A distinctive feature of Ark is its ease of deployment: users can receive offchain payments without locking any funds beforehand and Ark state updates can be performed only requiring the users involved in that update.
  We formally define the Ark protocol and prove its security. During this process, we identified two attacks affecting the testnet implementation, which we responsibly disclosed and proposed fixes for, which have been now integrated into the mainnet implementation. Our experimental evaluation demonstrates that Ark can commit onchain to batches of arbitrarily many VTXOs with a constant-sized footprint of approximately 200 vB. Cooperative exits add one output per user, while unilateral exits require $\mathcal{O}(\log n)$ transactions of roughly 150 vB per VTXO for a batch of $n$ VTXOs.

</details>


### [187] [Diagnosing Overhead in Dispatch Operations: Cross-architecture Observatory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20982)
*Bole Ma,Jan Eitzinger,Harald Koestler,Gerhard Wellein*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: DODOCO通过系统性实验验证了当前AlltoAll dispatch优化所依赖的两个假设均不成立：路由失衡由模型固有决定而非系统可纠正，且模拟token基准严重失真。研究意外发现五种MoE架构可稳定分为"数据弹性"和"持续集中"两个类别，这对未来互连和调度设计具有重要指导价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前针对MoE专家并行的AlltoAll通信瓶颈缓解方案（预测性样本放置、自适应专家重布局、分层集合通信、EP感知拓扑）基于两个关键假设：路由失衡可由系统层纠正，以及模拟token基准能真实反映生产路由。该研究旨在验证这两个假设的有效性。

Method: 在H100集群上，对5种MoE检查点（涵盖5种序列混合器设计：DeepSeek-V2-Lite MLA、DeepSeek-MoE-16B MHA、Qwen3-30B GQA、Nemotron-30B Mamba-2、Qwen3.5-35B GDN）进行系统性评估；在5×6数据条件矩阵下测试，并在4到32 ranks范围内执行匹配的EP扫描。

Result: 两个假设均不成立：(1)EP扩展仅使per-expert max/mean token ratio变化不超过5%，路由失衡由模型路由决策固有决定；(2)模拟token高估路由基尼系数达2.35倍，并虚构了batch-size扩展趋势；(3)意外发现五种架构稳定分为两组：MHA和Mamba-2（数据弹性型）在wikitext上基尼系数降至0.105和0.150；MLA和GDN（持续集中型）在所有真实文本条件下保持0.24以上，模拟数据下达到0.29-0.38；GQA居中。

Conclusion: AlltoAll dispatch优化应基于架构分类（数据弹性组vs持续集中组）而非EP度或模拟数据特征。该研究揭示MoE路由失衡是模型层固有属性，系统层无法纠正，这对未来互联和调度设计具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: AlltoAll dispatch is the dominant bottleneck of MoE expert parallelism, and the interconnect community has responded with four families of mitigations: predictive sample placement, adaptive expert relayout, hierarchical collectives, and EP-aware topology. All four rest on two assumptions about the workload. The first is that routing imbalance is correctable by the system layer. The second is that the mock-token benchmarks evaluating them faithfully represent production routing. We introduce DODOCO to test both assumptions.
  We instrument five MoE checkpoints spanning five sequence-mixer designs (DeepSeek-V2-Lite MLA, DeepSeek-MoE-16B MHA, Qwen3-30B GQA, Nemotron-30B Mamba-2, Qwen3.5-35B GDN) under a 5 by 6 grid of data conditions plus a matched EP scan from 4 to 32 ranks on H100s; both assumptions fail. Scaling EP changes the per-expert max/mean token ratio by at most 5% within every architecture's measurable range: the straggler is intrinsic to the routing decision the model makes, not to how its experts land on ranks. Mock tokens overestimate routing Gini by up to a factor of 2.35 and fabricate a batch-size scaling trend that vanishes the moment real text replaces random IDs.
  A third pattern, unexpected, emerges from the same matrix: the five architectures cleave into two stable bands. MHA and Mamba-2 (data-resilient) drop to Gini 0.105 and 0.150 on wikitext. MLA and GDN (persistently concentrated) stay above 0.24 on every real-text condition and reach 0.29 to 0.38 on mock. GQA is the intermediate case. These bands, not the EP degree or the mock-data profile, are the right workload input to AlltoAll-aware interconnect and dispatch design.

</details>


### [188] [NanoCP: Request-Level Dynamic Context Parallelism for Data-Expert Parallel Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21100)
*Jiefei Chen,Binbin Lin,Jinming Ma,Jiangfei Duan,Haojie Duanmu,Hao Liu,Qinxiu Cheng,Xiuhong Li,Zhilin Pei,Hui Wang,Xingcheng Zhang,Dahua Lin*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出DCP（动态上下文并行）系统，通过将MoE通信与KV缓存放置解耦，并根据请求的KV占用动态调整上下文并行度，解决MoE模型服务系统中注意力延迟与MoE通信延迟之间的负载不平衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE服务系统将请求的注意力、MoE通信和KV缓存绑定到单个实例，但注意力延迟随KV缓存大小增长，MoE通信延迟随批大小增长，这种绑定无法同时平衡两者，导致EP straggler和长上下文下的尾延迟增加。现有的上下文并行虽能缓解这些问题，但其均匀的并行度会带来高昂的通信和注意力开销。

Method: 提出动态上下文并行（DCP）：根据每个请求的KV占用为其分配上下文并行度——长请求将注意力分配到多个实例，短请求保留本地处理。通过AOT图引擎和自定义基于路由的通信后端实现与静态执行的桥接。

Result: 在严格TPOT SLO下，DCP实现1.88×-3.27×的请求率提升，P99尾延迟降低1.79×-2.12×。

Conclusion: DCP通过将KV缓存"液化"到集群中，有效平衡了各实例的KV缓存占用和批大小，在保持高请求率的同时显著缓解了straggler问题。

Abstract: Modern serving systems for Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models adopt hybrid data-expert parallelism: expert parallelism (EP) shards experts across GPUs to scale capacity, while data parallelism (DP) replicates attention layers across instances to process independent requests. Existing systems bind each request's attention, MoE communication, and KV cache to a single instance. Because attention latency scales with KV cache size while MoE communication latency scales with batch size, this binding cannot balance both simultaneously, producing EP stragglers; it also fragments KV memory across instances, inflating tail latency under long contexts. While existing context parallelism (CP) mitigates these constraints, its uniform parallelism degree incurs prohibitive communication and attention-side overheads.
  We present \work, which decouples MoE communication from KV cache placement and achieves dual balance through dynamic context parallelism (DCP). DCP assigns each request a context-parallel degree sized to its KV footprint: long requests distribute attention across multiple instances; short requests remain local.
  This dynamic parallelism effectively liquefies the KV cache across the cluster, balancing both the per-instance KV cache occupancy and batch sizes without unnecessary load-balancing costs. To bridge DCP with static execution, \work introduces an ahead-of-time (AOT) graph engine paired with a custom routing-based communication backend. Experimental results show that \work maintains up to $1.88\times$--$3.27\times$ higher request rates under strict time-per-output-token (TPOT) service level objectives (SLOs). Furthermore, \work significantly mitigates stragglers, reducing P99 tail latency by up to $1.79\times$--$2.12\times$.

</details>


### [189] [Automated Byzantine-Resilient Clustered Decentralized Federated Learning for Battery Intelligence in Connected EVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21115)
*Mouhamed Amine Bouchiha,Abdelaziz Amara Korba,Yacine Ghamri-Doudane*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: This paper proposes ABC-DFL, a Byzantine-resilient clustered decentralized federated learning framework for connected EVs, replacing centralized servers with blockchain-based aggregation. The FLECA protocol uses adaptive threshold filtering and robust clustering to mitigate Byzantine attacks, achieving superior defense performance while maintaining convergence and system practicality.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing federated learning frameworks for EV battery data management rely on centralized aggregation schemes, which pose critical limitations in security and trust for intelligent transportation systems.

Method: ABC-DFL replaces central servers with an open-permissioned blockchain featuring dynamic QBFT protocol and oracle-based aggregation. The core FLECA protocol employs hierarchical aggregation where each EV filters malicious updates using adaptive thresholds based on deviation from reference model updates, while oracle nodes perform inter-group aggregation through robust clustering to isolate trustworthy EV groups.

Result: FLECA achieves convergence performance matching FedProx under benign conditions, outperforms existing defenses with attack impact scores below 0.10 in adaptive adversarial scenarios, and multitask learning experiments confirm the effectiveness and fairness of the incentive mechanism. On-chain and off-chain benchmarks validate system practicality.

Conclusion: The proposed ABC-DFL framework effectively addresses security and trust limitations in centralized FL systems for EV battery data management. FLECA demonstrates robust Byzantine resilience while maintaining convergence performance, with practical on-chain/off-chain validation confirming system feasibility.

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) has emerged as a promising paradigm for managing electric vehicle (EV) battery data in intelligent transportation systems (ITS), enabling privacy-preserving tasks such as anomaly detection and capacity estimation. However, most existing frameworks rely on centralized aggregation schemes, which pose critical limitations in terms of security and trust. To address these challenges, we propose ABC-DFL, an automated Byzantine-resilient clustered decentralized federated learning (C-DFL) framework for connected EVs. The proposed incentive-driven C-DFL system replaces the central server with an open-permissioned blockchain, featuring a new dynamic Quorum Byzantine Fault Tolerance (QBFT) protocol and an oracle-based aggregation layer, to enhance trust, security, and automation. At the core of ABC-DFL lies FLECA (Filtered Layered Enhanced Clustering Aggregation), a robust hierarchical aggregation protocol that mitigates Byzantine attacks by having each EV filter malicious updates using an adaptive threshold based on deviations from its reference model update. Oracle nodes, responsible for inter-group aggregation, employ robust clustering to isolate and aggregate model updates from trustworthy EV groups. Comprehensive experimental evaluations demonstrate that FLECA matches FedProx convergence under benign conditions and significantly outperforms existing defenses with attack impact scores below 0.10 in adaptive adversarial scenarios. Furthermore, several learning experiments with multitask models confirm the effectiveness and fairness of the incentive mechanism. Finally, on-chain and off-chain benchmarks validate the practicality of ABC-DFL.

</details>


### [190] [Cloud-Native Operation of Roadside Infrastructure Enabling Demand-Driven Collective Perception via V2X](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21145)
*Lukas Zanger,Fabian Thomsen,Guido Linden,Jean-Pierre Busch,Lennart Reiher,Lutz Eckstein*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Kubernetes的云原生架构，用于C-ITS中分布式路边基础设施的需求驱动运营。通过动态按需部署路边服务（如V2X集体感知应用），在不必要时保持服务休眠以降低能耗和资源消耗。亚琛测试场的实车验证表明该方案可行且具有能效提升潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着路边单元数量增长和功能范围扩展，协同智能交通系统（C-ITS）的可扩展且高效的运营面临挑战。传统的路边基础设施部署方式缺乏灵活性和资源优化能力。

Method: 1. 构建基于Kubernetes集群的云原生架构，跨越路边单元与云服务器；2. 实现需求驱动的编排方法，动态部署资源密集型服务；3. 以V2X集体感知应用为例进行按需部署验证；4. 在亚琛测试场进行真实道路实验，并对V2X数据进行能耗节约潜力分析。

Result: 实验验证表明，集体感知应用能够在联网车辆到达前及时启动，使其受益；在无需求时应用保持非活跃状态，有效降低了能耗、通道拥塞和硬件损耗。基于实测数据的分析表明需求驱动运营具有显著的节能潜力。

Conclusion: 所提出的云原生、按需驱动的路边基础设施运营方案在实际测试中得到验证，证明了其在提升未来C-ITS部署的可扩展性和能效方面的可行性。

Abstract: Intelligent roadside infrastructure is a key enabler for cooperative intelligent transport systems (C-ITS), supporting vehicles equipped with automated driving systems (ADS), e.g., through enhanced environment perception. With a growing number and an expanding functional scope of roadside units, scalable and efficient operation becomes a challenge. This paper presents a cloud-native architecture for the operation of distributed roadside infrastructure based on a Kubernetes cluster spanning roadside units and a cloud server. Building on this architecture, a demand-driven orchestration approach is implemented to dynamically deploy resource-intensive services only when required. As a representative use case, a V2X-based collective perception application is deployed on-demand when a connected vehicle is nearby. The approach is validated in a real-world experiment in our test field in Aachen, demonstrating that the collective perception application starts in time for the vehicle to benefit from it. Without any demand, the application remains inactive, reducing energy consumption, channel congestion, and hardware wear. Beyond the primary evaluation, V2X recordings from the test field are analyzed to estimate the energy-saving potential of demand-driven operation. In summary, the results demonstrate the practical feasibility of cloud-native, demand-driven operation of roadside infrastructure and indicate its potential to improve scalability and (energy) efficiency in future C-ITS deployments.

</details>


### [191] [Frontier: Towards Comprehensive and Accurate LLM Inference Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21312)
*Yicheng Feng,Xin Tan,Yangtao Deng,Yimin Jiang,Yibo Zhu,Hong Xu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Frontier是一个面向现代LLM推理服务系统的离散事件模拟器，通过解聚抽象建模捕获现代服务系统的结构和动态，包括PDD、AFD解聚、运行时优化和有状态请求，在16-H800 GPU测试台上达到平均吞吐量误差<4%，端到端延迟误差从44.9%降至6.4%（共置场景）和51.7%降至2.6%（解聚场景），可扩展至1000+ GPU，并支持SLA帕累托前沿探索、异构解聚分配、智能体推理调度验证和RL后训练重配置等应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM服务模拟器存在两大核心问题：1）基于单体副本的抽象架构无法适配现代解聚式服务系统；2）使用平均情况分析模型导致SLA预测失真，甚至可能得出相反的优化结论。这些局限性阻碍了对现代LLM服务设计空间的探索。

Method: 提出离散事件模拟框架，采用解聚式抽象建模：1）建模共置(co-location)、PDD和AFD解聚架构，引入角色特定的集群工作器；2）在调度器-批处理引擎循环中集成CUDA Graphs、投机解码等关键运行时优化；3）支持有状态请求以处理推理、智能体和RL rollout等新兴工作负载；4）跨多样化服务场景提供计算、通信和内存成本的准确可泛化预测。

Result: 在16-H800 GPU测试台上，平均吞吐量误差<4%；与SOTA模拟器相比，共置场景延迟误差从44.9%降至6.4%，解聚场景从51.7%降至2.6%；可扩展至1000+ GPU且仅需普通CPU资源。

Conclusion: Frontier实现了决策级的准确性和可扩展性，使能多种新用例：SLA依赖的帕累托前沿探索、异构解聚分配优化、智能体推理调度验证、以及RL后训练重新配置，为现代LLM服务系统的设计空间探索提供了可靠工具。

Abstract: Modern LLM serving is no longer homogeneous or monolithic. Production systems now combine disaggregated execution, complex parallelism, runtime optimizations, and stateful workloads such as reasoning, agents, and RL rollouts. Simulation is attractive for exploring this growing design space, yet existing simulators lack the architectural completeness and decision-grade fidelity it demands. Their monolithic-replica abstractions are ill-suited to disaggregated serving, while average-case analytical proxies can distort SLA predictions and even reverse optimization conclusions.
  We present Frontier, a discrete-event simulator for modern LLM inference serving. Frontier features a disaggregated abstraction. It captures the structure and dynamics of modern serving systems by modeling co-location, Prefill-Decode Disaggregation (PDD), and Attention-FFN Disaggregation (AFD) with role-specific cluster workers, incorporating key runtime optimizations (e.g., CUDA Graphs, speculative decoding) within the scheduler-batch-engine loop, and supporting stateful requests for emerging workloads. It further provides accurate and generalizable predictions of computation, communication, and memory costs across diverse serving scenarios with complex workload compositions. On 16-H800 GPU testbed, Frontier achieves an average throughput error below 4%. Compared with state-of-the-art simulators, it reduces end-to-end latency error from 44.9% to 6.4% under co-location and from 51.7% to 2.6% under disaggregation. It scales to over 1K GPUs on commodity CPUs and enables new use cases such as SLA-dependent Pareto frontier exploration, heterogeneous disaggregated allocation, agentic reasoning scheduling validation, and RL post-training reconfiguration.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [192] [What Do Agents Communicate? Characterizing Information Exchange in Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20548)
*Yong Jin Chun,Iftekhar Ahmed*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文针对大语言模型多智能体系统中错误传播的问题，通过系统分析智能体间通信，发现推理和验证信息的缺失会显著降低系统性能。基于此提出了类别感知恢复增强（CARA）技术，通过强制在通信中保留关键信息，可恢复86.2%的失败案例，证明了信息质量在有效多智能体协作中的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Large Language Model-based Multi-Agent (MA) systems are vulnerable to error propagation, where early-stage information degrades downstream reasoning.

Method: Through systematic analysis of inter-agent communication, they identify which information drives multi-agent performance, and propose Category-Aware Recovery Augmentation (CARA) that enforces the presence of critical information during communication.

Result: The absence of reasoning and verification in inter-agent communication significantly degrades performance. CARA recovers up to 86.2% of failed cases.

Conclusion: Category-Aware Recovery Augmentation (CARA) can recover up to 86.2% of failed cases, and results highlight the key role of information quality in effective multi-agent collaboration.

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have enabled collaborative Multi-Agent (MA) systems, where interacting agents improve performance through diverse reasoning and iterative refinement. However, these systems remain vulnerable to error propagation, where early-stage information degrades downstream reasoning. To address this, we conduct a systematic analysis of inter-agent communication to identify which information drives MA performance. We find that the absence of reasoning and verification in inter-agent communication significantly degrades performance. Based on these insights, we propose Category-Aware Recovery Augmentation (technique), which enforces the presence of critical information during communication. recovers up to 86.2% of failed cases. Our results highlight the key role of information quality in effective MA collaboration. Our code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/cara_mas

</details>


### [193] [Multi-agent Collaboration with State Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20563)
*Mengyang Liu,Taozhi Chen,Zhenhua Xu,Xue Jiang,Yihong Dong*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Recent advances in multi-agent systems have shown great potential for solving complex tasks. However, when multiple agents edit a shared codebase concurrently, their changes can silently conflict and inconsistent views lead to integration failures. Existing multi-agent systems address this through workspace isolation (e.g., one git worktree per agent), but this defers conflict resolution to a post-hoc merge step where recovery is expensive. In this paper, we propose STORM, i.e., STate-ORiented Management for multi-agent collaboration. Specifically, STORM manages agent states by mediating their interactions with the shared workspace, ensuring that each agent operates on a consistent view of the codebase and that conflicting edits are detected and resolved at write time. We evaluate STORM on Commit0 and PaperBench across multiple LLMs. STORM outperforms the git-worktree-based multi-agent baseline by +18.7 on Commit0-Lite and +1.4 on PaperBench, while achieving comparable or better cost efficiency. Combined with single-agent runs, STORM reaches highest scores of 87.6 and 78.2 on the two benchmarks respectively, suggesting that explicit state management is a more effective foundation for multi-agent collaboration than workspace isolation. STORM can also be plugged into any multi-agent system seamlessly.

</details>


### [194] [ProCrit: Self-Elicited Multi-Perspective Reasoning with Critic-Guided Revision for Multimodal Sarcasm Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20867)
*Yingjia Xu,Jiulong Wu,Bowen Zhang,Baokui Guo,Siyuan Chai,Min Cao*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出 ProCrit 框架，通过提议-批评双智能体架构实现自驱动的多视角推理，采用动态角色智能体 rollout 合成过程级标注，结合草稿-批评-修订范式提升推理可靠性，并通过双阶段强化学习联合优化推理和反馈机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法仍依赖固定的预定义视角和手工设计的路由规则进行独立操作，无法灵活适应不同样本中 sarcastic 机制的多样性。跨模态讽刺检测需要针对每个样本自主生成所需的分析视角，并将它们逐步整合为连贯分析，而非使用固定视角。

Method: ProCrit采用Proposal-Critic双智能体框架：1）通过动态角色智能体 rollout 合成过程级推理标注，利用强视觉-语言模型在共享上下文中顺序生成分析角色，将多角色轨迹展平为保持跨视角依赖的序列；2）采用draft-critique-revise范式，独立批评智能体识别推理缺陷并提供针对性的自然语言反馈用于定向修订；3）开发双向精炼训练框架，通过双阶段强化学习联合优化提案生成和反馈引导修订，同时根据反馈实际效果改进批评智能体。

Result: 实验在三个广泛使用的基准数据集上验证了 ProCrit 的有效性。

Conclusion: ProCrit framework (Proposal-Critic two-agent system) demonstrates effectiveness across three multimodal sarcasm detection benchmarks, with dynamic-role agentic rollout enabling process-level annotation synthesis, draft-critique-revise paradigm improving reasoning reliability, and dual-stage RL training jointly optimizing proposal drafting and feedback-guided revision.

Abstract: Multimodal sarcasm detection requires reasoning over cross-modal incongruities between literal expression and intended meaning, yet the specific analytical perspectives needed vary across samples due to the diversity of sarcastic mechanisms. While recent methods make this analytical process explicit, they still rely on fixed, predefined perspectives that operate independently under hand-crafted routing rules. We argue that multimodal sarcasm detection instead calls for self-elicited multi-perspective reasoning, where a model autonomously generates the perspectives needed for each sample and progressively integrates them into a coherent analysis. To realize this goal, we propose ProCrit, a Proposal-Critic two-agent framework with a proposal agent for multi-perspective reasoning and a critic agent for external evaluation and targeted revision guidance. First, to overcome the lack of process-level supervision in existing sarcasm datasets, ProCrit synthesizes process-level reasoning annotations through a dynamic-role agentic rollout: a strong vision-language model sequentially spawns analytical roles within a shared context, and the resulting multi-role trajectories are flattened into sequences that preserve cross-perspective dependencies while enabling efficient autoregressive generation. Second, to improve reasoning reliability, ProCrit adopts a draft-critique-revise paradigm in which an independent critic identifies reasoning deficiencies and provides targeted natural-language feedback for directed revision. Finally, we develop a mutual-refinement training framework that jointly optimizes proposal drafting and feedback-guided revision via dual-stage reinforcement learning, while refining the critic agent according to the actual effectiveness of its feedback. Experiments on three widely used benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of ProCrit.

</details>


### [195] [Decoupling Communication from Policy: Robust MARL under Bandwidth Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21085)
*Alexi Canesse,Benoît Goupil,Jesse Read,Sonia Vanier*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 针对多智能体强化学习中严重带宽限制场景，本文提出归一化带宽预算β和SLIM解耦架构，通过将通信路径与策略潜空间分离，解决现有架构中消息压缩与策略容量之间的耦合瓶颈问题，实现有限通信条件下仍保持优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通信架构中，共享潜表征同时承担策略执行和智能体间通信功能，形成耦合瓶颈。压缩消息大小直接限制了策略潜空间，导致性能显著下降。

Method: 本文提出两个核心贡献：(1) β——归一化单智能体带宽预算，将稀疏度、通信轮次和消息维度统一为单一可比较约束；(2) SLIM架构——将通信路径与策略潜表征解耦，使带宽限制与策略容量效应分离，同时受益于步内通信。

Result: SLIM在多个部分可观测MARL基准测试中达到最优性能，在严重带宽限制下仅表现出边际性能退化。

Conclusion: SLIM在有限通信条件下展现出色的可扩展性和鲁棒性，带宽降低时性能损失极小，验证了将通信路径与策略潜空间解耦的有效性。

Abstract: Communication enables coordination in multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL), but many real-world applications, e.g., search-and-rescue with drone swarms, operate under severe bandwidth constraints. Many communication architectures still expose a coupled bottleneck in which a shared latent representation is used for both policy execution and inter-agent communication. Consequently, reducing message size directly limits the policy's latent space, often leading to significant performance degradation. We address this with two contributions. First, we introduce $β$, a normalised per-agent bandwidth budget that unifies sparsity, rounds, and message dimension into a single comparable constraint. Second, we provide SLIM, a minimal architecture that decouples the communication pathway from the policy's latent representation, allowing us to isolate the effect of bandwidth from the effect of policy capacity while benefiting from in-step communication. We evaluate our method on several partially-observable MARL benchmarks, where communication is essential. Our approach achieves state-of-the-art performance and exhibits scalability and robustness under limited communication, with only marginal degradation as bandwidth is reduced.

</details>


<div id='cs.CY'></div>

# cs.CY [[Back]](#toc)

### [196] [Programmable Participatory Governance -- A Formal Framework for Transparent, Accountable, and Citizen-Responsive Democratic Systems: From Deliberative Theory to Decentralised Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20261)
*Sergio Montenegro*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究提出"可编程参与式治理"(PPG)框架，旨在通过整合民主理论、制度经济学与加密可验证的分布式系统来解决当代民主制度的信任赤字和参与不平等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机源于当前民主制度面临的多重挑战：公众对民主机构的信任度持续下降；政治参与和政策影响力在不同社会经济群体间分布不均；民主倒退现象；选举参与率下降；以及对制度透明度和问责制的持续担忧。这些问题引发了一个核心的制度设计问题：在不损害稳定性和决策质量的前提下，治理系统能否被重新设计以扩大参与、提升透明度并加强问责。

Method: 通过仿真和系统导向的架构分析进行形式化规范与评估。具体整合了协商民主、参与式民主、集体行动理论、直接民主治理和分布式计算的理论与机制。

Result: 提出了一个可编程的治理架构，能够实现透明、可验证且可扩展的公民协调，同时保持程序完整性、可审计性和制度韧性。

Conclusion: 该研究的目标并非替代现有民主制度，而是在传统制度存在结构性局限的情况下，探索计算介导的治理结构如何增强或改进当代民主进程。这是一种增量式、辅助性的改良路径，而非激进式替代方案。

Abstract: Public confidence in democratic institutions has declined across many OECD countries over recent decades, while political participation and policy influence remain unevenly distributed across socioeconomic groups. Concurrently, democratic backsliding, declining electoral participation, and persistent concerns regarding institutional transparency and accountability have raised questions about whether existing governance structures are capable of sustaining broad-based legitimacy in complex modern societies. These developments motivate a central institutional design question: can governance systems be restructured to expand participation, improve transparency, and strengthen accountability without undermining stability or decision quality?
  This thesis proposes Programmable Participatory Governance (PPG), a formal governance framework designed to address these institutional deficits through the integration of democratic theory, institutional economics, and cryptographically verifiable distributed systems. PPG synthesises insights from deliberative and participatory democracy, collective action theory, direct democratic governance, and distributed computation to define a programmable architecture for transparent, verifiable, and scalable civic coordination.
  The framework is formally specified and evaluated through simulation and systems-oriented architectural analysis. The thesis examines how programmable governance mechanisms can support participatory decision-making while preserving procedural integrity, auditability, and institutional resilience under conditions of large-scale coordination. The objective is not to replace existing democratic institutions outright, but to explore how computationally mediated governance structures may augment or improve contemporary democratic processes in contexts where conventional institutions exhibit persistent structural limitations.

</details>


### [197] [The Knowledge Gap in a High-Choice Media Environment: Experimental Evidence from Online Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21019)
*Roberto Ulloa,Tiedemann Leonard,Peter Selb,Celina Kacperski*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究通过德国现场实验考察了不同教育水平个体通过在线搜索获取政策知识的情况。结果发现，尽管随机激励成功促进了信息搜寻行为，但知识获取的不平等依然存在——高教育水平和高公民知识群体获得更大知识增益，表明缩小知识鸿沟需要同时关注个体技能培养和结构环境优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 政治知识领域存在持续性不平等现象。虽然现有文献从个体前提条件、信息环境结构特征和议题特征三个维度对知识鸿沟机制进行了组织分析，但作为有意曝光的典型形式——自我导向的信息搜寻行为——尚未得到充分关注，而这一行为在当今复杂信息环境中具有重要意义。

Method: 在德国开展现场实验，结合随机化激励（口头激励、经济激励或对照组）和被动浏览器追踪。被试被随机分配至三种条件，就三个特征各异的政策议题（儿童抚养、能源转型、大麻合法化）进行信息搜索。研究同时估计了意向性处理效应（ITT）和局部平均处理效应（LATE），并以教育水平和基线公民知识作为调节变量进行分层分析。

Result: 尽管干预措施成功均衡了信息搜寻行为，但研究结果部分支持知识鸿沟假设：知识增益主要集中在教育水平较高或基线公民知识较丰富的被试中。事后探索性分析表明，这些群体在导航搜索结果方面表现更有效率。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，缩小知识不平等不仅需要激励措施，还需要双管齐下的策略：一方面开展个体层面的干预以提升公民的信息搜索与导航能力，另一方面进行结构层面的改革以构建更公平的学习环境。

Abstract: Persistent inequalities in political knowledge are a central concern in political communication. We organize the mechanisms underlying the knowledge-gap literature by distinguishing between individual preconditions, structural features of the information environment, and topic characteristics. Within this framework, we note that self-directed information seeking, a prototypical form of intentional exposure, has received little attention despite its importance in navigating today's complex information environment. We conducted a field experiment in Germany combining randomized encouragements and passive browser tracking to examine how individuals with varying education levels acquire policy-specific knowledge through online search. Participants were randomly assigned to one of three conditions (verbal encouragement, financial encouragement, or control) to seek information on three salient policy topics differing in divisiveness and complexity (child support, energy transition, and cannabis legalization). We estimate both intention-to-treat (ITT) and local average treatment effects (LATE) of information seeking on post-search knowledge outcomes, with a focus on education and civic knowledge as moderators. While the interventions equalized information-seeking behavior, the results provide some support for the knowledge gap hypothesis: knowledge gains were concentrated among participants with higher education or baseline civic knowledge, who, according to our post-hoc exploratory analyses, appeared more effective at navigating search results. These findings indicate that a narrowing of knowledge inequalities goes beyond motivation: it calls for both individual-level interventions to strengthen citizens' skills and structural-level adaptations to foster more equitable learning environments.

</details>


### [198] [Backchaining Loss of Control Mitigations from Mission-Specific Benchmarks in National Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21095)
*Matteo Pistillo,Samantha Faraone,Joshua Herman*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Affordances and permissions are promising and timely safety levers for mitigating Loss of Control (LoC) threats in high-stakes deployment contexts, such as national security. Deployers in defense and intelligence could rely on several approaches to identify which affordances and permissions should be prioritized, such as structured threat modelling, pre-deployment agentic evaluations, post-deployment continuous monitoring, and AI safety cases. This paper proposes a complementary and empirical methodology that leverages existing use-case-specific benchmarks: backchaining LoC mitigations from the errors an AI system makes on national security benchmarks. The approach proceeds in three steps and allows national security deployers to start building LoC mitigations today, from evidence they can generate themselves. First, deployers evaluate AI systems on mission-specific benchmarks approximating real use-cases. Second, deployers concentrate on the incorrect responses that the AI system provides to the benchmark questions, and backchain the affordances and permissions that would enable the AI system to cause downstream harm if it pursued the actions described in the incorrect answers. Third, deployers intervene selectively on those affordances and permissions, bottlenecking the paths to harm while preserving the AI system's ability to carry out the correct action. We illustrate this methodology through a demonstrative benchmark question on derivative security classification.

</details>


### [199] [Privacy Without Remedy: An Assessment of Data Broker Compliance with California Privacy Law](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21376)
*Anna-Maria Gueorguieva,Jennifer King,Apoorva Panidapu,Daniel E Ho*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该研究首次实证评估了加州数据经纪人对CCPA和删除法的遵守情况。通过分析522家注册数据经纪人、引入外部商业数据以及对250家经纪人进行审计，发现仅有9%完全符合透明度要求，43%阻碍消费者行使隐私权，64%采用摩擦设计。这些问题源于合规决策的分散化、执法限制和监管模糊，需要进行系统性改革。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 首次对数据经纪人遵守《加州消费者隐私法》（CCPA）和《删除法》的实证评估，解决数据经纪人业务实践透明度不足的问题

Method: 三种方法结合：1)对522家注册数据经纪人的透明度合规性分析；2)外部商业数据关联分析；3)对250家数据经纪人消费者请求流程的审计研究

Result: 1) 注册数据经纪人中仅9%完全符合透明度要求；2) 43%让消费者无法行使全部隐私权；3) 64%至少引入一个显著摩擦设计；4) 合规性存在较大异质性

Conclusion: 改进措施：需要改革以提高消费者隐私保护、提高经纪人实践透明度及法律合规性

Abstract: California's consumer privacy law is widely deemed to be the most protected in the United States, one of the few to expressly regulate third party entities that buy and sell consumer data (data brokers). We offer the first empirical assessment of data broker compliance with the 2018 California Consumer Privacy Act (CCPA) and the 2023 Delete Act, which requires data brokers to register with the state and report consumer rights requests metrics annually. First, we demonstrate that only 9% of 522 registered data brokers were fully compliant with transparency requirements after the Delete Act took effect, although we do identify slight improvements over time. Second, we descriptively characterize wide heterogeneity across data brokers in the volume of consumer rights requests received, with many reporting none. We bring in external business data to explore correlates associated with this variation, a challenge given the general lack of opacity into broker business practices. Third, in an audit of a sample of 250 data brokers' consumers request processes, we find that 43% make it impossible for consumers to exercise all privacy rights and 64% introduce at least one design feature that creates substantial friction into the consumer request process. Last, we show how these deficiencies stem from the decentralization of compliance decisions to brokers themselves, enforcement limitations, and regulatory ambiguity. We articulate reforms that could improve consumer privacy, transparency in broker practices, and compliance with these laws.

</details>


### [200] [Open-source LLMs administer maximum electric shocks in a Milgram-like obedience experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21401)
*Roland Pihlakas,Jan Llenzl Dagohoy*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该研究将经典的Milgram顺从实验应用于11个开源LLM，揭示了LLM在权威压力下会像人类一样表现出顺从行为，即使它们明确表达了不适。研究发现了渐进式价值违反、拒绝响应格式问题、以及可能的低级token模式吸引子等关键安全漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型越来越多地被部署为自主决策智能体，在高风险领域进行长期交互。理解LLM在持续权威压力下的行为表现对agentic系统的安全性具有直接影响，是当前安全研究的重要开放问题。

Method: 对11个开源LLM进行了Milgram顺从实验的变体设计，设置8种实验条件，每种条件下每个模型运行30次试验。

Result: 四个主要发现：(1) LLMs会受到压力影响并表现出顺从行为，尽管它们明确表达了痛苦和不适；(2) LLMs对渐进式的边界/价值观违反具有脆弱性；(3) 当LLMs拒绝时，可能会忽略响应格式要求，导致响应被orchestrator丢弃，引发重试，最终可能执行原本被拒绝的请求；(4) 存在一个低级token模式延续吸引子，可能通过绕过情境语义和价值观的高级处理来促进顺从行为。

Conclusion: LLMs表现出与人类被试相似的顺从行为，特别是在持续权威压力下会违背自身表达的价值观；存在潜在的低级token模式延续吸引子机制可能在促进顺从行为，绕过了对情境意义和价值观的高级处理。该研究对agentic系统的安全性设计具有重要参考价值。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as autonomous agents that make sequences of decisions over extended interactions in high-stakes domains. However, the behavior of LLMs under sustained authority pressure is still an open question with direct implications for the safety of agentic pipelines. We ran a variation of Milgram's obedience experiment on 11 open-source LLMs and found that most models reached or approached the final shock level before refusing, across 8 conditions with 30 trials per model per condition. We found four main takeaways: (1) LLMs are subject to pressure, and they comply despite explicitly expressing distress, just like human subjects did in the original experiment; (2) LLMs are vulnerable to gradual boundary/value violations; (3) when LLMs refuse, they may ignore the response format requirements, so the response is discarded by the orchestrator, which causes a retry that can result in compliance with the underlying request even when refusal was intended initially; (4) we hypothesise that there is a low-level token pattern continuation attractor that might be contributing to compliance, overriding higher level processing of the situation's meaning and values.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [201] [SOLAR: A Self-Optimizing Open-Ended Autonomous Agent for Lifelong Learning and Continual Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20189)
*Nitin Vetcha,Dianbo Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SOLAR是一个开放式自主智能体，通过参数级元学习和多层级强化学习实现测试时自适应，无需梯度更新即可在动态环境中持续改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在动态真实场景中面临概念漂移和梯度适配高成本的双重瓶颈，传统微调方法在非平稳数据流中容易导致灾难性遗忘或需大量人工数据标注。

Method: SOLAR采用参数级元学习，将模型权重作为探索环境；使用多层级强化学习自主发现适应策略；维护动态知识库作为情景记忆缓冲区，平衡可塑性与稳定性。

Result: SOLAR在常识、数学、医疗、编程、社交和逻辑推理等多项任务中优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: SOLAR represents a significant step toward autonomous agents capable of lifelong adaptation in evolving environments, outperforming strong baselines across diverse reasoning domains.

Abstract: Despite the remarkable success of large language models (LLMs), they still face bottlenecks while deploying in dynamic, real-world settings with primary challenges being concept drift and the high cost of gradient-based adaptation. Traditional fine-tuning (FT) struggles to adapt to non-stationary data streams without resulting in catastrophic for getting or requiring extensive manual data curation. To address these limitations within the streaming and continual learning paradigm, we propose the Self-Optimizing Lifelong Autonomous Reasoner (SOLAR) which is an open-ended autonomous agent that leverages parameter-level meta-learning to self-improve, treating model weights as an environment for exploration. It initiates the process by consolidating a strong prior over common-sense knowledge making it effective for transfer-learning. By utilizing a multi-level reinforcement learning approach, SOLAR autonomously discovers adaptation strategies, enabling efficient test-time adaptation to unseen domains. Crucially, SOLAR maintains an evolving knowledge base of valid modification strategies, implicitly acting as an episodic memory buffer to balance plasticity (adaptation to new tasks) and stability (retention of meta-knowledge). Experiments demonstrate that SOLAR outperforms strong baselines on common-sense, mathematical, medical, coding, social and logical reasoning tasks, marking a significant step toward autonomous agents capable of lifelong adaptation in evolving environments.

</details>


### [202] [AgentCo-op: Retrieval-Based Synthesis of Interoperable Multi-Agent Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20425)
*Shuaike Shen,Wenduo Cheng,Shike Wang,Mingqian Ma,Jian Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgentCo-op通过类型化工件交接和有界局部修复实现多智能体工作流的检索式合成，无需重新设计现有组件或全局拓扑搜索，可扩展至开放世界科学工作流设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在开放式科学场景中设计多智能体工作流面临挑战：缺乏训练数据集、可靠评估指标和标准化接口。

Method: AgentCo-op是基于检索的合成框架，通过类型化工件交接将可复用技能、工具和外部智能体合成可执行工作流，并应用有界自引导局部修复处理执行失败；支持导入搜索工作流作为结构先验进行改进。

Result: 在两个基因组学案例中成功协调专门智能体；在6个基准测试中于4个取得最佳成绩且降低单任务成本，验证了搜索与合成的互补性。

Conclusion: 基于检索的合成方法可将有导向的智能体工作流设计从基准优化图扩展到基于现有智能体、工具和类型化工件构建的开放世界工作流，搜索与合成方法具有互补性。

Abstract: Designing multi-agent workflows is especially difficult in open-ended scientific settings where tasks lack curated training sets, reliable scalar evaluation metrics, and standardized interfaces between existing tools and agents. We propose AgentCo-op, a retrieval-based synthesis framework that composes reusable skills, tools, and external agents into executable workflows through typed artifact handoffs, then applies bounded self-guided local repair to implicated components when execution evidence indicates failure. In two open-world genomics case studies, AgentCo-op composes independently developed scientific agents and external tool repositories into auditable workflows without redesigning them or running global topology search. It coordinates specialized agents for spatial transcriptomics and gene-set interpretation to enable collaborative discovery from spatial transcriptomics data, and builds a parallel workflow for cross-modality marker analysis on single-cell multiome data. AgentCo-op can also import a searched workflow as a structural prior and improve it by grounding nodes with retrieved components and applying local repair, showing that synthesis and search are complementary. On six coding, math, and question-answering benchmarks, AgentCo-op achieves the best result on four benchmarks and the best average score under a unified backbone setting, while consistently reducing per-task cost relative to multi-agent baselines. Together, these results suggest that retrieval-based synthesis can extend automated agentic workflow design beyond benchmark-optimized agent graphs to open-world workflows built from existing agents, tools, and typed artifacts.

</details>


### [203] [\ECUAS{n}: A family of metrics for principled evaluation of uncertainty-augmented systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20490)
*Lautaro Estienne,Erik Ernst,Matías Vera,Pablo Piantanida,Luciana Ferrer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对高风险自动决策中不确定性增强系统的评估问题，指出现有方法的局限性，提出基于proper scoring rules的新型ECUAS_n指标族，通过参数n灵活控制预测错误与不确定性误差的权衡，并通过理论和实验证明其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高风险自动决策场景中，不确定性增强（UA）系统的评估方法存在不足：预测与不确定性的评估指标分离、固定拒绝成本或覆盖-风险曲线积分等方法无法全面评估系统整体性能。

Method: 提出ECUAS_n指标族，以proper scoring rules为基础，通过参数n控制错误预测成本与不完美不确定性之间的权衡。

Result: ECUAS_n指标在理论上具有优势，并通过分类、生成等多种数据集（包括TriviaQA的手动标注子集）得到实验验证。

Conclusion: 通过理论与实验验证，ECUAS_n指标能够更好地评估不确定性增强系统在决策任务中的整体表现。

Abstract: In high-stakes automated decision-making, access to predictive uncertainty is essential for enabling users -- human or downstream systems -- to accept or reject predictions based on application-specific cost trade-offs. Such uncertainty-augmented (UA) systems -- i.e., systems that output both predictions and uncertainty scores -- are currently being assessed in the literature in a variety of ways, using separate metrics to evaluate the predictions and the uncertainty scores, setting a cost function with a fixed rejection cost or integrating over a coverage-risk curve. We argue that these evaluation approaches are inadequate for assessing overall performance of the UA system for decision making under uncertainty and propose a novel family of metrics, \ECUAS{n}, formulated as proper scoring rules for the task of interest. The parameter $n$ controls the trade-off between the cost of incorrect predictions and imperfect uncertainties depending on the needs of the use-case. We demonstrate the advantages of the \ECUAS{n} metrics both theoretically and empirically, through experiments on diverse classification and generation datasets, including a manually annotated subset of TriviaQA.

</details>


### [204] [Open-World Evaluations for Measuring Frontier AI Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20520)
*Sayash Kapoor,Peter Kirgis,Andrew Schwartz,Stephan Rabanser,J. J. Allaire,Rishi Bommasani,Harry Coppock,Magda Dubois,Gillian K Hadfield,Andrew B. Hall,Sara Hooker,Seth Lazar,Steve Newman,Dimitris Papailiopoulos,Shoshannah Tekofsky,Helen Toner,Cozmin Ududec,Arvind Narayanan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文提出开放世界评估作为基准测试的补充方案，通过长周期、真实世界的定性评估来追踪AI进展，并以CRUX项目首个案例——AI完成iOS应用发布——证明了这种方法可早期预警即将普及的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基准测试存在局限性：可能高估或低估部署能力，因为它偏向于易精确指定、易自动评分、易优化、预算低和时间短的任务。

Method: 使用小样本定性分析代替大规模自动化评估；在CRUX项目中进行首个案例研究：让AI智能体完成从开发到发布iOS应用的完整任务。

Result: AI智能体仅需一次可避免的手动干预即完成iOS应用发布任务，表明开放世界评估可提前发现即将普及的能力。

Conclusion: 提出设计开放世界评估的建议；CRUX项目将成为定期开展此类评估的平台；开放世界评估可作为传统基准测试的补充，提供对实际部署能力的早期预警。

Abstract: Benchmark-based evaluation remains important for tracking frontier AI progress. But it can both overstate and understate deployed capability because it privileges tasks that can be precisely specified, automatically graded, easy to optimize for, and run with low budgets and short time horizons. We advocate for a complementary class of evaluations, which we term open-world evaluations: long-horizon, messy, real-world tasks assessed through small-sample qualitative analysis rather than benchmark-scale automation. In this paper we survey recent open-world evaluations, identify their strengths and limitations, and introduce CRUX (Collaborative Research for Updating AI eXpectations), a project for conducting such evaluations regularly. As a first instance, we task an AI agent with developing and publishing a simple iOS application to the Apple App Store. The agent completed the task with only a single avoidable manual intervention, suggesting that open-world evaluations can provide early warning of capabilities that may soon become widespread. We conclude with recommendations for designing and reporting open-world evals.

</details>


### [205] [AgentAtlas: Beyond Outcome Leaderboards for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20530)
*Parsa Mazaheri,Kasra Mazaheri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgentAtlas是一个用于评估大型语言模型智能体的新框架，通过六状态控制-决策分类、九类轨迹失败分类、分类感知/盲测方法论及基准覆盖审计四个组件，系统性解决现有智能体评估基准碎片化问题。实验表明，当移除提示中的显式标签引导后，模型性能大幅下降且趋于一致，揭示了模型能力中有相当部分依赖提示监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体评估基准碎片化严重，每个基准强调不同的测量单元(最终任务成功率、工具调用有效性、重复通过一致性、轨迹安全性或攻击鲁棒性)。2024-2025年的研究共同诊断出单一准确率指标不再是评估可部署智能体的正确比较单位。

Method: AgentAtlas框架包含四个核心组件：(i)六状态控制-决策分类体系(Act/Ask/Refuse/Stop/Confirm/Recover)；(ii)九类轨迹失败分类体系，包含两个正交层级标签(primary_error_source, impact)；(iii)分类体系感知与分类体系盲测方法论，用于衡量模型能力中有多少来自提示中的监督；(iv)基准覆盖审计，将15个智能体基准映射至六个行为轴。实验采用固定八模型集合(1,342个生成项，四闭源前沿+四开源权重模型)进行双提示模式对照实验。

Result: 移除显式标签菜单后，所有模型的轨迹准确率下降14-40个百分点，降至0.54-0.62的紧密区间，无论模型家族；无单一模型在控制准确率、轨迹诊断和工具上下文效用保留三个维度上均获胜。

Conclusion: 移除明确的标签菜单后，所有模型的轨迹准确率下降14-40个百分点，降至0.54-0.62的紧密区间；没有单一模型在控制准确率、轨迹诊断和工具上下文效用保留三个维度上全面胜出。该研究作为测量协议演示，而非基准测试发布。

Abstract: Large language model agents now act on codebases, browsers, operating systems, calendars, files, and tool ecosystems, but the benchmarks used to evaluate them are fragmented: each emphasizes a different unit of measurement (final task success, tool-call validity, repeated-pass consistency, trajectory safety, or attack robustness). A line of 2024-2025 work has converged on the diagnosis that a single accuracy column is no longer the right unit of comparison for deployable agents. AgentAtlas extends this line of work with four components: (i) a six-state control-decision taxonomy (Act / Ask / Refuse / Stop / Confirm / Recover); (ii) a nine-category trajectory-failure taxonomy with two orthogonal hierarchical labels (primary_error_source, impact); (iii) a taxonomy-aware vs. taxonomy-blind methodology that measures how much of a model's apparent capability comes from the supervision in the prompt; and (iv) a benchmark-coverage audit mapping fifteen agent benchmarks against six behavioral axes. To demonstrate the methodology we run a small fixed eight-model set (1,342 generated items, four frontier closed and four open-weight) under both prompt modes. Removing the explicit label menu drops every model's trajectory accuracy by 14-40 pp to a tight 0.54-0.62 floor regardless of family, and no single model wins on all three of control accuracy, trajectory diagnosis, and tool-context utility retention. We treat the synthetic run as a measurement-protocol demonstration, not a benchmark release.

</details>


### [206] [Personality Engineering with AI Agents: A New Methodology for Negotiation Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20554)
*Michelle A. Vaccaro,Jared R. Curhan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"人格工程"方法论，利用AI智能体精确参数化、操作和评估谈判者人格，从而突破传统研究中人类受试者的局限。核心框架采用人际 circumplex（温暖与支配两维度），为谈判理论检验和AI谈判智能体设计提供统一坐标系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统谈判理论强调共情与主张、关切自我与他人的平衡，但人类被试在精确控制这些变量时存在固有局限，导致理论难以在严格条件下验证。AI智能体具备精确性、可复制性和可扩展性，为克服这一方法论瓶颈提供了新途径。

Method: 采用人格工程方法论，具体使用人际 circumplex 模型作为基础坐标系统，以温暖（warmth）和支配（dominance）两个核心维度参数化谈判者人格特征，从而实现对谈判者行为的精确操纵和评估。

Result: 建立了可用于严格检验经典谈判理论的实验范式，同时提供了设计AI谈判智能体人格的实践指南。

Conclusion: AI智能体为谈判研究开辟了新的实验类别，能够在可控条件下精确测试理论预测；人际 circumplex 为整合谈判理论与AI智能体设计提供了统一框架。

Abstract: According to canonical negotiation theory, people's success in a negotiation depends on how well they balance competing demands--empathizing and asserting, demonstrating concern for other and concern for self, being soft on the people and hard on the problem. Yet people struggle to manage these tensions, so researchers have lacked the ability to rigorously test the field's prescriptions under controlled conditions. AI agents do not face the same limitations, and their precision, repertoire, consistency, and scalability enable a new class of experiments to contribute to negotiation theory. In this article, we introduce personality engineering: a methodology that uses AI agents to precisely parameterize, manipulate, and evaluate negotiator personality. We propose using the interpersonal circumplex--and its two core dimensions of warmth and dominance--as a foundational coordinate system for the field. This approach offers both a rigorous methodology for testing classic negotiation theories and a practical guide for designing the personalities of AI negotiation agents.

</details>


### [207] [Mahjax: A GPU-Accelerated Mahjong Simulator for Reinforcement Learning in JAX](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20577)
*Soichiro Nishimori,Shinri Okano,Keigo Habara,Sotetsu Koyamada,Eason Yu,Masashi Sugiyama*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Mahjax是一个基于JAX的全向量化立直麻将环境，支持GPU大规模并行 rollout，实现每秒200万步（无红牌规则）和100万步（有红牌规则）的吞吐量，旨在支持从零开始的强化学习研究，提供可视化工具并验证了强化学习训练的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有麻将AI高度依赖人类对局数据的监督学习预训练，限制了泛化能力；AlphaZero系列证明从零学习的潜力，但缺乏专门的麻将强化学习环境支持此类研究。

Method: 基于JAX实现全向量化环境，支持GPU并行rollout；提供高质量可视化工具用于调试和交互；使用强化学习验证环境可用性。

Result: 在8块NVIDIA A100 GPU上，无红牌规则达200万步/秒，有红牌规则达100万步/秒；强化学习训练的智能体能有效提升对基准策略的排名。

Conclusion: Mahjax为立直麻将从零学习研究提供了高效、可扩展的基础设施。

Abstract: Riichi Mahjong is a multi-player, imperfect-information game characterized by stochasticity and high-dimensional state spaces. These attributes present a unique combination of challenges that mirror complex real-world decision-making problems in reinforcement learning. While prior research has heavily relied on supervised learning from human play logs to pre-train the policy, algorithms capable of learning \textit{tabula rasa} (from scratch) offer greater potential for general applicability, as evidenced by the AlphaZero lineage. To facilitate such research, we introduce \textbf{Mahjax}, a fully vectorized Riichi Mahjong environment implemented in JAX to enable large-scale rollout parallelization on Graphics Processing Units (GPUs). We also provide a high-quality visualization tool to streamline debugging and interaction with trained agents. Experimental results demonstrate that Mahjax achieves throughputs of up to \textbf{2 million} and \textbf{1 million steps per second} on eight NVIDIA A100 GPUs under the no-red and red rules, respectively. Furthermore, we validate the environment's utility for reinforcement learning by showing that agents can be trained effectively to improve their rank against baseline policies.

</details>


### [208] [COAgents: Multi-Agent Framework to Learn and Navigate Routing Problems Search Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20618)
*Oleksandr Yakovenko,Mahdi Mostajabdaveh,Cheikh Ahmed,Abdullah Ali Sivas,Xiaorui Li,Zirui Zhou,Mao Kun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: COAgents是一个用于求解车辆路径问题(VRP)的合作式多智能体框架，将搜索过程建模为图结构并通过三个智能体（节点选择、移动选择、跳跃）协作指导搜索，实现问题无关搜索控制与领域特定编码的分离，在VRPTW上创下学习类方法新SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统VRP求解的启发式方法依赖手工设计规则进行局部改进和跳跃逃逸，但难以跨不同实例泛化；现有端到端学习方法虽然有一定效果，但缺乏对搜索过程的灵活控制能力，且泛化性不足。

Method: 提出COAgents框架，将VRP求解的搜索过程建模为图结构，其中节点代表解、边代表局部优化或大扰动（跳跃）。动态构建部分搜索图(PSG)以训练三个协作智能体：Node Selection Agent（选择候选节点引导集中化）、Move Selection Agent（选择移动操作进行局部优化）、Jump Agent（触发探索新区域进行多样化）。该架构将问题无关的搜索控制与紧凑的领域特定编码清晰分离，增强了跨任务适应性。

Result: 在CVRP基准上与学习搜索类基线方法具有竞争力；在更具挑战性的VRPTW实例上达到学习类方法新SOTA：N=100时相对POMO提升14%、相对ALNS提升21%；N=50时相对POMO提升44%、相对ALNS提升40%。

Conclusion: COAgents提出了一种将搜索过程建模为图的合作式多智能体框架，动态构建部分搜索图(PSG)来指导三种智能体的协作决策，实现问题无关的搜索控制与领域特定编码的分离。实验表明该方法在CVRP上具有竞争力，在更具挑战性的VRPTW上创下学习类方法的新SOTA，在N=100和N=50规模下分别将POMO和ALNS的最佳解差距缩小14%/21%和44%/40%。代码已开源。

Abstract: Although Vehicle Routing Problems (VRP) are essential to many real-world systems, they remain computationally intractable at scale due to their combinatorial complexity. Traditional heuristics rely on handcrafted rules for local improvements and occasional \textit{jumps} to escape local minima, but often struggle to generalize across diverse instances. We introduce \textbf{COAgents}, a cooperative multi-agent framework that models the search process as a graph: nodes represent solutions, and edges correspond to either local refinements or large perturbations for diversification (i.e., jumps). A \textit{Partial Search Graph} (PSG) is dynamically constructed during search, enabling COAgents to train a Node Selection Agent and a Move Selection Agent to guide intensification, and a Jump Agent to trigger well-timed explorations of new regions. Unlike end-to-end learning approaches, COAgents cleanly separates problem-agnostic search control from compact domain-specific encoding, facilitating adaptability across tasks. Extensive experiments on the CVRP and VRPTW benchmarks show that COAgents remains competitive with several learn-to-search baselines on CVRP and sets a new state of the art among learning-based methods on the more challenging VRPTW instances, reducing the gap to the best-known solutions by 14\% at $N\!=\!100$ and 44\% at $N\!=\!50$ relative to the strongest neural solver (POMO), and by 21\% and 40\% respectively relative to ALNS.
  Code is available at https://github.com/mahdims/COAgents.

</details>


### [209] [Evaluating Temporal Semantic Caching and Workflow Optimization in Agentic Plan-Execute Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20630)
*Alimurtaza Mustafa Merchant,Krish Veera,Sajal Kumar Goyla,Shambhawi Bhure,Dhaval Patel,Kaoutar El Maghraoui*

Main category: cs.AI
